mahārthamañjarī

svopajñaparimalākhyavyākhyopetā

maheśvarānandapraṇītā

namo nālayate śuṇḍāṃ viṣāṇena mṛṇāline |
pratyakkamalakandāya karṇābhyāṃ parṇaśāline || 1 ||

jayatyamūlamamlānamauttaraṃ tattvamadvayam |
spandāspandaparispandamakarandamahotpalam || 2 ||

kāruṇyāmṛtasindhoruditamivāvartamīkṣaṇāpāṅgāt |
mauktikamayaṃ dadhānā tāṭaṅkaṃ jayati gauravī mūrtiḥ || 3 ||

sphūrtaye viśvaśilpasya śrīśivānandamūrtaye |
nityonmeṣanimeṣāyai nistuṣāyai namastviṣe || 4 ||

yasmādanuttaramahāhradamajjanaṃ me
	saubhāgyaśāmbhavasukhānubhavaśca yasmāt |
tatsvātmacitkramavimarśamayaṃ gurūṇā-
	movalliyugmamuditoditavīryamīḍe || 5 ||

namo nikhilamālinyavilāpanapaṭīyase |
mahāprakāśapādābjaparāgaparamāṇave || 6 ||

gorakṣo lokadhiyā deśikadṛṣṭyā maheśvarānandaḥ |
unmīlayāmi parimalamantargrāhyaṃ mahārthamañjaryām || 7 ||

svakriyāyā api vyākhyāṃ svayameva prayuñjmahe |
uparyapyātmasaṃrambhasambhogāmreḍanotsukāḥ || 8 ||




yadvā vineyajanacittacamatkriyārtha-
	matrodyamo'yamudito'stu tadetadāstām |
saṃkṣepavistaravibhāgaviviktaśobhaḥ 
	puṣpāñjalirbhavatu vāṅmaya eṣa śambhoḥ || 9 ||

avagataśivadṛṣṭipratyabhijñārthatattva-
	kramasaraṇirahasyollāsasarvasvavedī |
gurucaraṇasaparyācāturīcidghano'haṃ
	gahanamapi hṛdantarvyoma tad vyākaromi || 10 ||

iha mahati rahasyonmīlane maṅgalāya 
	prabhavati mama saṃvidyoginīnāṃ prasādaḥ |
api tu kulasaparyābimbasaṃbandhyavandhyāḥ
	sakṛdapi matimanto nainamudghāṭayantu || 11 ||

svapnasamayopalabdhā sā sumukhī siddhayoginī devī |
gāthābhiḥ saptatyā svāpitabhāṣābhirastu samprītā || 12 ||

vardhatāṃ deśikaḥ śrīmān saṃvinmārgaśca vardhatām |
māheśvarāśca vardhantāṃ vardhatāṃ ca maheśvaraḥ || 13 ||

atha yadetadātmasvarūpāvibhinnaparameśvaraparāmarśopāyaprati-
pādanapravṛttamabhyupagamasiddhāntasthityā tātparyataḥ 
pratijñādyava(yava)pañcakātmakaṃ mahārthamañjaryāhvayaṃ mahat 
tantram, atra sūtrāyamāṇā gāthāḥ saptatirbhavanti | tatra cādyāyāṃ 
maṅgalācārapūrvakamādivāakyopakṣepaḥ | dvitīyāyāṃ 
tantrapratipādyasya vastuno nirdeśaḥ | tatastisṛṣu svātmatattve 
pramāṇānupayogaprapañcanam | ṣaṣṭhyāmadhikārivibhāgavyapohaḥ | 
saptamyāṃ vidhiniṣedhaniṣṭaṅkanam | aṣṭamyāṃ 
saṃsārasvarūpanirūpaṇam | navamyāṃ svātmanaḥ 
sphuṭasyā'pyasphuṭatvaucityānuśāsanam | daśamyāṃ 
vimraṣtṛsvarūpavimarśasya puruṣārthatvāvasthāpanam | tato 
dvayorvimarśasvarūpavimarśaḥ | tatastrayodaśasu ṣaṭtriṃśattattvavivekaḥ 
| ṣaḍviṃśyāmuktārtha prati paramārthaparyālocanam | 
saptaviṃśatitamyāṃ viśvasya prakāśavimarśāntarbhāvodbhāvanam | 


 


aṣṭāviṃśyāmekatraiva vastuni śivasaktivibhāgādhyavasānam | 
ekonatriṃśyāṃ parameśvarasya viśvaśarīratayā 
śaktyutkarṣopapādanam | triṃśyāṃ viśvavaicitryasya 
svātmanyavaikalyenāvasthānapradarśanam | ekatriṃśyāṃ 
pramātrāditrikasyādvaitaparyavasāyitvonmīlanam | tato dvayoḥ 
satyāsatyavibhāgavyudāsaḥ | tato'pi dvayoḥ parameśvarasaparyāyā 
vaiśiṣṭyāvabhāsanam | tataśca ṣaṭsu pūjyatayā 
śrīpañcārthakramāvamarśaḥ | tataśca pañcasu saparyāyāḥ 
svarūpaniṣkarṣaḥ | tato'pi dvayordevatāsvabhāvanirṇayaḥ | 
ekonapañcāśyāṃ mantratattvoddhāraḥ | pañcāśyāṃ vāgvṛttivicāraḥ 
| ekapañcāśyāṃ mudrāsatattvonmudraṇam | dvipañcāśyāṃ 
vimarśaśakterbhogāpavargaphalapradatvolliṅganam | tripañcāśyāṃ 
jīvanmuktyupapattiḥ | catuṣpañcāśyāṃ kṣaṇabhaṅgavādabhaṅgaḥ | 
pañcapañcāśyāṃ svātmasvarūpasyānandaspandatānuvarṇanam | 
tataścatasṛṣu tattvāvabodhaṃ prati sphuṭa upāyopadeśaḥ | tataśca ṣaṭsu 
vimarśānupraveśināṃ naiścintyaniścayaḥ | ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamyāṃ 
svātmavimarśasya sadyaḥ siddhidatvapratyāyanam | saptaṣaṣṭitamyāṃ 
vimarśalābhasya gurukaṭākṣādhīnatvaprakhyāpanam | 
aṣṭaṣaṣṭitamyāmuktārthasya sarvadarśanasāratvasādhanam | 
ekonasaptatitamyāṃ tantravistarasya saṃgraheṇopanyāsaḥ | saptatitamyāṃ 
vyāsādiyogināmapyanuttarārthaikaśaraṇatvaprakāśanam | antyāyāṃ 
tantrakṛtastantropadeśahetuprayogaḥ | iti tantrārthatattvatātparyārthaḥ |

atha grantho vyākhyāyate | śrīmadanuttarādvaitasiddhihetordvaita-
prathāsatattvapratyūhavyapohadakṣaṃ deśikendrabhaṭṭāraka-
svātantryamanusaṃdadhānastantrakṛt tantropanyāsaṃ 
pratyupodghātamudghāṭayati -

natvā nityaśuddhau guroścaraṇau mahāprakāśasya |
grathnāti mahārthamañjarīmimāṃ surabhi maheśvarānandaḥ || 1 ||

iha khalu sarvasyāpi janasyopāsyatayā kācid 
devatāstyevetyatrāvipratipattiḥ | kevalaṃ tasyā 
nāmarūpādivyapadeśamātre vaiṣamyam | sā ca yuktiparyālocanāyāṃ 





svātmasaṃvitsphurattāmātrasvarūpeti prakāśa eva viśvopāsyā 
devatetyāpatitam | tasya ca mahattvam -

	sā sphurattā mahāsattā deśakālāviśeṣiṇī |
	saiṣā sāratayā coktā hṛdayaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ ||

iti śrīpratyabhijñānītyā sarvasaṅkocollaṅghitayā 
sphurattaikasvarūpatvam | sa ca guruḥ gṛṇāti prakāśayati 
viśvavyavahāramiti niruktyā sarvānugrāhakaḥ | tādṛkprakāśakavyatireke 
viśvasyāndhabadhiratādiprāyatāpatteḥ | sa ca paryantataḥ 
paramaśivabhaṭṭārakāparaparyāyaḥ svātmarūpo mahān pramātā | 
yaduktaṃ śrīśivasūtreṣu - gururupāyaḥ iti | tasya caraṇau 
jñānakriyālakṣaṇaṃ svātantryaṃ caryate gamyate prāpyate budhyate 
bhakṣyate cābhyāṃ viśvamiti hi caraṇāvityucyete | yaduktaṃ 
śrīkubjikāmate -

	bhogyabhoktṛṣu bhāveṣu miṣatsvanimiṣatsu ca |
	deśakāladigākhyeṣu sthūlasūkṣmapareṣu ca ||
	sattāsphuraṇakavyāptā gatibhakṣaṇayogataḥ |
	karmaṇā caraṇākhyeyaṃ na tu pādatayā priye ! || iti |

	tṛtīyastu caraṇaḥ sākṣād guruḥ svalakṣaṇa eveti dvivacanena 
vyākhyā | yaduktaṃ tatraiva -

	sito vāme'ruṇo dakṣe varābhayakaro guruḥ |
	pretāmbujagato'ṅkastharaktaśaktistrilocanaḥ ||
	pañcamuṇḍadharaḥ sragvī hṛdi dhyeyaḥ smitānanaḥ | iti |

	ayameva sakalaniṣkalātmā sarvavidhūnanāvasthāyāṃ 
nirvāṇarūpasturīyaḥ pādaḥ ityupaniṣat | laukiko vyavahārastu sakalena 
niṣkalena ca dvābhyāmeva caraṇābhyāmupakalpyate | yadāhuḥ -

	śuklo'ṅghriḥ śuklamāviṣṭo raktaṃ rakto'ṅghrirāśritaḥ |
	pitroraṅghridvayenedamamunā jāyate'khilam || iti |





tau ca nityaśuddhau unmeṣanimeṣavibhāgavyudāsena 
padārthāntarapratibimbanakṣamau | etadeva hi parameśvarasya tat 
pāramaiśvaryam, yadantarbahiśca 
jñānakriyāpratibimbanānuprāṇitāśeṣaviśvavilāsatvam | tacca 
svātantryaspandasphurattodyamādiśabdairāgameṣūdghoṣyate | 
nityaśuddyā ca tayorvakṣyamāṇavimarśānuprāṇitatvaṃ pratyāyyate | 
yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	vimarśa eva devasya śuddhe jñānakriye yataḥ || iti |

tau natvā utkarṣakakṣyārūḍhatayā vimṛśya | vāṅmanaḥkāyānāṃ 
tadekaviṣayīkāralakṣaṇaḥ prahvībhāvo hi namanam | mahāprakāśatvaṃ 
ca parameśvarasya mahānutkarṣaḥ | taṃ prati tadupāsakasya 
prahvībhāvaśceti dvitayamapi kaṇṭhenoktam | 
yajananamaskārādyupanyāsamātre tu 
ekatarasyābhidheyatvamanyatarasyārthākṣiptatā ceti pratipatṝn 
pratyubhayapratipattisaukaryaṃ na sambhavati | ayaṃ ca mahān 
maṅgalācāro yat parameśvarotkarṣānusmaraṇam, yasya ca tantrārambhaṃ 
pratyavaśyakartavyatvam | yaduktaṃ śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	sarvāśaṅkāśaniṃ sarvālakṣmīkālānalaṃ tathā |
	sarvāmaṅgalyakalpāntaṃ mārgaṃ māheśvaraṃ numaḥ || iti,

	vyāpārāḥ siddhidāḥ sarve ye tvatpūjāpurassarāḥ | iti ca |

	etena maṅgalādīni maṅgalamadhyāni maṅgalāntāni ca śāstrāṇi 
prathante | vīrapuruṣāṇi bhavantyāyuṣmatpuruṣāṇi ca iti 
mahābhāṣyamaryādayā dvitrapradeśāvacchinnamaṅgalānāmapi 
śāstrāṇāmūrīkāryatvam | kimuta 
pratigāthānubhūyamānaparameśvaraiśvaryotsavatayā 
dhanasāraśakalaparimalavat sarvatraiva 
maṅgalyollāsamudagramunmīlayato'sya mahātantrasyetyāsūtryate | etacca 
vyākhyātṛśrotṛśikṣāmātratātparyāt tantropodghāte 
sākṣādupanibaddham, anyathā parameśvarapraṇāmasya kriyāmātrādapi 
pratyūhavyapohasiddheḥ | uktarūpaṃ ca 
parameśvaraiśvaryotkarṣānusandhānamanusandhātustādātmyamevopa-
sthāpayati | yathoktaṃ śrīśivadṛṣṭau -





	asmadrūpasamāviṣṭasvātmanātmanivāraṇe |
	śivaḥ karotu nijayā namaḥ śaktyā tatātmane || iti |

ata eva hi natveti parameśvarapraṇāmakriyottarakālaṃ maheśvarānanda 
ityuktam | maheśvarānando hi nāma śrīmatsadāśivaparyantāśeṣa-
śuddhāśuddhādhikārānupraviṣṭapramātṛparamparāprabhāvasarva-
svābhibhāvī nityānavacchinnaprakāśānandaparamārthasvātantrya-
lakṣaṇaḥ paramaśivabhaṭṭāraka eva | tadbhāvāpanno'yaṃ tantrakṛditi 
yāvat | tadbhāvāpattiśca tasya deśikakaṭākṣapātaśaktipāta-
saubhāgyasya samayānupraveśamaṅgalamahābhiṣekasampatsaṃskāro-
pārohaprakṣālitamalopalepatvāt, auttarāmnāyādyaśeṣaśāstrārthā-
vabodhaśikṣāśālitvāt, svasya ca parameśvaraiśvaryotkarṣavimarśānu-
syūtyupakṣīṇāntaḥkaraṇatvāccetyavagantavyam | yaduktaṃ śrīniśāṭane 
- tripratyayamidaṃ jñānam iti | taccoktaṃ śrīkiraṇāyām - gurutaḥ 
śāstrataḥ svataḥ iti | tataśca yo'vipastho jñāhetuśca, 
dānamātmajñānam iti śrīśivasūtrasthityā 
svayamaparokṣitātmīyapāramaiśvaryollāsasya 
parānujighṛkṣāveśavaivaśyena tantrakṛtastantronmīlanaṃ 
pratyaucityamāsutryate | anyathā bāhyaśāstrapraṇetṛvad 
vipralambhakatāmātrameva paryavasyet | yaduktaṃ śrīhaṃsabhede -

	sevyante guravo'neke jñānavijñānabhāsurāḥ |
	durlabho'yaṃ gururdevi yo'haṅkārakṣayaṅkaraḥ ||
	tenādhītaṃ śrutaṃ tena tena sarvamanuṣṭhitam |
	yenāhaṅkāranirmuktaḥ kevalo viditaḥ svayam || iti |

bāhyaśāstrāṇāṃ tu -

	anyathā devapāṇḍityaṃ śāstrapāṇḍityamanyathā |
	anyathā tatpadaṃ śāntaṃ lokāḥ paśyanti cānyathā ||

iti sthityā sākṣāt svātmaparāmarśopāyatvaṃ na sambhavati | yathā 
śrītantrāloke -



	ye'pyasākṣādupāyena tadrūpaṃ pravivindate |
	nūnaṃ te sūryasaṃvittyai khadyotādīcchavo jaḍāḥ || iti |

yathā ca śrīkulakamale -

	śāktena tejasā śūnyaṃ ye mārgaṃ paryupāsate |
	te vahnirahite kuṇḍe sthālyā homaṃ prakurvate || iti |

tatra ca tāratamyaṃ kiñcidālocanīyam | yaduktam -

	nararṣidevadruhiṇaviṣṇurudrādyudīritam |
	uttarottaravaiśiṣṭyāt pūrvapūrvaprabādhakam || iti |

sa ca maheśvarānando mahārthamañjarīṃ grathnāti | 
viśvataduttarobhayaspandatayā pūrṇāhambhāvasvabhāvasyārthasya 
arthyate iti vyutpattyā sarvaprārthyasya vastunaḥ 
śivaśaktyādibhedaprathāprarohe'pi pāramārthikādvaitaprathāsatattvāṃ 
sphurattāmupadarśayati | granthasandarbhadvārā 
lokamanubhāvayatītyarthaḥ | mañjaryapi hi pṛthak 
puṣpabhedapratibhāse'pyekākārā'nubhūyate | grathanaṃ ca 
puṣpāderupeyasyānāyāsagrahaṇopāyatayā prasiddham | sā ca surabhiḥ, 
sarvābhilaṣaṇīyaprakāśarūpasaurabhāspadatvāt | anena ca viśvasya 
bhogyatayā'vasthāpanād vakṣyamāṇasya 
vimarśopāyasyaucityamunmīlyate | yato 
nirbhayabhogyamityetattantramāmnāyate | yaduktaṃ śrīsiddhāmate -

	arthaṣaṭkaṃ ca dīkṣā ca śivaśāstramiti smṛtam |
	dīkṣādhvā nirbhayo bhogaśāstre bhairavasaṃjñake || iti |

	imāmiti | 
prākaṭyotkarṣādanapahnavanīyāmatisulabhāsvādāmanunmīlitapūrvā 
cetyarthaḥ | yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

iti prakaṭito mayā sughaṭa eva mārgo navo
	mahāgurubhirucyate sma śivadṛṣṭiśāstre yathā || iti |

atra ca yo'yaṃ mahārthamañjarīparyāyo viśvavaicitryavilāsaḥ, sa eva ca 
vakṣyamāṇasya pratyabhijñāparaparyāyasya vimarśasyopāyatayā 
tantrapratipādyo'rthaḥ | yataḥ 
śabdasparśādyanubhavastadanubhavitṛdvārā mahāprakāśaparyāye 
kvacinmahānubhavitari 





paryavasyati | tatparijñānaṃ ca prayojanam | tatprayojanaṃ ca 
vimarśasvarūpasiddhiḥ | tasyā api prayojanaṃ bhogāpavargalakṣaṇaḥ 
svasya māheśvaryollāsaḥ | upari ca prayojanāntarāpekṣā, tasyaiva 
prāpyatayābhilaṣyamāṇatvādanavasthāprasaṅgācca | yacchrutiḥ - 
ātmalābhānna paraṃ vidyate iti | saṃbandhaśca tantrasya pratipādyasya 
cābhidhānābhidheyabhāvaḥ | pratipādyasya cidvimarśasya 
copāyopeyabhāvaḥ, tayoranyonyaṃ kāryakāraṇabhūtatvāt | tantrasya 
pratipattṝṇāṃ ca sambandho guruparvakramātmā vyaktamupalabhyate, 
natvā nityaśuddhau guroścaraṇāvityupakrāntatvāt, desi akaḍakkhapāde 
ityupasaṃhāriṣyamāṇatvācca | tattvadṛṣṭyā tu svātmarūpa eva 
sarvo'yaṃ sambandhaḥ | yathā śrīsvacchande -

	guruśiṣyapade sthitvā svayaṃ devaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
	pūrvottarapadairvākyaistantraṃ samavatārayat || iti |

yathā coktamācāryābhinavaguptanāthapādaiḥ -

	sadābhinavaguptaṃ yat purāṇaṃ ca prasiddhimat |
	hṛdayaṃ tat parollāsaiḥ svayaṃ sphūrjatyanuttaram || iti |

svayamavagatārthatattvasya ca tantrakṛtaḥ kāruṇyotkarṣapravṛttena 
sarvajanasāmarasyalakṣaṇena tāṭasthyena 
grathnāmītyavicchinnāhambhāvasvabhāvottamapuruṣavyapohād 
grathnātīti tantropapādane saṃrambhodbhāvane vineyavargasya 
tantrapravṛttyaunmukhyaṃ vidhīyamānatvenārthataḥ siddham | etena 
tantrakṛtaḥ prasiddhaprabhāvasya saṃbhāvanāpratyayotpādanena 
pratipattṛjanapravṛttyartha nāmopādānamityapyāsūtryate | pratipatṝṇāṃ 
coktamartha pratyadhikāritāyāṃ na kaścinniyamaḥ | yaduktaṃ śrīspande -

	labdhvā'pyalabhyametajjñānadhanaṃ hṛdguhāntakṛtanihiteḥ |
	vasuguptavacchivāya hi bhavati sadā sarvalokasya || iti |

	kiñca, natvā guroścaraṇāvityanena śrīcaraṇavidyāsaṅketo'pyatra 
kiñcidunmudryate | so'pi paryālocyamānaḥ -

	vidyeti mātṛkāpīṭhaṃ tat pārthivamudāhṛtam |
	maṇḍalaṃ kuṇḍalīpīṭhaṃ tadāpyaṃ parikīrtitam ||





	mantrasaṃjñaṃ kriyāpīṭhaṃ taijasaṃ tat prakīrtitam |
	jñānapīṭhaṃ tu mudrākhyaṃ tad vāyavyaṃ sureśvari ||

	parecchāmukhato vyoma pīṭhatveneha nādṛtam |
	tanmūlasyopacārasya bāhyasyābhāvataḥ priye ||

	ceṣṭātmako bhaved vāyustataḥ syānnatiradhvare |
	dīpaḥ syāt taijasastoyaṃ caruriṣṭo rasātmakaḥ ||

	pṛthivyā gandhavat puṣpamupacārāya pādayoḥ ||

iti śrīcaraṇasūtranītyā śrīpañcavāhakramamevānupravekṣyati, 
sarveṣāmapi śrīrasānvayānāmatraiva tātparyāt | 
tadvyākhyānavaidagdhyaprakāśanaṃ tu nātyantamupayujyate | natveti ca 
nateḥ kaṇṭhoktyā mantramaṇḍalāderapi vāyuśaktitattvāyāḥ 
svaparispandanānuprāṇanāyā 
mudrāyāstantrāntarārambharūpodyogānuguṇyāt prādhānyaṃ 
pratyāyyata ityalamupakrama eva prasaktānuprasaktikaprācuryeṇa | upari 
prapañcayiṣyamāṇāśeṣatantrārthasūkṣmaśarīraprāyeyaṃ gāthā | ata 
evātiprapañcyamāneyamatiprasaṅgāya bhaviṣyatīti saṃkṣipyaiva 
vyākhyātā | uktārthaprapañcopapādanaṃ ca tatra tatrāgrata 
udbhāvayiṣyate || 1 ||

	atha tantrapratipādyamarthatattvaṃ tatparijñānasya 
prayojanatayā'nveṣyatāṃ cābhidadhānastantrakṛt tatraivāvṛttyā 
tatprayojanamātmavimarśasvarūpamapyupeyatayopapādayati -

	vardhatāṃ mahāprakāśo vimarśavicchuritaniśchalodyotaḥ |
	saṃjñāviśeṣanirṇayamātrapravṛttāni yatra śāstrāṇi || 2 || iti |

	atra yo'yaṃ mahāprakāśastattatpramātṛprakāśāveśādhīna-
prakāśamānatāsvabhāvamātrapāriśeṣyāt svarūpaniṣkarṣe kriyamāṇe 
-

	tattadrūpatayā jñānaṃ bahirantaḥ prakāśate |
	jñānādṛte nārthasattā jñānarūpaṃ tato jagat ||





	nahi jñānādṛte bhāvāḥ kenacid viṣayīkṛtāḥ |
	jñānaṃ tadātmatāṃ yātametasmādavasīyate ||

iti śrīdevikākramasthityā prakāśaikasvabhāvaḥ 
ṣaṭtriṃśattattvasaṃpiṇḍanātmā 
pratyekatattvaparyālocane'pyanantaprakārāyamāṇo viśvavilāsaḥ, sa 
vardhatām uparyupari svasphurattāmanubhavatu | ayameva mahānupāyo 
vakṣyamāṇasya vimarśasyeti yāvat | nanu śūnyatāmātrasvabhāvena 
mithyātvamātrānuprāṇitavyavahāreṇa vā viśvavaicitryeṇopāyabhūtena 
paramārthabhūtasvātmarūpavimarśalābha 
ityetadanaucityotkarṣakāṣṭhāprāptiriti 
mādhyamikānirvacanīyavādamaryādāmāśaṅkyāha - niśchalodyota iti | 
sphuṭaprakāśātmani prapañcodyote na kasyacid 
mithyātvopapādakādeśchalasyāvakāśa ityarthaḥ | astvevam, 
tathāpyavaghātasvedādivadanyathāsiddhasānnidhyena lokavyavahāreṇa 
kathamātmavimarśotpattirityāśaṅkyāha - vimarśavicchuriteti | na khalu 
svasattāmātreṇa viśvasyātmavimarśa pratyupāyatvam, api tu tathā tathā 
vimṛśyamānāvasthāyāmeva | tatra ca viśvarūpasyeva 
tanmithyātvādestadviparyayasya ca vimraṣṭavyatvodbhāvanāya 
niścchalodyota ityasya vimarśavicchuritetyanena sahaikapadyam | nanu yadi hi 
viśvavyavahārasya kiñcinnaiyatyaṃ tadupapadyetāpi nāma tasyopāyatvam 
| tacca na sambhavati | adyāpi siddhāntibhirdravyaguṇādayaḥ ṣaḍiti, 
pramāṇaprameyādayaḥ ṣoḍaśeti, rūpavedanādayaḥ pañceti ca pṛthak 
pṛthag vikalpyamānatvādityāśaṅkyāha - saṃjñāviśeṣetyādi | yatra 
viśvavilāsātmanyarthe kāṇādīyākṣapādīyaprabhṛtīni śāstrāṇi 
tattatparibhāṣānuguṇyena saṃjñālakṣaṇā ye 
vyapadeśaviśeṣāstanmātropakṣīṇavyāpārāṇi, na punaḥ 
pratyakṣādipramāṇopagṛhyamāṇapṛthivyādipadārthaparamparāpari-
spandāpalāpapragalbhānītyādyavākyārthaḥ | dvitīye tu yo'yaṃ 
mahāprakāśa uktalakṣaṇo'navacchinnaḥ pramātā, yadvimarśaḥ 
puruṣārthatayā'vasthāpayiṣyate, sa vardhatām | 
māyīyamalopalepasaṃskārocchedaparyantamullasatu | sā ca vṛddhirna 
kaivalyena | kiṃ tarhi ? evamahamalaukikaḥ kaścit pramāteti 
pratyabhijñānātmā yo vimarśaḥ, tena yad vicchuraṇaṃ 
viśeṣato'nyaprakāśavailakṣaṇyena laukikasambandhasvabhāvātikrāntyā 





tādātmyaparyavasāyitayopaśleṣaḥ, tadvattayā niśchalo 
nirgalitopādhikalaṅka udyotaḥ sphurattā yasya tādṛśatayā 
vardhatāmityarthaḥ | darpaṇādiprakāśānāṃ hi 
pratibimbādiprakāśanakṣamatve'pi 
pramatṛprakāśasāpekṣatvādastyupādhimattvam | pramātṛprakāśasya 
tvananyamukhaprekṣitvamevetyarthaḥ | etaduttaratrāpi bhaviṣyati | nanu 
vimraṣṭavyasyātmasvarūpasya vyapadeśanaiyatyābhāvāt tadvimarśaṃ 
pratyasadbhāvaparyavasāyī kaścid hṛdayaṅgamībhāvaḥ 
syādityāśaṅkyāha - saṃjñāviśeṣetyādi | yatra paramapramātṛviṣaye 
vyavahriyamāṇāni sarvāṇyapi śāstrāṇi śivo viṣṇurbuddha 
ityādivyapadeśamātravyāpṛtāni, na 
punararthasvabhāvātyantabhedopapādanapravīṇāni bhavanti | tathāhi - 
cārvākāstāvad bhūtānyeva cetayanta iti caitanyaviśiṣṭameva 
śarīramātmānamācakṣate | asadevedamagra āsīt 
ityabhāvabrahmavādinaḥ śūnyatābhimānino 
mādhyamikāścānākhyākakṣyāyāmiva 
svātmamātrasphurattālakṣaṇamātmatattvamāhuḥ | sāṅkhyādayastu 
vijñānākalaprāyatāmasyāṅgīkurvate | śabdabrahmavādinaśca 
paśyantīpadābhimāninaḥ śrīmatsadāśivatattvabhūtamenaṃ manyante | 
mīmāṃsakā api sukhādyuparāgayogyamātmānaṃ manvānāḥ 
saṃvitsvabhāvatāmasya nāpahnuvate | naiyāyikādayo 
jñānādiguṇagaṇāspadamenamicchanti | saugatāḥ 
punarjñānalakṣaṇasantānarūpamenaṃ saṃgirante | 
evamanyatrāpyūhyamiti śrautāśrauteṣu sarveṣvapi siddhānteṣu 
nātyantamatra visaṃvādaḥ | kintu spṛṣṭāspṛṣṭikayā'vasthānam, 
tannibandhanamātraśca vādināmanyonyakalahakolāhalakleśaḥ | taduktaṃ 
mayaiva śrīpādukodaye -

	ato viśvātmako nāthaḥ ityārabhya

	lokāyatānāṃ dehātmā śūnyātmā śūnyavādinām |
	kṣaṇātmā kṣaṇaniṣṭhānāṃ bhinnātmā bhedadarśinām ||
	mīmāṃsināmapūrvātmā brahmātmā brahmavādinām |
	advaitināmabhedātmā bahunā vā kimucyate ||
	ākrīḍanaṃ ca bālānāṃ sa eva paramo guruḥ |
	sa eva ca mahārthātmā mahārthikamanīṣiṇām || iti |





	tathā ca śrīpratyabhijñāhṛdaye - tadbhūmikāḥ 
sarvadarśanasthitayaḥ iti | ata eva hi -

	sa hi bhairavasiddhantapūrvapakṣo'pi yanmayaḥ |

iti paryantapañcāśikāyāmuktam | evaṃ ca vastuvṛttyā siddhāntibhiḥ 
sarvairapi parameśvarasya viśvātmakatvasvabhāvamaiśvaryamevodghoṣyate 
| yanmayaivoktaṃ śrīsaṃvidullāse -

	anyonyamapratīkārā bādhyabādhakabhāvataḥ |
	vadanti tava vaiśvātmyaṃ vādivipratipattaye ||

iti | etaduktaṃ bhavati - bahuvidhasiddhāntavikalpyamānānyonya-
bādhyabādhakabhāve'pi viśvāvabhāse stambhakumbhādayo bhāvā 
āpāmarapaṇḍitaṃ pratyakṣamevānubhūyanta ityatra na kācid vipratipattiḥ 
| anubhūyamānatvaṃ ca teṣāmanubhavanakriyāviṣṭatvam | sā ca kriyā 
sāmānyānatilaṅghisvabhāvatayā pādapacchedādiriva paraśvadhādinā 
kenacit karaṇaviśeṣeṇa vinā na saṃgacchata ityetat tāvadaṅgīkāryam | 
tataśca grāhyagrahaṇavelāyāṃ cakṣurādyavaśyambhāvaḥ | taduktaṃ 
mayaiva saṃvidullāse -

	avināśini maṅgalapradīpe manasi prajvalite mahāprakāśe |
	bahirindriyagolakairgavākṣairaviśeṣādavabhāsyate trilokī || iti |

cakṣurādicakraṃ ca bahiṣkaraṇamātrasvaspandāspadatvāda-
vikalendriyasyāpi pramāturaudāsīnyādyavasthāsvarthāvabhāsā-
saṃbhavācca kiñcidantaḥkaraṇamapekṣata 
ityetadapyavaśyābhyupagantavyam | tataśca manovimarśasyāvarjanīyatvam | 
taduktamasmadgurubhirmanonuśāsanastotre -

	haṃsāḥ patanti gamane pravisārya pakṣau
		svau sarvadaiva kila mānasarājahaṃsaḥ |
	atyadbhutā tava gatirgagane parasmin
		vikṣepaśūnyamayapakṣayugaṃ vihāya || iti |



karaṇānāṃ ca kartṛvyatireke kāryakāritvānaucityāt 
svasvabhāvāvasādaprasaṅgācca 
stambhakumbhādiparyālocanakriyāyāmantaḥ kaścit kartṛviśeṣo'pyarthata 
ākṣipyate | yaduktamasmatparamagurubhiḥ śrīsaubhāgyahṛdayastotre -

	netrādijālakopānte hṛtpadmāsanalīlayā |
	vāraṃ vāraṃ tvayā devi ! rūpādi madhu sevyate ||

sa ca vedyavikalpoparaktatvād deśakālādyavacchedamanubhavati | 
tadavacchedollaṅghinā ca bhāvymalaukikena kenacit pramātṛviśeṣeṇa, 
anyathā viśvavaicitryasyaiva viparyayaprasaṅgāt | viśvavyavahāro 
hyavaccinnānavacchinnapramātṛdvayāṅgīkārādṛte na saṃgacchate | 
tatrā'kalpitaḥ pramātā sphurattaikasvabhāvatayā vedyavargasya 
prakāśaikārṇavībhāvamudbhāvayati | kalpitastu stambhaḥ kumbha 
ityādibhedaprathopaśleṣaṇaprāgalbhyāt 
tattadvikalpavikṣobhasvabhāvabhāvānāṃ 
vyavahāravibhāgamudbhāvayatītyubhayāvaśyambhāvaucityam | evaṃ ca 
grāhyagrahaṇavelāyāmindriyādipraṇāḍikayā 
viśvapratiṣṭhābhūmirakṛtrimaḥ pramātā kaścidantarvimraṣṭavya iti 
pāramparyādāpatitam | yathoktamajaḍapramātṛsiddhau -

	idamityasya vicchinnavimarśasya kṛtārthatā |
	yā svasvarūpe viśrāntirvimarśaḥ so'hamityayam || iti,
	prakāśasyātmaviśrāntirahambhāvo hi kīrtitaḥ | iti ca |

yathā coktamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ - 
sphuṭatarabhāsamānanīlasukhādipramātranveṣaṇadvāreṇaiva 
pāramārthikapramātṛlābha ihopadiśyate iti | evamabhiprayeṇaiva hi 
śrīvijñānabhairave -

	gītādiviṣayāsvādasamasaukhyaikatātmanaḥ |
	yoginastanmayatvena manorūḍhistadātmatā ||

ityādyupāyatayopapādyate | smārtādayo'pyatra nātyantaṃ visaṃvadante | 
yadāhuḥ -

	vīṇāvādanatattvajñaḥ svaraśāstraviśāradaḥ |
	tālajñaścāprayāsena mokṣamārgaṃ nigacchati || iti |

vardhatāmiti loṭpratyāyuktyā 



	khyātimapurṇāṃ pūrṇakhyātisamāveśadārḍhyataḥ kṣapaya |
	sṛjabhuvanāni yathecchaṃ sthāpaya hara tiraya bhāsaya ca ||

ityādivadupāyopeyabhāvaṃ dyotayantyā 
parameśvarasyātyantadurghaṭaghaṭanaprāgalbhyalakṣaṇaṃ 
svātantryamāsūtryate | yadanena darśanāntarapraṇetṛmanīṣayā 
saṃsāranibandhatvenopakalpitaḥ śabdasparśādiviṣayavikṣobho 
jīvanmuktilakṣaṇāhlādasvabhāvaṃ svātmano vimarśaṃ 
pratyupāyatayopadiśyate | tathā ca tantrānte vakṣyate -

	o saṃsārasuhellī o sulahaṃ mokkhamaggasohaggam | iti 

	asya copāyopeyabhāvasyāvyabhicārasvabhāvasāmarasyod-
bhāvanāya yāvadgati yatitavyamiti nītyā vākyadvayaikyāvabhāsātmanā 
tantrāparaparyāyeṇa śleṣeṇopanyāsaḥ | evamatrābhidheyaṃ prayojanaṃ 
tatprayojanaṃ ca vitatyonmīlitam | pāryantikaṃ tu prayojanaṃ hi a a 
ṭṭhāṇaparūḍho iti prapañcayiṣyate |

	nanūktayuktyā vimraṣṭavyaḥ kaścit kartṛviśeṣaḥ kulālādivad na 
kutracidaparokṣamupalakṣyate, anumeyatvādau tu tatra pramāṇādhīnā hi 
vastusthitiriti sthityā tādṛk kiñcit pramāṇaṃ vaktavyam | tadanuktau ca 
tasyāsatkalpatvāpattirityāśaṅkyāha -

	ātmā khalu viśvamūlaṃ tatra pramāṇaṃ na ko'pyarthayate |
	kasya vā bhavati pipāsā gaṅgāsrotasi nimagnasya || 3 ||

	ihātmaiva hi prakāśasvabhāvatvād viśvavyavahāre nibandhanam, 
mamāvabhāsate mayāvalokyata iti pramātṛprakāśopaśleṣeṇaiva 
stambhakumbhādīnāṃ prakāśamānatvāt | tadvyatireke ca teṣāṃ stambha 
eva kumbhaḥ kumbha eva stambha ityanayoranyonyaṃ 
svalakṣaṇāpahāropakrame na kasyacidapi niyantṛtvamiti stambharūpeṇa 
stambhaḥ kiṃ bhavatyastambho veti sandeho vā tasyāpyasambhavo veti 
sarvathā tūṣṇīmbhāva eva svabhāvaḥ syād bhuvanavyavahārasya | yadi 
ca stambhādirūpatayaiva teṣāṃ prakāśamānatvaṃ na punaḥ 
pramātṛprakāśānugrahātmā 



kaścidatiśayaḥ, tarhi sarveṣāmapi tathā prakāśeran, na vā kasyacidapīti 
pramātṝṇāṃ vyavahārocchedaprasaṅgaḥ | kiñca, 
stambhakumbhādyātmano viśvasya svātmanaiva 
prakāśamānatāṅgīkāre sarvasyāpi pramātṛvargasya 
sārvajñyamatyantājñatvaṃ vā prasajyeta, niyāmakābhāvāt | 
tasmādātmano jagadvyavahāraprayojakatvamanicchatā'pyaṅgīkāryam | 
prayojyaprayojakabhāvaśca paryantato'nayostādātmyameva 
paryavasāyayiṣyatītyātmaprakāśamayo'yaṃ viśvavartivedyavargollāsa 
ityāpatitam | yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	prāgivārtho'prakāśaḥ syāt prakāśātmatayā vinā |
	na ca prakāśo bhinnaḥ syādātmārthasya prakāśatā || iti |

khaluravipratipattau | evaṃ sthite tatraivātmanīdametādṛgiti 
vastuvyavasthāpakatvalakṣaṇaṃ svābhāsaviśeṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ prati 
paṇḍitasya vā pāmarasya vā na kasyacidapekṣotpadyate, 
pramātṛprakāśādavyatirekāt | pratyuta tenaiva vyavasthāpyamānatvācca 
pramāṇasya | pramāṇaṃ hi nāma navanavodayaḥ svābhāsaviśeṣaḥ | 
so'pi svābhāso'bhinava ityuktatvāt | navanavodayatvaṃ ca tasya 
tattaddeśakālopaśleṣāvacchidyamānānyonyavilakṣaṇaprameyadvāro-
pārūḍhaḥ kaścid viśeṣaḥ | na punaḥ svābhāviko dharma 
ityavadhīritākhilavikalpavikṣobhamakṣubhitābdhikalpamekasvabhāva-
mātmatattvaṃ pratyasya vyāpāra eva nopapadyata ityarthaḥ | na 
ko'pyarthayata iti | ātmani pramāṇamicchannātmā syānna vā | 
yadyanātmā jaḍavargaḥ, tatpramāṇāpekṣaiva nopapadyate | ātmaiva cet, 
tarhi svasmin svayameva pramāṇamapekṣata ityāpatet | yadāhuḥ -

	akhyātiryadi na khyātiḥ khyātirevāvaśiṣyate |
	khyātiścet khyātirūpatvāt khyātirevāvaśiṣyate || iti |
	
pramātṛtāspade tatra pramāṇaṃ nopayujyate |
	karaṇāt karturādhikyamanyairabhyupagamyate ||
	
	svatantraḥ khalu karteti śābdikairapyudīryate |
	tacca svātantryamanyebhyaḥ kārakebhyaḥ pradhāntā ||





	yatprabhāvāt pramāṇānāṃ pramāṇatvavyavasthitiḥ |
	tatraiva tadapekṣeti na kiṃ vyarthaṃ vaco bhavet ||

	laukikānāṃ pramāṇānāmanāśvāsāddhi yauktikaiḥ |
	īśvarasya pramāṇatvaṃ pramātṛtve'pyudāhṛtam ||

	pramātari pramāṇaṃ kimiti pṛcchan pramātṛtām |
	vijahāti na vā nādyaḥ praśnasyānupapattitaḥ ||

	na dvitīyaśca kalpaḥ syāt pramāṇaṃ kimu visphuret |
	iti hi praśnatātparyaṃ tatrātmanyaikyasammatau ||

	mayi tvayīti bhedo'yaṃ na kadācana yujyate |
	nānātmatve svaviṣayaṃ pramāṇaṃ prati bhāvanā ||

	pṛcchyate prativādīti mahanmaurkhya prasajyate |
	bhrāntiścedātmani bhrāntyā buddhe rajjubhujaṅgavat ||

	vācyaṃ pramāṇamityetadunmattavacanāyate |
	atha nāstyeva me tatra pratītiriti kathyate ||

	tatra pramāṇaṃ pṛcchyeta kīdṛg vottaramucyatām |
	agocaraḥ pramāṇānāmata ātmā maheśvaraḥ ||

yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	pramātari purāṇe tu sarvadābhātavigrahe |
	kiṃ pramāṇaṃ navābhāsaḥ sarvapramitibhāgini || iti |

tathā copaniṣat - vijñātāramare kena vijānīyāt iti |

	svapadā svaśiraśchāyāṃ yadvallaṅghitumīhate |
	pādoddeśe śiro na syāt tatheyaṃ vaindavī kalā || iti |

śrītantrāloke'pi -

	pramāṇānyapi vastūnāṃ jīvitaṃ yāni tanvate |
	teṣāmapi paro jīvaḥ sa eva parameśvaraḥ || iti |

	uktamarthaṃ dṛṣṭāntadṛṣṭyā pratipādayati - kasya vetyādi | gāṅgo 
hi pravāhaḥ śaityaprasādādyanekaguṇotkarṣādantarbahistāpāpahāra-
pravīṇo bhavati | 





tatraiva sarvāṅgīṇāplavapūrvakamaghamarṣaṇādyavasthāsu ko nāma 
narastṛṣitaḥ syāt | na kaścidapi tathā syāditi yāvat | pipāsetyanena 
gaṅgāsrotonimagnasyāpi yadi kasyacit salilapānecchā, kastatra pātuṃ 
pratyūha iti pratyāyayatā dārṣṭāntike'pi sthale 
viśvavyavahāranibandhanaṃ parameśvaraṃ prati pramāṇajijñāsāyāṃ 
pratyakṣādīni sarvāṇyapi pramāṇāni sambhavanti, na punareṣāmatra 
kācidapekṣetyudbhāvyate | etena -

	tanvādi buddhimatkartṛ sanniveśaviśeṣavat |
	ghaṭavad yadanevaṃ tannaivaṃ khaparamāṇuvat ||

ityādipramāṇopapādanopakṣīṇasyeśvarasiddhyādeḥ prabandhasya 
pravṛttivaiphalyaśaṅkākalaṅko'pyuṭṭaṅkita ityavagantavyam || 3 ||

	nanu stambhakumbhādivadātmanaḥ prakāśo na kadācidapi 
sphuṭatayopalabhyate | asphuṭe cārthe 
pramāṇavyāpāreṇaivāvataritavyamityāśaṅkyāha -

	yaṃ jānanti jaḍā api jalahāryo'pi yaṃ vijānanti |
	yasyaiva namaskāraḥ sa kasya sphuṭo na bhavati kulanāthaḥ || 4 ||

	yaṃ vaiśvātmyena prasiddhimantaṃ prakāśātmano vīreśvarā 
ivānudriktaprakāśā jaḍā ābhīrādayo'pi jānanti, yaṃ ca vimarśamayyo 
vīreśvarya iva vaidagdhyābhāsaśālinyo 
ghaṭadāsīprabhṛtayo'pyavabudhyante | sarveṣāmapi sthūlo'haṃ 
sampanno'hamityādeḥ svātmasphuraṇasya sphuṭamevopalabhyamānatvāt | 
yacchrutiḥ -

	utainaṃ gopā adṛśan adṛśannudahāryaḥ iti |

vimarśaprādhānyājjalahārījñānaṃ prati vaiśiṣṭyamuktam | 
jñānaśaktyeva pramātṝṇāṃ kriyāśaktyā'pyayaṃ kroḍīkriyata ityāha 
- yasyaiva namaskāra iti | jaḍajalahāryādirhi sarvo'pi 
jīvavargastattatphalakāṅkṣayā tatra tatra namaskurvāṇo 



lakṣyate | sa sarvo'pi namaskāro yatsambandhenaiva bhavati, yathā śrutiḥ - 
yasmai namastacchiraḥ iti | sarvasyāpi svātmaiva devatetyagre bhaviṣyati | 
yataḥ - 

	tvamevātmeśa ! sarvasya sarvaścātmani rāgavān |

iti śrīmatstotrāvalyāmuktam | atha ca jaḍāḥ stambhakumbhādayo 
bhāvāḥ, jalahāryaḥ śabdasparśādyādānakṣamā indriyaśaktayaḥ, te'pi 
yajjānantīti parameśvarasya prākaṭyotkarṣa upapādyate | yataḥ 
stambhakumbhādayo'pi tattatpramātṛviṣayīkāradvārā 
jñānakriyāśrayatayā niścīyante | yaduktam - draṣṭaiva hi tato jagat iti | 
kartaivetyasyāpyupalakṣaṇametat | yat prayuktaṃ śambhvaikyadīpikāyām - 
viśvamidaṃ manmayam, matkāryatvāt | yaditthaṃ tat tathā | yathā śarīram 
| idaṃ ca mama kāryaṃ tasmānmanmayam iti | 
tadvaccakṣurādīndriyāṇyapi pramātṛparibarhatayaiva tathā tathā 
bhavantīti | uktarūpaśca sa parameśvaraḥ kulasya 
dehākṣabhuvanāderviśvavilāsasya tādātmyaparya vasāyitayā 
smṛtyādinirvāhako bhavan kasya padārthasya sphuṭo na bhavati | kasyeti yadi 
kaściducyeta sa eva svabhāvato na syādityarthaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - 
viṣayendriyādervedyaviacitryasya gopālajalahāryādeḥ pramātṛvargasya ca 
-

	samalo vimalo vāpi vyavahāro'nubhūyate |

iti śrīpratyabhijñāprakriyayā grāmaṃ gacchati, sandhyāmupāste 
ityādilaukikaśāstrīyavyavahārasvabhāvaṃ jñānakriyāyugalameva 
sākṣāt pāramparyeṇa vā'nuprāṇanamavalokyate | yathā 
śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	tathāhi jaḍabhūtānāṃ pratiṣṭhā jīvadāśrayā |
	jñānaṃ kriyā ca bhūtānāṃ jīvatāṃ jīvanaṃ matam || iti |

te ca jñānakriye mametyahamiti vā'hambhāvavyatireke kutrāpi nopapadyete 
iti sarvāhambhāvasvabhāvaṃ parameśvaraṃ pratyasphuṭatvaśaṅkāyā na 
kaścidavakāśa iti || 4 ||

	evaṃ sāmānyataḥ pramāṇānāmanupayogamātmanyupapādya 
viśeṣato'pyupapādayannāgame kañcidanugrahaṃ darśayati -



	avacchinnaṃ pratyakṣamavacchinnaṃ tanmukhaṃ cānumānam |
	āgamadīpālokastasya prakāśayati kimapi māhātmyam || 5 ||

	grāhyārthasannikarṣa eva pratyakṣasya prāmāṇyamityatyantametat 
saṅkucitam | anumānaṃ ca vyāptyupalambhasāpekṣatvād 
vyāptyupalambhasya ca pratyakṣamūlatvādavacchinnameva | 
arthāpattyāderatrāntarbhāvo vaiśeṣikādibhirabhyupagataḥ | anantarbhāve 
vā tasya saṃkocaḥ kalpakādyupalambhavikalpāt svayamupakalpanīyaḥ | 
evaṃ cāvacchinnānāṃ pramāṇānāmanavacchinne svātmani pravṛttiriti 
mahatīmanaucityakakṣyāmavataratīti prāgapyuktam | āgamastu 
yadyāptamātravākyatvāt prāmāṇyamarhati, tarhi tasyāptiḥ kīdṛgiti 
cintāyāmanyasmādāptāt, tasyāpyanyasmāditi 
mūlakṣayakāriṇyanavasthetyaprāmāṇyameva | māṇikyādiparīkṣāvat 
tricaturakakṣyāviśrāntau tu saiva saṅkocāya kalpate | yadi punaḥ 
āgamastvanavacchinnaprakāśātmakamāheśvaravimarśaparamārthaḥ 
ityācāryābhinavaguptoktasthityā 
parapramātṛvimarśaśaktimayatayāyamaṅgīkriyate, tadānīmasau tasya 
svātmanaḥ svabhāvabhūte prakāśe pradīpollekha iva tamaḥsthagite 
stambhakumbhādāvabhivyañjakatayā kañciccamatkāramupajanayati | 
tatrāpi yathā dīpālokena dhvāntagarbhavartināṃ 
stambhādīnāmārohapariṇāhādisvabhāva unmīlyate, tadvadasyāpi 
svātmano yanmāhātmyamanavacchinnasvabhāvatvaṃ 
svavyatiriktāśeṣabhuvanākṣādyanuprāṇanakṣamatvaṃ 
viśvataduttirṇatvalakṣaṇasvalakṣaṇadvitayasāmarasyasampatpātratvam, tat 
kimapyalaukikaṃ tattvamāgamenonmudryate | 
yaduktamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ -

	yaṃ prāpya sarvāgamasindhusaṅghapūrṇatvamabhyeti kṛtārthatāṃ 
ca |

	taṃ naumyahaṃ śāmbhavatattvacintāratnaughasāraṃ 
paramāgamābdhim || iti |

atha yadi śākhāśikhāyāṃ śaśāṅkamaṇḍalamityādivadupacārādatra 
pramāṇavyavahārakautūhalam, tadā svasminnātmani pratyakṣaṃ 
paratrānumānaṃ sarvatrāgama ityalaṃ 
tantrāntarapānthaghaṇṭāpathaprasthānena || 5 ||




	nanu pramāṇapathātivṛttaṃ cedidamātmatattvam, tat sarvo'pi 
janastadvimarśa pratyadhikārī na kaścid vā 
syādapramitatvāviśeṣādityāśaṅkya nātra kaścidadhikārivibhāgakleśa 
ityāha -

	yeṣāṃ nirupaṇīyo vyatiriktaḥ ko'pyātmano bhāvaḥ |
	ātmavimukhānāṃ teṣāmadhikārivibhāgavibhramo bhavatu || 6 ||

	iha yeṣāṃ puṃsāṃ svātmarūpāt parameśvarād vyatirikto bhavan 
kaścijjyotiṣṭomādiḥ padārtho nirūpaṇīyo vibhāvanīyo bhavati, 
teṣāmanyapadārthaunmukhyāvinābhūtādātmavaimukhyādeva 
hetorayamatrādhikārī na tvayamiti yo vibhramo viśiṣṭo bhramo 
vilāsarūpatvādaprāmāṇiko vyavahāro vā sa bhavatu | tatsadbhāve vayaṃ 
na pratibandhāraḥ | atisarge loṭ | tataśca teṣāmupālambhanīyatā ca dyotyate 
| yaduktamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ -

	ātmānamanabhijñāya vivektuṃ yo'nyadicchati |
	tena bhautena kiṃ vācyaṃ praśne'smin ko bhavāniti ||

	ko'pīti | asmanmaryādāyāṃ bhāvāntarasyātmavyatireka-
sphuraṇamātre satyapi paryantatastanmayatāyā evāvadhāraṇādātmano 
vyaktiriktaḥ ko'pi bhāva ityayuktaiva vācoyuktiḥ | yaduktaṃ 
saṃvitprakāśe -

	yadidaṃ dṛśyate kiñcid darśanāt tanna bhidyate |
	darśanaṃ draṣṭṛto nānyad draṣṭaiva hi tato jagat || iti |

āgame'pi -

	yāvanna vedakā ete tāvadvedyāḥ kathaṃ priye | iti |

athāpyupari kathyata ityarthaḥ | yeṣāṃ punaḥ svātmana eva bhāvaḥ 
svātantryasvabhāvaḥ kaścidatiśayito dharmo vimraṣṭavyatayā'vatiṣṭhate, 
teṣāmātmaunmukhyaśālināṃ na 
kadācidapyayamadhikārivibhāgavyākṣepaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - 
jyotiṣṭomādayo hi vibhāvanīyā arthā vibhāvayitṝn pramātṝn prati 
pṛthagbhāvena prasiddhāḥ | 





atasteṣāṃ svargakāmatvādiradhikārivibhāgaḥ kaścit saṃgaccheta | 
yeṣāṃ punarātmasvarūpameva nirūpaṇīyam, teṣāṃ 
nairātmyaśaṅkāyāmastvanadhikāritvam | tadabhāvācca 
kathamadhikāritāratamyamiti | etenehāmutrārthabhogavirāgādyapi 
nādhikārivaiśiṣṭyāyeti vyākhyātam, yata ihāmutrārthabhogodreka eva 
mokṣaḥ puruṣārthātmanaḥ svātmavimarśasya prayojanatayā 
pratipādayiṣyate | tarhi mumukṣutvamastvadhikāra iti ced ? na,

	vastusthityāṃ na bandho'sti tadabhāvānna muktatā |
	vikalpaghaṭitāvetāvubhāvapi na kiñcana ||

iti nyāyād bandha eva nāsti | kuto mokṣaḥ, kutastarāṃ tadicchā | astitve vā 
saiva kriyāśaktiparyantamujjṛmbhata iti vimarśasya tadicchāyāśca 
paryantato bhedābhāvāt prāpya evārtho'dhikāra ityuktaṃ bhavatīti || 6 ||

	nanu pramāṇādhikārivibhāgādyanupayoge vidhiniṣedhayoreva 
vaiyarthyaṃ syāt, viparyaye bhādhābhāvādityāśaṅkya tayoḥ 
svarūpamunmīlayati -

	yatra rucistatra vidhiryatreyaṃ nāsti tatra ca niṣedhaḥ |
	ityasmākaṃ viveko hṛdayaparispandamātraśāstrāṇām || 7 ||

	yadetadastīti jñānalakṣaṇo vidhirnāstīti jñānasvabhāvo 
niṣedhaśca codanārthaḥ | tatra sandhyopāstyādau vidhiḥ, 
kalañjabhakṣaṇādau niṣedhaścetyucyate | tatredamālocanīyam - 
anuṣṭhātṝṇāṃ pravṛttinivṛttiprayojanatayā hi vidhiniṣedhayorvyāpāraḥ, 
ajñātajñāpakatve satyapravṛttapravartako vidhiḥ ityuktatvāt | tatra kiṃ 
tajjñānamātreṇa te pravartante nivartante ca, uta svecchānuguṇyāt | yadi 
jñānamātreṇa, sandhyānupāsakaḥ kalañjabhakṣako vā na 
kaścidālokyeta | yadi tu svecchānuguṇyāt, tadanuṣṭhātṝṇāṃ rucimeva 
vidhiniṣedhāvanuvartete ityartho bhavati | tataśca teṣāṃ 
sandhyopāsanādāvarthe yatra rucistatra vidhiḥ, yatra vā 
kalañjabhakṣaṇādau na rucistatra ca niṣedha ityanayā bhaṅgyā 
sandhyopāsanādā -



vevāruciścet tatra ca niṣedhaḥ, kalañjabhakṣaṇādāveva ced rucistatra ca 
vidhirityarthatattvaniścayaḥ syāt | ityuktakrameṇāsmākaṃ 
pārameśvaravimarśaparyāyapūrṇo'hambhāvabhūṣitānāṃ viveko 
vidhiniṣedhayostātparyato niṣkarṣaḥ | etādṛśe ca tattvaniścaye tadeva naḥ 
śāstram, yat sarvasaṃvitsaṃghaṭṭanātmanā svahṛdayasya paritaḥ 
pṛthivyāditattvaparamparākroḍīkāraprāvīṇyena spandaḥ 
parāmarśātmā camatkāraḥ | pārameśvaraḥ parāmarśa eva hyāgama iti 
prāgapyavocāma | tadanuṣṭhātṝṇāmicchāśaktireva 
pravṛttinivṛttyaunmukhyād vidhirniṣedha iti vyapadeśabhedamanubhavatīti 
yāvat | yaduktaṃ saṃvitprakāśe -

	tyāgaḥ śakyakriyo yasya sa heya iti niścitaḥ |
	tyaktuṃ na śakyate yacca tadupādeyamityapi || iti |

mayā coktaṃ saṃvidullāse -

	prāmāṇikī vidhiniṣedhakathā yadi syāt 
		paryantataḥ parama eva śivaḥ pramāṇam |
	sarvottaraḥ sa khalu tatra vidhi niṣiddhe
		kartu kṣameta vihite ca vibhurniṣedham || iti |

etena śrutismṛtyāderapyasmattantrasya prāmāṇyotkarṣo vyākhyātaḥ | 
yathoktaṃ śrībhagavadgītāsu -

	evaṃ trayīdharmamanuprapannā gatāgataṃ kāmakāmā labhante iti |
	traiguṇyaviṣayā vedā nistraiguṇyo bhavārjuna iti ca |

yathā śrīvijñānabhairave -

	kiñcijjñairyā smṛtā'śuddhiḥ sā śuddhiḥ śambhudarśane |
	na śucirhyaśucistasmānnirvikalpo bhavennaraḥ || iti |

yathā ca śrīkālikāmate -

	patanti jantavo yena karmaṇā narake dhruvam |
	utpatanti ca tenaiva trikālajñā bhavanti ca ||



etadāśayenaiva hi śrīvijñānendukaumudyām -

	mano yatraiva viśrāntyā pūrṇabhāvamupāśnute |
	ataḥ paraṃ hi kiṃ nāma śubhakṣetraṃ bhaviṣyati ||

iti puṇyakṣetrādilakṣaṇamunmīlitam | etena -

	aprabuddhamatīnāṃ hi etā bālabibhīṣikāḥ |
	mātṛmodakavat sarva pravṛttyarthamudāhṛtam ||

iti śrīvijñānabhairavāṃśo vyākhyātaḥ || 7 ||

	nanu vidhiniṣedhādinā hi lokayātrā, tayośca svecchāmātrajīvitatve 
tasyā na kiñcidrūpamityuktaṃ bhavati | saiva ca saṃsāra ityucyate | 
tadullaṅghanaṃ ca puruṣārthaḥ | tatprayojanatayā pravṛttaṃ caitat tantraṃ 
kākadantaparīkṣāprāyamāpadyetetyāśaṅkyāha -

	paryālocanavimukhe vastusvabhāvasyātmano hṛdaye |
	śaṅkāviṣavegeneva saṃsārabhayena muhyati lokaḥ || 8 ||

	yo'yaṃ loko dṛśyavargavailakṣaṇyād draṣṭṛtvadharmā tata eva 
parameśvaravadātmano viśvaśarīratvapañcakṛtyakāritvādyaiśvarya-
yoge'pi paśutvābhimānī pramātṛvargaḥ, tasyāyaṃ prāyaśaḥ svabhāvo 
yadyuktitattvānveṣaṇe parameśvarābhinno'pi svayaṃ 
svecchāmātratvādahambhāvavadidambhāvamapyavagāhamānasya svasya 
yaddhṛdayamicchājñānakriyātmakaśaktitritayame(lāpa 
?la)narūpamantastattvam, tatparāmarśaṃ pratyaudāsīnyamavalambata iti | 
tacca tasya svacittasvabhāvādāpatitam | cittaṃ hi nāma 
caitanyapathāvarohāccetyasañcayāntaścaryācāturyopāttasaṅkocā-
cicchaktirityavadhāryate | cittamayatvaṃ cāsyā'vacchinnasya pramātuḥ 
tanmayo māyāpramātā iti śrīpratyabhijñāhṛdayamaryādayā 
saṃpratipannam | yataḥ śrīśivasūtreṣu caitanyamātmā itivat cittamātmā 
ityapyāṇavadaśaucityena punarupadiṣṭamiti 
svābhāvikamasyedamaudāsīnyam | ata eva cāyaṃ 
saṃsārājjananamaraṇā -



dirūpāllokayātrāvyavahārād bibhyad dvitīyād vai bhayaṃ bhavati 
ityupaniṣatprakriyayā bhedaprathopārūḍhaṃ cākityamudvahan muhyati | 
ātmanaḥ paramaiśvaryāvasthāsmṛtipramoṣādantaḥ saṃkliśyate | yathā 
sarpadaṃśābhāve'pi viṣāveśaśaṅkāśālī svasya manasi 
sarpabhramadāyino rajjvādeḥ padārthasya 
vastubhūtasvabhāvaparyālocanopekṣāyāṃ 
tāttvikasarpadaṃśavanmūrcchāmaraṇādikāmantarvyathāmupagacchati, 
tadvadityupamayā pratipāditamarthatattvaṃ hṛdayaṅgmīkriyate | uktarūpe 
cāsya mohe svaśaktaya eva pravartante | tāśca vāgbhūmau 
parāpaśyantyādimayyo vāci brāhmīmāheśvaryādayaḥ | saṃvitkrame 
svātmasphurattāsārā vāmeśvarīkhecaryādyāḥ | prāṇaparvaṇi ca 
śarīranirvahaṇopakṣīṇavṛttayaḥ prāṇāpānaprabhṛtayaḥ | tāsāṃ 
hyahantedantādvitayāvagāhanasāmarthyādātmasvarūponmīlanavat 
tadācchādane'pyaucityamasti | yadyapyasya paśvabhimatasyātmana 
icchādiśaktitritayānuprāṇanatvamaparihāryam, tathāpi yā etāḥ 
pārameśvaryāmavasthāyāmapratihatasvātantryalakṣaṇecchā 
viśvabhedaprathānurūpeṇa sārvajñyopabṛṃhitā jñānaśaktiḥ, tadvat 
sarvakartṛtvātmikā kriyāśaktiśca, tā eva paśudaśāyāṃ 
kūrmāṅgabhaṅgyā saṃkucantyaḥ 
kramādapūrṇatākhyātirūpamāṇavam, vedyabhedaprathāsvarūpaṃ 
māyīyam, śubhāśubhānuṣṭhānātmakaṃ kārmaṇaṃ ca 
malamunmīlayanti, tataścāyaṃ saṃsārītyucyate | enameva hi vyāmohaṃ 
pratyūhayituṃ sadācāryacaraṇarājīvasaparyā kāryatayodghoṣyate | yadā 
punaḥ sa eva lokaḥ svahṛdayasya vāstavaṃ svabhāvaṃ 
paryālocayitumunmukhībhavati, tadā na kaścit 
saṃsāraśabdasyārthatayopalabhyate | 
svasaṃrambhavijṛmbhātmakatayaivāsya vimṛśyamānatvāt | 
yathoktamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ -

	svatantraḥ svacchātmā sphurati satataṃ cetanaśivaḥ 
		parāśaktiśceyaṃ karaṇasaraṇiprāntamuditā |
	tadābhogaikātmā sphurati ca samastaṃ jagadidaṃ
		na jāne kutrāyaṃ dhvaniranupatet saṃsṛtiriti || iti |

yathā cādvayollāse -

	bhramaḥ saṃsāro yat kathamiva bhavenmuktiramuta-
		stvitīyaṃ yā vāñchā bata jaḍadhiyāṃ sā prasarati |




	tvadanyannāstīti prabhavadavikalpādvayamateḥ
		sthirīkāro mokṣo dvitayaghaṭanā saṃsṛtirataḥ || iti |

yathā ca saṃvitprakāśe -

	tasmād vikalpaḥ saṃśuddhād vijñānānnātiricyate |
	tenaiva nirvikalpo'yaṃ vikalpaḥ svātmani sthitaḥ || iti |

	sarvo vikalpaḥ saṃsāra ityukterayamāśayaḥ |
	tadasattvaṃ sṛteḥ sattvaṃ śuddhāyāḥ saṃvidaḥ sthitam ||

iti ca | atrāpyukta eva dṛṣṭāntaḥ | yathā - rajjvāditattvāvabodhe 
bhujaṅgādibhrāntyabhāvānmūrcchādyananubhava iti | ayamarthaḥ - 
sarvasyāpi janasya bāhyavyavahāravyatirekeṇa svahṛdayonmukhaḥ 
kaścidahamityudyogaḥ parisphurati | sa ca jānāmīti 
saṃvidviśeṣavapurevopapadyata iti jñānaśakteranapahnavaḥ | 
jānāmītyatra vimarśākārasaṃrambharūpā kācit kriyāpyarthākṣiptā | 
etaddvitayānuprāṇanāvasthāyāmicchāśaktivyapadeśa 
ityavyākulo'yamicchājñānakriyāsāmarasyātmā svabhāvaḥ, yo 
hṛdayamiti vyavahriyate | tasya ca vastunaḥ paryālocanāyāṃ vitarka 
ātmajñānam iti śrīśivasūtrasthityā sa evāhaṃ paramaśivabhaṭṭārakaḥ 
śaktitritayavattvāditi vimarśalakṣaṇā śaktirāvirbhavati | yaduddeśena 
śaktyāviṣkaraṇeneyaṃ pratyabhijñopadiśyate iti śrīpratyabhijñoktiḥ | 
yathoktamīśvarasiddhau -

	svātmaivāyaṃ sphurati sakalaprāṇināmīśvaro'ntaḥ
	kartā jñātāpi ca yadi paraṃ pratyabhijñāsya sādhyā | iti |

yatha coktaṃ śrīvijñānabhairave -

	sarvajñaḥ sarvakartā ca vyāpakaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
	sa evāhaṃ śaivadharmā iti dārḍhyācchivo bhavet || iti |

śrīśivadṛṣṭau ca -

	atha sthite sarvadikke śivatattve'dhunocyate |
	tasmin jñāte'thavā'jñāte śivatvamanivāritam ||



	bahnirjñāto'thavā'jñātaḥ prakāśaṃ janayenna kim |
	ajñātaṃ na suvarṇaṃ kiṃ tadā kimupalaṃ bhavet ||

ityākṣipya,

	satyameva tathāpīha suvarṇaṃ jñātamātrakam |
	mūlyādinopabhogāya dānārthamupayujyate ||

	cintāmaṇiravijñāto bhaveccintāmaṇiḥ sphuṭam |
	tathāpi kāryabhogārthamajñātaḥ kena dhāryate ||

ityādinopapāditam | etacca purastādapyudghāṭayiṣyate | evaṃ ca 
svātmanaḥ parameśvaratayā parāmarśāt parameśvarasya ca 
dharmigrāhakapramāṇaprābalyād viśvaśarīratayā'ṅgīkārāt 
saṃsāracākityādeśca svarganarakādivaicitryavad 
viśvāntarbhāvenopalabhyamānatvāt śarīriṇaśca śarīraṃ 
pratyahambhāvabhāvanotkarṣādabhedānubhavaikabhājanatvāt 

	parameśvara! teṣu teṣu kṛcchreṣvapi nāmopanamatsvahaṃ bhaveyam |
	na paraṃ gatabhītastvadaṅgasaṅgādupajātādhikasaṃmado'pi 
yāvat ||

iti śrīmatstotrāvalisthityā 
maheśvarādvaitasaṃvidāhlādāmṛtamahāhradāvagāhacamatkāracarbaṇa
cāturyameva paryavasyati | na punarbandhamokṣādivikalpavikṣobhā 
taṅkakalaṅkaśaṅkāpyatra saṃpadyate | yacchrutiḥ - taratiśokamātmavit iti 
| yathā ca śrīmadanubhavastotre -

	tvaṃ pṛthagvigatabāhyasaṃbhramastiṣṭhasīśvara! yadātmacityalam |
	lūnamūlakalanaiḥ kutastadā gamyatāmatanumāyitā guṇaiḥ || iti |

evaṃ sthite'pi -

	yathā nṛpaḥ sārvabhaumaḥ prabhāvāmodabṛṃhitaḥ |
	krīḍan karoti pādātadharmāstaddharmadharmataḥ ||
	tathā prabhuḥ pramodātmā krīḍatyevaṃ tathā tathā |

iti śrīśivadṛṣṭidṛṣṭyā svātmasvātantryollāsāt svahṛdayaparāmarśa -
śunyatvaṃ ca keṣāñcit saṃbhavati, yena saṃsārādivibhāgavyavahāraḥ | 
yathā 

 

śrutiḥ - adevād devaḥ pra ca tā guhā yan ityādi | atra ca 
śrīcidgaganacandrikā -

	hṛdguhāmabhilaṣannahantayā dṛkkriyāvapuradṛkkriyāt padāt |
	santamemi samabhāvadaṃ śivaṃ sakhyahāniramunā hi sūtaye || iti |

yathā ca samādhipañcadaśyām -

	yadetasyāparijñānaṃ tat svātantryopakalpitam |
	sa eva khalu saṃsāro jaḍānāṃ yastu bhīṣaṇaḥ || iti |

śrīsarvavīrabhaṭṭāarake'pi -

	ajñānācchaṅkate lokastataḥ sṛṣṭiśca saṃhṛtiḥ |
	mantrāḥ śivātmakāḥ sarve sarve varṇāḥ śivātmakāḥ || iti |

mokṣopāye ca -

	svāparāmarśamātro'yamaparādhaḥ kiyānasau |
	tāvanmātreṇa tajjātaṃ yad vaktuṃ naiva pāryate || iti |

uktarūpārthaparijñānameva mokṣaḥ, tadviparyayaśca bandha 
ityalamatiprapañcena || 8 ||

	nanvātmano vimarśamayaḥ kaścid viśeṣaḥ 
saṃsārādyaśeṣakleśopaśamanasāmarthyaśālitayonmīlitaḥ | sa ca 
tasyāsphuṭasvabhāvasya sphuṭīkārātmetyavadhāryate | na ca 
tatrāsphuṭatvaśaṅkā'pyavataritumarhati, atyantasphuṭatayopapāditatvāt | 
tataḥ pramāṇaprameyavyavahāravikalpasyānupayogo'tra vitatya vyākhyāta 
ityāśaṅkyāha -

	māṇikyapraveka iva nicolito nijamayūkhalekhayā |
	pratibhāti laukikānāmatyantasphuṭo'pyasphuṭa ātmā || 9 ||

ātmarūpo hi parameśvaraḥ 
prakāśotkarṣavattvādaśeṣabhuvanavyāpanakṣamatvācca 
viśvavikalpakalpanāmayīmarciḥprarohaparamparāmuparyuparyunmīlayan, 
tat sṛṣṭvā 

 


tadevānuprāviśat ityupaniṣatprakriyayā tathaiva nicolitaḥ kañcukitaprāyo 
nityamāste | tatra laukikānāṃ prameyatayālokyamāne viśvasmin 
vyavahartṝṇāṃ pramātṝṇām -

	atyantasvacchatā sā yat svākṛtyanavabhāsanam |

iti śrītantrālokasthityā prākaṭyodrekakakṣyārūḍhatayā 
vimraṣṭavyo'pyasau madhyāhnamārtāṇḍamaṇḍalanyāyena 
tattadraśmiparamparāsphuraṇaprācuryeṇa 
raśmimadgrahaṇāsāmarthyādaprākaṭyāvasthāmadhitiṣṭhati | yathā 
trāsādirāhityādatyantaślāghyo māṇikyopalakhaṇḍaḥ 
sphuradurumarīcimañjarīpiñjaritaparyantatayaiva 
māṇikyametadutkṛṣṭamiti nādhyavasituṃ śakyate, tadvadātmano'pi 
svaśaktiprasaratirohitatvādevāsphuṭatvaśaṅkā | 
evamubhayasvabhāvatāyāmevāsya viśvavyavahāraucityam | taduktaṃ 
mayaiva saṃvidullāse -

	arciḥprarohe mahataḥ pramāturnātisphuṭatve ca bahiḥ prathā me |
	apekṣate prekṣitumātmadarśaṃ sūryodayaṃ tadvyavadhiṃ ca lokaḥ 
|| iti |

iyaṃ ca laukikāneva mātṝn pratyupapadyate | taduktaṃ 
śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	kintu mohavaśādasmin dṛṣṭe'pyanupalakṣite |
	śaktyāviṣkaraṇeneyaṃ pratyabhijñopadiśyate || (iti),
	taistairapyupayācitairupanatastanvyāḥ sthito'pyantike
		kānto lokasamāna evamaparijñāto na rantuṃ yathā |
	lokasyaiṣa tathānavekṣitaguṇaḥ svātmāpi sannīśvaro
		naivālaṃ nijavaibhavāya tadiyaṃ tatpratyabhijñoditā || iti ca |

alaukikānantarmukhān prati tu -

	na dhyāyato na japataḥ syādasyā'vidhipūrvakam |
	evameva śivābhāsastaṃ numo bhaktiśālinam ||

iti śrīmatstotrāvalyuktireva yuktipathamanuvartata iti || 9 ||

	nanvātmanaḥ sphuṭatvamasphuṭatvaṃ 
cetyanyonyaviruddhametaddharmadvayaṃ kathaṃ nāma saṃgacchata 
ityāśaṅkya tasya tādṛśaṃ kiñcit svātantryamasti, yad vimarśa



ityākhyāyate, yadvattayā cāsya parāmarśaḥ puruṣārthatayā sarvathā 
sādhya ityupapādayati -

	ūrdhvo jvalati prakāśo lokālokasya maṅgalapradīpaḥ |
	vimarśadaśāmukhānītadahyamānamalālitailavicchardaḥ ||

	yo'yamahaṃ jānāmi mama sphuratītyādyaśeṣavyavahārānu-
syūtaḥ prakāśarūpo'rthaḥ, sa khalu lokyamānasya 
bhāvarāśeralokyamānasya khapuṣpādyaviśeṣamabhāvajātasya ca 
pradīpavat prakāśakatayā'nubhūyate | tathā loko mukhyayā vṛttyā 
prakāśasvabhāvatvāt pramātā, alokaśca tadadhīnaprakāśatvāt 
prameyam, grāhyagrahakobhayakoṭyupaśleṣāllokālokaḥ pramāṇamiti 
pramātrāditrikaparamārthasya viśvasya prakāśako bhavati | 
tādṛkprakāśābhāve viśvasyāndhatamasatvāpattiriti prāgapyavocāma | 
ata eva hyasau maṅgalatayopanyastaḥ | idameva hi tadatimahanmaṅgalam, yat 
stambhakumbhādīnāmanyonyasvabhāvāpahāraśaṅkāṃ 
nissvabhāvatvasaṃbhāvanāṃ ca vyapohya teṣāṃ 
tathābhāvenāvasthāpanam | sa ca ūrdhvaḥ svayaṃprakāśatayā 
prakāśyavargottirṇo bhavan jvalati sarvasaṃpratipattyā parisphurati | tasya ca 
vimarśākhyo'tiśayaḥ kaścit svabhāvatayā svīkartavyaḥ | anyathā 
darpaṇādiprakāśavadasya jāḍyakakṣyānupraveśaprasaṅgāt | yaduktaṃ 
śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	prakāśo'rthoparakto'pi sphaṭikādijaḍopamaḥ iti |

sa ca kāryavargavailakṣaṇyena prakāśasya kartṛtvākhyo dharma 
ityanantaramupapādayiṣyate |

	atra khalvativistīrṇe lokayātrāvijṛmbhite |
	vyavahriyante bahavo bhāvā bhūbhūdharādayaḥ ||
	vyavahāravidhisteṣāmasaṃkīrṇaśca lakṣyate |
	tatra kiṃ tannimittaṃ syādityālocayatāṃ satām ||



	saṃvittivyatirekeṇa naivānyadupalabhyate |
	tasyā vyapohaśaṅkāyāṃ bhūḥ svātmani na bhūrbhavet ||
	nāpyabhūrbhūdharādasyāḥ pṛthaktvaṃ ca kathaṃ bhavet |
	mitho rūpāpahāre ca phūtkartuṃ kasya kauśalam |
	evaṃ vedyasya pṛthvyāderasaṃkīrṇe prakāśane |
	sāmānyato viśeṣācca saṃvideva pragalbhate ||
	sāpi vedyavadanyena prakāśyā cet pravartate |
	prakāśastanmayo na syājjāḍyayogāviśeṣataḥ ||
	saṃvido'nyaprakāśyatve saṃvittvaṃ lupyate na vā |
	na ced vyarthaṃ tadanyat syād yena seyaṃ prakāśyate ||
	lupyate cet kimākārā sā tenāstu prakāśitā |
	tasyānyasya prakāśaśca kuta ityavalokane ||
	anyadanyaditi vyaktamanavasthitirāpatet |
	ato'syāḥ svaprakāśatvaṃ svabhāvādeva sidhyati ||
	prakāśaścoktayā bhaṅgyā svaprakāśo bhavannapi |
	bhāvamātrasvabhātve na jāḍyaṃ vedyavat tyajet ||
	svaprakāśo'pyasau kāñcit kartṛtāmaśnute na cet |
	anyathā tvarthavannāsya svabhāvaḥ syāt prakāśanam ||
	tataśca kartṛtā tasya svātmaviśrāntilakṣaṇā |
	anicchatāpi svīkāryā yāmahantāṃ vidurbudhāḥ ||
	pāryantikī pratiṣṭhābhūrvedyavargasya yo vibhuḥ |
	tasya svayaṃpratiṣṭhatvamahambhāvaḥ prakīrtyate ||
	vimarśaścāyameva syānmukhyamaiśvaryamīśituḥ |
	ataḥ svatantro bhagavān sarvaṃsaha iti sthitam ||

	tasya ca vimarśasya yā daśā sphuraṇāvasthā, tasyā mukhena 
aunmukhyayogena malānāmāṇavakārmaṇamāyīyānāmāliḥ 
pāramparyātmā sañcayaḥ prakāśena 



kartṛbhūtena dahyate, svātmānupravesitayā svīkriyate | tatra 
bhinnavedyaprathā māyā | tadāyattaṃ malaṃ māyīyam | karmaṇā 
puṇyapāpavāsanātmanā sambhūtaṃ kārmaṇam | aṇurnāma 
pūrṇāhambhāvaparāmarśaśūnyatvāt saṅkucitammanyo jīvaḥ | 
tadbhāva evāṇavam | taccānātmanyātmajñānamātmanyanātma-
jñānaṃ ca | etadāśayenaiva hi śrīśivasūtreṣvakārapraśleṣā-
praśleṣābhyāṃ caitanyamātmājñānaṃ bandha ityuktam | yaduktaṃ 
śrītantrāloke -

	caitanyamātmajñānaṃ bandha ityatra sūtrayoḥ |
	saṃśleṣetarayogābhyāmayamarthaḥ prakāśitaḥ || iti |

parameśvaro hi jñānakriyārūpatayā prakāśavimarśassvabhāvaḥ | tasya 
ca jñātṛtve vimraṣṭṛtve caikatarasminnapyaparāmṛśyamāne 
malamāṇavaṃ nāmotpadyata iti tātparyārthaḥ | yathā 
śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	svātantryahānirbodhasya svātantryasyāpyabodhatā |
	dvidhāṇavaṃ malamidaṃ svasvarūpāpahānitaḥ || iti |

sarvo'pi malollāsaḥ

	malamajñānamicchanti saṃsārāṅkurakāraṇam |

iti śrīmālinīvijayottarasthityā svaparāmarśānānmukhyamātrasvabhāvaḥ 
| sa ca -

	auṣṇyaṃ hutāśa iva śītalimānamindau 
		śayyāsu mārdavamivāśmasu karkaśatvam |
	bāhyeṣu mohamiva yogiṣu ca prabodhaṃ
	svātantryamasti hi niyantryayituṃ mahanme ||

iti saṃvidullāsasthityā pārameśvarasvātantryameveti vivekaḥ | etena -

	eko'pyanekaśaktirdṛkkriyayośchādako malaḥ puṃsām |
	tuṣakambukavad jñeyastāmrāśritakālikāvad vā ||

ityādinā malo nāmātmagataṃ kiñcid dravyamityācakṣāṇāḥ 
pratyākhyātāḥ | tatra coktamalatrayoparaktāḥ sakalā 
māyātattvāntarālavartino devādayaśca, 
āṇavakārmaṇamaladvayānubaddhāḥ pralayākalāḥ | āṇave ca 
teṣāmātmajñā -



tṛtvavimarśaśūnyatānibandhanaṃ malamityanusandheyam | teṣu ca keṣucid 
vedyabhedaprathopādhiko māyīyo'pi malaḥ saṃbhavati | anyeṣāṃ 
tvapavedyaprathānupraviṣṭānāmasau na vidyata iti viśeṣaḥ | ekamalā 
vijñānakalāḥ | tatrāpi tanmalaṃ svātmano 
vimraṣṭṛtvavimarśaśūnyatvāt | vidyeśvarāṇāmapi 
māyīyamātrānubandhādekamalatvameva | malakṣayopalakṣitā 
mantrādayaḥ | tatrāpi tatkṣayaunmukhyamātre mantrāḥ, tadupakrame 
mantreśvarā vidyeśvarāśca, yeṣāmīśvaro'bhimantā | 
kṣīyamāṇamalatve mantramaheśvarāḥ, yeṣāṃ ca sadāśivo'dhiṣṭhātā 
| vāsanāmātroparaktamalatāyāmanāśritaśivatattvam | 
sarvākārānutpannamalollāsastu bhagavān paramaśivabhaṭṭāraka eveti 
pramātṛvaicitryam | ānīteti | malatrayadāhasya cāyamupāyo 
yadvimarśamukhenāntaḥprāpaṇam | svātmānupraviṣṭānāṃ ca -

	yathā hi vahninā līḍhamindhanaṃ tanmayaṃ bhavet |
	evaṃ citā samālīḍhaṃ caityaṃ cinmayatāṃ vrajet ||

ityādinītyā tadvadbhāvāvaśyambhāvāt teṣāṃ malasvabhāvatvameva 
viparyasyatīti yāvat | yaduktaṃ śrībhagavadgītāsu -

	jñānāgniḥ sarvakarmāṇi bhasmasāt kurute'rjuna iti |

yaccoktaṃ mayaiva saṃvidullāse -

	vedyoparāgavibhavena vimohyamānaṃ
		lohāntaravyatikarādiva vyaktakārṣṇyam |
	jvālāvalībhirabhitapya vimarśavahniḥ 
		svacchaṃ hiraṇyamiva me hṛdayaṃ vidhatte || iti |

malaśaṅkāparityāga eva malakṣayopāya iti yāvat | taduktaṃ śrīniśākule 
-

	sphaṭikopalago reṇuḥ kiṃ tasya kurutāṃ priye |
	vyomnīva nīlaṃ hi malaṃ malaśaṅkāṃ tatastyajet || iti |

yathā kila caitramaitrāderjīvakadambakasya 
stambhakumbhāderbhāvarāśeśca prakāśako 
gṛhadevatādyupacāradvārā maṅgalyaḥ pradīpo 
vahniviśeṣatvādagnerūrdhvajvalanamiti nyāyādūrdhvo bhavan jvalati | 
yathā ca tasya vartimukhenānīya 



tailaprasaro dahyata iti rūpakaśleṣau | upamādyalaṅkāreṇa 
vākyavaicitryaṃ tadarthasya hṛdayaṅgamīkaraṇāyetyuktaprāyametat || 10 ||

	atha prakrāntaṃ vimarśasvarūpamātmanaḥ svabhāvatayā 
svayamevopapādayati -

	san hṛdayaprakāśo bhavanasya kriyāyāṃ bhavati kartā |
	saiva kriyā vimarśaḥ svasthā kṣubhitā ca viśvavistāraḥ || 11 ||

svahṛdayasya prakāśo hi sarvasyāstīti vaktavyam, asattve 
svavyāghātaprasaṅgāt | yathopaniṣat -

	asanneva sa bhavati asad brahmeti veda cet |
	asti brahmeti ced veda santamenaṃ tato viduḥ || iti |

tasmāt svātmarūpo hṛdayaprakāśaḥ | sannityatra saṃpratipattireva | tatra 
sannityetat prakṛtitaḥ pratyayataśca paryālocanīyam | asterdhātoḥ śatari 
khalvevamutpadyate | tatra cāstyaṃśasyārtho bhāvanākhyā kriyā, 
pratyayāṃśasya tu kartetyaṅgīkāryam | tataśca sannityasya 
bhavanalakṣaṇāyāḥ kriyāyāḥ kartetyartho bhavati | saiva bhavanakriyā 
kartṛtāmayī vimarśa ityucyate | punaḥ kriyāśabdopanyāsena bhavanaṃ 
sattvam, tacca sāmānyarūpam | tato na tasya kriyātvamiti vadanto 
nirākriyante | yato bhāvaḥ kriyeti nītyā dhātvarthamātrasya 
kriyātvamabhyupagantavyamityāvedyate | sā ca kriyā -

	avasthāyugalaṃ cātra kāryakartṛtvaśabditam ||
	tathāhi saṃvideveyamantarbāhyobhayātmanā |
	svātantryād vartamānaiva parāmarśasvarūpiṇī ||

iti śrīspandatantrālokasthityā yadā svasmin hṛdayaprakāśarūpa evātmani 
tiṣṭhati, tadā vimarśaḥ śuddho vimarśa ityeva vyavahriyate | yadā tu 
vikalpopaśleṣalakṣaṇaṃ kṣobhamanubhavati, tadā viśvavistāraḥ 
prapañcasphuraṇavaicitryātmā 



vimarśa iti tatrādhiko viśeṣaṇāṃśaḥ kaścidutpadyate | evaṃ ca 
prakāśasya vimarśaḥ svabhāva ityaṅgīkāryam | etadvyatirekaśca 
svabhāvabhaṅgaparyāyatvāt tasyāprakāśatvameva prayojayati | 
tataścāyaṃ sphaṭikamukurādivadalpaprakāśaḥ 
stambhakumbhādivadaprakāśo vā syādityuktaṃ bhavati | kiñca, sannityatra 
yā sattoktā, saiva cittvamucyate | sattvacittvayośca sāmarasyamānandaḥ | 
yaduktaṃ śrīrasānvaye -

	yā cit sattaiva sā proktā sā sattaiva ciducyate |
	atra citsattayorvyāptistatrānando virājate ||
	yatrānando bhaved bhāve tatra citsattayoḥ sthitiḥ | iti |

āhatya ca saccidānandasvarūpaḥ svahṛdayātmā parameśvara 
ityavagantavyam | yadāhuḥ -

	saccitsukhamayaḥ śambhustrirūpaḥ sarvavastuṣu | iti |
	itthamantarbahirbhāvavaicitrye'pi prasarpati |
	yaugapadmamayīṃ svasya vimraṣṭṛtvamayīṃ sthitim ||
	anuśīlya janaḥ svacchāmaiśvarīṃ śriyamaśnute |
	evametadupekṣāyāṃ pāśavena vimuhyati ||
	yathā yathā vijṛmbheta svasaṃrambhacamatkriyā |
	tathā tathā vikalpānāṃ vikṣobhaḥ kṣayamaśnute ||
	kalayāpi vimarśasya vīryavattā vipaścitaḥ |
	tanvyāpi viṣakākiṇyā durlaṅgho hi bhujaṅgamaḥ || iti || 11 ||

	nanu viśvavyavahāro hi bahirgrāhyārthatayā vartamānaḥ 
kathamātmasvabhāvo vimarśaḥ syādityāśaṅkyāha -

	pṛthvīparamaśivayoḥ pratyāhāre prakāśaparamārthe |
	yo'nyonyaviśeṣaḥ sa eva hṛdayasya vimarśonmeṣaḥ ||



iha hi viśvavyavahārānubandhī sarvo'pi saṃbandhaḥ 
kāryakāraṇamātraparyavasāyī | kāraṇaṃ ca paryantataḥ 
paramaśivātmā karteti svīkāryam, yataḥ saṃyogasamavāyau 
tadanubandhino'nye'pi sambandhāḥ 
saṃyojakādyavāntarakartṛsadbhāve'pi paryantataḥ 
parameśvarecchānuvidhāyitayā tasyaiva kartuḥ kāryatayā'nubhūyante | 
anyathā dvayoḥ padārthayoranyonyaprāptirūpa ekaḥ sambandha ityuktau 
kathamekatropakṣīṇāśeṣaśarīrabhāro'yamanyadvastu spraṣṭumapi 
pragalbheta | avaiṣamyeṇa ca dvayorvṛttau kathaṃ sambandha eva 
dvidhābhāvādasambandhakalpo na syāt | yatra punaḥ samavāyādau 
laukikasya karturanupalambhaḥ, tatra parameśvara eva kartetyabhyupeyam | 
nityo'pi samavāyaḥ parameśvarakartṛkatāṃ nātikrāmati | 
kṣaṇamātramaguṇaṃ tiṣṭhatīti sthityā dravyāṇāṃ kṣaṇanirguṇatvasya 
tairevoktatvāt tatsaṃyojanaviyojanavaidagdhyaṃ tasyaiveti siddham | api ca, 
amī saṃyogasamavāyādayaḥ svasvocitaviṣayavyatirekeṇānyatra na 
kvacidapi pravartanta ityabhyupeyam | anyathā'tiprasaṅgāt | taccaucityaṃ 
kiṃvapuriti paryālocane dravyaguṇādisaṃbandhisvabhāvādyatikrānta-
manyadeva tattvamityāstheyam, yadasmābhiḥ pārameśvaraṃ 
svātantryamityākrandyeta iti sarvasyāpi prapañcasya tatkāryatāyāṃ na 
vipratipattiḥ | sāmānyādīnāṃ ca lokamaryādayā nityānāmapi 
parameśvarāpekṣayā kāryatvameva, yenākāśasya nityābhimatasyāpi 
tasmād vā etasmādātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ iti śrutyā 
kāryatvamāsūtryate | upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat | ataśca viśvalakṣaṇasyāsya 
kāryasya parameśvaraḥ karteti bhaṅgyā kāryakāraṇabhāvo'yaṃ 
karmakartṛbhāvaparamārtha ityuktaṃ bhavati | kāraṇatvaṃ ca 
paryālocyamānaṃ jaḍasya nopapadyate, 
mṛddaṇḍacakrādisāmagrīsadbhāve'pi kulālādyaudāsīnye 
kumbhādyanutpatteḥ | bījādaṅkura utpadyate ityādau tu 
kartṛviśeṣānavalokane'pyaṅkurasya tāvat tādṛk sāmarthya na sambhavati, 
tasya tadā sattvābhāvāt | bījasya tatsāmarthyamiti cet ? na | kathamaṅkura 
utpadyata iti sāmarthya vaiyadhikaraṇyena bījasyetyucyate | kiñca, tad bījaṃ 
santaṃ vā aṅkuramutpādayati, asantaṃ vā ? santaṃ cet, 
ko'yamatrotpādanarūpo'rthaḥ, prāgeva tatsattvasyoktavāt | yadyasantam, 



kathaṃ sattvāsattve tatra na virudhyeyātām | tadabhyupagame vā kathaṃ 
nātiprasaṅgaḥ | ataḥ kaścidajaḍa eva kāraṇaṃ kāryavargasya | 
taccājāḍyamasya kartṛtvameva | yacca kvacit kulālādīnāṃ kartṛtvam, 
tatrāpi -

	tathā hi kumbhakāro'sāvaiśvaryeva vyavasthayā |
	tattanmṛdādisaṃskārakrameṇa janayed ghaṭam ||

iti śrīpratyabhijñāprakriyayā yatsvātantryādamī mṛdaiva 
ghaṭastantubhireva paṭa iti vyavasthāmanuvartante, na punarvyatyāsena 
kartuṃ śaknuvanti, tatra sa eva karteti kulālādīnāṃ kumbhādyapekṣayā 
kiñcidajāḍye'pi parameśvarāpekṣayā jāḍyameveti suṣṭhūktaṃ 
kāryakāraṇabhāva eva sarve sambandhāḥ, kāraṇaṃ ca kartaiveti | evaṃ ca 
sati tatra paramaśivādupari na kartrantarasphuraṇam, pṛthivyā upari na 
kāryāntarotpattiśca | tayoryaḥ pratipadārthamānupūrvyeṇa 
vyavahriyamāṇo buddhyākṛṣyamāṇo madhyavartī 
tattvasaṃghātarūpo'rthaḥ, tasya pratyakṣādipramāṇopagṛhyamāṇasya 
paryantataḥ prakāśa evānuprāṇanatayā svabhāva 
ityanvayavyatirekābhyāmavadhāryate, anyathā cakṣurādisāmagryāmapi 
teṣāmaprakāśamānatvāpatteḥ | yaduktaṃ nareśvaraviveke -

	na prathetāprathārūpaṃ nāśvetaṃ śvetate yathā iti |

yathā ca śrītantrāloke -

	nahyaprakāśarūpasya prākāśyaṃ vastutāpi ca iti |

tataścāntaḥsthaḥ svātmaprakāśa eva bahiḥ prakāśyaprapañcatayā 
sphuratītyetat tāvadabhyupagantavyam | tataśca ṣaṭtriṃśato'pi tattvānāṃ 
prakāśasvabhāvatvāviśeṣāt pṛthivyāpa ityādiranyonyaviśeṣato 
vyavahāro na saṃbhavati | vyavahriyate ca viśeṣaḥ pratyakṣameva lokena | 
anyathā jalasthalavibhāgāderarthakriyāṃ pratyaniyamāpatteḥ | sa ca 
viśeṣaḥ svahṛdayagatayā vimarśasattayaivotpadyate | utpattiśca tādātmye 
paryavasyati | yad yajjātaṃ tat tadeva hītyuktatvāt | unmeṣapadenaitadāha - 
svātmavimarśadrumasya pallavapuṣpādiprāyametad 
viśvavaicitryasphuraṇamiti | etena parāparapramātroḥ 
prakāśavimarśānuprāṇanatvaṃ paryantata-



stayoraikātmyaṃ ca vyākhyātam | paramaśiva iti | uktarūpaṃ cāsya 
svātantryaṃ viśvottirṇatvamātre viśrāmyati, na 
punarviśvamayaviśvottirṇatvalakṣaṇe paramasvātantryasaṃpatsāmrājye, 
tādṛksvabhāvasya ca pratyāhārānaucityāt, pratyuta tasya 
pratyāhartṛtvameva svabhāva iti || 12 ||

	athainaṃ vimarśameva draḍhayituṃ ṣaṭtriṃśattattvānāmanyonyato 
viśeṣaṃ sṛṣṭikramānuguṇyenopapādayannādau śivatattvamālocayati -

	tathā tathā dṛśyamānānāṃ śaktisahasrāṇāmekasaṃghaṭṭaḥ |
	nijahṛdayodyamarūpo bhavati śivo nāma paramasvacchandaḥ || 13 ||

	pṛthivyādiḥ śaktiparyanto yo'yaṃ viśvasphuraṇaprakāraḥ, 
yasyecchā jñānaṃ kriyeti, jñānaṃ smṛtirapohanamiti, sṛṣṭiḥ sthitiḥ 
saṃhāro'nākhyā bhāseti, anyathā ca tattatsrotontareṣu pūjyamānā 
yoginyo yoginaśceti vedyavikṣobhollāsaḥ, tanmayīnāṃ śaktīnāṃ tena 
tena prakāreṇa tatra tatrānubhūyamānānāmekaikavyaktiparyā-
locane'pyānantyam, kimuta kārtsnyānusandhāne | tathā hi - icchā 
tāvajjijñāsācikīrṣādibhedād bahuprakārā | jñānaṃ ca 
smṛtyanubhavasaṃśayaviparyayotprekṣādivaicitryādanantaprakāram | 
kriyāpi gamanāsanaśayanabhojanabhāṣaṇādibhedādanantā | teṣāmapi 
pratyekaṃ bhedaprabhedavikalpavikṣobha ityānantyotkarṣāśayenoktam - 
śaktisahasrāṇāmiti | teṣāṃ ca ya ekaḥ saṃghaṭṭa aikyamanubhavanneva 
sṛṣṭisaṃhārādyanyonyaviruddhakriyāyaugapadyabhūmirbhavati | yaśca 
udyamo bhairavaḥ iti śivasūtrasthityā 
svahṛdayodyogasvabhāvatayā'nubhūyate, 
yaścoktaprakārārthadvitayasāmarasyacamatkāragocarībhāvayogādanya-
tattvavailakṣaṇyenātyantotkṛṣṭamananyamukhaprekṣitvalakṣaṇaṃ 
svacchandatvaṃ svātantryamanubhavatītyavadhāryate | anyāni hi 
prakṛtipuruṣādīni tattvānyadho'dhaḥparvapratiyogitayā 
svātantryamuttarottaratattvāpekṣayā pāratantryaṃ va? pratipadyante | 
paramasvacchanda iti prāgvad viśvottīrṇa evocyate | anyastu 
viśvottīrṇaviśvamayaḥ 



paramaśivabhaṭṭārakaḥ paścāt prakaṭayiṣyate | evaṃvidhaḥ śivo nāma 
bhavati | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - unmeṣanimeṣātmanā tattvāntaradurlabhena 
svātantryeṇa cidāhlādalakṣaṇaśaktidvitayasāmarasyamātrakroḍī-
kṛtecchājñānādyuttarottaraśaktiparamparāparigrahaunmukhyāt 
svaśaktipracayo viśvam iti śriśivasūtrasthityā 
viśvavikṣobhātmakānantabāhyaśakticakravaicitryavijṛmbhaṇād-
bhutodbhāvanapragalbhasvātmacaitanyodyantṛtāparispandasāraḥ 
pramātṛviśeṣaḥ śivabhaṭṭāraka iti | yaduktaṃ śrīśivadṛṣṭau -

	ātmaiva sarvabhāveṣu sphurannirvṛtacid vibhuḥ |
	aniruddhecchāprasaraḥ prasaraddṛkkriyaḥ śivaḥ || iti |

yathā śrīvijñānendukaumudyām -

	sarvavyāpakatābhūmirjñatvakartṛtvasammatā |
	nijābhāsacamatkāramayī śivadaśā smṛtā || iti |

bhavatīti | sarvamapyetadupapāditamaiśvaryaṃ svarūpasattāyāmeva 
paryavasyati | sā ca cittvaṃ na vyabhicaratīti prāgevoktam -

	yā cit sattaiva sā proktā sā sattaiva ciducyate |

ityuktatvāt | yā ca sā sphurattā mahāsattā iti pratyabhijñāpyate, tasyāśca 
nityapravartamānatayā laṭprayogaḥ || 13 ||

	atha kramaprāptaṃ śaktitattvaṃ parāmṛśati -

	sa eva viśvameṣituṃ jñātuṃ kartu conmukho bhavan |
	śaktisvabhāvaḥ kathito hṛdayatrikoṇamadhumāṃsalollāsaḥ || 14 ||

	sa uktasvabhāvaḥ śiva eva śaktisvabhāvaḥ kathitaḥ | tasyaiva 
kiñciducchūnatāvasthāyāṃ śaktiśabdavyapadeśa ityarthaḥ | tasya 
cāyaṃ svabhāvaḥ - yat svahṛdayarūpeṇecchājñānakriyātmaka-
viśvavikalpaparyāyakoṇatrayasāmarasyalakṣaṇena 
nityapravṛttacarvaṇotsavatvādantarmagnasaṃvidānandaspandasandhukṣaṇa-
kṣameṇa madhunā



māṃsalamatyantabṛṃhitaṃ 
parivāhakriyārhaṃmahātaṭākāmbhaḥsamhārakalpaṃ bahu syāṃ 
prajāyeya ityāmnāyasthityā svayameva svahṛdayodyamavamanopa-
kramātmānamullāsamāhlādātiśayamanubhavatīti | yaduktaṃ 
śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	sphārayasyakhilamātmanā sphuran viśvamāmṛśasi rūpamāmṛśan 
|
	yat svayaṃ nijarasena ghūrṇase tat samullasati bhāvamaṇḍalam || iti |

tataścāyam -

	gacchato nistaraṅgasya jalasyātitaraṅgitām |
	ārambhe dṛṣṭimāpātya tadaunmukhyaṃ hi gamyate ||

ityādiśrīśivadṛṣṭidṛṣṭyā yadā svahṛdayavartinamuktarūpamartha-
tattvaṃ bahiḥkartumunmukho bhavati, tadā śaktiriti vyavahriyate | 
aunmukhyaṃ ca tadicchayā jñānena kriyayā ca bhavati | kathita iti 
āgamairbahubhirevaṃsvabhāvatvenodghoṣitaḥ | yathā śrīśivadṛṣṭau -

	itthaṃ śivo bodhamayaḥ sa eva paranirvṛtiḥ |
	saiva conmukhatāṃ yāti svecchājñānakriyātmatām ||
	saiva śāktaśarīrādinārakāntaṃ hi bhūtatā |

ityādi | yathā śrīkulamūlāvatāre -

	anādinidhanācchāntācchivāt paramakāraṇāt |
	icchāśaktirviniṣkrāntā tato jñānaṃ tataḥ kriyā ||

ityārabhya,

	tayotpannāni bhūtāni bhuvanāni caturdaśa |
	vāṅmayaṃ caiva yatkiñcit tatsarvaṃ mātṛkodbhavam ||

ityantam | yathā ca śrīpratyabhijñāvṛttau - cidvapuṣaḥ svatantrasya 
viśvātmanā sthātumicchaiva jagat prati kāraṇatā kartṛtārūpā ityādi | 
tathā śrītantrāloke -

	akulasyāsya devasya kulaprathanaśālinī |
	kaulikī sā parā śaktiraviyukto yayā prabhuḥ || iti |



yathā ca śrīmālinīvijaye -

	yā sā śaktirjagaddhātuḥ kathitā samavāyinī |
	icchātvaṃ tasya sā devī sisṛkṣoḥ pratipadyate ||
	evametaditi jñeyaṃ nānyatheti suniścitam |
	jñāpayantī jagatyatra jñānaśaktirnigadyate ||
	evambhūtamidaṃ vastu bhavatviti yadā punaḥ |
	jñātvā tadaiva tad vastu kurvantyatra kriyocyate ||
	evameṣā trirūpāpi punarbhedairanantatām |
	arthopādhivaśād yāti cintāmaṇiriveśvarī || iti |

tathā ca śrīparāsukte'pi -

	kṛtyeṣu devi! tava sṛṣṭimukheṣu nityaṃ
		svābhāvikeṣu visaratsu yadunmukhatvam |
	iccheti tat kila nirūpitamāgamajñai-
		rjānāsi yena vidadhāsi ca taṃ tamartham || iti |

pūjārahasye ca -

	aunmukhyamicchā jñānaṃ ca kriyetyetaccatuṣṭayam |
	spandanaṃ devadevasya bodhabhairavarūpiṇaḥ ||
	audāsīnyaprahāṇena yadaunmukhyaṃ maheśituḥ |
	tadidaṃ syājjagat sarvamityasmin kinnu yuktibhiḥ || iti || 14 ||

	atha sadāśiveśvarau paryālocayati -

	jñānaṃ kriyeti dvayorapi prathamonmeṣe sadāśivo devaḥ |
	dvitīyāyā ullekhe dvitīyaḥ sa bhavatīśvaro nāma || 15 ||

	jñānaṃ hi nāmāhambhāvāvabhāsanātmā sarvaprāṇināṃ 
svasaṃvedanasiddhaḥ svabhāvaḥ | kriyā ca karacaraṇādyanubandhinī 
sarvasākṣātkārayogyā parisphurati | tatra jānāmi 
karomītyādivajjānāsītyādāvapi ādarśāvalokanādinyāyā-



dasmacchabdārthaṃ eva yuṣmadādyarthatayā'vabhāsate | tathā 
caitroktāveva caitraḥ praṇamatītyādivadahambhāva eva 
vyavadhānopadhānena prathamapuruṣāditayā pratīyata ityahantaiva 
sarvatrātmatattvam | yaduktam -

	astiśeṣā kriyā sarvā kartṛśeṣaṃ ca kārakam |
	ekaśeṣaṃ ca vacanaṃ puruṣaścottamāvadhiḥ || iti |

ahantollekhaśca jñānaśabdārthaḥ | evaṃ karoṣi karotītyādāvapi 
karomītyasyaiva pāramārthyāt kriyāpi 
jñānavadahambhāvānupraviṣṭetyavadhāryate | kevalamasyā 
idambhāvaprācuryeṇānubhūyamānatayā bhedavyavahāraḥ | evaṃ ca 
jñānaṃ kriyeti yau dvau bhāvau, tayormadhye prathamasya jñānasyodreke 
dvitīyāyāścārthādasphuṭatvarūpe nyagbhāve sati sadāśivākhyaṃ 
tattvamityākhyāyate, yadadhiṣṭhātṛtayā sadāśiva ityeva nāmnā kaścid 
viṣṇurudrādisādhāraṇyenopāsyate | sa ca devo dṛkśakteraulbaṇyāt 
kriyāyāścātyantāpakarṣābhāvāt 
krīḍāvyavahārādyanekārthānusandhāne samarthaḥ | kiñca, 
etadupakramameva tattvasvabhāvatayā 
viśvasisṛkṣālakṣaṇasvātmaśaktyavibhīnnasya parameśvarasya 
dyotanamityāsūtrayituṃ deva ityuktam | 
yaduktamācāryābhinavaguptanāthapādaiḥ -

	śrīmatsadāśivodāraprārambhaṃ vasudhāntakam |
	yadantarbhāti tattvānāṃ cakraṃ taṃ saṃstumaḥ śivam || iti |

śivaśaktyostu tattvavyapadeśo nātyantaṃ mukhyayā vṛttyā, kiṃ tarhi 
sadāśivādyuttaratattvavrātanirvāhakatākāravikalpasaṃsparśamātrā-
daupacārikaḥ | upacāreṇāpyanayostattvavyapadeśasadbhāvāt tattvānāṃ 
ṣaṭtriṃśattvaṃ prati na kiñcidvyāhatatvam | yā ca tatra dvitīyā kriyā, 
tasyā ullekhe sphuraṇāparaparyāye prācurye jñānasya cārthataḥ staimitye 
sati īśvaro nāma bhavati | īśvarākhyaṃ tattvamityāmnāyate | 
tadadhiṣṭhātā ca kaścidīśvara ityeva pramātā | etaduktaṃ bhavati - 
ahantedantālakṣaṇayorjñānakriyayorādyodrekādanunmīlitacitranyāyena 
vyaktāvyaktamayaviśvapramātṛtāsvabhāvaṃ sadāśivākhyaṃ tattvam, 
etadviparyāsena kriyāśaktyaujjvalye vyaktākāraviśvānusandhātṛrūpa-



mīśvaratattvamiti | prathamonmeṣa iti | unmeṣanimeṣau nāma 
parameśvarasya paramasvātantryalakṣaṇaṃ spandatattvam | tatra ca 
yadāhantonmeṣastadānīmidantāyā nimeṣaḥ, idantonmeṣe cāhantāyā 
nimeṣa ityanena 

	īśvaro bahirunmeṣo nimeṣo'ntaḥ sadāśivaḥ ||

iti śrīpratyabhijñānītyā tattadāgamāntaraprasiddhamanayornāma-
dheyāntaradvitayamapyāsūtryate | dvitīya ityaneneśvarasya 
sadāśivatattvād vaiṣamye'pyavyavadhānaṃ dyotayatā sarvatrāpi 
tattvavrāte tattadvailakṣaṇyaprabandhopanyāse'pyahantānusyūtitāra-
tamyakramo'nusandheya ityunmīlyate | yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	bahirbhāvaparatve tu parataḥ pārameśvaram iti |

ahantāyāśca śivaśaktidvitayarūpatvādatraiva tadudbhāvanaucityam | etena 
gāthāyāḥ pūrṇopādānaṃ vyākhyātam || 15 ||

	atha śuddhavidyāmudyotayati -

	jñātā sa ātmā jñeyasvabhāvaśca lokavyavahāraḥ |
	ekarasāṃ saṃsṛṣṭi yatra gatau sā khalu nistuṣā vidyā || 16 ||

	jñātṛtvadharmāpi vibhinnāhambhāva ātmā parameśvaraḥ, 
tasyaiva cicchaktikroḍīkāryātmīyasadbhāvo lokavyavahāraḥ 
sphurastāparāmarśānuprāṇanasya tasya prapañcaprasarasya 
hānopānādibhirarthakriyā cetyetau dvāvapyanyonyaṃ 
pratītisāmarasyalakṣaṇāṃ saṃsṛṣṭi prāptāviti yo'rthaḥ, sā nistuṣā 
śuddhā vidyā | sā ca saṃsṛṣṭirekarasā | ekamabhinnarūpamanya-
tattvadurlabhoktacamatkārādhāratayā rasyamānamadhikaraṇamasyā iti | 
tataśca -

	sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca sadvidyāhamidaṃdhiyoḥ |

ityuktaṃ bhavati | yaduktamasmadgurubhiḥ śrīmātaṅgīstotre -

	stanau ghanau pūrṇasudhau sukāntī taveśi! nandāmi parasparābhau |
	sthitau samānādhikṛtau suvidye ! samau tavaivāhamidamprakāśau || 
iti |



māyā pramātṛvarge pramātāraṃ prati ahamiti prameyaṃ prati tvidamiti 
vaiyadhikaraṇyamanayoḥ saṃpratipannam | atra tu na tatheti 
sāmānādhikaraṇyamekarasaśabdenocyate | śuddhiḥ punarasyā 
bahiṣṭvenāvabhāsamānasyāpi vedyavargasya yadahamityeva 
prathanarūpaṃ svābhāvikamaucityottaraṃ ca vapuḥ, tallaukikairidamiti 
vyavahriyate | tadvyudāsena yathāvastvavabhāsanātmakamutkṛṣṭatvam | 
yathā paryantapañcāśikāyām -

	śuddhirbahiṣkṛtārthānāṃ svāhantāyāṃ nimajjanam iti |

śrītantrāloke ca -

	cidātmakeṣvapyeteṣu yā buddhirvyatirekiṇī |
	saivāśuddhiḥ parā proktā śuddhistaddhīvimardanam || iti |

anye punaḥ śuddhimitthamasyā manyante - yat sadāśivādiṣvahamidamiti 
sāmarasyaśālinī saṃvidasti, tatra sāmānādhikaraṇyādaikarasyayoge'pi 
māyīyasyedantāṃśollāsasyāvarjanīyatvāt sadāśivādīnāṃ ca 
māyāvārtānabhijñatvāt so'yamidantāṃśo 
māyīyatādoṣavyudāsādahambhāvabhāgavadanayā 
pratīyamānatvenāvasthāpyate | tatpratītivyatireke tu 
tatsāmānādhikaraṇyameva bhajyeteti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - bhagavataḥ 
paramaśivabhaṭṭārakasya paśūnapi kāṃścit pramātṝn prati 
sadāśivādinirviśeṣamanujighṛkṣotkarṣādaśuddhavidyākalaṅka-
prakṣālanāvinābhūtā svasvabhāvapratyabhijñāpanātmikā 
saṃvitsvātantryaśaktiḥ śuddhavidyā, yāmavalambya vijñānakevalā 
rudro'gniśceti pramātṛvaicitryamiti sa ityanenedantākrāntasyārthasya 
kārtsnyena koṭyantaratvadyotanārthamahambhāvasyāvāpoddhāra-
buddhyupalabdhaṃ kaivalyamunmīlyate | sā khalviti 
prasiddhyutkarṣopapādanenaitadudbhāvyate - yaḍḍākinīrākinyādayo 
dhātudevatāḥ, caryākriyādayaḥ puruṣārthopāyāḥ, 
śāmbhavaśāktādayo dīkṣāviśeṣāḥ, 
jāgratsvapnāvasthābhedāścāsyā eva vibhūtiparispandatayā tathā tathā 
vyavahriyante | yatreti vibhaktipratirūpako nipātaḥ || 16 ||



	atha māyāmunmīlayati -

	ekarase svabhāve udbhāvayantī vikalpaśilpāni |
	māyeti lokapateḥ paramasvatantrasya mohanī śaktiḥ || 17 ||

parameśvarasya hyasādhāraṇo bhāvo viśvasphurattātmaka-
mahāsattollāsarūpo yuktiparyālocanāyāmekarasaḥ 
svasaṃvidānandaparispandasaundaryamātrasāratayāste, na 
punarvikalpaśaṅkolliṅganaucityena, bhedavādasyāpavadiṣyamāṇatvāt | 
evaṃ sthite'pi tatraiva caitro maitraḥ stambhaḥ kumbha ityādayo ye vikalpāḥ 
kaṭakamukuṭādyākalpaśilpavad bhedāvabhāsabāhulye'pi 
cārutvacamatkārakāritayā hṛdayaṅgamāḥ santo 
vedyavilāsāstānuparyuparyunmīlayanti | tata eva muktamapi 
baddhabibhīṣikayā baddhamapi ca muktyabhimānena vyatyāsayantī 
śaktirmāyetyucyate | yato'yamīśvaraḥ paramasvatantraḥ | iyameva hi tasya 
svātantryotkarṣakāṣṭhā, yat svātmāvabhāsādvaitajīvite jagati 
bhedaprabhedavaicitryotpādanaprāvīṇyam, yenātidurghaṭakārī 
parameśvara ityāghoṣyate | ata eva cāsau lokapatiḥ, dehākṣabhuvanādeḥ 
prapañcasyeśvaraḥ | māyāvyatireke bhedaprathāpāramārthyasya 
prapañcasyābhāvaḥ | tadabhāve ca tatpratiyogikasya 
parameśvaraiśvaryasyānupapattiriti na kiñcidapyujjṛmbheta | tadiyaṃ 
māyā nāma tasyotkṛṣṭaṃ svātantryam | yathoktamasmadgurubhiḥ 
śrīmanonuśāsanastotre -

	svātantryāparaparyāyamāyājāḍyavilāpanāt |
	vilīya cidrasībhūtaṃ viśvānandamupāsmahe || iti |

yathā ca paramārthasaṃgrahe -

	paramaṃ yat svātantryaṃ durghaṭasampādanaṃ maheśasya |
	devī māyāśaktiḥ svātmāvaraṇaṃ śivasyaitat || iti |

śacīmate ca -

	svātantryaśaktirme'si tvamatidurghaṭakāriṇī |
	māyeti kīrtyase devi! bharturme vaiśvarūpyadā || iti |



śrīmatstotrāvalyāmapi -

	na ca vibhinnamasṛjyata kiñcidastyatha sukhetaradatra vinirmitam |
	atha ca duḥkhi ca bhedi ca sarvathā'pyasamavismayadhāma namo'stu 
te || iti |

śrītantrāloke'pi -

	tasya svatantrabhāvo'yaṃ kiṃ kiṃ yatra vicitrayet iti |

enāmevāvalambya pralayakevalā viṣṇuḥ sūrya iti pramātāraḥ prathante | 
yadyapi -

	vāyvagnisalilendrāṇāṃ dhāraṇānāṃ catuṣṭayam |

iti śrītriṃśikāśāstrasthityā māyāyāḥ 
kalāvidyādyantarbhāvenonmīlinaucityam, tathāpi -

	kālāgnimāditaḥ kṛtvā māyāntaṃ brahmadehagam

ityuddhṛtasya śrīparābījaprathamāṃśasyābhivyaktyarthameva 
pṛthakkṛtyocyate |

	nanu kathaṃ bhedavādāpavādaḥ, yena viśvasvabhāvasyaikarasyaṃ 
syāditi cet, ucyate | ko'yaṃ bhedo nāma | kimanyonyābhāvaḥ, uta 
vaidharmyam, āhosvit svarūpameva | nādyaḥ, sa khalu stambhaḥ kumbho na 
bhavati kumbhaśca na stambha ityetādṛśena vapuṣā 
dvayoranyonyatārūpamupādhimapekṣyaivotpadyate | sa ca 
vicāryamāṇastayoranyatve vīpsyamāna evāvirbhavati | taccānyatvaṃ 
padārthānāṃ svabhāvo vā, bhedanibandhanaḥ kaścidupādhirvā | 
yadyādyaḥ, tarhyekatvākrāntānāmapi bhedaḥ prasajyeta | dvitīyaścet, 
tadanyatvaṃ bhede satyevopapadyate | bhedaśca nādyāpi siddhasvarūpa 
ityātmāśrayatvamanyonyāśrayatvaṃ vā syāt | na dvitīyaḥ | vaidharmya 
nāma stambhādīnāṃ stambhatvakumbhatvādirūpo'rthaḥ | tatra 
stambheṣveva stambhatvaṃ kumbhatvaṃ ca kumbheṣveveti yadi 
kiñcinnaiyatyam, tadupapadyeta bhedaḥ | niyāmakaṃ ca na kiñcidālokyate | 
nanvasti dārumayatvaṃ pṛthubudhnodarākāratvaṃ ca tadvyavasthāpakamiti 
cet, na | tadasādhāraṇyenāpratīteḥ | tādṛśī ca pratītiḥ stambhādīnāṃ 
bhede satyeva saṅgaccheta | sa ca sādhyakoṭyārūḍha iti 
pūrvavadātmāśrayatvādyāpātaḥ | kiñca, dārumayatvādīnāmapi 
bhedastattadvaidharmyātmaka iti paramāṇvantaṃ 
paryālocanāyāmāmūlaviparyāsinyanavasthā syāt | nāpi tṛtīyaḥ, 



svarūpaṃ hi stambhādeḥ svamananyasvabhāvānuṣaktaṃ rūpamiti 
vaktavyam, anyathā bhāvānāṃ svabhāvasāṅkaryaprasaṅgāt | 
anyasvabhāvānuparāgaśca stambhādeḥ kinnibandhana ityanveṣaṇe tatrāpi 
bhedasadbhāvādityevoktiryuktimālambate | sa cādyāpi 
manorathāyamānasvabhāva iti prācīna eva doṣānuṣaṅgaḥ | kiñca, 
svarūpameva bhāvānāṃ bhedaścet, idaṃ rajatamityādibhrāntyullekho 
dattāñjaliḥ syāt | tatra hi śuktikāyāḥ svarūpaṃ 
vyaktamevādhyakṣīkriyate, rajatatayā'dhyavasīyate ca | 
akhyātivādapadavīprasthāne'pi bhrāntyātmikāyāḥ pratīterevāpalāpo 
na punastadanuguṇasya vyavahārasyāpītyabhyupagantavyam | api cāyaṃ 
bhedo bhāveṣveva bhāsamānastattadbhāvebhyaḥ svayaṃ bhinno vā na vā 
? na cet, abheda evetyāpatati | bhinnaścet, so'pi bhedaḥ kathamiti kathamiti 
nirūpyamāṇa uparyupari bhedaparamparāparigrahaprācuryādana-
vasthāmevopasthāpayati | bhedaścāyaṃ prakṛtyā bhinnasya vā syāt, 
utābhinnasyāpi bhāvasya | yadi bhinnasya, kimanenāgantukena bhedena | 
kṛtakāritvaṃ ca mahān doṣaḥ | abhinnasya cet, vyaktaṃ vyāghātaḥ syāt | 
anyaccedamālocanīyam | stambhāt kumbho bhinna ityādirhi 
bhedavyavahāraḥ | tatra bhedaviṣayanirdeśārthamanayordvayorapyeka-
hṛdayakroḍīkāryatayā bhāvyam | tathābhāve ca tayoḥ,

	idamasmādiha pṛthagiti bahiraṅgulyabhinayakriyākalpaḥ |
	antargatasvabhāvastāttvikamanayorabhedamācaṣṭe ||

ityādisthityā vastubhedasvabhāvatvādabheda eva pratitiṣṭhati | 
prayogaścātra - yat prakāśate tadekaprakāśātmakam, prakāśamānatvāt | 
ahampratyavamarśavaditi | atra copādhividhūnanādyupanyāso 
granthagauravāyeti vandhyo'yaṃ bhedavādāsvādadohalaprayāsaḥ | nanu 
stambhaḥ kumbha ityādiranyonyamasaṅkīrṇo vyavahāraḥ kathamiti cet, 
bhedābhedamaryādayeti brūmaḥ | pārameśvaro hi prakāśaḥ sarvatrāpi 
prapañce nirviśeṣamunmiṣati, tasyaiva svātantryāt | 
sāgarataraṅgabhaṅgyā stambhaḥ kumbha ityādipṛthagvyavahāropapattiśca 
| tarhyaśato'trāpi bhedavādadūṣaṇāpattiriti cet, na | śuddho hi 
bhedastādṛśamapavādamanubhavati | atra 



tvabhedopaśleṣasaubhāgyāllavaṇākarāvagāḍhasarvalāvaṇyanyāyena 
bhedo'pi tadvad dūṣyatayaivāvatiṣṭhate | yadasmābhirvibhāganibandhana 
eva bhedo viśvasyābhyupāgamyate, na tu pṛthaktvopādhikaḥ, 
tayordvitīyenaivābhedasya virodhāt | nanu 
bhedānuṣaṅgadaurbhāgyādabhedasyāpi dūṣyatvamiti viparyayaḥ kiṃ na 
syāditi cet, na | prakṛtirhi vikṛtimanugṛhṇāti | tatra cābhedaḥ prakṛtiranyo 
vikṛtirityabhyupagantavyam | yato bhinnānāmapi padārthānāṃ 
prātisvikena rūpeṇaikyamaparihāryam | tadeva cābheda iti 
viśvavilāsasyāsya vāstavasvabhāvo'yamabheda evetyatra na 
kācidanupapattiḥ | yadatraiva vastuni ekamevādvitīyaṃ brahma, neha 
nānāsti kiñcana ityādayo'nantā upaniṣada unmiṣanti | nanu 
vyāhato'yamartho bhedaścābhedaśceti cet ? hanta prakrāntaṃ 
prasmṛtamāyuṣmatā, evamatidurghaṭanakāryaghaṭanahetorhi bhagavato 
māyā nāma kācidati mahatī śaktirastītyaṅgīkriyate | etenādvaitameva 
sarvasāraḥ siddhāntaḥ | tacca pāryantikī pratiṣṭheti parāvasthā | 
bhedābhedastu vyavahārasarvasvaṃ nirvahan viśvasya viśvottirṇasya ca 
sambandhasvabhāvo vijṛmbhata iti parāparāvasthā | bhedaśca 
viśvottirṇaparameśvaraprakāśaparāmarśaprāgalbhyapallavaparamparā-
prāyatā viśvavaicitryaśilpakalpanācitramaṇḍapāyamānavibhramaḥ 
prasarpatītyaparāvasthā | yāsu kramāt suprabuddhaḥ prabuddho'prabuddha 
iti yogitāratamyam | āsu ca sarvāsvapi pārameśvaraprakāśānusyūterna 
kvacidapi vailakṣaṇyamityativicakṣaṇaikaśikṣaṇīyo'yamāsmākīnaḥ 
pakṣa iti | taduktaṃ paryantapañcāśikāyām -

	anantaitāvadākārasvīkāre'pyekalakṣaṇām |
	tāṃ svasaṃvidamāviśya vikalpānna vikalpayet || iti |

	etā evopāsakajanāpekṣayā tattaddevatātvenārādhyante | yaduktaṃ 
śrītantrāloke -

	parā candrasamaprakhyā raktā devī parāparā |
	aparā sā parā kālī bhīṣaṇā caṇḍayoginī || iti |

evaṃ māyāsvarūpaparāmarśa eva jīvanmuktirityupaniṣat || 17 ||



	atha māyāvibhūtyātmakaṃ kalādipañcakamarthadvārā parīkṣate-

	sarvakaraḥ sarvajñaḥ pūrṇo nityo'saṃkucaṃśca |
	viparīta iva maheśo yābhistā bhavanti pañca śaktayaḥ || 18 ||

	parameśvaro hi prakṛtyā viśvasya kartā, jñātā ca, tata eva 
svātantryāt pūrṇaḥ svātmatṛptaśca, prārthanīyābhāvāt | 
svavyatiriktasya svāvacchedakasya kasyacid bhāvasyāsaṃbhavānnityaḥ 
prākpradhvaṃsābhāvātilaṅghī | tata eva 
saṃkocalakṣaṇaniyantraṇāśūnyaśca | tādṛśo'pi sannasau yābhiḥ 
śaktibhirnibandhanībhūtābhirviparīta iva kiñcitkartṛtvādidharmayukta 
ivāvabhāsate tāḥ pañca śaktayo bhavanti | taduktaṃ śrīkramodaye -

	rāgo māyā kalā'vidyā niyatiḥ kāla eva ca |
	pañcavṛttyāśrayāḥ sarve pāśāśceti prakīrtitāḥ || iti |

tāśca kalā avidyā rāgaḥ kālo niyatirityucyante | kalā tasya 
kiñcitkartṛtvahetuḥ | avidyā kiñcijjñatvakāraṇam | rāgo 
viṣayeṣvabhiṣaṅgaḥ | kālo bhāvānāmavabhāsanānavabhāsanātmā 
kramaḥ | niyatirmamedaṃ na mamedamityādiniyamahetuḥ | etatpañcakaṃ 
cāgameṣu svarūpāvarakatvāt kañcukamityucyate | etadabhāve hi puruṣaḥ 
parameśvaravadatiprakaṭabodhaśaktiḥ, pāṣāṇavadatyantanimagnaiśvaryo 
vā bhavet | tatra ca rāgo māyā'vidyā kalā kāla iti krameṇa 
puruṣasyordhvādhaḥparvānupraveśaparihārāt triśaṅkuvanmadhyasthā-
nāvasthānaṃ prati pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśarūpatayā tāsāmupayogaḥ | 
etena dhāraṇātvametāsāṃ vyākhyātam | yathā śrītrṃśikāśāstre -

	vāyvagnisalilendrāṇāṃ dhāraṇānāṃ catuṣṭayam iti || 18 ||

	atha puruṣasvabhāvamudbhāvayati -



	ya eṣa viśvanāṭakaśailūṣaḥ śuddhasaṃvicchambhuḥ |
	varṇakaparigrahamayī tasya daśā kāpi puruṣo bhavati || 19 ||

	parameśvaro hi ahameva sarvamiti vaiśvātmyaprathānubhūtisphāra-
camatkārottaratayā śuddhāṃ saṅkocakalaṅkaśaṅkāśūnyāṃ 
saṃvidaṃ svasvātantryasvabhāvavidyāmayīmanubhavannanenaiva hetunā 
nartaka ātmā iti śrīśivasūtrasthityā viśvanāṭakasya śailūṣo naṭa iti 
vyapadiśyate | yaduktaṃ śrīnaiśvāse -

	tvamekāṃśenāntarātmā nartakaḥ kośarakṣitā iti |

viśvaṃ ca pṛthivyādiśivāntatattvasandohātmakam -

	jananaśaiśavayauvanavārddhakavyayamayairakhilairapi sandhibhiḥ |
	abhinayannapi pauruṣanāṭakaṃ pariṇatau sa śivo'smi mahānaṭaḥ ||

ityādinītyā sṛṣṭisthityādyavasthāpañcakāvinābhūtatvādārambhaya-
vādyavasthāpañcakalakṣaṇasya nāṭakasyānukaroti | taduktaṃ 
śrībhaṭṭanārāyaṇena -

	nisṛṣṭānekasadbījagarbhaṃ trailokyanāṭakam |
	prastāvya hara ! saṃhartu tvattaḥ ko'nyaḥ kaviḥ kṣamaḥ || iti |

sa ca śambhuḥ śṛṅgārakaruṇādirasāsvādasthānīyaṃ 
śabdasparśādyanubhavātmakaṃ śaṃ sukhaṃ 
prekṣakāṇāmindriyāṇāṃ bhavatyasmāditi kṛtvā | tasya ca 
viśvanāṭyābhinayonmukhasya bhūmikāvalambanalakṣaṇenārthena 
prakṛtā yāvasthā sā puruṣo bhavati | puruṣa iti 
vyapadeśaucityamanubhavati | yaduktaṃ śrīsāraśāstre -

	svayaṃ badhnāti deveśaḥ svayaṃ caiva vimuhyati |
	svayaṃ bhoktā svayaṃ jñātā svayaṃ caivopalakṣayet || iti |

yadavasthānuguṇyāducchvāsaniśvāsādipravartakaḥ prāṇaḥ, 
hānopādānādyupayogī vyānaḥ, śarīrādipoṣaṇaḥ samānaḥ, 
dhātvādyunnayana udānaḥ, viṇmūtrādivisarjano'pāna iti 
tattadudyamaprakārāḥ prakhyāyante | yadyapi asau śaktipātādyabhāvāt 
kumbhakārasyāpi ghaṭakaraṇe sarvaśaktiśivātmatā, tadaparijñānāt tu 
kumbhakāratetyarthaḥ iti padasaṅgatiprakriyayā svahṛdayena 
parameśvarībhāva-



parāmarśaśūnyo bhavati, tathāpi tattvadṛṣṭyā tasya 
parameśvaratvamāmnāyodghoṣitamavarjanīyam | tathāhi parameśvarasya 
hyayamevāsādhāraṇasvabhāvo yat sarvadā 
sṛṣṭyādipañcakṛtyakāritvam | taccottaratra vyaktamālocayiṣyate | 
etadanaṅgīkārāddhi māyāvedāntādinirṇītasyātmanaḥ 
svasphuraṇāmodamāndyalakṣaṇamasatkalpatvamāpatitam | puruṣaścāyaṃ

	kintu durghaṭakāritvāt svācchandyānnirmalādasau |
	svātmapracchādanakrīḍāpaṇḍitaḥ parameśvaraḥ ||

iti śrītantrālokasthityā māyāpathāvatīrṇo'pi parameśvaravat sarvadā 
pañcāpi kṛtyāni karoti | yato'sya na kasyāñcidapyavasthāyāṃ 
saṃvitsaṃskāravandhyatvam | suṣuptyādāvapyauttarakālikapra-
bodhānusandhānabalādantarmagnaṃ kiñcillokayātrāvyavahāra-
vijṛmbhaṇaṃ sūkṣmamupalakṣaṇīyam | kevalamavasthāntareṣu 
saṃvidastāratamyameva bhedaḥ | taduktaṃ śrīspande -

	jāgradādivibhede'pi tadabhinne prasarpati |
	nivartate nijānnaiva svabhāvādupalabdhṛtaḥ || iti |

taduttaratra jo ī jā a rasiviṇa a ityatra spaṣṭīkariṣyate | tataśca 
saṃvitsvātantryasārasyāsya stambhādyavalokanāvasthāyāṃ yadā 
stambhāvalokanaunmukhyam, tadā tasya sṛṣṭiḥ | 
kumbhādivailakṣaṇyenāvalokyamānatayā sṛṣṭiranenaiveti kṛtvā tatraiva 
yadā dvitrakṣaṇamātramavasthāsnutā, tadā tasya sthitiḥ | padārthānāṃ 
tattadrūpatayā dhāryamāṇatvasya sthititayoktatvāt | yadā 
kumbhādibhāvāntarānupraveśaunmukhyam, tadā stambhasya saṃhāraḥ 
kumbhasya sṛṣṭiśca | yadā punaḥ stambhaparityāgasya 
kumbhānupraveśasya ca madhyasthāvasthā, tadā turīyasattā | 
vedyoparāgaśūnyatayā śuddhasaṃvinmayatvāt | yaduktaṃ mayaiva 
śrīkomalavallīstave -

	jñātamekamavamucya cetaso bhāvamanyamavagāḍhumicchataḥ |
	antarālabhuvamamba!  lambinīmāmananti tava tattvamadvayam || iti |

uktasarvakramānusyūtā tadullaṅghanakṣamā ca svātmagatā
