saṃvicchaktirbhāsayati | yadvā stambhādyanusandhāne taṃ prati yadā 
stambhatvāvadhāraṇam, 



tadā tasya dārumayatvādikaṃ nyagbhavati | dārumayatvādyavadhāraṇe ca 
stambhatvasya nyakkāra iti ekatarasṛṣṭiranyatarasaṃhāraśceti 
vyaktamupalakṣyate | stambhatvadārumayatvādimelakaparāmarśe tu sthitiḥ, 
pratītidvayasyāpi nyagbhāvābhāvāt | stambhatvādyaśeṣavikalpo-
paśāntau anākhyā | svātmasphurattāyāḥ parāmarśe bhāseti 
sṛṣṭyādiprakāro draṣṭavyaḥ | tatraiva ca yadā stambhaṃ 
niyatadeśakālākāratayā'valokayati, tadā tadābhāsāṃśenāsya 
sraṣṭṛtā | anaitaddeśakālādyābhāsāṃśena tasya saṃhartṛtā | 
stambhādisāmānyāṃśena tu sthāpakatā | avikalpāvalokane 
turīyānubhavitṛtvam, prakāśaikyena prakāśane punaranugṛhītṛtā ceti 
siddhamasya svahṛdayaṅgamībhāvaṃ vināpi yaugapadyena 
sṛṣṭyādipañcakṛtyakāritvalakṣaṇamaiśvaryam | ata eva hi 
saṃkucitasvabhāvo'pyasau tattvavṛttyā vikasitatvāt puruṣa iti vyapadiśyate 
| yacchrutiḥ - tenedaṃ pūrṇa puruṣeṇa sarvam iti | 
śrīcidgaganacandrikāyāṃ ca - pūraṇāt puruṣatāmupeyuṣi iti | 
kṛtyapañcake ca parameśvarasya sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhāreṣu triṣu na kvacidapi 
vaiṣamyam | uparitanayoḥ punaryadā vyāmohyamānapaśujanāpekṣayā 
samayabhraṃśāpādanādirūpametaccittasya vyākulīkaraṇam, yadā ca 
kāruṇyotkarṣādetaddoṣavyudāsenāsya bhūyaḥ 
svarūpalābhapradāyitvam, tadā tirodhānamanugrahaśceti tatkṛtyadvayaṃ 
vyapadiśyate | yadā punaḥ,

	yathā ca vistṛte vastre yugapad bhāti citratā |
	tathaiva yogināṃ dharmasāmastyenaiva bhāti bhūḥ ||

iti śrītantrālokasthityā paśupramātrādyavacchedavyapohena viśvameva 
kāryatayā'nusandhīyate, tadānākhyā bhāseti tatkṛtyadvayavyapadeśa 
ityalamavāntareṇa | naṭaṃ prati ca bhūmikātvena yo rāmādirābhāsate, sa 
tu samyaṅmithyāsaṃśayasādṛśyādivailakṣaṇyena 
saṃvidantaraviṣayatayā sāmājikairanubhūyate | taṭasthaistu 
śrotriyādibhirnaṭatayaiveti | ya eṣa iti purāṇāgamādiprasiddhasya 
parameśvarasya svātmatvenātyāsattyā vimraṣṭavyatāṃ dyotayan 
tādṛgvimarśonmeṣe parameśvarasakāśāt puruṣaṃ prati 
prathamāno'yaṃ bhedo vātatūlalaulyalīlāmanubhavatītyudbhāvayati | 



daśā kāpītyanena parameśvaratāpannasyāpi puruṣasya 
svaparāmarśaśūnyatayā vedyavargānupraveśābhimantṛtvena mahān 
khalvasya mohotkarṣa iti śocanīyatā dyotyate | yaduktaṃ 
śrītriṃśikāśāstre -

	pṛthivyādīni tattvāni puruṣāntāni pañcasu |
	kramāt kādiṣu vargeṣu makārānteṣu suvrate || iti |

yathā ca vyākhyātamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ - puruṣasya hi 
saṃvedyarūpasyaiva parimitasya vedyarāśau gaṇanam iti | tasya ca -

	sampanno'smi kṛśo'smi snihyattāro'smi modamāno'smi |
	prāṇimi śūnyo'smīti hi ṣaṭsu padeṣvasmitā dṛṣṭā ||

iti śrīvirūpākṣapañcāśikāprakriyayā dehaprāṇādyupaśleṣavaśād 
bahutvamapyupapadyate | yata etadālambanena brahmā candraḥ sakala iti 
pramātṛbhedaḥ parisphurati | etena tasya svarganarakādyupabhogaucityaṃ 
vyākhyātam | yathā śrīśivadṛṣṭau -

	krīḍayā duḥkhavedyāni karmakārīṇi tatphalaiḥ |
	sampatsyamānāni tathā narakārṇavagahvare ||
	nivāsīni śarīrāṇi gṛhṇāti parameśvaraḥ | iti |

sa ca kadācit svātmasphurattāmeva vyāmohavaśāccharīrendriyādi-
svaspandatayā'bhimanyate | na cāsau vāstavī dṛṣṭiḥ | yaduktaṃ 
śrīsvacchande -

	supradīpte yathā vahnau śikhā dṛśyeta nāmbare |
	dehaprāṇasthito'pyātmā tadvallīyeta tatpade || iti |

vāstavī tu dṛṣṭiretasphurattāpi parameśvara eva paryavasyatīti | 
yaduktamajaḍapramātṛsiddhau -

	yadyapyarthasthitiḥ prāṇapuryaṣṭakaniyantrite |
	jīve, niruddhā tatrāpi paramātmani sā sthitā || iti |

kiñca -

	parameṣṭhinamārabhya kīṭaparyantamullasan |
	bahudhopādhimāsthāya sa ekaḥ san prakāśate ||



	śarīrāṇāṃ prabhede'pi na śarīrī vibhidyate |
	ādarśānāṃ prabhede'pi paśyanniva mukhaṃ pumān ||
	ekameva yathā tailaṃ sthūle'pi tilasañcaye |
	tathā viśvavilāse'sminneko lokottaraḥ prabhuḥ ||
	pratirandhraṃ gavākṣāṇāmantarikṣe vyavasthite |
	yathā na vastuto bhedastadvadātmani dṛśyatām ||
	ekameva hi sāmānyaṃ sattā gotvādi kalpyate |
	tadvadeko'yamātmeti vadan kasmānna mṛṣyate ||
	ekenaivātmanā siddhe lokayātrā'nuvartane |
	nānātvakalpanaṃ tatra gauravāya na kiṃ bhavet ||
	kiñca bhedasya na kvāpi viṣayo'stīti darśitam |
	ato'pyadvaitapakṣe'sminnānātvaṃ kathamātmanaḥ ||
	aupādhike'pi tadbhede dvāsuparṇādivedavāk |
	sukhaduḥkhādibhedānāṃ vyavasthāpyupapadyate ||
	aikyenaiva nimittena tattadvyaktyānuvartanāt |
	tasya vyāpakatā siddhā yā'nyairapyupapādyate ||
	tataśca gajakīṭādestattaddehapramāṇataḥ |
	ātmāvasthānamityetat tyajyatāmarhatāṃ matam ||
	yattu tairucyate tasminnaśeṣavyāpake sati |
	śarīreṣviva caitanyaṃ śilāsvapi bhavediti ||
	tatrottaraṃ śilādāvapyasti caitanyavāsanā |
	naulbaṇyamasyāḥ prāṇādisāhāyyānudayāditi ||

ye punarenaṃ śarīrādimevāhuḥ, te punaratyantamūrkhāḥ | tathāhi -

	sa ca dehamayo na syāccaitanyaikaprasārabhūḥ |
	no cecchavaśarīre'pi ceṣṭā dṛśyeta pūrvavat ||
	stambhaḥ kumbhaḥ sarid grāvā mṛgaḥ pakṣī pumāniti |
	tāratamye nimittaṃ kiṃ bhūtacaitanyavādinām ||



	evaṃ nendriyarūpo'yaṃ suṣuptyādau śarīriṇām |
	satyevendriyasaṃsarge caitanyā'nupalambhanāt ||
	dehendriyāderātmatve kartṛtvaṃ paryavasyati |
	prapañcasyaiva kartṛtvaṃ kāryamanyat kimiṣyate ||
	aṃśato yadi kartṛtvaṃ kāryatvaṃ ceti kathyate |
	koṃ'śaḥ kasyāstu karteti vyavasthā kena kalpyate ||
	uktarūpaviparyāsaśaṅkā vā kena vāryate |
	ato'smanniścitasyaiva tattvasyātmatvamiṣyatām ||
	atha yaḥ śūnyamāhainaṃ so'pi paryanuyujyate |
	viśvāntarbhāvataḥ kiṃ vā śūnyo'yaṃ svayameva vā ||
	svataḥ śūnyatvapakṣo'sya svaśāstre'pi na likhyate |
	vedyavargasya śūnyatve vyavahāro vilupyate ||
	sarvaṃ vyavaharatyeva śūnyatāṃ sādhayannapi |
	viśvaṃ satyaṃ vadatyanyaḥ śūnyaṃ vadati kaścana ||
	yānāsanādivelāyāmubhayoḥ sadṛśī sthitiḥ |
	vyavahāraikarūpyaṃ ced vāgvaiṣamyeṇa kiṃ phalam ||
	na ca sarvaṃ jagacchūnyamityarthaṃ sādhayan sudhīḥ |
	labhate vādinaṃ kvāpi svamātmānaṃ kathāmapi ||

ityalamatiprapañcopanyāsaprācuryeṇa | prakṛtamevānudhāvyate | itthaṃ ca 
sarvasyāpi prapañcavaicitryasya

	tasmācchabdārthacintāsu na sā'vasthā na yā śivaḥ

iti śrīspandaprakriyayā pārameśvaraprakāśamayatvābhyupagame 
kuto'sya puruṣamenaṃ prati pakṣapātapratikṣepa iti tātparyārthaḥ | 
yaduktaṃ nareśvaraviveke -

	sarve vaite pramātāraḥ prakāśatvācchivātmakāḥ |
	sarvajñāḥ sarvakartāraḥ sarvataścāvibhedinaḥ || iti |

etadarthadyotanāya hi puruṣo'pi puruṣa ityucyate | yadanayoḥ pure vasatīti 
puruṣaḥ puramoṣati dahatīti puruṣa iti svabhāvabhedaḥ |



	buddhiprāṇaśarīrākhye yadetasmin puratraye |
	ahaṅkārād vasatyātmā tenāyaṃ puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
	etat puratrayaṃ dagdhaṃ yena sa tripurāntakaḥ |
	sa eva puruṣaḥ proktaḥ smarārirapi sa smṛtaḥ ||

iti śrīhaṃsabhedasthityā yadyapyaucityenonmīlyate, tathāpi 
paryantatastāttvikārthacintāyāṃ tayoruktarūpamaikāntyameva | 
tādṛgavacchede ca tasya svasvātantryarūpasya malatrayasyopalālanaṃ 
heturiti | nanu bhedaprathāprakarṣe'pi yadyanayostādātmyaṃ 
tannibandhanaḥ pativat paśoraiśvaryocchrāyaścopapādyate, tarhi tenaiva 
tādāmyena paśuvat patirapi māyāvyāmohakadarthanagrathitahṛdaya-
vṛttirastviti cet ? naivam, aindrajālikadṛṣṭāntasyātra prāptāvasaratvāt | sa 
khalu svayamakhilamapi lokaṃ vyāmohayan na kenacid vyāmohyate | 
etadarthameva hi parameśvaraṃ prati śailūṣavyapadeśa iti || 19 ||

atha prakṛti prakaṭayati -

	jñānakriyāmāyānāṃ guṇānāṃ sattvarajastamassvabhāvānām |
	avibhāgāvasthāyāṃ tattvaṃ prakṛtiriti śāmbhavī śaktiḥ || 20 ||

jñānaṃ nāma prakāśaḥ | kriyā vimarśaḥ | māyā tu ahamidamiti 
idamahamiti kiñcidbhedaprathāprarohe'pi 
sadāśiveśvarāvasthāvadahantāparyavasāyinī śaktiḥ | tāsāṃ 
parameśvaraśaktibhūtānāmapi vyāmohyamānapaśujanāpekṣayā 
guṇatvam | yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	svāṅgarūpeṣu bhāveṣu patyurjñānaṃ kriyā ca yā |
	māyātṛtīye te eva paśoḥ sattvaṃ rajastamaḥ || iti |

tāśca paśau sattvaṃ rajastama iti bhavanti | yābhi 
sukhaduḥkhamohātmako'yaṃ lokavyavahāraḥ | tanmayīnāṃ ca tāsāṃ 
yā vibhāgaśūnyā tulādhāraṇavadatyantāvaiṣamyaśālinyavasthā, 
tasyāṃ paryālocyamānāyāṃ prakṛtiriti tattvaṃ bhavati |



yasyāṃ śubhāśubhasvabhāvānekasahasraguṇadoṣonmeṣa-
bhūmikāyāṃ śūnyabuddhiprāṇaśarīraviṣayāvacchinnāḥ 
pañcāhantā nivṛttipratiṣṭhāvidyāśāntiśāntyātītāḥ kalāśca 
vipaścidbhirvibhūtitayā vikalpyante | sā ca śāmbhavyeva śaktirityanena 
sāṅkhyādisiddhānto'pahāsyate | te khalu -

	mūlaprakṛtiravikṛtirmahadādyāḥ prakṛtivikṛtayaḥ sapta |
	ṣoḍaśakastu vikāro na prakṛtirna vikṛtiḥ puruṣaḥ ||

ityādinā puruṣādupari na kiñcit tattvāntaram, puruṣaścodāsīnaḥ 
prakṛtiśca tadadhīnā, sā ca nityā, na vikṛtiḥ, ṣoḍakastu nityaṃ 
vikārātmaka evetyācakṣate | naivamasmatsamayaḥ | yataḥ puruṣādupari 
bahūni tattvāni paramaśivaparyantam | puruṣaśca sarvadā sarvajñaḥ 
sarvakartā ca svātmākhyātivaśāt saṃkucita iva bhāsate | prakṛtiśca na 
kevalaṃ puruṣeṇaiva preryate | tasya parameśvarasvātantryavyatireke 
tādṛkprerakatvasāmarthyābhāvāt | yaduktaṃ śrīspande -

	nahīcchānodanasyāyaṃ prerakatvena vartate |
	api tvātmabalasparśāt puruṣastatsamo bhavet || iti |

sā ca nityaṃ vikṛtā kadācittvavikṛtā | ṣoḍaśako'pi kadācidavikṛtaḥ, 
kadācicca vikṛto'pīti |

	atha ye tamudāsīnamāhurnityoditodyamam |
	ta evaṃ prativaktavyāḥ sarvadā cedanudyamaḥ ||
	tena mṛtpiṇḍakalpena jagataḥ kiṃ prayojanam |
	kimityaṅgīkṛtistasya kiṃpramāṇo'pi vā bhavet ||
	yaducyeta tadevaitadaudāsīnyaṃ cidātmanaḥ |
	pravartayati viśvasmin prakṛti vikṛtispṛśam ||
	anyathā prakṛteḥ kiñcinna prāgalbhyaṃ pravartate |
	tadarthaṃ svīkṛtistasya viśvātikrāntatejasaḥ ||
	tenaiva ca pramāṇena tasya siddhirbhavediti |
	atrocyate yathā sā cedasyaudāsīnyakalpanā ||




	anudāsīna eveti tātparyaṃ paryavasyati |
	vailakṣṇyāt prakṛtyādeḥ sūkṣmamudyamamāsthitaḥ ||
	ityasau niṣkriyo na syād yataḥ sūkṣmāpi sā kriyā |
	sthavīyasīḥ kriyāḥ sūte prakṛtau vikṛtiṣvapi ||
	jaḍā api svabhāvena dehākṣabhuvanādayaḥ |
	yatprabhāvāt pravartante sa kathaṃ niṣkriyo bhavet ||

atra ca madīyameva sūktaṃ yathā -

	aśveṣu gacchatsu rathaḥ prayātītyudetu vādo rathamadhyavartī |
	sukhaṃ niṣaṇṇo viṣayāntaraṃ ca prāptaḥ pumān kinnu cakāra 
śambho || iti |

atraitadavadhāryamantarvidbhiḥ | yaduta -

	śivārambhaḥ prakṛtyantastattvaughaḥ pratyapādi yaḥ |
	tatra viśvottarasyāsya śivasya parameṣṭhinaḥ ||
	sarvakriyā ca sārvajñyaṃ sarvadodayatā tathā |
	sarvavyāpakatā pūrtiḥ prathante pañcaśaktayaḥ ||
	atha śaktau tadicchāyāmunmanā samanetyapi |
	vyāpinī nādabindū ca raśmayaḥ pañca jāgrati ||
	anugrahatirodhānasaṃhṛtisthitisṛṣṭayaḥ |
	jagatkṛtyāni pañcāpi śaktitattvabhuvaḥ prathāḥ ||
	sadāśive punastattve īśānastatpumāniti |
	aghoro vāmadevaśca sadyojātaśca śaktayaḥ ||
	tadūrdhvamīśvare tattve rūpātītottaraṃ mahat |
	rūpātītaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca padaṃ piṇḍa iti kramaḥ ||
	turyātītaṃ ca turyaṃ ca suṣuptisvapnajāgarāḥ |
	avasthāḥ pañca śuddhāyā vidyāyāḥ śaktivibhramaḥ ||
	saiveyaṃ śāmbhavīṃ śaktimāṇavīṃ śodhanīmapi |
	bodhanīmapi tā dikṣā prasūte sphūrtiśālinī ||



	nirupāyaprakāśākhyo jñānaṃ yogaḥ kriyetyapi |
	caryeti ca tadutthaiva pumarthopāyakalpanā ||
	tata eva mahāśaktirhākinī nāma jāyate |
	ḍākinī rākinī tadvallākinī kākinītyapi ||
	sākinīti ca dehe'smin yadvaicitryaṃ vijṛmbhate |
	atha māyeti yat tattvamucyate tasya śaktayaḥ ||
	kalāvidye rāgakālau niyatistatra pañcamī |
	prāṇo'pānaḥ samānaḥ syādudāno vyāna ityapi |
	śarīrayātropakṣīṇaḥ puruṣe śaktisaṃgrahaḥ |
	prakṛtau tu guṇāḥ sattvaṃ rajaśca tama ityapi ||
	vikṛtyavikṛtī tadvaducyante pañca rociṣaḥ |
	athaitayoḥ pumprakṛtyoḥ sphuraṇakriyayā bhavet ||
	vakṣyamāṇamaśeṣeṇa viśvavaicitryakalpanam |
	tatprakāro'pi vidhivad vitatya pratipādyate ||
	pumprakṛtyoḥ samudyogād dhāturātmeti jāyate |
	tvagasṛṅmāṃsamedosthimajjāśuklāhvayairayam ||
	vātapittakaphākhyaiśca svāṃśodyotairvijṛmbhate |
	anayoreva saṃghaṭṭād yo mano dīrahaṅkriyā ||
	ityantaḥkaraṇollāsastraividhyenānubhūyate |
	tatra buddhyudyamād dharmo jñānaṃ vairāgyamityapi ||
	aiśvaryaṃ varadatvaṃ cetyunmīlati vicitratā |
	kramādetadviparyāso manasaḥ śilpamiṣyate ||
	madīyatvāmadīyatvakārpaṇyamadamatsarāḥ |
	ahaṅkārasphurattāḥ syuḥ saṃsārasphūrtihetavaḥ ||
	tadvat tayorvibhūtyaiva guṇāḥ sattvaṃ rajastamaḥ |
	iti trayaḥ pratīyante vikṛtāvikṛtodayāḥ ||





	tatra sattvasya saundaryaṃ saubhāgyaṃ sādhuśīlatā |
	saumukhyamatha saujanyamiti sphuraṇavibhramaḥ ||
	vaśīkaraṇamākarṣaḥ śāntiḥ poṣaṇapālane |
	iti karmāṇi rajaso guṇasyāhuḥ parigraham ||
	atha vidveṣaṇaṃ yat syād yaccoccāṭanamucyate |
	stambhanaṃ mohanaṃ ceti māraṇaṃ ceti yāḥ kriyāḥ ||
	tadetadakhilaṃ tasya tamasaḥ kṣobhavihramaḥ |
	guṇairebhirupaskāramahaṅkāro yadāśnute ||
	sāttvikatvādibhedena tadā traividhyamṛcchati |
	tatra śabdasparśarūparasagandhā iti kramāt ||
	sāttvikaḥ svena rūpeṇa prathate bhogyavastuṣu |
	rājasastu vijṛmbheta vacanādānadhāvanaiḥ ||
	visargānandanābhyāṃ ca pañcabhiḥ svasvabhāvavān |
	tāmaso'pi khavāyvagnipāthobhūmisvalakṣaṇaiḥ ||
	bhūtairvapuṣmān prathate viśvopādānahetubhiḥ |
	kiñca traividhyavatyasminnahaṅkārāhvaye pade ||
	vibhūtivibhramotkarṣāt tridhā vitatiriṣyate |
	puṇyecchā tattvajijñāsā lokottirṇārthagṛdhnutā ||
	vastuṣvadhyavasāyaḥ śrīrdhīḥ saṃkalpavikalpinī |
	śāntidāntyādimitrasya sātvikasya vijṛmbhitam ||
	śrotratvagakṣirasanāghrāṇendriyamayīṃ daśām |
	rājasasyāhuraiśvaryaṃ harṣabhītyādidāyinaḥ ||
	vāṇī pāṇiratho pādaḥ pāyūpasthamiti kramāt |
	mohālasyādisuhṛdastāmasasya paricchadaḥ ||
	atha yā mahatī siddhiḥ prasūtā prastutadvayāt |
	tasyāḥ pañcavidhā sphūrjā sādhakeṣvanubhūyate ||



	yathepsitaśarīrāptiḥ praveśo'nyasya varṣmaṇi |
	dūrādhvayānāyānaṃ ca dūraśravaṇadarśanam ||
	adṛśyakaraṇaṃ ceti tatprakāraḥ pratīyatām |
	puṇyakriyeti kāpyasti viśvotpattyai sthitistayoḥ ||
	vīryaṃ gāmbhīryamaiśvaryaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ dātṛtetyapi |
	tasyāḥ prathanavaicitryaṃ yallabhyaṃ bhāgyaśālibhiḥ ||
	itthaṃ tatraiva cinnāḍī jñānasūtramiti smṛtā |
	citrapadmaśivākhyāśca śaktyātmābhihite api ||
	nāḍīḥ pañca prapañce'smin prasuvānā pravartate |
	evaṃ hāṭakakālāgnī kūṭasthaḥ kūrma ityapi ||
	anantaḥ śaktyupaśliṣṭaḥ kapilarṣiriti kramāt |
	tadvijṛmbhaiva boddhavyā saptapātāladhāriṇī ||
	uktaitadakhilāabhogasvabhāvatve'pyanāvilaḥ |
	nistaraṅga ivāmbhodhirvahan gāmbhīryasampadam ||
	cidānandeṣaṇājñānakriyāpañcakaśaktimān |
	bhairavaḥ paramo nāthastattvānte kathayiṣyate ||
	prapañcavaicitryabhuvaṃ prapañcayan 
prarūḍhapañcārtharahaścamatkriyām |
	prakāśayāmāsa maheśvaraḥ svayaṃ paraṃ 
parāmarśamayīmahaṃsphurām ||
	ahaṅkriyā nityamahaṅkriyāyāṃ parākramo yasya prākrame ca |
	sa eva vīro nanu tasya śāstraṃ vimarśaśāṇollikhitā svaśaktiḥ ||

ityalamatirahasyonmīlanasāhasollāsena || 20 ||

	athāhaṅkārabuddhimanasāṃ tattvamarthadvāropadarśayati -

	kallolāyamānāni sadā hṛdayāmbunidhau trīṇi karaṇāni |
	ākarṣantīdantāṃ tatrāhantāṃ cātrārpayanti || 21 ||



	vyākhyātarūpaṃ svahṛdayaṃ hi vaitatyasarvatattvāspadatvādinā 
dharmeṇa 

	namaḥ pramātṛvapuṣe śivacaitanyasindhave

iti sthityā samudratayā'dhyavasīyate | tatra kālaviśeṣāvacchedavyudāsena 
mahātaraṅgavadācaranti trīṇi karaṇāni pramātṛrūpasya kartuḥ 
sādhakatamānīndriyāṇi vidyante | tāni ca yathā idantānuprāṇitāṃ 
viṣayavaicitryasampadaṃ tatra hṛdayāntarādākarṣanti, 
haṭhādanupraveśayanti, tadvadahantāsārāṃ svahṛdayacicchaktimapyatra 
vedyabhūmāvarpayanti, anaisargikatve'pi naisargikatayā'nubhāvayanti | 
tāni cāhaṅkāro buddhirmana iti ca vyavahriyante tatra mamedaṃ na 
mamedamityabhimānasādhanamahaṅkāraḥ, adhyavasāyanimittaṃ buddhiḥ, 
saṅkalpavikalpaheturmana iti pratyekaṃ lakṣaṇam | ayamarthaḥ - 
ahaṅkārādīnyātmano'ntaḥkaraṇāni na kevalaṃ bahiravalokitān 
viṣayānantaranubhāvayanti, kiṃ tarhi svasaṃkrāntayā pramātṛcicchaktyā 
taṃ bahirvartinamakhilamapi vedyavargaṃ 
viṣayībhāvayogyatānupraveśanārthaṃ 
prakāśamānasvabhāvatāpādanātmakapāvanīkaraṇayuktyā 
jighatsitauṣadhādyabhimantraṇanyāyenānugṛhṇantīti kallolāyamānānīti | 
etāni hi hṛdayamahāmburāśau mahātaraṅgāḥ | jñānakarmendriyāṇi tu 
taraṅgānutaraṅganyāyamanuvartanta ityarthaḥ | eṣāṃ ca 
vibhūtiparispandaprācuryamanantaramevātyantaṃ vitatya vyākhyātam || 21 
||

	atha jñānendriyāṇyunmudrayati -

	hṛdayasthitasya vibhorviṣayāloko viśṛṅkhalo bhavati |
	jñānendriyadīpeṣu nijanijagolāgranityalagneṣu || 22 ||

	vibhoḥ paramasvātantryaśālinaḥ svaśaktyavacchinnāśeṣa-
deśakālasvabhāvasya mahāprakāśasya taducitaṃ kiñcit sarvotkṛṣṭaṃ 
sthānamasti, yaddhṛdayaṃ nāma | yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	saiṣā sāratayā coktā hṛdayaṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ iti |



tatrāvasthitasya cāsya bāhyānāṃ śabdasparśādīnāṃ viṣayāṇāṃ ya 
ālokaḥ ā sāmantāllokanaṃ sākalyataḥ svāntaścarvaṇacāturyam, sa 
viśṛṅkhalo bhavati bahirbhāvasvabhāvaniyantraṇātikrānto bhavati | atra 
caitadeva nibandhanaṃ yad jñānapradhānānāmindriyāṇāṃ 
prakāśakatvaprakarṣeṇa pradīpaprāyāṇāṃ ye 
nijanijāstattadasādhāraṇā golāḥ śravaṇaśaṣkulyādayaḥ, tadagreṣu 
bahirviṣayaunmukhyānuguṇeṣu pradeśeṣu sārvakālikaṃ lagnaṃ 
sphurattayā'vasthānamiti | tāni ca śrotraṃ tvak cakṣurjihvā ghrāṇamiti 
pañcadhā bhidyante | śabdasparśarūparasagandhānāṃ grāhyāṇāṃ 
krameṇa grahaṇopakaraṇatvameṣāṃ lakṣaṇam || 22 ||

atha karmendriyāṇyunmīlayati -

	bhavanti karaṇāni pañca khalu karmapradhānāni lokanāthasya |
	spandate svairaṃ yairjano jaḍād vilakṣaṇo bhavan || 23 ||

	lokasya jaḍājaḍavibhāgenāvalokyamānasya viśvasya yo nāthaḥ 
sṛṣṭyādinirvāhakatayā svāmī tasya karmapradhānāni 
jñānendriyāṇāṃ kriyāsparśe'pi jñānaprācuryavad 
jñānānuṣaṅge'pi kriyāśaktyulbaṇāni pañcendriyāṇi bhavanti | khalu 
prasiddhau yairnimittabhūtaiḥ, jano jananamaraṇādyupadruto'pi jīvavargaḥ, 
jaḍāt stambhakumbhādeḥ paricchinnaprakāśādarthād 
vyatirekamaśnuvānaḥ, spandate īṣaccalati, tattadindriyaprāpyādarthādupari 
na kiñciccalati | tāvaccalati | svairamiti | svasyātmīyasyopakaraṇasya 
preraṇaṃ yathā bhavati tathetyarthaḥ | tādṛk svātantryameva 
cāsyā'paricchinnaprakāśatvam | tāni ca vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthā iti 
vibhidyante | tallakṣaṇaṃ ca kramād 
vacanādānaviharaṇavisargānandātmakakriyāsādhanatvam | atraivaṃ 
vivekaḥ -

	antaḥkaraṇavarge'sminnahaṅkāro'bhimānabhūḥ |
	sahakāritayā gṛhṇannanyaddhīmanasordvayam ||



	pravartayati kartṝṇāṃ bahirjñānaṃ kriyāmapi |
	kramādetaddvayākrāntyā jñānakarmendriyaprathā ||
	tatra jñānapradhānaṃ saccakṣurādyupapāditam |
	kāyapravṛttyabhāve'pi viṣayagrahaṇakṣamam ||
	karmottareṣu teṣveva viśeṣaḥ parikīrtyate |
	kriyā hi hānamādānamiti dvedhā'nubhūyate ||
	bāhyābhyantarabhāvena dvaividhyaṃ taddvayorapi |
	bāhyatāyāṃ tayoḥ pāyurhānaprādhānyabhāg bhavet ||
	ādānapravaṇaḥ pāṇiḥ pādastadubhayakriyaḥ |
	antaryadetadakhilaṃ prāṇo vitanute marut ||
	tad vāgindriyamākhyātaṃ śabdasṛṣṭivicakṣaṇam |
	yadetaduktavikṣobhaśāntyā viśrāntirātmanaḥ ||
	tadupastha iti jñeyamānandotāramindriyam |
	saṃbhūyavṛttāvapyeṣāṃ prādhānyaṃ kvāpi kasyacit || iti || 23 ||

	atha śabdādiviṣayapañcakaṃ vivinakti -

	viśvodyānavirūḍhāni gandhapramukhāni sugandhīni puṣpāṇi |
	pañcāpyājighran krīḍati trailokyadhūrtto devaḥ || 24 ||

	trailokye mānameyamātṛrūpe viśvasminnapi yo dhūrto 
vaidagdhyāvadhīritānyavyavahārakalāpaḥ, sa 
dyotanasvabhāvatvānmahān pramātā bhavan 
viśvalakṣaṇādudyānādutpannāni pṛthivyādyasādhāraṇaguṇagaṇaṃ 
gandhamādīkṛtya pravṛttāni sugandhīni 
prakāśaparimalaparisphuraṇolbaṇāni pañcāpi prasūnānyājighran 
grahaṇaṃ prati kiñcitkauṭilyasadbhāve'pyārjavenānusandadhānaḥ 
krīḍati harṣānusāreṇa spandate | svasvātantryamupadarśayatīti yāvat | te 
ca viṣayāḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhā 



iti bhavanti | eṣāṃ ca kramādākāśādiguṇatvaṃ śrotrādigrāhyatvaṃ 
vā prātisvikaṃ lakṣaṇamūhyam | gandhapramukhānīti 
saṃhārakrameṇoktistanmātrāsu gandhasya prādhānyaṃ prakaṭayitum | 
yaduktamabhiyuktaiḥ -

	indriyadvārasaṃgrāhyairgandhādyairātmadevatāḥ |
	svabhāvena samārādhyā jñātuḥ so'yaṃ mahāmakhaḥ || iti |

gandho'pi nirūpyamāṇaḥ pṛthivī | pṛthivyā upari na kiñcid 
bhūtāntaramityatrotkarṣa iti rahasyam || 24 ||

	atha bhūtapañcakamudbhāvayati -

	styānasya kramavaśādikṣurasasyeva śivaprakāśasya |
	guḍapiṇḍā iva pañcāpi bhūtāni madhuratāṃ na muñcanti || 25 ||

	śivātmā khalu prakāśaḥ śaktisadāśivādiparipāṭyanusāreṇa 
prastutabhūtapañcakaparyantaṃ styānībhavati | 
veditṛsvabhāvanyagbhāvādhīnavedyatotkarṣātmakaṃ 
kāṭhinyamanubhavati | yaduktaṃ śrīśivadṛṣṭau -

	...................... ityādiṣattriṃśattattvarūpatām |
	bibhrad bibharti rūpāṇi tāvato vyavahārataḥ ||
	yāvat sthūlaṃ jaḍābhāsaṃ saṃhataṃ pārthivaṃ ghanam | iti |

tādṛśasya cāsya madhuratāṃ sarvapramātṛsvātmasvabhāvatā'nu-
bhāvyaparamaśivaprakāśollāsātmakamahāhlādopalakṣaṇaṃ 
sāmarasyaṃ pañcāpi bhūtānyākāśaprabhūtāni na parityajanti, kintu 
svāntarakhilaṃ garbhīkṛtya pravartante | kevalaṃ śivāt svacchasvabhāvāt 
styānatādhikyameteṣāṃ bhedaḥ | yadvadikṣurasasya svapākayuktikramāt 
styānībhūtasya mādhurya guḍapiṇḍairna parityajyate | yathā ca 
paramārthasāre -

	rasaphāṇitaśarkarikāguḍakhaṇḍādyā yathekṣurasa eva |
	tadvadavasthābhedāḥ sarve paramātmanaḥ śambhoḥ ||



iti | yathā ca lakṣmītantre -

	saṃvideva hi rūpaṃ me svacchasvacchandanirbharā ||
	sāpīkṣurasavad yogāt styānatāṃ pratipadyate |
	ato nirūpyamāṇaṃ taccaityaṃ cittvamupaiṣyati || iti |

tataśca pṛthivyāmanāśritaśivaprabhṛtīni pañcatriṃśadapi tattvāni 
kāraṇavāsanānuvṛttidvārā parisphurantītyanayā bhaṅgyā tatra 
śivatattve pṛthivyādīni sarvāṇyapi satkāryavādamaryādayā'vatiṣṭhante 
| evaṃ prakṛtipuruṣādiṣu madhyavartiṣvapyuktobhayaprakriyayā 
sarvāṇyapyuparitanānyadhastanāni ca tattvāni saṃmilantīti sarva 
sarvātmakamityarthaniṣkarṣaḥ syāt | taduktaṃ śrītantrāloke -

	ekaikatra ca tattve'pi ṣaṭtriṃśattattvarūpatā iti |

sarvathā pārameśvaraprakāśasphurattātmakametadakhilamapi 
viśvavaicitryamityuktaṃ bhavati | yaduktaṃ śrīśivadṛṣṭau -

	evaṃ sarvapadārthānāṃ samaiva śivatā sthitā |
	parāparādibhedo'tra śraddadhānairudāhṛtaḥ || iti |

bhūtāni cākāśaḥ, vāyuḥ, tejaḥ, āpaḥ, pṛthivīti prasiddhāni | 
śabdādiguṇatvaṃ ca tallakṣaṇaṃ spaṣṭam | yadvā 
avakāśadhvanikaratvam, calanasaṃjīvanatā, pācakadāhakatvam, 
drāvakaplāvakatā, kāṭhinyadhārakatvaṃ ca kramādamīṣāṃ lakṣaṇāni 
| atra ca varṇakrame pṛthivyādipuruṣāntaṃ sparśākṣarāṇi iti 
śrītriṃśikāśāstraprakriyayā prāgeva pradarśitam | pañcakañcukyāṃ 
tvantasthāḥ | tatra niyatiḥ sarvānusyūteti na pṛthaggaṇanārhā | kālasya tu 
ākāśaśaktitvāccinmayataikopapādanapravṛttatayā puṃsaḥ -

	māyāpramātṛtādānaṃ pratyaucityaṃ na vidyate 

ityantasthāścatasraḥ saṃjātāḥ | yathoktaṃ tatraiva -

	vāyvagnisalilendrāṇāṃ dhāraṇānāṃ catuṣṭayam iti |



yathā ca vyākhyātamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ - ākāśaśaktyā tu 
nātra māyāpramātṛtādāne prayojanamasti iti | śuddhavidyādiśivāntaṃ 
punarūṣmāṇaḥ | tatra hakāraḥ śaktiḥ śivaścetyāvṛttyā'vatiṣṭhate, 
tasyānuttaravisarjanīyasāmarasyātmakatvāt | śivaśaktyostyaikarūpyameva 
svabhāvaḥ | bhedastu kālpanika ityupapāditatvācca | yathoktam -

	tadūrdhvaṃ śādi vikhyātaṃ purastād brahmapañcakam iti |
	ṣaṭtriṃśatyatra tattveṣu pṛthvyambupavanādayaḥ |
	te te padārthāḥ saṃyuktyā miśritā bahuśākhayā ||
	stambhakumbhādibhāvena kurvantyarthakriyāṃ nṛṇām |
	tatra karmendriyairuktairbahiṣṭhāste ghaṭādayaḥ ||
	arpyante sannikarṣeṇa vyavahartṛjanaṃ prati |
	sāṃvidairindriyaistvete nirīkṣyante tathā tathā ||
	antaḥkaraṇavargeṇa saṃkalpitavikalpitāḥ |
	niścitābhimatāścaite vidyayā ca vivecitāḥ ||
	kalākālādibhirbhāvairvedyatvenānurañjitāḥ |
	viśrāmyanti vimarśāḍhye svātmarūpe pramātari ||
	sa ca pramātā dehe'smin prāṇe dhīśūnyayorapi |
	badhnāti yairahambhāvaṃ te sadvidyādayaḥ kramāt ||

evaṃ parāmarśamayīṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ lakṣmāhurāptāḥ paramasya 
śambhoḥ |
	etadviparyāsavaśāt paśuḥ syāt pramāṇamasmin guruśāsanaṃ 
naḥ || 25 ||

	uktarūpasya tattvaprapañcasya piṇḍībhūtamarthatattvamāha -

	sarvasya bhuvanavibhramayantrollāsasya tantuvalliva |
	vimarśasaṃrambhamayī ujjṛmbhate śambhormahāśaktiḥ || 26 ||

	yo'yaṃ bhuvanātmanāṃ tattvātmanāṃ vibhramo vilāsaḥ 
prasaradrūpatā saiva dārvādisaṃghaṭṭanātmanaḥ 
pratimāputrakāderyantrasyollāso valanāvartanādirvikāraḥ, 



tasya sarvasyāpi tattadaśeṣasandhibandhānusyūtā sūtrasragiva śambhoḥ 
paramānandaprakāśaghanatayā viśvamayaviśvottirṇasyānāśritādi-
kālāgnirudrāntaviśvavaicitryasaṃyojanaviyojanavaidagdhyaśālinaḥ 
paramaśivabhaṭṭārakasya śaktiḥ svātantryalakṣaṇā mahatī 
tattvānāmanyonyaprayojyaprayojakabhāve paryantataḥ 
sāmānyaprayojakatve ca pragalbhā bhavatyujjṛmbhate pṛthivyādi 
bhāvavargamivānāśritaśivabhaṭṭārakamapi kroḍīkṛtyābhivardhate 
tadrūpatayā parisphuratītyarthaḥ | yathā ca paryantapañcāśikāyām -

	citsvābhāvyādasau devaḥ svātmanā vimṛśan prabhuḥ |
	anāśritādibhūmyantā bhūmikāḥ pratipadyate || iti |

sā ca vimarśasaṃrambhamayī vimarśākhyo yaḥ saṃrambhaḥ 
svāntaḥsphuratkriyāśaktisphārarūpaḥ, tena prakṛtetyarthaḥ | sā 
cidānandecchājñānakriyārūpaśaktipañcakasāmarasyasva-
bhāvetyākhyāyate | tataśca parameśvaraparāmarśaprasaraparipāṭī-
pāṭavaparamārthametadakhilaṃ tattvaparamparāparisphuraṇa-
prācuryojjvalaṃ viśvavijṛmbhāvaicitryamiti tātparyārthaḥ | yathoktaṃ 
śrīpratyabhijñāhṛdaye - citiḥ svatantrāviśvasiddhihetuḥ iti | so'yaṃ 
svātmasātkṛtāśeṣaṣaṭtriṃśattattvakalāpo mahān 
paramaśivabhaṭṭārakāhvayaḥ pramātā saptatriṃśatayā svīkriyate | tasya 
tādṛgrūpatāpi paryantato vikalpakakṣyāmanupraviśati | avikalpātmanā ca 
bhāvyaṃ viśvottareṇetyato'trāpyavikalpavṛttiraṣṭātriṃśaḥ 
kaścidāgameṣvaṅgīkriyate | yaduktaṃ śrītantravaṭadhānikāyām -

	ṣaṭtriṃśattattvaparyāyastadabhinnaḥ paraḥ śivaḥ |
	upadeśyatayā so'pi syādavacchedabhāgataḥ ||
	aṣṭātriṃśaṃ paraṃ dhāma yatredaṃ viśvakaṃ sphuret | iti |

kiñca, śambhuratrānuttarātmā tasya tādṛśī śaktiśca 
tadavibhinnasvabhāvā svarasamudāyamayī visargāparaparyāyeti 
varṇakramānuguṇyena vyākhyā | sā 
cānuttarānande(ccho?ccheśa)nonmeṣonatābhiricchāyā īśanasya ca 
kramādasthairya-



bhedācca catuṣprakārairvedyollāsairicchonmeṣayorīśanonata-
yoścānuttarānandābhyāmubhābhyāṃ saha sandhānopārūḍhau dvau, 
punastatsaṃhitau ca dvāviti caturbhiruktāśeṣavedyavargavāsanātmanā 
bindunā conmiṣantī tatraivānuttaratattvāntarviśrāmyati | 
tattaścānuttarahakārābhyāṃ tatparyantapaṭhitena bindubhaṭṭārakeṇa ca 
saha pratyāhārakramāt sarvasaṃvitsamāveśalakṣaṇaḥ kaścidahamiti 
parāmarśa udeti | yaḥ mahāhradānusandhānānmantravīryānubhavaḥ iti, 
mātṛkācakrasambodhaḥ iti ca śrīśivasūtreṣūpadiśyate | yathoktaṃ 
śrīkaṇṭhīyasaṃhitāyām -

	ādimāntavihīnāstu mantrāḥ syuḥ śaradabhravat |
	gurorlakṣaṇametāvadādimāntaṃ nivedayet ||

yathā cāsmadgurubhiḥ śrīsaṃvitstotre -

	ādimāntimagṛhītavarṇarāśyātmikāhamiti yā svataḥ prathā |
	mantravīryamiti sādhitāgamaistanmayo gururasi tvamambike || iti |

vīrasaṃbandhī hi dharmo vīryamityucyate | tatra vīro nāma vividhamīrayati 
viśvavaicitryamiti bhagavān śabdarāśibhaṭṭāraka ucyate | taduktaṃ 
śrīmaccidgaganacandrikāyām -

	īraṇena vividhena vīratāṃ yo'yamakṣaragaṇaḥ prapadyate iti |

śrīprabhākaule ca -

	vāme vīrāḥ samākhyātāḥ iti |

etacca -

	sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ vidyānāṃ ca yaśasvini ! |
	iyaṃ yoniḥ samākhyātā sarvatantreṣu sarvadā ||

iti śrītriṃśikāśāstra unmīlitam || 26 ||

	nanvadhvāno hi ṣaḍiti āgameṣu prasiddhāḥ | tatra tattvavrātātmā 
kaścidevāṃśaḥ | anyatra tu kathamuktārthopapattirityākāṅkṣāṃ 
kṣapayan prakṛtagāthāyāstātparyārthamāha -



	yadadhvanāṃ ca ṣaṭkaṃ tatra prakāśārthalakṣaṇamardham |
	vimarśaśabdasvabhāvamardhamiti śivasya yāmalollāsaḥ || 27 ||

	adhvāno hi ṣaḍityāgameṣu prasiddham | te ca varṇāḥ, padāni, 
mantrāḥ, kalāḥ, tattvāni, bhuvanānītyākhyāyante | tatra varṇāḥ 
pañcāśallokaprasiddhāḥ | padānyānantye'pi 
vyomavyāpyādimantramaryādayaikāśītiḥ | mantrāśca tadvadānantye'pi 
brahmapañcakamaṅgaṣaṭkaṃ ceti saṃkocadṛṣṭyā ekādaśa | kalā 
nivṛttyādayaḥ pañca | tatvānyuktarūpāṇi ṣaṭtriṃśat | bhuvanāni ca 
kālāgnirudrādīnyanāśritāntānīti caturviṃśatyadhikaṃ śatadvayam | 
etadakhilamapi kalāpañcakena kroḍīkriyate | tathāhi - nivṛttau kalāyāṃ 
kṣakāra eko varṇaḥ | aṣṭāviṃśatiḥ padāni | hṛdayasadyojātau mantrau | 
pṛthivī tattvamekam | kālāgnirudrādīni bhadrakālyantānyaṣṭottaraśataṃ 
bhuvanāni | pratiṣṭhāyāṃ hādayaṣṭāntā varṇāstrayoviṃśatiḥ | 
ekaviṃśatiḥ padāni | śirovāmadevau mantrau | aptattvādīni prakṛtyantāni 
trayoviṃśatistattvāni | amareśādīni śrīkaṇṭhāntāni ca ṣaṭpañcāśad 
bhuvanāni | vidyāyāṃ ādighāntāḥ sapta varṇāḥ | viṃśatiḥ padāni | 
śikhāghoraśceti mantradvayam | puruṣaprabhṛtīni māyāparyantāni sapta 
tattvāni | bhīmādīnyaṅguṣṭhamātrāntāni ca saptaviṃśatirbhuvanāni | 
śāntau tu gakhakāstrayo varṇāḥ | padānyekādaśa | kavacatatpuruṣau 
mantrau | śuddhavidyeśvarasadāśivāstrīṇi tattvāni | 
vāmādisadāśivāntamaṣṭādaśa bhuvanāni | śāntyatītāyāṃ ca 
varṇāḥ ṣoḍaśa svarāḥ | padamekam | (śivaḥ ?) īśāno netramastramiti 
mantratrayam | śivastattvamekaṃ yaḥ śaktisvabhava ityāmnāyate | 
nivṛttyādīnyanāśritāntāni pañcadaśa bhuvanānīti vivekaḥ | etacca 
siddhāntāditantreṣvatyantaṃ vitatyopapāditamiti saṃkṣepeṇoktam | eva ca 
sati yadetadadhvanāmuktarūpāṇāṃ ṣaṭkam, tatra yadekamardhaṃ 
bhuvanatattvakalāsvabhāvam | anyat tu mantrapadavarṇātmakam | pratyekaṃ 
ca sthūlasūkṣmaparaprakriyayā traividhyam | tatra 
prathamamabhidheyabhūtaṃ dvitīyamabhidhānakārakamiti vibhāgaḥ | 
tathā ca sati vācyavargaḥ sarvo'pi prakāśaparamārthaḥ, vācakollekhastu 
vimarśavapuriti śivasya 



prakāśavimarśātmanaḥ parameśvarasya yāmala 
ubhayavisargāraṇisvabhāva ullāsaḥ | 
unmeṣanimeṣaśaktidvitayayaugapadyānubhūticamatkāra ityarthaḥ | yathā 
paryantapañcāśikāyām -

	tatra vācakavācyātmaspandayorekaśaḥ prabhoḥ |
	sthūlasūkṣmaparābhāsakramayoḥ ṣaḍvidhādhvatā || iti |

yathā ca śrīvirūpākṣapañcāśikāyām -

	yasya vimarśasya kaṇaḥ padamantrārṇātmakastridhā śabdaḥ |
	puratatvakalātmārtho dharmiṇa itthaṃ prakāśasya || iti |

śrīcidgaganacandrikāyāṃ ca -

	yā'hamityutyuditavāk parābhidhā yaḥ prakāśa uditārthavigrahaḥ |
	dvau mithaḥ samuditāvihonmukhau tau ṣaḍadhvapitarau śraye śivau || 
iti |

evamāśayenaiva hyasmatparamagurubhiḥ śrīsaubhāgyahṛdayastotre -

	varṇaḥ kalā padaṃ tattvaṃ mantro bhuvanameva ca |
	ityadhvaṣaṭkaṃ deveśi bhāti tvayi cidātmani ||

etyadhvanāmuddeśastattadyāmalatayonmīlitaḥ | etena vāgarthāviva 
saṃpṛktau ityādīni mahākavivākyāni vyākhyātāni | ataśca 
śabdārthasāmarasyātmani sāhitye'pyasmadāgrahaḥ 
pārameśvaro'nugraha eva | yadanuprāṇanāḥ 
kuṇḍalābharaṇamukundakeliparimala(guhā?)komalavallīstavanakha-
pralāpādayaḥ prabandhāḥ prakhyāyante | 
tayorvimarśaprakāśasvabhāvatāyāḥ - citiḥ pratyavamarśātmā parā 
vāk svarasoditā iti, ātmārthasya prakāśatā iti ca pratyabhijñāpitatvāt | 
evaṃ ca prācīnagāthāyāṃ śambhoḥ prakāśasvarūpasya 
śaktirvimarśamayītyapi vyākhyātam | svaparāmarśopāyabhūtatvād 
varṇakalādīnāmadhvaśabdavyapadeśaḥ | yathā śrīsvacchande -

	muktānāṃ śivatāvyaktikāraṇaṃ viśvameva hi |
	śivatāvyaktimārgatvāt te sarve'pi kalādayaḥ ||
	adhvaśabdena kathyante te sarve bandhanā api | iti |



śrīvijñānabhairave ca -

	bhuvanādhvādirūpeṇa cintayet kramaśo'khilam |
	sthūlasūkṣmaparasthityā yāvadante manolayaḥ || iti || 27 ||

	evamuktanītyā viśvavaicitryasya sarvasyāpi 
prakāśavimarśadvayāntarbhāvamupapādya tayorapi bhedaḥ paryantata 
aupacārika ityāha -

	ālekhyaviśeṣa iva gajavṛṣabhayordvayaḥ pratibhāsam |
	ekasminnevārthe śivaśaktivibhāgakalpanāṃ kurmaḥ || 28 ||

	citrakṛto hi svavaidagdhyaprakaṭanāya gajavṛṣabhādīnāṃ 
bhinnasvabhāvānāmapi bhāvānāmekatarasanniveśayuktyaikāva-
bhāso'nyatarasanniveśayuktyā'nyasphuraṇaṃ ca yathā bhavati, tathā 
vilikhantītyavisaṃvādinīyaṃ maryādā | evaṃ sthite yathā 
gajavṛṣabhobhayākārasamarpake citraviśeṣe gajavṛṣabhayordvayorapi 
paryālocakapramātṛjanānusandhānadhārādhirohavaiśiṣṭyena 
kumbhamaṇḍalaśuṇḍākuṇḍalībhāvādiyogāt 
kakudakūṭaprothapuṭaudvaṇyādikramācca pratiniyato'vabhāsaḥ 
prakāśanaṃ bhavati | tadvadekasminnevārthe sarveṣāmadhvanāṃ 
paryantataḥ prāpye tatve śivaḥ śaktiriti prakāśo vimarśa ityevaṃvidho yo 
yo vibhāgo vicitrasvabhāvānatikrānto'pyatikrānta 
ivā'vabhāsamānoṃ'śaḥ, tasya yatkalpanaṃ svatastathāsamarthī-
bhavatastatsamarthācaraṇenonmīlanam, tat kurmo jānīmo 
vadāmo'nutiṣṭhāmaśca | yadāhuḥ - phalabhedāropitabhedaḥ 
padārthātmā śaktiḥ iti | atra ca gajavṛṣabhayoriti dvandve gajau ca 
vṛṣabhau cetitaretarayogānuguṇo vigrahaḥ | bhāṣyakṛtāpyevameva 
vigṛhītam - dhavau ca khadirau ca iti | tataśca gajaśabdo gajavṛṣabhau 
dvāvapi vakti | tathā vṛṣabhapadamapi vṛṣabhagajāvubhāvapītyāpatitam | 
tat tu nānyeṣāṃ śobhate | padārthānāṃ pratiniyatarūpaparityāge 
pratyavāyabhīrutvāt | asmākaṃ tu 
svatantrādvaitasaṃvitsamayasāmrājyaśālinām 



	dyaṭa iti pṛthivīti dravyamityaṅga tadvad
		bhavati nanu vikāso bhāva ityāvighātam |
	yadi vilasati yuktistatpurastho ghaṭo'yaṃ
		kathaya na kathameko viśvamūrtirvibhāti ||

iti nyāyādekasyāpi bhāvasya svavyatiriktāśeṣabhāvātmakatvama-
bhyupagatamityalamanyajanadoṣodbhāvanopakrameṇa | 
etenābhihitānvayavādādanvitābhidhānavādasyaucityamastītyuktaṃ 
bhavati | tatra hi stambhaṃ paśyetyādau stambhaśabdārthānvitaiva 
darśanakriyā kriyāpadenābhidhīyate | evaṃ paśyatyarthānvitaśca 
stambhaḥ karmakārakeṇetyanantaropapāditārthānuguṇyenānvaya 
ityupapāadyata iti | evaṃ ca prākaraṇikayorapi prakāśavimarśayoḥ -

	svargo bhūriti sāgaraḥ sariditi svāmin ! drumo vīrudi-
	tyarthaḥ śabda iti svaraḥ śrutiriti pratyak parāgityapi |
	śuklaṃ raktamiti prabhā tama iti jñānaṃ kriyetyādi te
	yugmaṃ sarvamayugmanetra ! vitatirvidyāvimṛṣṭyordvayoḥ ||

ityādinītyā yadyapi sarvaprapañcasphuraṇaparyavasānasthalatayā 
tattadyāmalollāsatvenobhayathā vyavahārāvaśyambhāvaḥ, tathāpi tatra -

	na śivena vinā śaktirna śaktirahitaḥ śivaḥ |

ityādinītyā prakāśo na kadācid vimarśakriyatāṃ vimarśo'pi na 
prakāśakartṛkatāṃ vyabhicaratītyanayorvāstavaṃ 
vapuraikyasvabhāvasaubhāgyabhavyamiti tātparyārthaḥ | yaduktaṃ mayaiva 
śrīkomalavallīstave -

	tvaṃ yathā śivamayī tathā śiva-
		stvanmayo hi śivayorabhedinoḥ |
	tattvamekamabahirmukhāspadaṃ
		yatra bhinna iva viśvavikriyā || iti || 28 ||

	nanu bahavo hi purāṇāgamādiprasiddhāḥ pramātāraḥ, 
bahutarāśca samprati pratyakṣamavekṣyamāṇāḥ | teṣu na 
kasyacidapyetādṛśī śaktirasti, yādṛk parameśvarābhimate prakhyāpyata 
ityāśaṅkyāha -



	tilamātre'pi śarīre prekṣadhvaṃ kīṭasyaitāvatī śaktiḥ |
	sā svacchandaśriyo viśvaśarīrasya kiyatī bhavatu || 29 ||

	parameśvaro hi viśvaśarīra ityāgameṣvākhyāyate | yaduktaṃ 
śrīśivasūtreṣu - dṛśyaṃ śarīram iti | yathā ca 
śrīvirūpākṣapañcāśikāyām -

	vimatipadamaṅga ! sarvaṃ mama caitanyātmanaḥ śarīramidam |
	śūnyaśyapadānnīlāvadhi dṛśtvāt piṇḍavat siddham || iti |

tacca tasya svacchandaśrīkatvāt | svenātmīyena chandenecchāśaktyā 
śrīryathābhilaṣitamaiśvarya yasyeti kṛtvā | viśvaśarīratayā ca tasya 
sarvātiśāyinī sā kācicchaktirupakalpyate | svaśarīrānuguṇaśaktikatvaṃ 
cānyatrāpi dṛṣṭam | yathā tilapramāṇaśarīro yūkādiḥ 
kīṭaviśeṣastaccharīraucityena tadādhikyena vā śaktimattayopalakṣyata iti | 
granthayojanā tu - paramāṇudvyaṇukādisanniveśāstāvadāsatāṃ 
prāṇiviśeṣāḥ, teṣāmapratyakṣatvād dṛṣṭāntasyāhṛdayaṅgamībhāva-
prasaṅgāt | yaḥ punaryūkādiḥ kīṭastasya śarīraṃ tāvat 
tilasadṛśasannīveśanam | tāvatyapi tasya śaktiḥ kiyatīti prekṣadhvam | 
parisphuraṇaparibhramaṇādibhedādanekarūpā hyanubhūyate | loṭpratyayo 
bahuvacanaṃ ca tatrārthe prāmāṇyaṃ spaṣṭamudghāṭayitum | (tasmāditi 
?) | etaddṛṣṭāntābhyupagame -

	tvaddhāmni viśvavandye'smin niyatikrīḍane sati |
	tava nātha ! kiyān bhūyānānandarasasaṃplavaḥ ||

iti śrīmatstotrāvalisthityā tasya sā śaktiḥ kiyatī bhavatu, etāvatyetādṛśī 
veti kathaṃ pariccetuṃ pāryata iti | itthaṃ ca vipratipannajanāpekṣayā 
bhagavato viśvaśarīratvamanumānena prasādhya tato'yaṃ 
tasyaiśvaryotkarṣa upapāditaḥ | sampratipattau tu pratyuta tayaiva śaktyā 
tadviśvaśarīratvam | taduktaṃ śrīśivasūtreṣu - śaktisandhāne 
śarīrotpattiḥ iti, svaśaktipracayo viśvam 

 

iti ca | evaṃ cātmano viśvaśarīratā parāmarśaśūnyatāmātra-
nibandhanaṃ bahirvibhūtispandānubhūtyutsavastaimityam | anyathā piṇḍa 
ivāṇḍe'pi sarvatra svasya svātantryaśaktiraparokṣamupalakṣyeta | 
yaduktaṃ śrīvirūpākṣapañcāśikāyām -

	dehe'smitayā yadvajjaḍayorāsphālanaṃ mitho bāhvoḥ |
	icchāmātreṇetthaṃ giryorapi tadvaśājjagati || iti || 29 ||

	nanu viśvaśarīratayā parameśvarasya śaktyucchrāyopakalpanaṃ cet 
kriyate, tarhi viśvasaṃhāre tasya śaktiśūnyatāprasaṅga ityāśaṅkyāha -

	viśvonmeṣadaśāyāṃ deśikanāthasya yāvān prasaraḥ |
	kalalāvasthayā sthito'pi viśvanimeṣe'pi tāvān bhavati || 30 ||

	deśikatayā sarvānugrāhakatayā nāthyamānasya parameśvarasya 
sā kācidunmeṣayaugapadyalakṣaṇā śaktirasti | tatra ca svarūponmeṣe 
viśvanimeṣaḥ, viśvonmeṣe ca svarūpanimeṣa iti dvitayamapi 
tulādhṛtavadutpadyate | tataśca viśvātmako viśvottirṇaśca parameśvara 
ityavaṭāṭaṅkasaṅketaucityamupapādyate | tayośca 
nityamanyonyasāpekṣatvādekataravyudāsaśaṅkāyāmanyatara-
bhaṅgaprasaṅga ityubhayorapi samaprādhānyena parigrahaucityam | 
taduktaṃ mayaiva śrīparāstotre -

	eke bhūjalakhānilānalakalārabdhāṃ bahiḥ prakriyā-
	muttirṇātviṣamantareva katicit citkākaṇīmūcire |
	anye kecana yāmalāmṛtasaritsaṃbhedasaṃbhogino 
	mātastvāmapṛthakprarohamubhayoraucityamācakṣate || iti |

tathā ca sati viśvasyonmeṣalakṣaṇāyāmavasthāyāmantaścicchakti-
nimeṣāparaparyāyāyāṃ yāvān prasaraḥ ṣaḍadhvollāsaprathāsatattvo 
bhavati, tathā viśvasya nimeṣe svātmāntaḥkroḍīkāraikapāriśeṣye satyapi 
tāvānunmeṣadaśāsamasvabhāvo 



bhavati | na tarhi viśvanimeṣa iti cet ? svairaṃ nimeṣa eva | api tu 
kalalāvasthayā'vasthitiriti brūmaḥ | yathā śikhaṇḍyaṇḍe 
sarvaśikhaṇḍyavayavānupraviṣṭabarhaparibarhādivarṇarekhāvaicitrya-
śilpakalpanākauśalamakhilamapyatyantasūkṣmekṣikayā'vadhārya-
mastītyaṅgīkriyate, evamatrāpi | yaduktaṃ śrītriṃśikāśāstre -

	yathā nyagrodhabījasthaḥ śaktirūpo mahādrumaḥ |
	tathā hṛdayabījasthaṃ jagadetaccarācaram || iti |

kalalatā nāmāvasthāyāḥ sarvākāramavibhaktaṃ rūpam | yadi ca tad 
viśvavaicitryaṃ nimeṣadaśāyāṃ kalalāvasthāyāmapi na syāt, 
tarhyunmeṣadaśaiva na syāt, prāk sata evotpattyaucityāt | tathā hi 
viśvonmeṣāvasthāyāmātmarūpasya kevalaṃ tirodhānamātram, na 
punaratyantopaplavaḥ | evamanyatrāpīti | etadarthaparāmarśaparihāṇyā 
pāśavavedāntasya paryudāsaḥ | tatra 
hyadvaitamāgraheṇopapādyamānamapi dvaitakakṣyāmevādhirohati | 
yadatra satyāsatyavyavasthayā heyopādeyakalpanāyāṃ tenaivākāreṇa 
dvaitamaryādāparyavasāyitvamanivāryam | taduktaṃ mayaiva saṃvidullāse 
-

	dvaitādanyadasatyakalpamaparairadvaitamākhyāyate
	tad dvaite bata paryavasyati kṛtaṃ vācāṭadurvidyayā |
	ete te vayamevamabhyudayinoḥ kasyāpi kasyāścida-
	pyālasyojjhitamaikarasyamubhayoradvaitamācakṣmahe || iti |

	advaitagarvo mahataḥ pramātuḥ ko nāma mithyā bhavati prapañce |
	svayaṃvare te paripanthivarge kiṃ tajjigīṣorasahāyaśauryam || iti ca || 
30 ||

	nanūnmeṣa eva nimeṣo nimeṣa evonmeṣo viśvasya viśvottirṇasya 
ceti mahatyeṣā vyāhatirityāśaṅkyāha -

	tripuṭīmayaṃ khalu samastaṃ tatra ca jñeye jñātari ca samam |
	dṛḍhagranthirjñānakalā kalayati trailokyamekalam || 31 ||



	viśvavyavahāre vādināṃ sarvo'pi vacanaparamparāprapañco 
jñātā jñānaṃ jñeyamityevaṃrūpapuṭatrayasamāhārātmanyarthatattve 
paryavasyati | yad gurumatānusāriṇaḥ spaṣṭatotkarṣādanyonyanirviśeṣaṃ 
sākalyena pratyakṣatayācakṣate | puṭatvaṃ 
caiṣāmaśeṣaviśvakroḍīkārasāmarthyāt | tatra ca 
tripuṭyāmantarālavartinī yā jñānakalā cicchaktiḥ, sā khalu -

	vedyametadakhilaṃ sa īśvaro veditā yadanayoḥ svalakṣaṇam |
	tvāmṛte sumukhi ! vittimantarā kā nu tad ghaṭayituṃ pragalbhate ||

iti śrīkomalavallīstavasthityā viṣayatayāvarodhyaṃ vedyavargaṃ 
bhoktṛtayā sphurantaṃ veditāraṃ ca prati samaṃ viśeṣaśaṅkāśūnyaṃ 
yathā bhavati tathā dṛḍhagranthiḥ koṭidvayāvagāhena 
tādātmyenopaśleṣād vajralepavadaprakampyasambandhā bhavantī 
trailokyamekalamekasvabhāvaṃ kalayati | sa ca svabhāvo vedyaṃ 
vittirvediteti, sthulaṃ sūkṣmaṃ paramiti jāgrat svapnaḥ suṣuptamityādinā 
traividhyenāpyupapadyate | dṛḍhagranthirityuktatvāt | sambandhaṃ 
pratyadārḍhyaśaṅkāyāṃ hi ekamanyasmād bhidyeta | tacca na 
saṃbhavatīti | trailokyapadena -

	devānāṃ tritayaṃ trayī hutabhujāṃ śaktitrayaṃ trisvarā-
		strailokyaṃ tripadī tripuṣkaramatha tribrahma varṇāstrayaḥ |
	yat kiñcijjagati tridhā niyamitaṃ vastu trivargātmakaṃ 
		tat sarvaṃ tripureti nāma bhagavatyanveti te tattvataḥ ||

iti śrīlaghubhaṭṭārakasthityā trivargabhūtamakhilamapi kroḍīkriyate | 
evañca veditaiva vedyaṃ vedyameva veditā vittireva 
vedyamityādyaśeṣasvabhāvasāmarasyābhyupagamasya paryantataḥ 
siddhāntarahasyatvādunmeṣa eva nimeṣa 
ityādivyāhatidoṣodbhāvanamasmatsañcārasaraṇīsīmasu na ka?ñcidapi 
kaṇṭakāṅkuramunmīlayitumudyuṅkta iti | taduktaṃ śrīspandasandohe - 
evamiyamekaivāvibhāgarūpā parāmarśabhūmirunmeṣanimeṣamayī 
unmeṣanimeṣaśabdābhyāmabhidhīyate iti | etena vimarśaḥ sarvaṃsaha 
ityuktaṃ bhavati | yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāvimarśinyām - vimarśo 



hi sarvaṃsaha ātmānamapi parīkaroti, paramapyātmīkaroti, 
dvayamapyekīkurute, ubhayamapi nyagbhāvayati iti | śrīparāmate ca -

	parīkartuṃ nijaṃ tattvaṃ svātmītkartuṃ tathobhayam |
	ekīkartuṃ na kiṃ kartuṃ vimarśo jagati kṣamaḥ || iti || 31 ||

	nanu sarvaikarasavādasvīkāre ko'yaṃ satyāsatyayorbheda 
ityāśaṅkya na kaścidityudāharaṇadvāropadarśayati -

	kaḥ sadbhāvaviśeṣaḥ kusumād bhavati gaganakusumasya |
	yat sphuraṇānuprāṇo lokaḥ sphuraṇaṃ ca sarvasāmānyam || 32 ||

	prasiddhakusumād gaganakusumasya sadbhāve ko viśeṣaḥ | na 
kaściditi vākyopasaṃhāraḥ | yaditi hetau loko hi 
viśvavyavahārasvabhāvaḥ | saṃvideva bhagavatī viṣayasattvopagame 
śaraṇamiti gurumatamaryādayā sphuraṇaṃ 
prakāśamānatvamevānuprāṇanatayā jīvabhūtatayā parigṛhya vartata 
ityasakṛdavocāma | tacca sphuraṇaṃ vipratipanne khapuṣpe sampratipanne 
campakādau ca sāmānyamapakṣapātena prasarati | yadi ca tasya tatra 
pakṣapātena pravṛttiḥ, tat khapuṣpamityapi na sphuret | ata 
evoktamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ sā sphurattā mahāsattetyatra 
mahāsattetyetatpadavyākhyānāvasare - sā hi khapuṣpādikamapi vyāpnoti 
iti | yathā śrīśivadṛṣṭau -

	śaśaśṛṅgādikenāpi syād vibhaktyā samanvayaḥ iti |

padasaṅgatau ca - tasmāt tatrāpi vibhaktiyogena kārakatve sati sattaiva 
śivatākhyā iti | etena stambhakumbhādīnāṃ bhāvānāṃ prakāśa eva 
sāmānyātmānuprāṇanaḥ pravartate, na punaḥ sāmānyaṃ nāma 
padārthāntaramityuktaṃ bhavati | tatra ca vikalparūpā vyaktayaḥ | 
avikalpātmā mahāprakāśaḥ sāmānyam | tataścānuvṛttipratyayanimittaṃ 
sāmānyamityācakṣāṇā vaiśeṣikādayaḥ pratyākhyātāḥ, 
prakāśasyāhetukatvāt | pratyuta viśvavilāsaṃ pratyasyaiva hetutvāt 
sarvasāmānyamiti |  



etādṛśasya sāmānyasya hi samavāyaviśeṣādyaviśeṣeṇa viśvameva 
viṣayo na punardravyādiravacchinnaḥ padārtha ityarthaḥ | atha ca 
praṣṭavyo'yamanyapuṣpāt khapuṣpasya viśeṣaḥ | tucchatvamiti cet | kiṃ tat 
tucchatvaṃ nāma | na tāvacchūnyatvam | gaganasya puṣpasya ca pṛthak 
pṛthak sadbhāvāt | anayoranyonyaṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvasaṃbandhā-
bhāvāt taddvārā viśiṣṭasyāpyasattvamiti cet ? na | anyonyatvaṃ 
nāmānayorekaikaparyālocanāyāṃ na saṃbhavati | saṃbhave coktasyaiva 
doṣasyānuṣaṅgaḥ | yadi ca tanmelanāṃśa ityucyate, 
tarhyasaṃbandhādityasya vyāghātaḥ syāt, melanasyābhyupagatatvāt | 
melakaścāsau pratīto vā syānna vā? nāntyaḥ, vyavahārānupapatteḥ | 
tataśca pratītipakṣa evāvaśiṣyate | sā ca 
pratītiranayormelanamupasthāpayantī tucchatvameva tat 
tucchīkartumudyacchatīti campakapāṭalādiprasūnaprakriyayā 
vyomakusumasyāpi vyavahārakakṣyāvaiṣamyābhāvād yadasat tatra 
pratibhāprarohāsaṃbhavād mūkībhāva eva śaraṇaṃ prativādino janasya 
| ata eva hi yoginaḥ kecidākāśāderapi prasūnānyānayanto dṛśyante | na 
ca tatra svopādānadravyotpannaṃ tad yogibhirabhivyajyata iti vaktuṃ 
śakyam, tasya tādṛksaṅkalpavyatirekeṇopādānāntaraśunyatvāt | 
yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	cidātmaiva hi devo'ntaḥsthitamicchāvaśād bahiḥ |
	yogīva nirupādānamarthajātaṃ prakāśayet || iti |

saujanyataḥ kathāyāṃ tu saṃvitsvabhāvāyattāyāṃ sattāyāṃ na kaścit 
puṣpāntarāt khapuṣpasya bhedaḥ | māyīyasattānurodhe tu 
vidhiniṣedhobhayavyavahāraucityamanyapuṣpāṇām | antarikṣaprasūnasya 
tu niṣedhaviṣayamātrābhilāpayogyatvamityalamitarajanasulabhapada-
kapallavāvakhaṇḍana(?)pāṇḍityapradarśanena | 32 ||

	nanvāsatāmete vigītā vyomakusumādayaḥ, yatra mūkībhāvo 
dūṣaṇaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ veti nātyanta niścitam | yau punarbhāvābhāvau 
lokavyavahārasiddhau, tatra kathamidamabhedavādavaidagdhyamityā-
śaṅkyāha -



	māṇikyamaratakayoriva bhāvābhāvayorbhedapratibhāsam |
	ekaraso'nyonyaṃ dvayorapyunmārṣṭi sphuraṇasaṃbhedaḥ || 33 ||

	viśvavyavahāro'pi hi stambhakumbhāderbhāvasya tadabhāvasya ca 
dvayoranyoyaṃ pratiyogyanuyogibhāvāghrāto bhedapratibhāsaḥ 
parasparavailakṣaṇyenāvadhāraṇaṃ tam, tayoḥ sphuraṇasambheda 
evonmārṣṭi yuktyā tiraskaroti | prakāśamānatā hi sphuraṇam | saiva 
saṃbhedaḥ sambandhaḥ | sa caikarasaḥ padārthadvayaṃ pratyekasvabhāvaḥ, 
tayordvayorapi prakāśata iti pratīti prati vaiṣamyābhāvāt | tataśca 
cidagnisātkṛtayoranayoḥ ko nāma vailakṣaṇyāvabhāsa ityarthaḥ | yathā 
māṇikyamaratakayoraruṇaśyāmalayoranyonyaviruddhayorapi 
kiñcitsannikarṣeṇāvasthāpitayoḥ 
parasparaprabhāpaṭalakarburībhāvādekaraso bhavan 
prakāśamānatāsambhedo bhedapratibhāsaṃ pratimārṣṭi, ubhayorapi 
svasvarūpaparityāgapūrvamekarūpatānupraveśāt | yato 
māṇikyamapyaruṇaśyāmalaṃ maratakamapi śyāmalāruṇamiti 
vaicitryaśālinī pratītiratrotpadyate | darśanāntaramaryādayā'pyabhāvo 
nāma stambhakumbhādikaivalyaśālī bhūtalādiḥ kaścid bhāvaviśeṣa 
evetyūrīkṛtam | yaduktam -

	bhāvāntaramabhāvo'nyo na kaścidanirūpaṇāt iti |

stambhāderbhāvasya ca kumbhādyanyonyābhāvadvārāvatīrṇama-
bhāvatvaṃ cāparihāryamiti bhāvābhāvayoryuktiparyālocanāyām-
aikarūpyameveti || 33 ||

	nanu paunaḥpunyena prasādhitamapyetadarthatattvaṃ taḍidvilasitameva 
kevalaṃ parisphurati | na punarhṛdayaṅgamo bhavati | hṛdayaṅgamībhāve ca 
kaścidupāyo vaktavya ityākāṅkṣāṃ śithilayituṃ pārameśvaryā 
saparyayā bhāvyam, sā ca lokataḥ pṛthagbhūtetyudbhāvayannādau 
pīṭhaṃ devatāṃ ca paryālocayati -



	aṇḍamaye nijapiṇḍe pīṭhe sphuranti karaṇadevyaḥ |
	prasphurati ca paramaśivo jñānanidhistāsāṃ madhye || 34 ||

	svaśarīramayo hi piṇḍaḥ pṛthivyādibhūtapañcakārabdhatvāda-
śeṣaviśvavaicitryalakṣaṇamaṇḍamityadhyavasīyate | 
aṇḍapiṇḍayoraikarūpyamāmnāyeṣu prasiddham | yathā triśirobhairave -

	sarvatatvamayaḥ kāyastaccedānīṃ śṛṇu priye |
	pṛthivī kaṭhinatvena dravatve'mbhaḥ prakīrtitam || ityādi |

yathā ca paramārthasārasaṃgrahe -

	ṣaṭtriṃśattattvamṛtaṃ vicitraracanāgavākṣaparipūrṇam |
	nijamanyadatha śarīraṃ ghaṭādi vā tasya devagṛham || iti |

tatra ca pīṭhībhūte karaṇadevya indriyalakṣaṇāḥ śaktayaḥ sphuranti | 
āvāhanasannidhāpanatirodhānādivyatireke'pi svayameva sphuṭaṃ 
prakāśante | tāsāṃ madhye ca paramaśivo mahāpramātṛsvabhāvaḥ 
prasphurati tāḥ śaktīḥ prati svasya śaktimattā yathā prakaṭībhavati tathā 
prakāśate | yato'yaṃ jñānanidhiḥ, tattadindriyaśaktyudayonmīlitānāṃ 
saṃvidvikalpānāṃ samudra iva saritpravāhāṇāṃ paryantataḥ 
pratiṣṭhābhūmirityāmnāyate | indriyaśaktīnāṃ ca tāsāṃ na 
kadācicchabdasparśādyanuvedhaṃ vinā'vasthānam | śabdasparśādaya 
eva ca pañca mahānti bhūtānīti viśvamayyastā ityuktaṃ bhavati | 
yaduktam -

	śaktiśca śaktimāṃśceti padārthadvayamucyate |
	śaktayo'sya jagat kṛtsnaṃ śaktimāṃśca maheśvaraḥ || iti |

	sarvāḥ śaktīścetasā darśanādyāḥ sve sve vedye yaugapadyena 
viṣvak |
	kṣiptvā madhye hāṭakastambhabhūtastiṣṭhan viśvākāra eko 
vibhāti ||

iti śrīkakṣyāstotrasthityā svātmarūpaḥ parameśvara evāntaścakradevatā 
bāhyāntarabhinnāḥ | karaṇaśaktayaśca tadāvaraṇadevatāsthānīyāḥ | 
tadarcanaṃ ca svaśarīrātmani mahāpīṭha evopapadyate | tatra ca yathā 
pratimāpustakādayo 



bhāvāḥ svātmabhūtāṃ mukhyāṃ devatāṃ prati 
pratinidhibhāvenādhyavasīyante, tadvat svadehasyaiva mukhyatayā 
pīṭhatvam | anukalpopakalpatayā tu sthaṇḍilamaṇḍalādīnāmaṅgīkāra iti 
tātparyārthaḥ || 34 ||

	nanu so'yamalaukikaḥ parameśvaraḥ kīdṛśyā prakriyayopāsya 
ityākāṅkṣāyāṃ sānugrahamāha -

	sa tatrārcanīyo vimarśapuṣpādhivāsasurabhibhiḥ |
	cittacaṣakārpitairvedyasudhāvīrapāṇavastubhīḥ || 35 ||

	saḥ vedāgamādiprasiddhasvabhāvaḥ parameśvaraḥ, tatra 
piṇḍātmani pīṭhe pūjanīyaḥ | pūjāyāṃ ca vedyamayī yā sudhā 
sarvendriyāhlādakatvādatyantasvīkāryā, sā svabhāvata ekāpi satī 
bhayaśokaharṣādyavasthāvaicitryādamlatiktamadhurādiprāyānekarasa-
viśeṣopaśleṣiṇī vīrasya parabhairavasya vīrāyāśca 
svātantryamayyāstatpradhānaśakteḥ, vīrāṇāṃ 
yonitatsiddhādirūpāṇāṃ bhairavamithunānāṃ ca pīyate 
svātmasātkriyata iti vyutpannānāṃ śrīmaccatussrotogopyamānānāṃ 
vastūnāṃ viśvavilāsāsavopalakṣitānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ prakṛṣṭatayā 
pūjāṅgatvenopayujyate | tataśca yāni tāni vedyāmṛtamayāni 
kuladravyāṇi tānyeva pūjāsādhanānītyarthaḥ | tāni ca svacittātmani 
caṣake grahaṇayuktyā kayācidarpaṇīyāni | cittasya caṣakatvaṃ ca tatra tatra 
bahupravāhaṃ prasarpatāṃ vedyavastūnāṃ svātmabhairavasyaikayaiva 
helayā tadgrahaṇasaukaryopāyatvāt | viśvavartino hi bhāvā 
bahirbhinnaprakāraṃ sphurantastattadindriyaparispandopagṛhyamāṇa-
svabhāvāḥ kṣaṇaṃ citte viśramya paścāt tatpraṇāḍikayā 
bhairavātmanyanupraviśantītyatisubhageyaṃ saraṇiḥ | yaduktaṃ 
śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	bhāvā vṛttiṣu tāścitte cittaṃ saṃvidi sā pare |
	vyomnyastaṃgamito yatra krama ullaṅghanātmakaḥ || iti |



	atha ca tāni vastūni parameśvaraṃ prati svātmatayā yo vimarśaḥ 
pratyabhijñānātmā parāmarśaḥ, sa eva puṣpam, svabhāvapoṣakatvāt 
śrīgururūpatvācca | tena mantraśaktyātmanā surabhīkartavyāni, anyathā 
paryuṣitādāviva tatra śāstrārthabhaṅgaprasaṅgāt | etaduktaṃ bhavati - 
pūrvaṃ tāvat pīṭhatayā dehamabhyarcya tanmadhye hṛdayavyomarūpe 
svātmarūpamahāprakāśalakṣaṇaṃ parameśvaramanusandhāya, 
tamabhitaḥ prasarantīrindriyaśaktīśca vicintya, tadanu 
sarvāntaḥkaraṇasamaṣṭilakṣaṇe svacitte 
viśvavedyavilāsalakṣaṇamarghyadravyamāpūrya, tacca 
pārameśvaraparāmarśamayyā mantraśaktyā saṃskṛtya, tenaiva 
kulāmṛtena nijāvaraṇadevatāparimaṇḍalito'yaṃ parameśvaraḥ pūjanīya 
iti | ayaṃ ca saṃkocavikāsayogādekāramārabhyā'saṅkhyāraparyanta-
māgameṣvarcanīyatayā āmnāyate | yaduktaṃ śrītantrāloke -

	ekavīro yāmalotthastriśaktiścaturātmakaḥ |
	pañcamūrtiḥ ṣaḍātmā'yaṃ saptāṣṭakavibhūṣitaḥ ||
	navātmā daśadikśaktirekādaśanijātmakaḥ |
	dvādaśāramahācakranāyako bhairavaḥ sthitaḥ ||
	evaṃ yāvat sahasrāre nissaṃkhyāre'pi vā vibhuḥ |
	viśvacakre maheśāno viśvaśaktirvijṛmbhate || iti |

etattātparyeṇaiva srotaścatuṣṭayopapāditānāṃ paddhatīnāṃ 
pravṛttirityetāvanmātraparyavasāyi pūjārahasyam | bāhyastu 
prasūnākṣatāsavadhūpadīpaghaṇṭādisvabhāvaḥ prapañco'smābhiḥ 
adhikaṃ naiva duṣyati iti nyāyādādriyata iti | etaduparyapyālocayiṣyate || 35 
||

	athārcanārahasyasyonmīlitatvāt krameṇārcyadevatācakrarahasya-
mapyudbhāvayitumādāvuddeśamupanyasyati -



	śrīpīṭhapañcavāhanetratrayavṛndacakrāṇi smarata |
	smarata ca gurūṇāṃ paṅkti pañca ca śaktīḥ sṛṣṭipramukhāḥ || 36 
||

	śrīpīṭhaṃ pañcavāho netratrayaṃ vṛndacakramiti yāni cakrāṇi 
tāni smarata parāmṛśata | yatprādhānyenaiṣāmāvarjanīyatayā 
parāmarśaḥ, tāṃ śrīgurunāthānāṃ paṅkti ca smarata | 
gurumaṇḍalaprādhānyadyotanāya punarapi smaratetyuktam | 
sṛṣṭipramukhāḥ pañca śaktīśca smarata, yā sṛṣṭiḥ sthitiḥ 
saṃhāro'nākhyā bhāseti bhidyante | śrīpīṭheti tasya pūjyatotkarṣeṇa 
mahattvaṃ dyotyate | mahattvaṃ ca tadoḍyāṇādīnāmavāntarapīṭhatve'pi 
sarvapratiṣṭhābhūmitayā pradhānapīṭhatvāt | iyaṃ 
cālpairevākṣariarmantrabījavadanekārthopapādanasamarthā 
tattaccakrakramānukramaṇikārahasyopadarśanī 
vakṣyamāṇasarvadevatāvikalpasaṃgrahaprādhānyāduddeśalakṣaṇā 
gāthā | yatheyamakṛtrimā mahāmnāyoktiḥ -

	yatpīṭhacakrārcitapañcavāhaprakāśamānandakhamūrticakram |
	aṣṭāṣṭacakraṃ pravirājate tad gurukramaughaṃ sacatuṣṭayārtham ||

iti || 36 ||

	atha śrīcakraṃ krameṇonmudrayiṣyannādau piṭhaniketanādi 
netratritayāntaṃ vibhāgadvārā paryālocayati -

	pīṭhe kalā nava pañcaiva pañcavāhapadavyām |
	saptadaśa phālanetre dvādaśa ṣoḍaśa cānyanetrayoḥ || 37 ||

	pīṭhaṃ hi nāma svaśarīrabhaṭṭārakātmakamityuktam, tatraiva 
parameśvarasya pañcadhā vahanāt | yadetatparyālocanāyāmanyonyā-
nandasaṃghaṭṭanonmukhamātāpitṛsvabhāvābhinnaśaktiśivadvitaya-
sāmarasyonmiṣatstrīpuṃsavīryasphurattārabdhatvanaiyatyāduparyud-
bhaviṣyadanekadevatācakrormiparamparāsphāraucityamatraivetyadhya-
vasīyate | taduktaṃ śrīmahānayaprakāśe -



	śivaśaktyubhayonmeṣasāmarasyodbhavaṃ mahat |
	vīryaṃ tasmād deha eva mahāpīṭhaḥ samudgataḥ || iti |

evaṃ pīṭhaparāmarśasya prādhānyaṃ pratipādayituṃ hyādau 
dūtīyajanamārambhaṇīyatayodbhāvyate | yathā śrīkramodaye -

	striyaḥ sarveṣu varṇeṣu yoginyaḥ syurna saṃśayaḥ |
	dehavad yoniśuddhistu ātmavalliṅgaśodhanam ||
	yonau navākṣarīṃ nyasya liṅge saptadaśākṣarīm |
	gurucakrasya pūjārthaṃ kuryād yoginimelanam ||
	navākṣaryā tu mantreṇa sthāpayelliṅgapīṭhavat |
	śivaśaktyātmabhāvena puruṣo manthakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
	manthayedātmanaḥ śakti madhuvacchulkaśoṇitam | iti |

yathā copaniṣadi - yathaivaṃ vidvān mithunamupaityagnihotrameva tasya 
hutaṃ bhavati iti | tatra ca kalāḥ śaktayo nava | parameśvarasya 
strīpuṃsādibhedavyudāsena sarvasyāpi śaktimayatvāt kalā ityuktam | 
tāśca paryālocyamānāḥ pramātraṃśamayaḥ kaścidādyaḥ spandaḥ | 
tadanu tasyaivopariprasaraṇaunmukhyarūpā śaktiḥ kācit | atha tasya 
pramāṇasphuraṇarūpaḥ kaścidindriyamayaḥ parispandaḥ | tataśca 
vastuvyavasthāpanātmikā tatsphurattā | paścāt prameyollāsaḥ | prameya 
vargaśca sūkṣmekṣikayā parīkṣyamāṇo 
bhūtapañcakāntarbhāvamevānubhavati | bhūtāni cātra - ākāśaḥ, 
pṛthivī, vāyuḥ, tejaḥ, āpa iti kramādupāsyante | tatra ca prārambha eva 
svātmanaścidrūpatāparāmarśaḥ tadanu tasya sthairyotpādanam | 
tadudyogarūpaspandānuvṛttiḥ | tatastasyaivojjvalīkaraṇam | tataśca 
svaviśrāntilakṣaṇamāpyāyitatvaṃ ceti kramavivakṣā | evañca pramātā, 
pramāṇam, pañcavidhaṃ ca prameyaṃ pīṭhaniketanamityartho bhavati | 
anye punaretatpīṭhaśmaśānakṣetreśamelāpayajanabhedāt 
pañcaprakāratayopāsate | navātmakatve'pyasya 
pramātṛpramāṇopagṛhītavyomādipañcakasvabhāvatvāt 
pañcavāhacakratādātmyaṃ na kiñcidapyatikramyate | yadvā pramātā 
kaścit, tacchaktiśca pramārūpā, tadupakaraṇaṃ ca 



pramāṇam, vastuvyavasthāpanātmikā ca tadvijṛmbhā, tatkroḍīkāryaṃ 
pañcavidhaṃ prameyajātaṃ cetyanayāpi bhaṅgyā pañcaprakārataiva 
pratyāyyate | evañca -

	ākaṭyorākandharamādoṣṇorā ca nābhisīmāntam |
	ṣaṭkoṇaṃ yaccharīraṃ saṃvitpīṭhaṃ trikoṇasāmyaṃ tat ||

iti sthityā svaśarīrasyaiva pīṭhatayopāsanaṃ prāk kartavyam | tadanu 
vakṣyamāṇānāmupacāraḥ kartavya ityuktaṃ bhavati | atra ca 
gaṇapativaṭukādidevatānāṃ nāmopādānaṃ teṣāṃ ca 
tattadarthānuguṇyena nirvacanaṃ ca kriyamāṇaṃ 
granthagauravamatyantarahasyonmīlanadoṣamapyunmeṣayiṣyatīti 
saṃkṣiptenaiva pathā prasthīyate | yathā śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	dhāmāditritayenaiva vaktrabhaṅgabhayānmanāk |
	prakāśyate mayā samyag vākyairaviṣamāśayaiḥ || iti |

yathā śrikramasadbhāve'pi -

	gopanīyāni nāmāni corebhyo draviṇaṃ yathā |
	gopanāt pālayantyeva mūrtayastu mahārthataḥ || iti |

vivekināṃ tu gaṇyate saṃkhyāyata iti gaṇaḥ ṣaḍadhvā bhogarūpollāsaḥ, 
taṃ pāti rakṣatīti gaṇapatirityādiprakriyayā'tyantasukhasādhyo'yaṃ 
niruktiprakāraḥ | yatasta evāhuḥ - apyakṣaravarṇasāmānyānnirbrūyād, 
na tveva na nirbrūyāt iti | pañcaiva pañcavāhapadavyāmiti | vāhāḥ 
parameśvarasya sphuraṇadhārāḥ | tāśca pañca, 
anyūnānatirekitayopalabhyamānatvāt | tanmayī ca yā padavī 
viśvagamāgamasthānabhūtā pravṛttiḥ | tāḥ pañcaiva kalā ityanvayaḥ | 
evakāreṇa pañcavāhaśabdasyānvarthatocyate | te ca vāhā 
vyomavāmeśvarī, khecarī, dikcarī gocarī, bhūcarīti ca bhavanti | anye 
punarāsāṃ kramaṃ godigbhūrūpaṃ bhūdiggorūpaṃ cācakṣate | tatra 
tatsampradāyānuguṇyāt tanniruktirūhyā | asmatkramastu nirdiṣṭakrama eva 
| yathā śrīkramasiddhau -



	vyomavāmeśvarīsaṃjñā nādabhūmiśca khecarī |
	ānandadikcarīsaṃjñā gocarī mantrabhūmikā ||
	mantrāṇāṃ dravyarūpatvādāvalirbhūcarī tathā | iti |

mayā'pyuktaṃ śrīkomalavallīstave -

	bhūtale kimatha kiṃ gavāṃ kule
		diṅmukheṣu kimatho kimambare |
	kiṃ tato'pyupari sarvataśca kiṃ 
		dṛśyase tadapi kiṃ na mṛṣyase || iti |

tatra 
vyomnāmomātmakapraṇavarūpatāvimarśavaiśiṣṭyānuprāṇanānāṃ 
vakṣyamāṇasarvapañcakātmanāṃ vāmaṃ vamanaṃ pratīśvarī 
sāmarthyaśālinīti vyomavāmeśvarī | sā ca 
parameśvarasyāvikalpabhūmyanupraviṣṭā cicchaktiḥ | khe bodharūpe 
pramātari caraṇāt khecarī | dikṣvantaḥkaraṇeṣu caratīti dikcarī | goṣu 
bahirindriyeṣu sañcaraṇād gocarī | bhuvi viṣayabhūmau caraṇād 
bhūcarīti | pārameśvarī hi saṃvitsvātantryasya śaktiḥ prakṛtyā 
nirvikalpakapadādhirūḍhāpi kvacit parimite pramātari sphurantī tadanu 
tadantaḥkaraṇānupraviṣṭā paścāt tasyaiva bāhyendriyānubandhinī ca 
bhūtvā bahirvedyalakṣaṇaṃ viṣayollāsamakhilamupayuṅkta iti 
sarvapathīno'yaṃ prakāraḥ | etā eva cidānandecchājñānakriyākhyāḥ 
śaktayaḥ parā sūkṣmā paśyantī madhyamā vaikharīti 
vāgrūpāścānubhūyante | yataścicchaktistāvat parameśvarasya 
caitanyalakṣaṇā sarvanirvāhakatvaparyāyaparamasvātantryamayī 
vyomavāmeśvarī | 
ānandaścāvacchinnapramātṛsvātmaviśrāntisvabhāvatvāt khecarī | icchā 
cābhyupagamarūpatvādantaḥkaraṇaprasādhyeti dikcarī | jñānaṃ ca 
bahirmukhaṃ cakṣurādibhirbāhyendriyairuddipyata iti gocarī | kriyāpi 
pārameśvarīṣu śaktiṣvatyantasthaulyād bahirvedyavargavaicitryasya 
evopalabhyata iti bhūcarī | evaṃ parādivākpañcako'pi |

	vimarśo bindunādau ca sphoṭaḥ śabdaśca vākakramaḥ 



iti śrīpādukodayaprakriyayā 
tattadudayānuguṇyāduktaśaktipañcakātmakatvaṃ svayamūhanīyam | 
anyacca sṛṣṭisthitiprabhṛtipañcakībhūtamakhilamatraivāntarbhavati | 
etāśca śrīkṣemarajādibhiḥ pustakeṣu likhitapaṭhitā ityasmābhirapi 
nāmopādānapūrvakaṃ cākityena vyākhyātā iti | athaivaṃ pañcadhā 
vahataḥ parameśvarasya pramātṛpramāṇaprameyatayā 
traividhyenopāstiprakāraṃ sūcayan mūrticakraṃ tāvadāha - 
saptadaśaphālanetra iti | phālo lalāṭam | tadgate netre saptadaśa śaktayaḥ | 
tacca mūrticakramityāmnāyate | mūrchanānmoharūpāt 
samucchrayaṇarūpād vā mūrtitvam | 
taccāntarbahirahantedantādvitayasphuraṇānuguṇyāt tatra yadāhantāyāḥ 
samucchrāyaḥ, tadedantāyā nyagbhāvarūpo moho bhavati | yadā 
punaridantāyāḥ samucchrāyaḥ, tadā'hantāyā uktarūpo moha iti 
dvayamapyekārtham | evañca pramātṛvahnirūpasya 
paramaprakāśarūpasya parabhairavasaṃvitparyantamutkarṣaḥ 
pāṣāṇādijaḍasaṃvitparyantaṃ nyūnībhāvaśceti mahatī 
svātantryaśaktirmūrtiśabdenocyate | tatra ca kalāḥ saptadaśocyante | 
agnerbāhyābhyantaravibhāgena kalādaśakaṃ śikhāsaptakaṃ 
cetyāmnāyeṣu prasiddham | yathāhuḥ -

	dhūmrārcirūṣmā jvalinī jvālinī viṣphuliṅginī |
	suśrīḥ surūpā kapilā havyakavyavahe kalāḥ ||
	hiraṇyā kanakā raktā kṛṣṇā caiva tu suprabhā |
	bahurūpā'tiraktā ca sapta jihvā havirbhujaḥ || iti |

tatsaṃkhyayā mūrticakraśaktayastāvatya iti | tāśca 
paryālocyamānāścaitanyaṃ prādhānyamabhimānaḥ 
kartṛtvamadhyavasāyo vacanamādānaṃ gamanamutsarjanamānanditṛtvaṃ 
jñānaṃ saṅkalpanaṃ śravaṇaṃ sparśanaṃ 
darśanamāsvādanamāghrāṇanaṃ ceti pramātṛsphuraṇaprakārā eva 
bhavanti, yeṣāmantaḥkaraṇabahiṣkaraṇādīnyupāyatayā'vatiṣṭhante | 
etāśca mūrtiśaktayaḥ saṃkucitasya 
pramātustadviṣayāsvādanabhedopaśleṣāt paśutvamupajanayanti, 
asaṃkucitasyātyantasvātantryaśaktivijṛmbhāsaṃrambhārambhakatayā 
pravartamānāḥ paramaśivībhāvalakṣaṇaṃ maṅgalyamunmīlayantīti | 
atha ca prakāśānandacakradvitayamāha - dvādaśa ṣoḍaśa 



cānyanetrayoriti | anyayordakṣiṇavāmarūpayornetrayoryathāsaṃkhyaṃ 
dvādaśa ṣoḍaśa ca śaktaya ityarthaḥ | tatra dakṣiṇaṃ netraṃ 
prakāśacakram | prakāśyate'nena prameyajātamiti vyutpattyā prakāśaḥ 
pramāṇamityartho bhavati | tatra ca śaktayo dvādaśa | haṃsamatyādīnāṃ 
gurutayā pramātṛrūpāṇāmapi mahāpramātrapekṣayā 
pramāṇībhāvayuktyā śaktitvameva | tāśca tattvadṛṣṭyā yathākramaṃ 
buddhipradhānāni vākpāṇipādapāyūpastharūpāṇi karmendriyāṇi, 
manaḥśreṣṭhāni śrotratvakcakṣūrasanāghrāṇātmakāni 
jñānendriyāṇi ca bhavanti | ahaṅkārasya sarvatrānusyūtatvānna pṛthag 
gaṇanam | evañca pramāṇarūpo bhagavān mārtāṇḍo dvādaśadhā 
prakāśate ityupaniṣat | vāmaṃ ca netramānandacakram | ānando nāma 
svātmaparameśvarasyedantayā sphurata ā samantāt samṛddhisvabhāvaḥ 
prameyavargollāsaḥ | sa ca saumyoṃ'śaḥ | tatra kalāḥ ṣoḍaśa | 
vastuvṛttyā tu tāḥ ṣoḍaśavikārasvabhāvāḥ | vikāraśca manaḥ 
karmendriyapañcakaṃ jñānendriyapañcakamākāśādibhūtapañcakaṃ 
ca | eteṣu śabdasparśādiḥ sarvo'pi vedyavilāso'ntarbhavati | 
śabdādīnāṃ sthaulyāvasthaiva mahābhūtāni | manasā ca 
buddhyahaṅkāradvayakroḍīkāraḥ | yadāhuḥ paurāṇikāḥ -

	mana eva manuṣyāṇāṃ kāraṇaṃ bandhamokṣayoḥ |
	bandhāya viṣayāsaṅgi muktyai nirviṣayaṃ manaḥ || iti |

evamanyadapyūhanīyam | etāni ca mūrtiprakāśānandacakrāṇi 
vāmeśvaryādiśaktipañcakātmakānyeva | tatra 
pramātṛvahnirūpamūrticakraṃ sarvordhvavartinīṃ 
sarvāvibhāgasvabhāvatvāt sarvasādhāraṇyena sphūrantīṃ 
vyomavāmeśvarīṃ prādhānyato nimittīkṛtya khecarīśaktyā pravartate | 
khecarī ca pramātṛsphuraṇatanmayīti prāgapyuktam | pramāṇārkarūpaṃ 
tu prakāśacakraṃ dikcarīgocaryubhayamelanārabdham, 
tayordvayorapyantarbahirindriyollāsarūpatvāt | 
prameyasomamayamānandacakraṃ tu bhūcaryanuprāṇanamiti vivekaḥ | 
anenaiva cakratrayeṇa icchā jñānaṃ kriyeti śaktayaḥ, jvalanastapanaḥ 
śaśīti bindavaḥ, bhūrbhuvaḥ suvariti vyāhṛtayaḥ, prāṇo'pāna udāna iti 
vāyavaḥ, pṛthivyantarikṣaṃ svarga iti sthānāni, sattvaṃ 



rajastama iti guṇāḥ, iḍā piṅgalā suṣumneti nāḍyaḥ, devayānaṃ 
pitṛyānaṃ mahāyānamiti yānāni, sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ 
paramityavasthāḥ, kulaṃ kaulamakulamiti vibhāgāḥ, mantro mudrā nirīhā 
iti rahasyānītyādyakhilamapi tritvaviśiṣṭaṃ kroḍīkriyate | yacchrutiḥ - 
tamekanemiṃ trivṛtaṃ ṣoḍaśāram ityādi || 37 ||

	evaṃ sthūlaṃ dehaṃ pīṭhatayopāsyamupapādya 
sūkṣmamapyevaṃ vimraṣṭavyamityunmīlayituṃ vṛndacakraṃ 
paryālocayati -

	prakaṭitapañcaskandhe catuṣṣaṣṭirbhavanti vṛndacakre |

	vṛndānāṃ cakraṃ vṛndacakram | vṛndaśca sandohātmā | 
tādṛktvaṃ ca tasya svataḥ puryaṣṭakamayatayā 
jñānasiddhādipañcakasamaṣṭisvabhāvatvāt | tacca 
prakaṭitapañcaskandham | jñānasiddhāḥ ṣoḍaśa, 
mantrasiddhāścaturviṃśatiḥ, melāpasiddhā dvādaśa, śāktasiddhā 
aṣṭau, śāmbhavasiddhāścatasra iti bhaṅgyā 
pañcavāhakramānuguṇyādudbhāvitapañcaprakāram | āhatya 
catuṣṣaṣ.ṭirbṛndacakram | tatra śrīkālasaṅkarṣaṇyaparaparyāyā 
rudraraudreśvarī pañcaṣaṣṭitamī sarvānuvṛtteti na pṛthag gaṇyate | tacca -

	dhāmamudrāvarṇakalāsaṃvidbhāvasvabhāvataḥ |
	pātā'niketadṛṣṭyā ca vṛndacakraṃ prakāśitam ||

iti nyāyādaṣṭadhā bhavati | tatra dhāmāni sthānāni 
jñānasiddhādīnāṃ kramāt kandanābhihṛtkaṇṭhabhrūmadhyarūpāṇi | 
mudrāḥ karaṅkiṇyādayaḥ | yaduktam -

	karaṅkiṇī krodhanī ca bhairavī lelihānikā |
	khecarī ceti mudrāyāḥ pañcātmakatayā sthitiḥ || iti |

tathā śrīvijñānabhaṭṭārake -

	karaṅkiṇyā krodhanayā bhairavyā lelihānayā |
	khecaryā dṛṣṭikāle ca parā vyāptiḥ prakāśate || iti |



mudrāṇāṃ bandhaprakāraśca tatraivopapāditaḥ | yathā -

	mṛdvāsane sphijaikena hastapādaṃ nirāśrayam |
	vidhāya tatprasaṅgena parā pūrṇā matirbhavet ||
	upaviśyāsane samyag bāhū kṛtvārdhakuñcitau |
	kakṣavyomni manaḥ kurvan śamamāyāti cillayāt ||
	sthūlarūpasya bhāvasya stabdhāṃ dṛṣṭiṃ nipātya ca |
	acireṇa nirādhāraṃ manaḥ kṛtvā śivaṃ vrajet ||
	madhyajihve sphāritāsye madhye nikṣipya cetanām |
	hoccāraṃ manasā kurvan tataḥ śānte pralīyate ||
	āsane śayane sthitvā nirādhāraṃ vicintayet |
	svaṃ dehaṃ manasi kṣīṇe kṣaṇāt kṣīṇāśayo bhavet || iti |

tattvavṛttyā tu karaṅkiṇī nāma svadehendriyākhyabhedavigalanapragalbhā 
mudrā | yathā śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

	antaramba ! bahirapyamī karā ye tavākṣatanavo'ṅka eṣa yaḥ |
	vigraho dvitayamapyataḥ paraṃ cinnabho nayasi naḥ karaṅkiṇī || iti |

pṛthivyādiprakṛtyantaṃ tattvasandohamantaḥsaṃjihīrṣālakṣaṇena 
krodhena svātmarūpatāṃ nayati krodhanī | yathā -

	yat prakṛtyavadhi tattvamaṇḍalaṃ kṣmāmukhaṃ 
parimitagrahāspadam |
	krodhanī tvamasi saṃjihīrṣayā mantramūrtiriha tasya jṛmbhikā || iti |

antarbahirbhāvayaugapadyena pūrṇasaṃvitsvabhāvā bhairavī | yathā -

	granthayo dviṣaḍume ! yayā dhṛtā sā nirāvaraṇacinnabhaḥpadā |
	spandamūrtibhṛtabhedaḍambarā bhairavī tvamasi viśvamelinī || iti |

iyaṃ ca nimīlanonmīlanasamādhidvayasāmarasyaucityādasmābhiratyanta-
mupalālyate | yadāhuḥ -

	antarlakṣo bahirdṛṣṭirnimeṣonmeṣavarjitaḥ |
	iyaṃ sā bhairavī mudrā sarvatantreṣu gopitā || iti |



yathā śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	mahāmelāpadharmiṇyaḥ krauṇḍalyunmeṣavigrahāḥ |
	nirāvaraṇacidvyomadhāmasthā bhānti nityaśaḥ ||
	āpūrya svabalodrekasamutthaṃ bhedaḍambaram |
	yā sthitā pūrṇavibhavā nirāvaraṇavigrahā ||
	bhairavī saiva vikhyātā mudrā sadasadujjhitā |
	nistaraṅgavikāsātmasāmarasyaikapālinī ||
	āsāṃ melāpasiddhānāṃ devīnāṃ mudritā sadā |
	mu(dri?dre)ṇeyaṃ dvidhā bhāsamudreyaṃ bhairavātmikā || iti |

yaccoktamasmadgurubhirmanonuśāsanastotre -

	stabdhadṛṣṭi bahirarthasaṃśrayaṃ nirvikalpamapi yat tavāsanam |
	muktapañjaraśukānukāri tad bhairavaṃ vapurudāramāhara || iti |

mayā'pyuktaṃ śrīparāstotre -

	ānandodayalakṣmaṇorakaluṣairutpakṣmaṇorūṣmalai -
		rakṣṇorunmiṣitaiḥ pratāpya pṛthivīmantaḥsthalaikaspṛśaḥ |
	pūrṇāhamprathanotsavāya sudhiyo yāmūrdhvamadhyāsate 
		sā kācit tvamacintyasiddhiracalā mudrā mahatyucyase || iti |

puryaṣṭakādivāsanāsarvasvagrāsalālasā lelihānā | yathoktam -

	aṣṭapuryuditabījavāsanāsaṃhṛtipravaṇaraśmipu~jayā |
	līḍhamātramukhabhedayā tvayā bhūyate janani ! lelihānayā || iti |

vācyavācakādyaśeṣavikalpavikṣobhavilāpinī 
sauṣumnasaraṇisīmollaṅghinī svātmasaṃvidavibhinnākārā ca khecarī | 
yathā -

	tvaṃ parāprabhṛtivaikharāntimollekhavistaravilāpanonmukhī |
	devyanāvaraṇaśambhusadmagā khecarī bhavasi cidvikāsinī ||
	bahnisūryaśaśidhāmaghasmarī kuṇḍalī tiḍadivotpatantyasau |
	śāmbhavaṃ janani ! bindumadhvanā madhyamena ca matāsi khecarī ||

iti mudrāvibhāgaḥ |



	varṇāḥ śrīmacchabdarāśibhairavātmānaḥ | tatra jñānasiddhāḥ 
ṣoḍaśa svaramayyaḥ | mantrasiddhāḥ kakārādimakārāntā 
varṇātmakāḥ | melāpakasidhāśca ṣaṇḍasvararūpāḥ | 
yakārādihakārāntasvabhāvāḥ śāktasiddhāḥ | akārakalākārāśca 
śāmbhavasiddhā iti | akārakalāśca 
bindvardhacandranirodhanādasvabhāvāḥ | tatra 
binduraśeṣavācyāvibhinnaprakāśātmā candrākṛtiranusvāraḥ | 
kiñcidvācyaprādhānyaśāntāvardhacandraḥ, 
maṇḍalasvabhāvādatyantakauṭilyavyapāyāt | tato'pi 
vedyakauṭilyavyapagamādṛjurekhārūpā nirodhikā | yadanayā 
parimitayogināṃ nādānupraveśaḥ, tadvadaparimitayogināṃ 
bhedadaśāveśaśca nirudhyate | nādaśca svaparāmarśaparamārtha 
ityakārakalārahasyam | śrīpañcapiṇḍanāthasthā akārādayaśca varṇā 
jñānasiddhādīnāṃ kramād bhavanti | kiñca, jñānasiddhādayaḥ 
kramād akārakalāmayyaḥ | śāmbhavyastu tatsamaṣṭirūpāḥ | 
yadasmābhirakārasya pañcapiṇḍasya ca dvayoraikātmyamevāṅgīkriyate | 
yaduktaṃ śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	avarṇapañcapiṇḍārṇarūpayoradvayī gatiḥ |
	yatastasmāducyate'laṃ devīnāṃ tadgataṃ vapuḥ || iti |

anena śrīmahārthatrikadarśanayoranyonyaṃ nātyantaṃ bhedapratheti 
vyākhyātam | evamoṅkāre'pyanusandheyam | 
yadayamoḍyāṇapīṭhasvabhāvo nādātmā paramavyomarūpaḥ 
sṛṣṭyādiprathātrayopasaṃhārapragalbho 
jāgradādyavasthātrayādhiṣṭhātṛbhūtabrahmaviṣṇurudrātmākāro-
kāramakārātmā sarvāvibhāgaprathāmayanādalakṣaṇārdha-
mātrānuprāṇanaḥ pratipāditatadvarṇacatuṣṭayasāmarasyonmīlito 
mahārāvabījaparyāyo bhavati | tathā śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	visargasthitisaṃhāraprathāgrāsaikatatparaḥ |
	nirupākhyamahādīptisamullasanatatparaḥ ||
	yo'dhyuṣṭakalanodrekasvabhāvaḥ praṇavābhidhaḥ |
	pīṭhābhidhaṃ tamevāhaṃ namāmyāgamasiddhaye ||

iti varṇakramaḥ |



	kalā raudryādayaḥ | tatra raudrī vāmā ambikā jyeṣṭheti kramād 
jñānasiddhādibirupaśliṣyante | etatsāmarasyalakṣaṇāśca śāmbhavyaḥ | 
yaduktaṃ śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

	jñānadīdhitiṣu raudryudeti te mantradīdhitiṣu vāmayoditam |
	yogadīdhitiṣu jṛmbhate'mbikā jyeṣṭhayetthamadhiśaktidīdhiti || 

	devyabheditakalācatuṣṭayī śambhudīdhitiṣu dīpyate svaraḥ | iti |

	atha saṃvitsvabhāvaḥ | tatra jñānaṃ nāma sāmānyātmikā 
prāthamikī prathā, yāmālocanetyācakṣate | mantraḥ 
padārthodayasaṃrambhātmā parāmarśaḥ | sa eva svātmaviśrānto 
melāpaḥ | tādṛgrūpasya ca vāsanāśāntirūpaḥ śāktaḥ | 
vilīnasarvavāsanopalepasya cāsya svātmasaṃvinmātratāpāriśeṣyaṃ 
śāmbhava iti |

	atha bhāvasvabhāvaḥ | pārameśvaro hi prakāśaḥ prakṛtyā 
prasannagambhīramanuttaraṅgamamburāśimanuharannavikalpātmaivāntara-
svātantryamayīmānandaśaktimanubhavati | tasya ca 
tādṛksvabhāvānupraviṣṭāḥ śaktayaḥ śāmbhavyaḥ | sa eva yadā kiñcit 
kallolanakriyaunmukhyamavalambate, tadā śāktollāsaḥ | tasyaiva kiñcit 
kallolībhāve melāpaparisphuraṇam | uparyuparyudriktakallolakalpanāyāṃ 
mantronmeṣaḥ | candrodayāderiva kutaścit 
pravṛddhihetorvelollaṅghanādirutkūlaḥ kṣobhakolāhalo jñānasiddhodaya 
iti | tataśca jñānasiddhāḥ prameyāṃśatayā paryālocanīyāḥ | 
mantrasiddhāḥ pramāṇamayatvena | melāpasiddhāḥ pramātrātmakatayā 
śāktasiddhāstadavacchedavyapohena śuddhapramātṛtayā | 
śāmbhavasiddhāśca pūrṇasaṃvitsvātantryamayaparamaśivabhaṭṭāraka-
svarūpatayeti | atha śāmbhavyādīnāṃ vāmeśyāditādātmyadyotakaḥ 
śāmbhavasiddhāsu vāmeśyāḥ pāta ityādikrameṇa 
pañcavāhavṛndacakrayoraikātmyānusandhānaṃ pāta iti pātaprakāraḥ | 
evaṃ sṛṣṭyādikrame'pi pāto'nusandheyaḥ | yathoktaṃ śrīkramasiddhau -



	saṃvitkramamimaṃ deva ! śṛṇu vakṣyāmi sundara |
	sṛṣṭiṃ sthitiṃ ca saṃhārānākhyābhāsāsvarūpakam ||
	jñānaṃ mantraṃ ca melāpaṃ śāktaṃ śāmbhavasaṃyutam | iti |

yathā śrīkramasadbhāve -

	jñānaṃ sṛṣṭiṃ vijānīyāt sthitirmantraḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
	saṃhāraṃ tu mahākālamelāpaṃ paramaṃ viduḥ ||
	anākhyaṃ śaktirūpaṃ tu bhāsākhyaṃ śambhurūpakam |
	pañcaprakārametaddhi vijñeyaṃ tattvadarśibhiḥ || iti |

	aniketo nāma jñānasiddhādīnāṃ bhāvakramaprātilomyāt 
tattatprameyatvādivikalpavikṣobhavyudāsena 
paramapratiṣṭhābhūmiprāptipāriśeṣyaparāmarśa ityaṣṭadhā vibhāgaḥ 
| tatra jñānasiddhānāṃ ṣoḍaśavikāraprathāmayyaḥ | 
mantrasiddhāścāntarbahirindriyaparispandavāsanāsvabhāvāḥ | indriyāṇi 
ca sarvānusyūtamahaṅkāramupādāya dvādaśopalabhyante | teṣāṃ ca 
kadācidātmasvarūpādavaruhya viṣayāvagāhanavaicitryādanyadā 
viṣayebhyaḥ pratyāvṛttya svātmaviśrāntimātravyāpṛtatvācca 
prakāradvayopapattyā dvaividhyamasti | tannibandhanaśca 
tadvāsanārūpāṇāmāsāṃ caturviṃśatitattvollāsaḥ | 
melāpakasiddhāstu pramātṛparamārthā ityuktam | tatra 
pramātṝṇāmayaṃ svabhāvo yajjñānasiddhatmakaṃ prameyajātaṃ 
mantrasiddhātmakapramāṇadvārā svātmani viśramayantīti | tataśca 
pramāṇabhūtānāmindriyāṇāmeva tatra pravṛttyaulbaṇyād 
dvādaśendriyamayaṃ melāpasiddhācakramityartho bhavati | atha 
śāktasiddhāḥ | śaktiśca vāsanāmayī | sā ca 
puryaṣṭakaviṣayatvādaṣṭadhā bhavati | puryaścāṣṭau buddhirahaṅkāro 
manaḥ śabdādipañcakaṃ ceti prasiddhamiti 
tadanuprāṇano'yametaccakraviṣphāraḥ | śāmbhavyaśca nāma 
pralīnāśeṣaviśvavāsanasya bhagavataḥ śambhubhaṭṭārakasya 
tatsvarūpāvibhinnāḥ śaktayaḥ | tāśca 
tattvadṛṣṭāvambājyeṣṭhāvāmāraudrīmayya iti |

	ityenāṃ gurumukhasampradāyalabdhāṃ vācālaḥ śuka iva 
vṛndacakracaryām |
	pañcārthakramapadavīrahasyasaṃvitsarvasvavyatikaragarbhiṇī-
mavocam ||



	itthaṃ svadehameva pīṭhaniketanatayā sthūlaṃ vṛndacakrātmanā 
sūkṣmaṃ pañcavāhasvabhāvatayā paraṃ ca pīṭhatayā paryālocya, tasya 
ca traividhyasya paramārthaṃ pramātṛpramāṇaprameyalakṣaṇairmūrti-
prakāśānandacakraiḥ parāmṛśya tadupari varivasanīyāṃ 
śrīgurunāthaparamparāṃ tadvyatirekadvārā prakāśayati -

	na khalu maṇḍale gurūṇāṃ niyamo niyamātilaṅghināṃ yuktaḥ ||

	ayameva pīṭhasya tatpratiṣṭhāpyāyā devatāyāśca bhedo 
yadanyonyamādhārādheyabhāve'pi prathamasya svataḥ 
sphurattārāhityamanyasyāśca svataḥ sphuratsvabhāvatayā 
nityamaujjvalyotkarṣa iti | tatra pīṭhadevatānāṃ yaḥ sphurattāṃ 
pratyaupādhikatvalakṣaṇo niyamaḥ, sa tu bhagavatīṃ 
śrīmaṅgalādevīmārabhya śrīmaheśvarānandaparyantaṃ pṛthak 
prathamānānāṃ gurunāthānāṃ maṇḍalaṃ paṅktistatra na saṃbhavati, 
yata ete prastutaṃ niyamamatilaṅghya vartante | tataśca 
pūrṇasaṃvitsvabhāvā śrīgurupaṅktiriti tātparyārthaḥ | yata ete 
deśakālādyavacchedarūpaṃ niyamatilaṅghayanti, tata eṣāṃ bahutvam | 
etat tattvavṛttyā na saṅgacchate, kevalaṃ 
tattadupādhyupaśleṣavaśādaupacārikatayaivāṅgīkriyate | yathaikameva 
vastu darpaṇasalilatailādyādhārabhedāt tathā tathā pratibimbati | 
bahutvavaccharīrādyuparāgo'pi na bhedaprathāmupapādayati | yathoktaṃ 
mayaiva śrīpādukodaye -

	tatra yat pūjyamasmākaṃ śaktīnāṃ maṇḍalaṃ mahat |
	svasvabhāvātmake śambhau tat kṛtsnaṃ paryavasyati ||
	so'pi deve gurau svātmanyaikātmyamupagacchati |
	sa ca svabhāvādekaḥ sannaneka iva bhāsate ||
	ārabhya maṅgalādīptiśivānandādikaṃ kramam |
	ambānāthapadādhyāsāt pāramparyeṇa pūjyate ||
	śaktiḥ śivo devapāṇirdakṣiṇāmūrtirityapi |
	anyathā vā samākhyāto loke so'yamalaukikaḥ ||



	tattatkālānuguṇyena devastaistairbubhutsubhiḥ |
	tattatpṛthaktvamāyāti kallolairavi bhānumān ||
	vaicitryaṃ ca śarīrādestasyaivecchāvijṛmbhitam |
	tadvikalpena nānātvaṃ jñeyaṃ tatpādukāsvapi || iti |

ataśca tattvavṛttyā svātmāvibhinnānuttarasvabhāvanādaśaktyātmā 
vimarśa eva śrīgurunātha ityuktaṃ bhavati | yathā śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	vastutastvasvarollāsaprathanocchūnatātmakaḥ |
	yo vibhāti mahānādastamajaṃ deśikaṃ śraye || iti |

śrīcidgaganacandrikāyāṃ ca -

	asvarollasanabījamagrimocchūnatāvapurapākṛtakramaḥ |
	nāda eṣa tava viśvadeśikaḥ ko'pi sa smṛtivimarśa īśvare || iti |

tasya ca tādṛksvarūpaparijñānopāyatayā śrīpādukāyāḥ 
parigrāhyatvam, śaivīmukhamihocyate ityuktatvāt | sā ca 
tādṛśyevāmnāyate | yathopadiṣṭamantarvidbhiḥ - parāparātmanaḥ 
svātmanaḥ parānandamayī svavyatirekakabalanodyuktā parā śaktiḥ 
pāduketi gīyate iti | tasyāśca -

	vāgurāmūlavalaye sūtrādyāḥ kavalīkṛtāḥ |
	tathā kulārṇavajñānaṃ pādukāyāṃ pratiṣṭhitam ||

iti sthityā pratipāditasarvārthakramagarbhatvamavarjanīyam | yathā 
śrīpūrve -

	kramasaṅkocagulikā tadvikāsaikamūlikā |
	tattatsvarūpā vīrādivīrāntā pātu pādukā || iti |

etacca tatpratipādanamātropakṣīṇavyāpāre madīye śrīpādukodaye tantre 
draṣṭavyam || 38 ||

	athaivamupapāditasya svātmarūpaparamaśivabhaṭṭārakā-
vibhinnasya śrīdeśikanāthasya svātantryasaṃvicchaktivijṛmbhaṇa-
svabhāvamasādhāraṇaṃ kṛtyapañcakaṃ prakaṭayituṃ sarvottirṇā 
bhāsāṃ pṛthagvaktavyatayā'vasthāpya prathamaṃ sṛṣṭyādicatuṣṭayaṃ 
spaṣṭayati -



	sṛṣṭau daśa kalāḥ sthitau dvāviṃśatirbhavanti śaktayaḥ |
	ekādaśa saṃhāre trayodaśa tāsturīyaparvaṇi || 39 ||

	sṛṣṭirhi nāmodyogāvabhāsacarvaṇātmavilāpananistaraṅgatva-
lakṣaṇaprathāpañcakasamaṣṭirityāmnāyate | sarveṣāmasmadādīnāmapi 
pramātṛṇāmetatprathāpañcakāvinābhāvaḥ svayamūhanīyaḥ | tathāhi - 
kulālādayaḥ svātmasaṃvidaikātmyāvasthitaṃ kumbhādi bhāvaṃ bahiḥ 
pṛthak kartuṃ prathamamudyuñjate | tameva bahirdaṇḍacakracīvarādinā 
paribarheṇāvabhāsayanti | avabhāsitaṃ 
tamāpākādhiropaṇapākapratiṣṭhāpanādinā paunaḥpunyena 
svātmīyatayā'nubhavanti | tadanu tadarthakriyāmātratātparyāt 
tatraudāsīnyamavalambante | audāsīnyameva ca tasya vilāpanam | 
tadarthakriyāsmṛtipramoṣe ca tatra nistaraṅgāḥ sampadyante | yataḥ 
kumbhādireva teṣāṃ kallolatayotpadyate | villiyate ca parameśvare | punaretat 
prathāpañcakaṃ svātmaparāmarśapāvanībhūtamityetāvāneva bheda iti 
prāgapyavocāma | tatra ca sṛṣṭau kalāḥ prathamapravṛttyaunmukhya-
śālinyaḥ śaktayo daśa | tāśca yonayastatsiddhāśca | teṣāṃ ca 
tattvadṛṣṭau udyogādikalāpañcakātmakatvaṃ tattatkalāvattayā tathā 
tathā kartṛsvarūpatvaṃ ca kramāt paryavasyati | tāśca 
kriyājñānecchodyogapratibhāsvabhāvasṛṣṭisthitisaṃhārānākhyā-
bhāsāsvarūpatayā niṣkṛṣyante | tataśca sṛṣṭyādipañca-
kṛtyāntargatamakhilamapi vaicitryamekasyāṃ sṛṣṭāveva 
parisphuratītyuktaṃ bhavati | yathoktaṃ śrīkramasadbhāve -

	tayā vyāptamidaṃ viśvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram iti |

evamanyatrāpi cakre tadvyatiriktacakrasvabhāvānupraveśasyā-
vaśyambhāvitvaṃ kevalaṃ pradhānena vyapadeśā bhavanti iti nītyā | 
tattadudriktāṃśopasaṃgrahāt sṛṣṭyādīnāṃ pṛthag vyavasthā | yathā 
śrīkramasiddhau -

	dohe vyāptaṃ gavi kṣīraṃ stanābhyāṃ prasṛtaṃ yathā |
	sarvagā vyāpinī sūkṣmā ekasmin prasṛtā śivā || iti | 13



ata eva śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	yeyaṃ tasya niraupamyaśarīrā diptayastu tāḥ |
	niyatagrahasaṃsthānaṃ nidhānāya samutthitāḥ ||
	tā eva gaditāḥ pañca yonayaḥ paradhāmagāḥ |
	niyatagrahasaṃsthānakalpanāparivarjitāḥ ||

ityādau niyatagrahasaṃsthānodyuktatvaṃ tadvivarjitatvaṃ cocyate | 
śrīkramasadbhāve'pi -

	śṛṇu me paramāṃ kālīṃ vidyāṃ sṛṣṭisvabhāvagām |

ityādi | etadupariṣṭādapyāsūtrayiṣyate | itthamekaikakṛtyaviṣaye'pi 
parameśvarasya pañcakṛtyapravṛttyaunmukhyamastītyuktaṃ bhavati | yathā 
śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	itthamekaiva pañcātmarūpeṇa sphurati svataḥ |
	svabhāvaṃ prakaṭīkartuṃ yā devī tāmahaṃ śraye || iti,
	āsāṃ madhyāt tu devīnāṃ yadaikā sphurati svataḥ |
	sarvāstadaiva satataṃ sāmarasyena bhāntyalam || iti ca |

śrīcidgaganacandrikāyāṃ ca -

	pañcasu sphurati devi ! vṛttiṣu tvanmayīṣu yadi kācana svataḥ |
	śaśvadāśu nikhilāstadaiva tāḥ sāmarasyamadhiruhya 
bhāntyamūḥ || iti |

yathā ca śrīśivadṛṣṭau -

	yadekataranirmāṇe kāryaṃ jātu na jāyate |
	tasmāt sarvapadārthānāṃ sāmarasyaṃ vyavasthitam || iti |

	atha sthitikramamāha - sthitau dvāviṃśatirbhavanti śaktaya iti | 
sthitirhi nāma sṛṣṭānāṃ padārthānāṃ 
yāvatsaṃjihīrṣodayamavaiyākulyenāvasthānam | yaduktam -

	sthitirnāma svarūpasya tattadrūpatayā dhṛtiḥ iti |

śaktayaśca tāḥ śiraścakre yuganāthāścatvāraḥ, taddevyaścatasra 
ityaṣṭau | dayaṣaṭkoṇe sādhikāraniradhikāravibhāgena rājaputrāṇāṃ 
dviṣaṭkamhṛ |




tanmadhye kuleśvaraḥ kuleśvarīti dvāviṃśatiḥ | tatra 
pīṭhānyoḍyāṇajālandharapūrṇagirikāmarūparūpāṇi 
catvāryadhiṣṭhāya yugānāṃ kalidvāparatretākṛtātmanāṃ krameṇa 
nāthabhūtā jāgratsvapnasuṣuptituryāvasthākrāntasya viśvasya 
sthāpanārthamakārokāramakāranādātmakapraṇavakalācatuṣkamantropa-
bṛṃhitaiśvaryāḥ, kartā jñātā vyavasitā ce(ti)tā ceti krameṇa 
paramakartṛsphurattānuprāṇanāścatvāraḥ kartṛviśeṣā yuganāthā 
ityucyante | taddevyaśca -

	śṛṇoti śabdamolambe prekṣate puṣkaradvaye |
	karālambe'bhidhatte ca sahye jighrati gandhavat ||
	mātaṅge tu mahāpīṭhe vyāpnotyakhilamīśvaro |

iti nītyā tattatpīṭhāvasthādyaiśvaryarūpāḥ kriyā 
jñaptirvyavasitiścicchaktiśceti tattvavṛttyopāsyante | rājaputrāśca -

	jagato rañjanād rājā sākṣād devo maheśvaraḥ |
	tatputrā rājaputrāḥ syuḥ ......................................... ||

iti śrīpādukodayasthityā tāni tānīndriyāṇyucyante | yathā ca 
śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

	rājanāt prakṛtirañjanācca māṃ rājasaṃjñamanubodhakarmaṇoḥ |
	ṣat tvayā pṛthagamī pravartakāḥ putrabhāvamadhiropitāḥ śive || iti |

tatra sādhikāratvaṃ karmaprādhānyāt, karmaṇyaiśvaryamadhikāraḥ 
ityuktatvāt | niradhikāratvaṃ ca tadviparyayeṇa jñānodrekāt | ataśca 
buddhirvāgādīni karmendriyāṇi ca sādhikārāḥ | manaḥśrotraprabhṛtīni 
jñānendriyāṇi niradhikārā rājaputrāḥ | kuleśvaro'haṅkāraḥ | 
tasyābhimānaśaktiḥ kuleśvarīti tattvārthaparyālocanam | atha 
saṃhāraprakāramāha - ekādaśa saṃhāra iti | saṃhāro nāma 
bahirudrāntānāṃ bhāvānāṃ pārameśvare prakāśe 
punaḥprasūtyaucityena vaṭadhānādinītyā vāsanātmatayā'vasthānam | 
tatra śaktaya ekādaśa | tāśca 
sarvāntaḥkaraṇasamaṣṭibhūtamahaṅkāraṃ bāhyendriyadaśakaṃ ca 
bhakṣayantyaḥ sphurantītyekādaśā'bhūvanniti tattvaniścayaḥ | tatra 
tādṛgahaṅkāra eva pramātā, tadvyatirekeṇa 



parimitasya tasyānupalambhāt | indriyāṇi pramāṇāni | tadgrāhyaṃ ca 
prameyamiti pramātrāditrikamayaṃ prameyamantaścarvayantyaḥ kalāḥ 
saṃhartrya ityuktaṃ bhavati | etacchaktyupaśleṣāvacchedācca 
parameśvarasyaikādaśarudratvaprasiddhiḥ | athā'nākhyākramaṃ 
prakhyāpayati - trayodaśa tāsturīyaparvaṇīti | turyamanākhyācakram | 
tadrūpe parvaṇyavasthāviśeṣe | anākhyamityākhyāśūnyatvamucyate | 
ākhyā ca paśyantyādisthūlavāktritayasvabhāvā | yathoktaṃ 
śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

	nādabindulipivigrahā girastisra ūrdhvagavimarśaśīkarāḥ |
	saṃhṛtisthitivisṛṣṭidhāmasu vyāpṛtāstvadadha īśavallabhe || iti |

tacca tattvadṛṣṭyā saṃhṛtānāṃ pramātrādīnāṃ 
saṃhartṛsvabhāvasaṃvidagnimātrapāriśeṣyasvarūpatayā niścīyate | 
tacca -

	udyogamayamālasyaṃ prakāśaikātmakaṃ tamaḥ |
	aśūnyaṃ śūnyakalpaṃ ca tattvaṃ kimapi śāmbhavam ||

iti saṃvidullāsanyāyādaśūnyamapi śūnyamayī kācit 
kakṣyevāvabhāsate | ata eva hi turīyaparvaṇītyuktam | yata 
etatprakāśe'pyaviklabena yoginā kimapyantarvimraṣṭavyaṃ 
yadalaukikasphurattatmakabhāsānubhavasaukhyasampadvijṛmbhātmakatayā 
paryavasyati | yaduktaṃ śrīspande -

	tadā tasmin mahāvyomni pralīnaśaśibhāskare |
	sauṣuptapadavanmūḍhaḥ prabuddhaḥ syādanāvṛtaḥ || iti |

tatra ca yadyapi mukhyayā vṛttyā śaktikalpanamevametāvattayeti vā 
nopapadyate, tathāpyupacārādāgameṣu tathāmnāyate | yaduktaṃ 
stotrabhaṭṭārake -

	akathyaṃ vā trayātītamupacāreṇa gīyate iti |

tatra ca tāḥ śaktayastrayodaśa | tā iti | yā sraṣṭryaḥ sthāpayitryaḥ 
sahaṃrtryaśca tāstādātvikāvasthāyāṃ 
saṃhartṛmātrapāriśeṣye'pyudbhaviṣyadvedyavaicitryāpekṣayā 
kiñcidantaḥkandavadavatiṣṭhamānā dvādaśendriyatatsamaṣṭyātmanā 
trayodaśa sambhavanti | tāśca ihaikaikatra sṛṣṭyādau cakrarūpatā vidyate 
iti śrīkrama-



kelikḷptyā sṛṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ, sṛṣṭisthitiḥ, sṛṣṭisaṃhāraḥ, sṛṣṭiturīyam; 
sthitisṛṣṭiḥ, sthitisthitiḥ, sthitisaṃhāraḥ, sthititurīyam; saṃhārasṛṣṭiḥ, 
saṃhārasthitiḥ, saṃhārasaṃhāraḥ, saṃhāraturīyam; iti 
dvādaśānāmindriya sphurattānām -

	anākhyābhāsayoratra nopadiṣṭaḥ pṛthaṅmanuḥ 

iti sthityā sarvānusyūtayā turīyasammilitayā bhāsābhaṭṭārikayā 
trayodaśībhūtānāṃ parispandatayā'dhyavasīyante | yathā 
śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	āsāṃ dviṣaṭkadevīnāṃ vamanagrāsatatparām |
	devīṃ trayodaśīṃ vande tādātmyapratipattaye || iti |

etāśca sṛṣṭikālyādivyapadeśena stotrabhaṭṭārakāvudghāṭyanta iti 
trayodaśasaṃvidhānopaniṣat || 39 ||

	atha sarvottirṇāṃ bhāsāmudbhāsayati -

	bhāsāyāṃ na vikalpaḥ sphurati sphuradekaniṣkalaśriyām |
	yadi pratibimbagatyā sphurati paraṃ ṣoḍaśādhikā devī || 40 ||

	bhāsā nāma sṛṣṭyādikṛtyākrāntaviśvavaicitryavyavahāra-
garbhiṇī sarvottirṇā sarvānugrāhiṇī ca pārameśvarī cicchaktiḥ, yā 
tadīyaṃ svātantryaṃ sa evetyadhyavasīyate | yathoktaṃ śrīpādukodaye -

	bhāsā ca nāma pratibhā mahatī sarvagarbhiṇī |
	svasvabhāvaśivaikātmadeśikātmakacinmayī ||
	yasyāṃ hi bhittibhūtāyāṃ mātṛmeyātmakaṃ jagat |
	pratibimbatayā bhāti nagarādiva darpaṇe ||
	svātantryarūpā sā kāciccicchaktiḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ |
	tanmayo bhagavān devo gururgurumayī ca sā || iti |



sā ca sphuradekaniṣkalaśrīḥ sphurantī svānyavibhāgaśūnyamullasantī 
ekā bhedaprathā'tikrāntā svātmatādātmyaśālinī ca bhavantī niṣkalā 
sṛṣṭyādivadvibhāgoddeśādivikalpavikṣobhamasahamānā 
śrīrudyadanurūpaiśvaryalakṣaṇā yasyāmiti kṛtvā | tasyāṃ vikalpa
