evamiyamityādirūpā viruddhā kalpanā na prakāśate | yadi kimapi sphurati, 
tat ṣoḍaśādhikaiva | sā ca pratibimbanītyaivetyakṣarārthaḥ | etaduktaṃ 
bhavati - parameśvarasya paramasaṃvitsvātantryasphārasphurattā-
svarūpāyāmasyāṃ bhāsāyāṃ vaiśvātmyaprathāpāriśeṣyāt svato na 
kācid vikalpodayaśaṅkā sambhavati | api tu, svacchatotkarṣaśālitayā 
prāgupanyastāni sṛṣṭyādīnyeva cakrāṇyasyāṃ pratibimbayuktyā 
parisphurantītyanayā bhaṅgyā tattacchaktīnāṃ vikalpenopāsanamapi 
kiñcit saṃgacchate | yaduktaṃ śrīkramakelau - ata eva ye nirvimarśa 
turyātītamicchanti, te nirupadeśā eva iti | ṣoḍaśādhiketi, saptadaśī kalā | 
tatra ṣoḍaśa śaktayo viśvapratibimbasvabhāvāḥ | antyā tu -

	viśvavaicitryacitrasya samabhittitalopamā |

iti śrīpratyabhijñāprakriyayā tatsamaṣṭyātmakatayā 
tadadhiṣṭhānabhūteti ṣoḍaśādhikayā viśvaṃ taduttirṇaḥ 
parameśvaraśca dvitayamapi saṃgṛhyate | pratibimbaprakriyā ca 
sphaṭikamukurādivyatirekādalaukikī kācidaṅgīkartavyā | yataḥ 
sphaṭikādeḥ pratibimbanaṃ prati bimbāpekṣāvaśyambhāvaḥ | asyāstu 
samastasyāpi prapañcasya pratibimbanaṃ prati 
bhittibhūtatvādetadviparyayaḥ | idaṃ coparyapi bhaviṣyati | 
etaccakrānupraviṣṭā ceyaṃ ṣoḍaśādhikā, 
ṣoḍaśāvikārapratibimbatatsamaṣṭirūpatvāt prathamaṃ 
saptadaśasphuraṇaprakārā bhavantī paścāt 
prakāśavimarśadvayabhedopaśleṣavaśād bhairavabhairavīvibhāgayuktyā 
catustriṃśadaṃśatayā'nubhūyate | sā ca vikāsayuktyā 
pañcāśadvarṇātmakaviśvaprasaraparāmarśaparamārthatayā 
parisphurantī saṃkṣepamudrayā paryālocyamānā, pīṭhaniketanaṃ prati 
pañcadhā vahato gurubhaṭṭārakāvibhinnasya parameśvarasya yāni 
mūrtiprakāśānandavṛndacakrarūpāṇi sṛṣṭyādipañcakātmakāni ca 
nava cakrāṇi, tanmayī śrīnavākṣarī sampadyate |



tato'pi saṃkṣepe sarvasyāpi prapañcasya pañcavāhapāriśeṣyāt 
pañcapiṇḍatvena, tasya ca vāgbhave bīje, tasyāpyanuttarakalāyām, 
amuṣyā api svātmaparāmarśamātre paryavasānamiti | eṣā ca siddhānteṣu 
tattadadhikārādhirohakramatāratamyādanyathā'nyathā vyavahriyate | 
yadāhuḥ -

	yena yena svarūpeṇa bhāvyate tasya tanmayī |
	māheśvarāṇāṃ śaktiḥ sā sāṃkhyānāṃ prakṛtiḥ parā ||
	mahārājñī ca saurāṇāṃ tārā sugatavandinām |
	lokāyatikamukhyānāṃ tadātvā sā prakīrtitā ||
	śāntā pāśupatādīnāmarhatāṃ śrīśca tadvidām |
	śraddhā hairaṇyagarbhāṇāṃ gāyatrī vedavādinām ||
	ajñānatimirāndhānāṃ sarveṣāṃ mohanī smṛtā | iti |

itthamāśayena laghubhaṭṭārakairapyuktam -

	lakṣmīṃ rājakule jayāṃ raṇamukhe kṣemaṅkarīmadhvani 
	kravyādadvipasarpabhāji śabarīṃ kāntāradurge girau |
	bhūtapretapiśācajambhakabhaye smṛtvā mahābhairavīṃ 
		vyāmohe tripurāṃ taranti vipadaṃ tarāṃ ca toyaplave || iti |

evamambāstave'pi -

	tvaṃ candrikā śaśini tigmarucau rucistvaṃ
		tvaṃ cetanāsi puruṣe pavane balaṃ tvam |
	tvaṃ svādutāsi salile śikhini tvamūṣmā 
		nissārameva nikhilaṃ tvadṛte yadi syāt || iti |

itthaṃ madīyaśrīparāstotre'pi -

	dātṝṇāṃ karapallaveṣu kariṇāṃ gaṇḍeṣu pṛthvīruhāṃ
		puṣpeṣu stanamaṇḍaleṣu sudṛśāmaṃseṣu bhūmībhujām |
	kaṇṭhāgreṣu ca gāyatāṃ kavayatāṃ jihvāsu cetasvināṃ
		saṅkalpeṣu ca kalpayanti katiciddhanyāstavaivodyamam || iti |



āgame'pi -

	saiva kālī mahādevī gīyate lokavedayoḥ |
	itihāseṣu tantreṣu siddhānteṣu kuleṣu ca || iti |

tathā ca śrībhagavadgītāsu -

	yadyad vibhūtimat sattvaṃ śrīmadūrjjitameva vā |
	tattadevāvagaccha tvaṃ mama tejoṃ'śasambhavam || iti |

evam -

	janmakāle bhavenmātā pūjākāle ca devatā |
	ratikāle bhaved dūtī mṛtyukāle ca kālikā ||

ityādyūhyam | tatroddiṣṭabhaṅgyā sṛṣṭyādibhāsāntaṃ cakraṃ 
śrīdevapāṇisampradāyānupraviṣṭairasmābhiranusandhīyate, na 
punaretadviparyayeṇa | yathā śrīkramasadbhāve -

	teṣāṃ madhyāt krameṇaiva ādau pūjyastu kaḥ kramaḥ |
	tanme kathaya suśroṇi ! vistareṇa yathāvidhi ||

iti praśnānantaram -

	purā yat kathitaṃ deva ! pañcavāhamahākramam |
	teṣāṃ tu kramarājānāṃ sṛṣṭirūpo'grataḥ sadā ||
	tatastu sthitisaṃhāramanākhyaṃ ca tataḥ param |
	bhāsākhyaṃ ca tataḥ pañcāt pūjayedakramakramam || iti |

etatpañcakapraṇetṛtvameva parameśvarasya 
tatpāramaiśvaryamityasakṛdavocāma | yannaṣṭārthacatuṣkāvabhāsanam, 
līnameyatrayavāsanānuvṛttitvam, meyamānaghasmaravettṛtvam, 
savikalpakameyavimarśaḥ nirvikalpakameyāvabhāsa iti kramādāgameṣu 
saṃkṣepeṇopapādyate | atra ca tattaddevatāmantroddhāro madīye 
mahārthodaye paryālocanīyaḥ || 40 ||

	athetthamupadiṣṭasya sṛṣṭyādipañcakasya pratyekaṃ 
sphuraṇaprakāre kiñcid yaugapadyaṃ vimraṣṭavyamityāha -



	sṛṣṭeḥ pañcamakalā bhāseti jano gaṇayati vyavadhānam |
	sṛṣṭermūlakando bhāsā bhāsāyāḥ pallavaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ || 41 ||

	parameśvaro hyalātacakracchāyayā sṛṣṭyādīni 
pañcakṛtyānyavicchinnamudbhāvayan 
sraṣṭṛtvasthāpayitṛtvādyaśeṣānuvṛttamātmanaḥ 
kartṛtvotkarṣamanubhavannāste, yenāyaṃ vimraṣṭṛtvāparaparyāyeṇa 
jaḍabrahmavādisiddhāntapaṅkapalvalottirṇaḥ 
kaulāgamamahāmṛtārṇavakarṇadhāratayā'vadhāryate | tathā ca sati 
yo'yaṃ jano vastutattvaparijñānābhāvājjananamaraṇādipīḍitaḥ 
pramātṛlokaḥ, sa sṛṣṭimārabhya bhāsāparyantacakrapañcakakrama-
gaṇanayā sṛṣṭeḥ sakāśāt sthityādicakratritayāntaritā pañcamī 
śaktirbhāsetyanayorvicchedamavabudhyate | tattvavṛttyā tu 
sṛṣṭeradhiṣṭhānabhūmirbhāsā | tasyāśca 
prathamaparispandasvabhāvatayā prasaradrūpatayā sṛṣṭiriti vimraṣṭavyam | 
upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat | tena sthitermūlakandaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ, sṛṣṭeḥ pallavaḥ 
sthitirityādikramo'pi svayamūhanīyaḥ | atrāyaṃ bhāvaḥ - sṛṣṭyādiṣu 
caturṣu kṛtyeṣu sṛṣṭisṛṣṭiḥ, sṛṣṭisthitirityādikrameṇa pratyekaṃ 
cāturvidhyam, paryantato bhāsāparyavasāyitvaṃ ca | bhāsā ca 
sarūpaniṣkarṣāvalokane saṃvidaikyaparāmarśacamatkārasārāpi 
viśvapratibimbayuktyanupraviṣṭaprapañcasvabhāvānukāritayā 
pañcakasvarūpaiveti | pañcāpi kṛtyāni pratyekaṃ pañcakātmakatāṃ 
nātikrāmanti | teṣu ca pūrvapūrvapañcakasya pañcamīṃ 
kalāmavalambyottarottarasya pañcakasya prāthamikī parisphurati | 
evamuttarottarapañcakaprathamasphurattānāmadhodhaḥpañcakaparyanta-
śaktiṣu viśrāntyanubhava ityutpaladaladaśaśatavidalanalāghavollāsavat 
kramasadbhāve'pyasaṃlakṣyakramā pārameśvarī 
pañcakṛtyacakranirvyūḍhiratyantagāḍhābhyāsaiḥ prauḍhaiḥ kaiścid 
vimraṣṭavyatayā'vatiṣṭhata iti | itthametatkramaparāmarśa eva 
svātmavimarśarūpo jīvanmokṣaḥ | yata ājñādharatvādayo 
bāhyavibhūtiparispandāḥ svayamavarjanīyatayonmiṣanti | sa ca 
gurukaṭākṣasamparkādṛte na sampadyate | yathā śrīkramasiddhau -



krakāraḥ krodharūpastu makāro maṅgalo bhavet |
krodhe tu maṅgalaṃ kuryāt kramaḥ kālakramo bhavet ||
gurvāyattaṃ kramajñānamājñāsiddhikaraṃ param |
kramajñānānmahādevi ! trailokyaṃ kabalīkṛtam || iti || 41 ||

evamiyatā prapañcena pūjyadevatācakrasvarūpaṃ paramārthataḥ 
parāmṛśyedānīmasyāḥ pūjāyāḥ pūrvaprastutāyā api niṣkṛṣṭaṃ 
vapurupapādayitumāha -

	nijabalanibhālanameva varivasyā sā ca durlabhā loke |
	sulabhāni viśvapaterāsavatāmbūlagandhapuṣpāṇi || 42 ||

pūjā hi cāro rāvaścarurmudreti caturvidhatayāmnāyate | yathā 
śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

cārarāvacarubhirvibheditairmudrayā ca yadupāsanaṃ tava |
tadvaśena bhajate paramparā tāvakakramagatā sphuṭīkṛtam || iti |

tatra cāraḥ samayācāraḥ | rāvo vimarśaḥ | caruḥ 
prathamadvitīyādidravyam | mudrā svātmanaḥ parameśvaratvopapādanāya 
svaśarīraṃ prati kalpyamānaḥ karacaraṇādisanniveśaviśeṣo 
veṣadhāraṇaviśeṣaśca | mahatī tu mudrā paryantato rāva evāntarbhavati | 
tatra caturṣvapi pūjākrameṣu prādhānyena rāva evopayujyate | anyeṣāṃ 
tu paryantatastatprayojakatayā parigrahaṇam | tasmāt svasvarūpaparāmarśa 
eva paramā pūjā | anyattu gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpādi āḍambaramātramiti 
tātparyārthaḥ | akṣarārthastu - svahṛdayasphurattārūpaḥ parameśvara eva 
devatetyuktaṃ vakṣyate ca | tatra yannijaṃ svātmatādātmyānupraviṣṭaṃ 
balaṃ viśvavikṣobhasahiṣṇutvalakṣaṇaṃ vimraṣṭṛtvam, 
tatparyālocanameva varivasyā | tacca balamityeva vyapadiśyate | yaduktaṃ 
śrīspande - api tvātmabalaṃ mantrāḥ iti, tadākramya balaṃ mantrāḥ iti ca 
| śrīpratyabhijñāhṛdaye ca - balalābhe viśvamātmasātkaroti iti | yathā ca 
śrutiḥ - na vā ojīyo rudra ! tvadasti iti | sā ca 



saparyā loke sāmānyena pūjakatayā'vasthite pramātṛvarge 
nirūpyamāṇe svātmadevatayā tena parameśvareṇa duṣprāpā, lokasya 
tathā nibhālakatvābhāvāt | katicana mahāyogina eva hi tathā 
paryālocayituṃ pragalbhanta ityetādṛśyaivārcanayā bhāvyam | anyā 
punarāsavādyarpaṇarūpā -

	dīpārpaṇaṃ davāgneḥ parjanyasya prapājaloddhāraḥ |
	vātyāyāśca paṭāñcalavījanametat tavādya naivedyam ||

ityādinītyā tasyātyantasulabhatayā'dhyavasīyate, yato'yaṃ 
viśvapatirviśvasya pūjādravyāṇāṃ tadanyeṣāṃ ca bhāvānāṃ 
svātmarūpatādātmyopapādakatayā'dhiṣṭhātā bhavati | tat svata 
evāśeṣaviśvavilāsavyāpakasya bhagavataḥ 
kimanyakartṛkāsavatāmbulādyarpaṇaḍambhaviḍambanenetyarthaḥ | 
yaduktaṃ śrīvijñānabhaṭṭārake -

	yaireva pūjyate dravyaistarpyate vā parāvaraḥ |
	yaściakaḥ pūjakaḥ sarvaḥ sa evaikaḥ kva pūjanam || iti |

yathā ca śrīprabhākaule -

	yāvat tat paramaṃ śāntaṃ na vijānanti sundari |
	tāvat pūjājapadhyānahomaliṅgārcanādikam ||
	vidite tu pare tattve sarvākāre nirāmaye |
	kva pūjā kva japo homaḥ kva ca liṅgaparigrahaḥ || iti |

yadi punastānyeva dravyāṇi tādṛgvimarśaśaktyā pavitrīkriyante, tat 
svairamāsatāṃ pūjopakaraṇatvena | yaduktaṃ śrībhagavadgītāsu -

	patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ toyaṃ yo me bhaktyā prayacchati |
	tadahaṃ bhaktyupahṛtamaśnāmi prayatātmanaḥ || iti |

mayāpyuktaṃ saṃvidullāse -

	svavibhavavimarśasurabhīṇyādadate devatāḥ prasūrnāni? |
	nanu kānaneṣu sulabhaṃ yadi tat sāmānyasaurabhāt tṛptiḥ || iti |



tādṛgvimarśābhāve tu -

	muhuḥ karāgreṇa nirudhya nāsāṃ muhuśca 
pārśvasthamavekṣamāṇāḥ |
	devān yajante katicid vayaṃ tu svānandamudraikamahāsaparyāḥ ||

ityādinītyā bāhyāḍambaraḥ kevalaṃ viḍambanāmātraphalakatayā 
paryavasyati | yaduktamarcanātriṃśikāyām -

bālikāracitavastraputrikākrīḍitena sadṛśaṃ tadarcanam |
	yatra śāmyati mano na nirmalasphītacijjaladhimadhyamāśritam || iti |

yathā ca śrīpaścime -

	pūjā homaḥ kramaścaryā vrataṃ śāstraniṣevaṇam |
	tapo dhyānaṃ japaḥ śaucaṃ tattvahīnasya niṣphalam || iti |

yathā ca śrīgītāniṣyande -

	na pādapatanaṃ bhaktirvyāpinaḥ paramātmanaḥ |
	bhaktirbhāvasvabhāvānāṃ tadekībhavabhāvanam || iti |

yathā ca śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	na yogo na tapo nārcā kramaḥ ko'pi praṇīyate |
	amāye śivamārge'smin bhaktirekā praśasyate || iti |

tathā ca śrīmahānayapaddhatau -

	paramanirāvaraṇātmani rūpe yo dṛḍhataraḥ parāmarśaḥ |
	pūjanametaditītthaṃ prabhuṇā niraṇāyi yadyapi prakṛtam | iti |

yathā ca śrīpādukodaye -

	pūjā ca svātmabhāvena deśikendravimarśanam iti |

etena - pūjā viśvasya vedyasya cidbhūmiviśrāntiḥ iti 
śrīmadṛjuvimarśinīsthityā -

	pūjā nāma na puṣpādairyā matiḥ kriyate dṛḍhā |
	nirvikalpe mahāvyomni sā pūjā hyādarāllayaḥ ||



ityādirāmnāyoktirapi vyākhyātā | yataḥ svātmavilayo nāma 
svaviśrāntilakṣaṇe svaparāmarśe paryavasyati | yadāhuḥ -

	yatrendhanaṃ dvaitavanaṃ mṛtyureva mahāpaśuḥ |
	alaukikena yajñena tena nityaṃ yajāmahe || iti |

etadāśayenaiva hi -

	karpūraśakalonmiśratāmbūlāpūritānanām |

ityādinā svātmadevatātṛptiḥ 
pūrvamāgameṣvanuṣṭheyatayā''khyāyate | yathā ca śrīpūrve -

	dravadravyasamāyogād snapanaṃ tasya jāyate |
	gandhapuṣpādigandhasya grahaṇaṃ yajanaṃ smṛtam ||
	ṣaḍrasāsvādanaṃ tasya naivedyāya prajāyate |
	yamevoccārayed varṇa sa japaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || iti |

uktarūpā ceyaṃ saparyā dhanyajanmanaḥ kasyacideva puṃṣaḥ 
pratītipathamavatarati | yaduktaṃ śrītantrāloke -

	eṣa yāgavidhiḥ ko'pi kasyāpi hṛdi vartate |
	yasya prasīdecciccakraṃ drāgapaścimajanmanaḥ || iti |

tatrāsavasyāntaḥkaraṇaprasādhakatvam | tāmbūlasya śarīraṃ prati 
bāhyāntarobhayaśodhakatvam | gandhasya candanādeḥ prācuryeṇa 
bahiraṅgopasaṃskārakatvam | puṣpāṇāṃ ca tatraiva 
keśādimātrādhivāsaprayojakateti pūrvapūrvaprādhānyāt 
kramavivakṣā | viśvapateriti sambandhamātre ṣaṣṭhī, na 
māṣāṇāmaśnīyāditivat | tena khalyoge'pyevaṃ prayogo na duṣyati | 
yaduktam -

	idaṃ hi śāstramāhātmyadarśanālasacetasām |
	apabhāṣaṇavad bhāti na ca saubhāgyamujjhati || iti || 42 ||

	evaṃ saparyāsvabhāvaṃ sāmānyataḥ parāmṛśya tadviśeṣānapi 
tathā yojayiṣyamāṇaḥ prathamaṃ prāṇāyāmasya pāramārthyaṃ 
prakhyāpayati -



	vimraṣṭuṃ nijasattvaṃ vibhave kāryonmukhe stimite'pi |
	bāhyavṛttāntānāṃ bhaṅgaḥ prāṇasya saṃyamo jñeyaḥ || 43 ||

	pārameśvaropāstyunmukhānāṃ hi pramātṝṇāmayaṃ svabhāvo 
yat svātmasphurattāviṣphāroparāgamahimnā 
tattatprasaraṇaprakāravaicitryākrāntaṃ prapañcocchrāyaṃ pravartayanto 
bahirantarvibhāgaśūnyāmalaukikīmātmabhūmimāruhya 
mahācidāhlādacarvaṇacāturyamātrasārāḥ svacchandamāsata iti | tatra 
tairupāstyupakrame prakalpyamānaḥ prāṇasaṃyamo nāma 
bubhutsubhiritthamavaboddhavyo yannijaṃ sattvam uktarūpabalasvabhāvaḥ 
sadbhāvaḥ, tasya vikalpavikṣobhopaśliṣṭatayaiva 
sarvadā'nubhūyamānatvādanvayamātrādeva tannibandhanaṃ 
kiñcidalaukikamantastattvamastītyadhyavasīyate, na punarvyatirekadvārāpi | 
vyatirekaśca nātyantaṃ vyapohakalpanayopapadyate | kevalaṃ 
saṅkocamātrādupacaryate | ataśca -

	kāryonmukhaḥ prayatno yaḥ kevalaṃ so'tra lupyate |
	tasmin lupte vilupto'smītyabudhaḥ pratipadyate ||
	na tu yo'ntarmukho bhāvaḥ sarvajñatvaguṇāspadam |
	tasya lopaḥ kadācit syādanyasyānupalambhanāt ||

iti śrīspandaprakriyayā vedyāvarohaunmukhyaśālini svasāmarthyarūpe 
vibhave kalalāvasthayā'vasthānātmakaṃ staimityamanubhavatyapi 
viśvottīrṇasya svātmaparispandamayo vimarśaḥ -

	viśvasyaiva vilāsaṃ me śarīraṃ cāśnute śivaḥ |
	śālāmiva viśālāṃ svāmādarśaṃ ca yathā dvipaḥ ||

ityādinītyā darpaṇamaṇḍalāntaḥpraviṣṭagandhagajendrādyanusandhāna-
sthānīyaṃ paryālocanam | tadupapādakatayā bahiṣṭhānāṃ 
vedyollāsasvalakṣaṇānāṃ vṛttāntānāṃ bhaṅgo bhañjanaṃ 
stambhanaṃ prāṇāyāma iti | tattadvikalpavikṣobhavyatireke'pi 
svātmasphurattānusandhānopāyaḥ prāṇāyāma iti yāvat | yathā 
śrīsvacchande -



	apasavyena recyeta savyenaiva tu pūrayet |
	nāḍīnāṃ śodhanaṃ hyetanmokṣamārgapathasya tu ||
	recanāt pūraṇād rodhāt prāṇāyāmastridhā smṛtaḥ |
	sāmānyād bahirete tu punaścābhyantare trayaḥ ||
	abhyantareṇa recyeta pūryetābhyantareṇa tu |
	niṣkampaṃ kumbhakaṃ kṛtvā kāryāścābhyantarāstrayaḥ || iti |

yathā -

	nābhyāṃ hṛdayasañcārānmanaścendriyagocarāt |
	prāṇāyāmaścaturthastu supraśāntapade sthitaḥ || iti |

prāk saṃvit prāṇe pariṇatā iti sthityā sarvasyāpi 
recanapūrakādiprapañcopagrāhyasya vāyucakrasya 
prāṇamātrānuprāṇanatvāt prāṇasyetyekavacanopanyāsaḥ || 43 ||

	prāṇāyāmaprasaṅgāt tadanantaropakalpyamānānāṃ 
śoṣadāhāplāvanānāṃ tattvamuttejayati -

	śoṣo malasya nāśo dāha etasya vāsanocchedaḥ |
	āplāvanaṃ tanūnāṃ jñānasudhāsekanirmitā śuddhiḥ || 44 ||

	arcakānāṃ hyarcanopakrama eva kācidalaukikatā sampādyā | sā ca 
malopalepaprakṣayādau paryavasyati | tatra tadīyānāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ śoṣo 
nāma tadāyattasya malasya saṃsārāṅkurakāraṇabhūtasyājñānasya 
karśanamevākhyāyate | dāhaśca nāma teṣāṃ prastutasyaiva malasya yā 
vāsanā saṃskārasāratayāvasthānam, tadvyudāsasvabhāvo bhavati | 
evamāplāvanamapyajñānavyapohāvinābhāvodbhūtaṃ 
svarūpalābhalakṣaṇāhlādadāyitvādamṛtāyamānaṃ yad jñānaṃ 
svātmāvabodhastatprasaradhārāvāhikopakalpitā śuddhiḥ 
pavitrīkaraṇamiti | yaduktamasmatparamagurubhiḥ śrīkramavāsanāyām -



	saṃvitsatattvanairmalyasiddhaye śoṣaṇādikam |
	vikalpasārvabhaumasya śarīrasyāśrayāmyaham || iti |

ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - pūjakānāṃ hi prathamaṃ prāṇaparispandatayā 
prāṇībhūtavāyutattvatādātmyānusandhānādupari 
kariṣyamāṇayordāhāplāvanayorubhayorapi yogyatvamutpadyate | anantaraṃ 
paramapramātṛtāparāmarśadvārā teṣāmavacchinnapramātṛtāvigala-
nātmano dāhasyopayogaḥ | yaduktaṃ śrīvijñānabhaṭṭārake -

	kālāgninā kālapadādutthitena svakaṃ puram |
	pluṣṭaṃ vicintayedante śāntābhāsaḥ prajāyate || iti |

tadanu tameva bhāvaṃ draḍhayitumalaukikāhantānandacandrikāmaya-
mahāprameyāmṛtapravāhābhiṣekānubhūtirāplāvanalakṣaṇā 
sampadyate | yathā coktaṃ tatraiva -

	sarva jagat svadehaṃ vā svānandabharitaṃ smaret |
	yugapat svāmṛtenaiva parānandamayo bhavet || iti |

paryantataḥ prāmāṇyena prakāśānandasāmarasyānubhavo 
bhūtaśuddhiḥ ityupaniṣat | sā khalvalaukikī sṛṣṭirityucyate | yathoktaṃ 
śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	asminneva jagatyantarbhavad bhaktimatāmapi |
	harṣaprakāśabahalamanyadeva jagat sthitam || iti |

yathā ca stotrabhaṭṭārake -

	prakāśānandayorantarlolībhūtā parā sthitiḥ iti |

yaccoktaṃ mayaiva śrīkomalavallīstave -

saṃplutā samarasaṃ sudhāplavairvahnivṛṣṭiriva viśvatomukhī |
caṇḍi ! cetasi vibhāsi kasyaciccinmayī samayinaścamatkriyā || iti || 44 ||

	athāṅganyāsārdhyaśuddhipuṣpādisvabhāvamupanyasyati -

	avikalpatayā marśo vikalpavargasyāṅgasannāhaḥ |
	arghyaṃ vedyavilāsaḥ puṣpāṇi svabhāvapoṣakā bhāvāḥ || 45 ||



	aṅgānāṃ hi karacaraṇādyavayavātmanāṃ pṛthak pṛthak 
kāryatayā'vabhāsamānānāmekena kenaciccharīrānupraviṣṭenātmanā 
kāraṇabhūtena sarvavikalpottroṭanayuktyā marśaḥ parāmarśo 
bhavannaṅganyāsa ityucyate | tadevametat paryavasyati yat 
karacaraṇādyupalakṣitasya vikalpavikṣobhaprapañcasya 
pūrṇāhambhāvabhāvanāsvabhāvayā kayācidavikalpatayā 
vimarśanamiti | yaduktamasmatparamagurubhiḥ śrīkramavāsanāyām -

	kāraṇātmaparāmṛṣṭakāryabhūtāṅgulisthitim |
	karomi cinmayīṃ śuddhiṃ karayoḥ pariśodhanīm ||
	sarvajñatvādiśaktīnāṃ satīnāmātmani prabhau |
	unmajjanaṃ bhāvayāmi ṣaḍaṅgavidhiyogataḥ || iti |

taccāṅgānāṃ sannāha ityucyate, yena bhedaprathāaśastraprahāraḥ 
parihriyata iti | atha svasiddhāntaikayogyāmarghyaśuddhimāha - arghya 
vedyavilāsa iti | prāgupapāditā pūjā khalvargha ityucyate | 
tadarhamalaukikaṃ kiñcid dravadravyamarghyam |

	yanmugdhānāṃ praṇayavacasi prauḍhimānaṃ vidhatte 
	yannirvighnaṃ nidhuvanavidhau sādhvasaṃ sandhunoti |
	yasmin viśvāḥ kalitarucayo devatāścakracarya-
	stanmādhvīkaṃ sapadi kurute yatra bhogāpavargau ||

iti śrītantrālokasthityā bāhyābhyantarobhayabhedāt 
svahṛdayadevatātṛptyekaprayojanatayā pratiṣṭhāpyate | tacca tattvadṛṣṭyā 
vedyavilāsa ityavagantavyam | vedyasya viśvavikalpaprasaralakṣaṇasya yo 
vilāsaḥ saṅkocāvasthāyāmapi tattatsvabhāvasarvasvānati-
krāntirityaupacāriko vyavahāraḥ | arthatastu 
tādṛgvilāsavadvedyamevārghyadravyamiti paryavasyati | tasya 
śabdasparśarūparasagandhalakṣaṇaguṇapañcakāvinābhāvāt 
pṛthivyādimahābhūtapañcakasyaitadguṇasthūlāvasthānupraveśamātrā
nuprāṇanatvāt sarvo'pi viśvavikṣobhastatraiva parisphuratītyāpatitam  
kiñcaitat svahṛdayapratiṣṭhāspadatvāt pṛthivī, tasyaivāntarāpyāya-
katvādāpaḥ, prācīnavāsanāpāśaploṣapragalbhatayā tejaḥ, 
svasaṃvittattvaparispandanapradāyitvena 



vāyuḥ, śuddhacaitanyamātraparyavasāyitayā vyometi 
pṛthivyādibhūtapañcakasamaṣṭisvabhāvatayā viśvavilāsa 
evetyavadhāryate | tatsaṃskārastadupayogaścānantaramāsūtrayiṣyate | 
evaṃ bāhyadravyeṣu svātmatṛptyarpaṇapravīṇaṃ dravyaviśeṣaṃ 
pratipādya tadaṅgatvenāṅgīkāryāṇāṃ puṣpādīnāṃ svarūpamāha - 
puṣpāṇi svabhāvapoṣakā bhāvā iti | svasya yo bhāvaḥ 
pūjyadevatāsvabhāvatayā nirūpitaścamatkāraḥ, tasya 
tādṛkparāmarśapratiṣṭhāpanalakṣaṇapoṣaṇaprayojanāni puṣpāṇi | 
svabhāvaṃ poṣayantīti vyutpattyā puṣpāṇīti tātparyārthaḥ | 
upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat | tataśca puṣpaśabdena svabhāvapoṣakāṇāṃ 
bāhyābhyantarāṇāṃ sarveṣāmapi dravyāṇāṃ svīkāraḥ | yaduktaṃ 
śrītriṃśikāśāstravimarśinyāmācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ - 
puṣpairhṛdayāntaḥsvarūpasamarpaṇādeva hṛdayasya 
poṣakairbāhyābhyantaraiḥ sarvairdravyaiḥ iti | gandho hi guṇaḥ 
sarvaprapañcasphuratātmaka ityākhyāyate | yaduktaṃ mayaiva 
śrīpādukodaye -

	svasvakāryeṣu sarveṣāṃ kāraṇānāmavasthitiḥ |
	ajahadrūpatāyogādavināśena vartate ||
	hetuhaitukabhāvaśca śivaśaktyādigocaraḥ |
	pāramparyakramāt prāptaḥ sarvo viśrāmyati kṣitau ||
	ato viśvamayī pṛthvī tasyā gandho'pi tanmayaḥ |
	tadgrāhakaṃ ca karaṇaṃ viśvasya grāhakaṃ bhavet || iti |

tādṛggandhaguṇānuprāṇanatayā ca puṣpapadena sarvopakaraṇasvīkara iti 
suṣṭhūktam | tathā śrītriṃśikāśāstre -

	tataḥ sugandhipuṣpaiśca yathāśakti samarcayet || iti || 45 ||

	evaṃ laukikālaukikasādhāraṇyenopātteṣu dravyeṣu 
dvitīyapakṣaparigṛhītaṃ sarvendriyapakṣapātaviṣayaṃ dravyaviśeṣaṃ 
pṛthakkṛtya saṃskāropayogābhyāṃ parīkṣitumāha -



	pūrṇāhantāyā mukhe viśvavikalpāṅkurāṇāṃ vikṣepam |
	mantrollekhaviśuddhaṃ pūrṇa kulabindutarpaṇaṃ bhaṇāmaḥ || 46 ||

	kulaṃ hi nāma vedyavargollāsasvabhāvatayā 
bhagavānarghyabhaṭṭāraka evetyākhyāyate | tatra tadīyā ye bindavaḥ 
kramāt kramamaṃśāṃśitayā parisphurantaḥ śaktiparispandāḥ | taiḥ 
kriyamāṇaṃ devatāprīṇanamitthaṃ bhaṇāmaḥ parāmṛśāma iti yāvat | 
bahuvacanamevaṃ vimraṣṭṝṇāmalaukikāhaṅkārotkarṣaprakhyāpanāya | 
bhaṇanaprakāraśca -

	uttamaḥ puruṣo'nyo'sti yuṣmaccheṣaviśeṣitaḥ |
	tvaṃ mahāpuruṣastveko niśśeṣapuruṣāśrayaḥ ||

iti śrīmatstotrāvalīsthityā prameyaprapañcapadāvacchinnān pramātṝnapi 
vedyavargāntarbhāvenāvasthāpya svayamaśeṣasaṅkocottirṇamahamiti yat 
tattvam, yacca -

	ahaṃ prāṇo manaścāhamahaṅkāro'pyahaṃ mataḥ |
	ahaṃ buddhirahaṃ śaktirahaṃ sa bhagavān śivaḥ ||
	kiṃ vā bahupralāpena jagatyasmiṃścarācare |
	yo'rthaḥ pramāṇopārūḍhaḥ so'haṃ sarvātmakaḥ sthitaḥ ||

iti śrīhaṃsabhedasthityā sarvaprapañcaparisphuraṇākāratayā 
parāmṛśyate, tasya yā caitanyamātmā iti, udyamo bhairavaḥ iti ca 
śrīśivasūtrasthityā tādṛksvarūpaniṣkarṣaikamātraphalatayā 
tatpratyayena pratipādyamānā mahāsphurattā, tasyāḥ 
svātmadevatāparyāyāyā yanmukhaṃ prāṇādyavaṣṭambhadvārā 
saṃvitprasaraṇopakramasthānaṃ svaṃ śarīram, tasminnadhikaraṇatayā 
parāmṛśyamāne na ha vā evaṃvidi kiñcanānannam 
ityupaniṣatprakriyayā viśvavartināṃ sarveṣāmeva 
vikalpānāmaṅkuratayā kāryavarga prati kāraṇabhāvena sūkṣmatayā 
samunmiṣanto ye kalāviśeṣāḥ, teṣām - 
cinmayasvātmaparameśvarārādhanena 
tadīyaspandavilāsātmaṣaṭtriṃśattattvamayaṃ jagat poṣayedityarthaḥ iti 
śrīśāmbhavadīpikānyāyādantarhomātmā vikṣepalakṣaṇo'rtha eveti | 
anvayastu tādṛk -



tarpaṇametādṛśaṃ vikṣepaṃ bhaṇāma iti bhavati | tacca tarpaṇaṃ 
mantrollekhaviśuddham | mantro hi vakṣyamāṇasvabhāvaḥ kaścit 
parāmarśaviśeṣeḥ | tena ya ullekhaḥ svātmatādātmyānu-
praveśalakṣaṇamujjvalīkaraṇam, tena saṃvitsvātantryasaṃskārātmanā 
viśuddhaṃ sarvapūrvavāsanāvyudāsoparūḍhālaukikānubhāvot-
karṣamiti yāvat | ata eva hyetat pūrṇaṃ puṇyamityucyate | sā hi 
pāvanatāyāḥ, parā koṭiḥ, yadbhedakalaṅkāśaucaśālibhiḥ paśubhiḥ 
spraṣṭumapi na pāryate, pratyuta -

	aśaktāstatpadaṃ gantuṃ tato nindāṃ prakurvate |

iti nītyā nindyate ca, yatkilānyadapavitramapi pavitrayituṃ pragalbhate, tasya 
svataḥ pavitratāyāṃ kaḥ sandehaḥ | yathā śrītriṃśikāśāstre -

	śivāsaṃkhyābhijaptena toyenābhyukṣayet tataḥ |
	puṣpādikaṃ kramāt sarvaṃ liṅgaṃ vā sthaṇḍilaṃ ca vā || iti |

śrītantrāloke'pi -

	arghyapātrāmbuvipruḍbhiḥ spṛṣṭaṃ sarva viśudhyati |
	śivārkakarasaṃsparśāt kānyā śuddhirbhaviṣyati || iti |

tataśca -

	yadyathopanatameva pūrṇatāmādadhāti hṛdayaṅgamatvataḥ |
	tat tathaiva parameśapūjane yogyamanyadiha nāsti lakṣaṇam ||

iti śrījñānendukaumudīnyāyādetadeva parāhantāyāḥ paramaṃ 
prīṇanaṃ yadalipiśitāṅganādyapayogaḥ, yaśca jīvanmokṣa ityāmnāyata 
iti tātparyārthaḥ | yaduktaṃ śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	prāyo hi maithune madye māṃse ca paridṛśyate |
	āsaktiḥ sarvajantūnāṃ viśeṣāt kasyacit kvacit ||
	yadi tat tyāgasaṃrambhapūrvaṃ teṣāṃ vidhīyate |
	upadeśo na sa manāgapi citte prarohati ||



ityārabhya -

	anenaivāśayenātra parabrahmopalabdhaye |
	lalanāmadyamāṃsāni pūjāgryāṇi viśeṣataḥ ||

ityantam || 46 ||

	nanu prastutāyā api saparyāyāḥ svarūpaṃ yatha niṣkṛṣyoktam, 
evaṃ devatāyā api vapurupapādanīyamityākāṅkṣāyāṃ 
tatsvarūpamevābhivyaṅktumāha -

	yo yasya bhāvayogastasya khalu sa eva devatā bhavati |
	tadbhāvabhāvitā abhilaṣitaṃ tathā phalanti pratimāḥ || 47 ||

	arcakānāṃ hi pramātṝṇāmarcyabhūtā devatā nāma nānyā 
kācidupapadyate | api tu teṣāṃ madhye yasya yasya 
svahṛdayasphurattālakṣaṇo bhāvaḥ, tasya tasya sa eva devatā 
bhavitumarhati, na punarmṛdupalalohapaṭṭakāṣṭhādipratimādisvabhāvā | 
yato jaḍājaḍayorjaḍaṃ stambhakumbhādi pratyajaḍasyaivātmano 
hānopādānādyarthakriyāprayojakatvalakṣaṇaṃ svātantryamālokyate | 
īpsitaprasādhakatvamanīpsitaniṣedhakatvaṃ cetyetāvān nanu devatānāṃ 
svabhāvaḥ | tacca tāsāṃ yadi svato na saṃbhavati, tat kinnibandhanamidaṃ 
pratimānāṃ devatāśabdavācyatvapaṭṭabandhābhiṣekagarvadaurlālityam | 
uktarūpaṃ ca tat svātantryaṃ saṃvit - svabhāvatāṃ nātikrāmati | 
tatsvabhāvatā ca -

	mahatāmamareśa ! pūjyamāno'pyaniśaṃ tiṣṭhasi pūjakaikarūpaḥ 

iti śrīmatstotrāvalīsthityā teṣāṃ pramātṝṇāmeva saṃgacchata iti 
śrīsvātmaprakāśa eva devatetyasmanniścaya ityasakṛdavocāma | yaduktaṃ 
śrījñānendukaumudyām -

	svātmānaṃ hi vihāya cetanamamuṃ kiṃ pūjayeyurjaḍam iti |

yaccoktaṃ nayasaṃgatau -

	nāsti nādāt paro mantro na devaḥ svātmanaḥ paraḥ |
	nānusandheḥ parā pūjā nahi tṛpteḥ paraṃ phalam || iti |



yathā ca gītāniḥṣyande -

	ujjhitvātmasamādhānaṃ ye dyāyantyanyadevatāḥ |
	bhikṣante bhūrivittāste bhikṣitvāpi bubhukṣitāḥ || iti |

arcakatvarūpeṇopādhinā pramātṝṇāṃ pṛthak pṛthag 
bhedaprathābhimantṛtvād yo yasyetyādinā bhāvānāṃ 
bāhulyopapādanadvārā devatātvamunmīlitam | yadāhuḥ - yad yad rūpaṃ 
kāmayate tat tad devatārūpaṃ bhavati iti | ata eva hi - rūpaṃ rūpaṃ 
maghavān bobhavīti iti bahvṛcā ācakṣate | mukhyayā tu vṛttyā 
tattadbhāvabhedasya prakāśaikātmyapāriśeṣyāt sarvasyāpi prapañcasya 
eka eva hṛdayasphuraṇasvabhāvo devatātvenārādhyaḥ | na punaḥ 
pratimāpustakādirjaḍaḥ padārtha iti tātparyārthaḥ | anena bhāvayoga 
ityatra yogaśabdo vyākhyātaḥ | yogo hi tādṛśī śaktiḥ | yaduktaṃ 
śrīśacīmate -

	yogastvamasi deveśi ! yogī cāhaṃ sanātanaḥ |
	yogenedaṃ tvayā viśvamāviṣṭaṃ śambhunā mayā || iti |

śrībhagavadgītāsvapi - paśya me yogamaiśvaram iti | tat ṇami ūṇa 
ityādivākyopakrānto mahāprakāśa eva devatā, nānyaḥ kaścit | 
etatsvātantryasaṃrambhādhīnasvasphuraṇavijṛmbhaṇo jaḍaḥ prapañca 
ityuktaṃ bhavati | tathā śrīpūrve -

	sarvamanyat parityajya cittamatra niveśayet |
	mṛddhātuśailaratnādibhavaṃ liṅgaṃ na pūjayet ||
	arcayeccinmayaṃ liṅgaṃ yatra līnaṃ carācaram | iti |

yathā ca śrīprabhākaule -

	paśya mohasya māhātmyaṃ svahṛdisthe'pi śaṅkare |
	liṅgasthaṇḍilavahnyapsu vīkṣayanti yathā śivam || iti |

ataśca -

	strīparāṅmukhamanāḥ kila krudhā puṣpaketumadahanmaheśvaraḥ |
	tvaṃ tadāpi nanu tasya saiva dhīḥ satyamamba! subhagā'bhidhīyase ||



iti śrīkomalavallīstavasthityā viśvaprakāśāt 
svahṛdayaprakāśasyaivātyantaślāghyatvāt svātmarūpā 
saubhāgyasaṃvinmayyeva devatetyatra na kiñcid vaimatyam | yaduktam -

	svātmaiva devatā proktā lalitā viśvavigrahā iti |

etaccāsmatparamagurukartṛke śrīmadṛjuvimarśinyādau vimarśanīyam | 
evaṃ ca parāmarśaprarūḍhau satyāṃ tasya devatātvenopapāditasya 
bhāvasya yo bhāvaḥ sphurattāparaparyāyā sattā, tayā bhāvitāḥ 
svahṛdayaprākaṭyaparyantaṃ rūṣitā yāḥ pratimāḥ 
skandagaṇapativaṭukādyākāravanto mṛdupalādisanniveśaviśeṣāḥ, 
tāsteṣāmarcakānāṃ putravittapramukhaṃ taṃ tamabhivāñchitamartha 
prasuvate | yathoktaṃ śrīpūrve -

	bahirliṅgasya liṅgatvamanenādhiṣṭhitaṃ yataḥ |
	ataḥ prapūjayedetat paramādvaitamāśritaḥ || iti |

tathā sidhānte'pi -

	antarliṅgaṃ dṛḍhaṃ baddhvā bahirliṅgaṃ tato'rcayet iti |

etadabhipretya hi śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	antarbhakticamatkāracarvaṇāmīlitekṣaṇaḥ |
	namo mahyaṃ śivāyeti pūjayan syāt tṛṇānyapi ||

ityuktam | tatra ca mukhyāyāḥ svahṛdayadevatāyāḥ sakāśādāsāṃ 
pratimānāṃ na kiñcidvailakṣaṇyam, yena 
siddharasaviddhadhātunyāyādatra tadbhāvāpattidārḍhya notpadyate | 
yathā ca śrīvirūpākṣapañcāśikāyām -

	yā devatā yamarthaṃ karoti tenārthino dṛḍhaṃ tasyām |
	vidhṛtāhaṅkārasya kṣaṇena so'rthaḥ samāyāti || iti |

bhāvanāyāśca mukhyavat phalapradatvamuktaṃ 
śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

	kalpanāpi na mṛṣā phalaṃ śive yat sthitaṃ garuḍabhāvanādijam 

iti || 47 ||



	athaivamudbhāvitasya devatāvapurniṣkarṣasya 
pratipattṛjanapratītisaukaryāya dṛṣṭāntaṃ kamapyupadarśayitumāha -

	citraṃ na likhati citraṃ citrakaraḥ paśyata likhati taccitram |
	tad bhaṇata kutra yogyā kartuṃ dvayorapi devatābuddhiḥ || 48 ||

	jaḍamajaḍaṃ vā vastu devateti sandihāno jana evaṃ 
pratibodhayitavyaḥ, yaduta ālekhyārpitavapuṣaṃ 
puruṣamahilāprabhṛtimacetanaṃ bhāvaṃ prati tajjātiyasyānyasya na 
kasyacidapi tādṛgvilekhanakriyāyāṃ sāmarthyamālokyate | pratyuta 
tadvijātīyasya tacchilpanirmāṇakauśalotkarṣaśālinaścetanasyaiva tat 
saṃbhavatītyavipratipanneyaṃ prakriyā | uktamartha hṛdayaṅgamīkartu 
paśyatetyuktam | cetanācetanayoḥ sāmarthyamasāmarśrya ca spaṣṭatayā 
prakhyāpayituṃ tadvṛttādiparāmarśavyatirekeṇa punaḥ 
punaścitraśabdaprayogaḥ | tasmāditi | yato'nvayavyatirekābhyāṃ 
cetanasyaivoktakriyākartṛtvaṃ na punastadvyatiriktasya kasyacit, tato hetoḥ | 
bhaṇata parāmṛśata, tayościtrasya citrakarasya cobhayormadhye 
katarasminnarthe devateti pratyetumucitamiti | 
śiśavo'pyenamaṃśamasaṃśayamavabudhyante yaccitrādeḥ 
kāryaprapañcāccitrakṛdādiṣu kartṛṣu svatantraḥ kartā iti nītyā 
svātantryalakṣaṇaḥ kaścidutkarṣo'stīti | asyārthasyātyantaspaṣṭatayā 
niścitasyāpi pakṣasya niṣkarṣeṇānupanyāsaḥ | etena cetanasyaiva 
mukhyayā vṛttyā devatātvam, cetyānāṃ tu tadāveśāspadatayā 
tadupacāreṇetyuktaṃ bhavati | ata eva tānyanyā devatā ityucyante | 
yaduktaṃ śrīgītāsu -

	ye'pyanyadevatābhaktā yajante śraddhayā'nvitāḥ |
	te'pi māmeva kaunteya ! yajantyavidhipūrvakam || iti |

devatātvena prasādhyamānaṃ parameśvaraṃ prati viśvavaicitryasya 
citraprāyatayā citrapadenaivārthopapādanam | tathoktaṃ 
śrīpratyabhijñāyām -



	viśvavaicitryacitrasya samabhittitalopame iti |

yathā coktaṃ padasaṃgatau -

	cidākāśamaye svāṅge viśvālekhyavidhāyine |
	sarvādbhutodbhavabhuve namo viṣamacakṣuṣe || iti |

tathā cābhiyuktoktiḥ -

	jagaccitraṃ samālikhya svātmatūlikayātmani |
	svayameva tadālokya prīṇāti parameśvaraḥ || iti |

citraṃ na likhati citramityādyuktayā bhaṅgyā prameyapadavyāmiva 
cittamantaḥkaraṇaṃ na likhati, citramuktarūpaṃ cittamantaḥkaraṇāntaraṃ 
veti pramāṇapathā'nuguṇyenāpyavagantavyam | atha ca cit cetanaḥ | tasya 
bhāvaścittvaṃ caitanyam | taccittvaṃ citraṃ prameyarūpaṃ cittam, 
pramāṇātmakaṃ cittvaṃ vā sajātīyaṃ jñaptisvabhāvamarthāntaraṃ na 
likhati nonmīlayatītyādipramārūpadharmakakṣyānukūlo'rthaḥ | 
itthamarthatraye'pyavekṣyamāṇe sarvathā cetanasyaiva 
ślāghyatvakāṣṭhāprāptirityāścaryacamatkārānubūtyarthatayāpi 
pāryantikaścitraśabdo'nusandheyaḥ, tadabhidheyasya sarvanāmnaivoktatvāt 
| likhatiścotpādana ivopasaṃhāre'pyasti, pārthivaṃ lohalekhyamityādivat | 
tadanuguṇo'pyarthastrividhamavatāryaḥ | traividhyaṃ ca 
tadālekhyāntaḥkaraṇasaṃvittvaparyāyāṇāṃ citracittacittvānāṃ 
tantreṇoccāraṇāditi || 48 ||

	nanu yadi svātmaiva devateti nirbandhaḥ, tat kimasya mantratantrādinā 
prapañcenopakriyate | sa khalu svavyatiriktadevatābhimukhyaprayojanatayā 
svīkriyata ityāśaṅkya mantrastāvaduktarūpadevatānuguṇyenaiva 
nirṇīyata ityāha -

	mananamayī nijavibhave nijasaṅkoce bhaye trāṇamayī |
	kavalitaviśvavikalpā anubhūtiḥ kāpi mantraśabdārthaḥ || 49 ||



	mananatrāṇadharmāṇo mantrāḥ iti hyāmnāyaḥ | tatra 
mantrānusandhātuḥ svecchāmātreṇopādhinā vibhavaḥ saṅkoca 
ityavasthādvayamasti | tayorvibhavo nāma 
viśvataduttarobhayasāmarasyayuktyā pūrṇāhambhāvabhāvanātmā 
vikāsaḥ, yat pāramaiśvaryamityucyate | saṅkocaśca 
tadviparyayādapūrṇatvābhimānaḥ, yat pāśavamityākhyāyate | atra ca 
pūrvasya svābhāvikatvamuttarasyāropitatā ceti vivekaḥ | evaṃ sthite 
tādṛśyātmano vikāse samullasati tasya yanmananamuparyupari tathā 
parāmarśānusyūtisvabhāvaścamatkāraḥ, tat prakṛtyā | tadvaduktarūpe 
svasya saṅkoce prastute tato yat trāṇam - saṅkoco 
vicāryamāṇaścidaikātmyena prathamānatvāccinmaya eva | anyathā tu na 
kiñcit iti śrīpratyabhijñāhṛdayamaryādayā tasyāpi saṅkocasya 
vaiśvātmyaprathānupraviṣṭatānusandhānotpādanadvārā 
svasvabhāvabhaṅgaprasaṅgarūpacākityavyapohalakṣaṇam, tanmayī ca 
bhavantī tenaiva hetudvayena vedyavikṣobhasarvasvagrāsaviśṛṅkhalollāsā 
yā'nubhūtiḥ svahṛdayaikasaṃvedyā vimarśaśaktiḥ, saiva mantra ityasya 
śabdasyābhidheyatayā'nubhūyata iti | yathā śrīrājarājabhaṭṭārake -

	varṇātmako na mantro daśabhujadeho na pañcavadano'pi |
	saṅkalpapūrvakoṭau nādollāso bhavenmantraḥ || iti |

yathā ca śrīvijñānabhaṭṭārake -

	bhūyo bhūyaḥ pare bhāve bhāvanā bhāvyate hi yā |
	japastotraṃ svayaṃ nādo mantrātmā japya īdṛśaḥ || iti |

yathā ca śrīśivasūtreṣu - cittaṃ mantraḥ iti | śrīstotrabhaṭṭārake'pi -

	cidagnisaṃhāramarīcimantraḥ saṃvidvikalpān glapayannudeti iti |

śrīkramakelau ca - seyamevaṃvidhā bhagavatī saṃviddevyeva mantraḥ iti | 
bhaṭṭaśrībhūtirājenāpyuktam - sarvakroḍīkāreṇa sthitatvād devyeva 
mantraḥ iti | evañca -

	pṛthaṅmantraḥ pṛthaṅmantrī na sidhyati kadācana |
	jñānamūlamidaṃ sarvamanyathā na prasidhyati ||



iti nītyā svātmasaṃvitsvarūpasyaiva mantraśabdārthatvaṃ mukhyam | 
akṣarasanniveśeṣu punarupacāreṇocyata ityuktaṃ bhavati | yadāhuḥ -

	uccāryamāṇā ye mantrā na mantrāṃścāpi tān viduḥ |
	mohitā devagandharvā mithyājñānena garvitāḥ || iti |

yaccoktaṃ mayaiva saṃvidullāse -

	puṇḍrekṣoriva mantrasya mādhurye hṛdayaspṛśi |
	ṛjīṣamānane tiṣṭhatyakṣaroccāralakṣaṇam || iti |

etadāśayenaiva hi -

	gurudevatāmanūnāmaikyaṃ saṃbhāvayan dhiyā śiṣyaḥ

ityabhiyuktā ācakṣate | etena japo vyākhyātaḥ | jananapālanasvabhāvatayā 
hi japa ityucyate | yaduktamasmatparamagurubhiḥ -

	janipālanadharmeṇa japenāntarmukhātmanā iti |

tacca svavibhavamanane svasaṅkocamātre ca paryavasyati | 
kevalamupapāditamantrasvarūpaparāmarśo japa ityucyate | mantrasya ca 
vaiśvātmyānubhūtirūpatvāt sarvo'pi 
vāgvyavahārastatparāmarśātmatayaivotpadyate | yaduktaṃ 
śrībhaṭṭanārāyaṇena - kā ca vāṅ nocyase yayā iti | ata eva 
śrīśivasūtreṣu - kathā japaḥ ityuktam || 49 ||

	nanu mantrāṇāmevamalaukikānubhūtyātmakatvaprasādhane 
tatsāmānyarūpasya sarvanirvivādaṃ śrotrendriyagrāhyasya śabdarāśeḥ 
kīdṛśī rītirityāśankya vāktattvasyaiva tattvamunmudrayitumāha -

	vaikharikā nāma kriyā jñānamayī bhavati madhyamā vāk |
	icchā punaḥ paśyantī sūkṣmā sarvāsāṃ samarasā vṛttiḥ || 50 ||

	vāktattvaṃ tāvat kramāt sūkṣmā paśyantī madhyamā vaikharīti 
caturdhā bhidyate | yathoktaṃ sāmbapañcāśati -



	yā sā mitrāvaruṇasadanāduccarantī triṣaṣṭi
	varṇānatra prakaṭakaraṇaiḥ prāṇasaṅgāt prasūte
	tāṃ paśyantīṃ prathamamuditāṃ madhyamāṃ buddhisaṃsthāṃ
	vācaṃ vaktre karaṇaviśadāṃ vaikharīṃ ca prapadye || iti |

tatra vaikharīti prasiddhā vāk tālvādikaraṇavyāpāropārūḍha-
sphuraṇatayā kriyāśaktirityadhyavasīyate | madhyamā ca 
buddhivṛttimātrapravartyamānatvād jñānaśaktiḥ | paśyantī punaricchā, 
bahiḥ prasaraṇābhyupagamarūpatvāt tasyāḥ, yataḥ parā vāk paśyantīti 
paśyantyā vyutpattiḥ | sūkṣmā tu 
śikhaṇḍyaṇḍarasanyāyāduktavāktrayaśabalībhāvasvabhāvā 
pratyagdraṣṭuḥ parameśvarasyodyogalakṣaṇā vṛttirityākhyāyate | parā 
vāk punastasyaiva parameśvarasya svarūpamanupraviśantī parisphurati | 
yaduktamasmatparamagurubhiḥ śrīmadṛjuvimarśinyām - mātṛkāṃ 
paravāgātmā'nāhatabhaṭṭārakaparamaśivasvarūpāṃ 
ṣaṭtriṃśattattvaprasaraṇahetubhūtāṃ saṃvidamityarthaḥ iti | tasya ca 
vaktīti kartṛvyutpattyā vāktvam, na punarucyata iti karmānuguṇyāt | 
yaduktamasmatparamagurubhiḥ śrīsaṃvitstotre -

	tvāmupāsitagurūttamāḥ parāṃ vācamāhuravibhaktaviśvakām |
	svaprakāśanavimarśanātmikāṃ vakti vāgiti niruktimāsthitāḥ || iti |

tasmādicchādiśaktitritayavistārātmā sarvo'pi vāgvilāsa ityuktaṃ bhavati 
| yathā yoginīhṛdaye -

	icchāśaktistathā seyaṃ paśyantīvapuṣā sthitā |
	jñānaśaktistridhā proktā madhyamā vāgudīritā ||
	kriyāśaktistu raudrīyaṃ vaikharī viśvavigrahā | iti |

tathā ca śrītantrāloke -

	prāk paśyantyatha madhyā'nyā vaikharī ceti tā imāḥ |
	parāparā parā devī caramā tvaparātmikā ||
	icchādiśaktitritayamidameva nigadyate |
	etatprāṇita evāyaṃ vyavahāraḥ pratāyate || iti |



icchādisvabhāvaiva ca svātmasphurattā | tanmayī ca 
kācidanubhūtirmantraśabdārtha iti sarvaṃ saṃgacchate | icchā punaḥ 
paśyantītyuddeśyopādeyayorvyatyāsenārthāntaramapi dyotyate |

	na so'sti pratyayo loke yaḥ śabdānugamādṛte |

iti nītyā sarvo'pi pramātṛsvabhāvaḥ paryantato vāṅmayatāṃ 
nollaṅghayati | tataśca prakāśarūpasyātmano 
vimarśaśaktirevānuprāṇanam | sā ca mantrajapapūjādyaneka-
śabdavyapadeśyā bhavati | kevalaṃ tattaddvāramātrameva bheda iti || 50 ||

	atha mantrasāhacaryānmudrāpyalaukikī kācidanusandheyetyāha -

	ānandollāsaśrīḥ kṣullakitāṣṭamahāsiddhisaubhāgyā |
	dṛśyate yatra daśāyāṃ saiva devasya sarvamudrāḥ || 51 ||

	yasyāmavasthāyāṃ devasya krīḍāvijigīṣādyanekaprakāra-
svātantryasāratvāt pūjyapūjakatvobhayasvabhāvasāmarasyaśālinaḥ 
parameśvarasya svaviśrāntilakṣaṇamānandaṃ prati ya ullāsaḥ, tathā 
parāmṛśyamānatayā sphurattā, tasyāḥ śrīḥ 
tadvaduparyuparyanusyūtilakṣaṇā prarūḍhirdṛśyate 
nirvivādamaparokṣīkriyate, saiva sarvāḥ karaṅkiṇyādayaḥ 
saṃkṣobhiṇyādayo'nyathā vā prasiddhastāstā mudrā ityavagantavyam | 
tayā cānandollāsaśriyā kṣullakitāṣṭamahāsiddhisaubhāgyayā bhāvyam 
| loke hi khaḍgagorocanoddiṣṭapātālaprabhṛtayo'pi vibhūtispandāḥ 
siddhaya ityucyante | tadapekṣayā kiñcit 
saṅkocaśūnyatvādaṇimādiśaktīnāṃ mahāsiddhitvam | tāsāṃ 
cāṣṭakatvaṃ tattatsādhakajanahṛdayollāsarūpāṇāṃ 
cidānandecchājñānakriyānubhavasmṛtyapohanānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ 
brāhmyādimātraṣṭakādhiṣṭhānadvārā bahirvibhūtirūpatvāt | 
yaduktamāgame -

	vargānukramayogena yasyāṃ mātraṣṭakaṃ sthitam |
	vande tāmaṣṭavargotthamahāsiddhyaṣṭakeśvarīm || iti |



yadyapi sarvatra sāmarasyādetadaṣṭakānupraveśaḥ tathāpi kvacit 
kasyacidaulbaṇyāvaśyambhāvādaṣṭamahāsiddhaya iti prasiddhiḥ | tatra 
svaśarīraṃ pratyaṇūkaraṇasāmarthyamaṇimā | 
gaganādivyāpakatvakauśalaṃ mahimā | samudrasalilādāvapi padbhyāṃ 
prayāṇe tadasparśo laghimā | yathābhilaṣitapadārthalābhaḥ prāptiḥ | 
saṃkalpamātrāt sarvatrāpratihatagatitvaṃ prākāmyam | sārvatrikaṃ 
prabhutvamīśitṛtvam | indriyārthopabhoge 
svecchāmātrādhīnahānopādānatvaṃ vaśitvam | svābhilāṣamātrāt 
sadyaḥ svarganarakādyanubhūtiryatra kāmāvasāyitvamiti vivekaḥ | 
tāsāṃ ca sādhakajanahṛdayahāritvotkarṣalakṣaṇaṃ yat saubhāgyam, 
tadupāditayā''nandasaṃpadā kṣullakīkriyate kṣudrīkriyata 
ityakṣarārthaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - karacaraṇādyākuñcanāvakuñcanādi-
saṅkocollaṅghanena nistaraṅgasamudrāvasthānasthānīyā tata eva 
svāntarvilīnaphenabudbuda bindvādiprāyā'śeṣabāhyānandaparispandā 
svaviśrānticamatkārā'paraparyāyaparāmarśakriyārūpā 
kācidalaukikānubhūtirānandasaṃpad mudaṃ rātīti vyutpattyā 
karaṅkiṇyādyanyamudrāprapañcodayavilayabhūmirmudrātvenānu-
sandheyeti | yathā śrīpaścime -

	sā siddhiḥ sarvasiddhīnāṃ siddhitve parikīrtitā |
	jñānaṃ hyakalitaṃ caikaṃ mudrā caikā grahātmikā ||
	dvāvetau yasya jāyete so'nvayī siddhaśāsane | iti |

	ānandaṃ prati 
parānandanirānandamahānandānandaviṣayānandasvabhāvā'śeṣānanda
sāmarasyamunmīlayitumullāsaḥ śrīriti cobhayopanyāsaḥ | yathoktaṃ 
śrīstotrabhaṭṭārake - kaulārṇavānandamayormirūpām iti | yathā 
śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

	yat paro nirupasargataḥ paraḥ syānmahānapi ca kevalaḥ śive |
	uttaraśca viṣayāt sa ca tvadānanda ullasasi tadghanāsi yat || iti |

tatra ca tāḥ siddhayo yadā prakāśarūpatayā 
sarvapadārthāntarbhāvasāmarthyamaṇimā, tathaiva vyāpakatvaṃ 
mahimā, bhedarūpagauravavyudāso laghimā, svātma-



viśrāntilābhaḥ prāptiḥ, vedyavilāsopalālanaṃ prākāmyam, 
anavacchinnaiśvaryaśālitvamīśitṛtvam, vimraṣṭṛtayā sarvasahatā 
vaśitvam, pūrṇāhambhāvabhāvanā yatra kāmāvasāyitvamiti 
saṃvinmayatvaucityena parāmṛśyate, tadānīmasmaduktamudrāparvānu-
praveśa iti na kiñcidāsāṃ kṣullakīkaraṇam, prayojanābhāvāt | 
mantramudrādivad dhyānahomādayo'nye'pi sādhakavyāpārāḥ 
saṃvidadvaitānuguṇyena svayamūhanīyāḥ | yathā śrīvijñānabhairave -

	dhyānaṃ yā niścalā cintā nirākārā nirāśrayā |
	na tu dhyānaṃ śarīrādimukhahastādikalpanam ||
	mahāśūnyālaye vahnau bhūtākṣaviṣayādikam |
	hūyate manasā sārdhaṃ sa homaścetanāsrucā || iti |

kulārghyasvīkaraṇaṃ cāntarahomaprāyamevetyavagantavyam | 
yaduktamasmatparamagurubhiḥ śrīsubhagodaye -

	parāhantāmaye saṃvidagnau saṃvedyatarpaṇe |
	idantālakṣaṇaṃ havyaṃ juhuyādabahirmukhaḥ || iti |

yaccoktaṃ mayaiva mahārthodaye -

	atha havyamidantākhyaṃ hāvaṃ hāvaṃ svacinmukhe |
	ullaṅghya māyāmālinyaṃ svairamāsīta meruvat ||

ityalamatirahasyonmīlanaprapañcena || 51 ||

	itthaṃ mahatā prapañcenopapāditasya vimarśasvarūpasya 
prayojanamupapādayitumāha -

	hṛdayasthānaprarūḍho vimarśakalpadrumo mahāśākhaḥ |
	puṣpyati bhogaśriyā phalati ca niṣkalasukhotsavālokam || 52 ||

	hṛdayaṃ hi nāma viśvapratiṣṭhābhūmitvādatimahat sthānam | 
yaduktamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ - hṛdayaṃ hi nāma 
pratiṣṭhāsthānamucyate iti |



yathā copaniṣat -

	hṛdayaṃ tad vijānīyād viśvasyāyatanaṃ mahat iti |

tatra prakarṣeṇa rūḍhaścarvyamāṇatāmāpannastata eva 
hetorbhedaprathādyanantavaicitryayogī | prakarṣaścāsya madhye prasava 
ityuktanītyullaṅghanena sakṛdvibhātatvarūpamaujjvalyam | tathābhāve hi 
tādṛgvimarśaśālināṃ svargādyupayogayoge'pi -

	vāsanāmātralābhe'pi yo'pramatto na jāyate |
	tamanityeṣu bhogeṣu yojayanti vināyakāḥ |
	tasmānna teṣu saṃsaktiṃ kurvītottamavāñchayā ||

ityādibibhīṣikāparyudāsena sarvatra naiścintyamutpadyate | yataḥ prakṛtyā 
cidānandasāmarasyacamatkārātmakamātmanaścaitanyam | 
taduchūnatāyāṃ kramādicchā jñānaṃ kriyeti śaktayaḥ | kriyā ca -

	kriyātmako hyayaṃ kālaṃ kriyā kārakamāśritā |
	ṣoḍhā ca kārakagrāmaḥ śaktyātmani hi tiṣṭhati ||

ityādiyuktyā karaṇādhikaraṇādikārakasvabhāvā satī kramāt kāryatayā 
parisphurati | yaduktaṃ śrītantravaṭadhānikāyām -

	krayā svātmaparispandastataḥ prāṇo'tha tatkṛtam |
	kālavaicitryamityetat saṃvitspandādhikaṃ nahi || iti |

kāryaṃ ca vidhiniṣedhaviṣayatayā bahuprakāraṃ bhavat 
tattadavāntarabhedabhinnadravyaguṇādyanekasahasravikalpavikṣobhavicitrā-
śeṣaviśvavyavahāratayā paryavasyatīti vimarśaśakterevāyametāvān 
vijṛmbhaṇollāsaḥ | tādṛśaśca yo'yaṃ 
bāhyābhyantarapratītipathasahasrasarvasvanirvāhakatayā 
tattadaśeṣābhilaṣitapradānapāṇḍityaśālī 
kalpaśākhītyadhyavasīyamāno vimarśo 
vimraṣṭṛlakṣaṇasvātmaparāmarśātmā camatkāraḥ, sa ca 
svasvarūpavedināṃ puṃsāṃ bhogaśriyā puṣpyati puṣpasthānīyāṃ 
bhogalakṣmīmudbhāvayati | bhogo hyabhimatāṅganāliṅganādi-
bahirvibhūtyanubhavaḥ | tasya ceyameva śrīryadvidhiniṣedhollaṅghinā 
saṃvida-



dvaitāsvādasaubhāgyena grāhyagrahaṇakautūhalopalambhaja eva 
svātmavijṛmbhāvabodhasādhanapragalbha ityapavargasaṃpatsvabhāva-
tvenāyamanubhūyate | kiñca, sa vimarśākhyaḥ kalpaśākhī 
niṣkalaṅkamahantedantāvibhāgavicchedāvacchedalakṣaṇakalākalaṅka-
śūnyaṃ yat sukhaṃ svaviśrāntisvabhāvaḥ svahṛdayāhlādaḥ, sa 
evotsavaḥ,

	śacīkucataṭe śakro narake viluṭhan kṛmiḥ |
	ubhau samasamādhānau vicitro vāsanābhramaḥ ||

ityādinyāyād (yad) anyadanubhavitṛjanaviśeṣahṛdayasaṅkalpamātropa-
kalpitaṃ saukhyāropaṇam, tadvyudāsena bahubhiḥ 
śrīśivānandamahāprakāśamaheśvarānandaprabhṛtibhiryogīndraiḥ 
saṃbhūyasaṃvādenopabhujyamānatvāt tādṛgutsavarūpamālokaṃ 
pratyakprakāśaṃ ca prasūte | atha ca niṣkalamityatra kalāśabdaḥ kalayati 
pārimityena bahiḥ paricchinattiti vyutpattyā māyāśaktirvā, 
kiñcitkartṛtvopodbalanopalakṣitā pañcakañcukī vā, brāhmyādīnāṃ 
vargādhiṣṭhātrīṇāṃ samaṣṭirvā, tadadhiṣṭheyā mātṛkā vā, 
svātmāpūrṇākhyātilakṣaṇaḥ svasya saṅkoco veti bahuprakāraṃ 
vyākhyeyaḥ |

	etaduktaṃ bhavati - viśvavaicitryavimraṣṭṛtvasvabhāvaḥ 
paramasvātantryaśālī parameśvaro'hamiti hṛdayaṅgamībhāvaparyanta-
manuśīlyamānaḥ sarvaprapañcavikalpaparisphuraṇakroḍīkāra-
vicakṣaṇaścāyaṃ vimarśaḥ pratipāditātmarūpāvabodhayogināṃ 
bhogamokṣalakṣaṇaṃ puruṣārthadvitayamapyupapādayati | tatra ca -

	tasyā bhoktryāḥ svatantrāyā bhogyaikīkāra eṣa yaḥ |
	sa eva bhogaḥ sā muktiḥ sa eva paramaṃ padam ||

iti śrīprabodhapañcadaśikāprakriyayā bhogo'pi mokṣātmā, mokṣo'pi 
bhogasvabhāva ityanayoranyonyamelakarūpā śrīmadanuttarasaṃvidadvaita-
siddhantaikasādhyā jīvanmuktirasya phalam | yaduktaṃ śrīmatadeve -

	yasyecchātaḥ satāṃ devi ! jīvanmuktiḥ kṣaṇād bhavet |
	tasyāhaṃ vacmi divyaughaṃ jīvanmuktipravṛddhaye || iti |

vṛkṣo'pi hi kutaścit sukṣetrādutpanno bahuśākhaśca pravardhamānaḥ 
puṣpaṃ phalaṃ ca prasūte | tatrāpi puṣpe satkāryavādabhaṅgyā 
phalasadbhāvaḥ phale'pyaja-



hatkāraṇarūpatayā puṣpānuṣaṅga iti puṣpamapi phalaṃ phalamapi 
puṣpamityeva vivecakajanaparyālocanaprakāra iti | asya ca 
drumatvamāgamaprasiddhyoktam | yathā ca śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	prārthanābhūmikātītavicitraphaladāyakaḥ |
	jayatyapūrvavṛttāntaḥ śivaḥ saṅkalpapādapaḥ || iti |

uktarūpaṃ caivaṃ? vimarśamavadhīrayan bāhyādvaitasiddhāntaḥ 
pāśavaśāstraśayyāmadhiśete | tathāhi -

	advaitavādanirvāho vedānteṣvapi vidyate |
	tatrātmā kaściduttirṇaḥ saccidānandalakṣaṇaḥ ||
	ekaḥ satyatayā sphūrjannirmalo nirahaṅkriyaḥ |
	anādinidhanaḥ śānto viśvotpattivipattibhūḥ ||
	saṃvinmayasvabhāvena bhāvābhāvadaśojjhitaḥ |
	svayamprakāśatāṃ bibhrannityamukto nirūpitaḥ ||
	samañjasamidaṃ sarva kintu tasyātmanaḥ prabhoḥ |
	avimraṣṭṛtvaparyāyamakartṛtvaṃ na yujyate ||
	sā cāsya kartṛtāśaktiḥ svabhāvānatilaṅghinī |
	jānāteśca karoteśca sādhāraṇyena vartate ||
	tatkriyāpi bhavejjñānaṃ jñāturdharmo yaducyate |
	evaṃ kartṛsvabhāvatvāt tasya jñānamapi kriyā ||
	aunmukhyamanayoricchā viśvametadvijṛmbhitam |
	tasmād vimarśo devasya svabhāvo'smābhirucyate ||
	bhavantyasyaiva paryāyāḥ śaktiraiśvaryamudyamaḥ |
	spandaḥ svatantratā sphūrtirūrmirojaḥ kaletyapi ||

ityalaṃ carvitacarbaṇopalālanacāpalena || 52 ||

	nanu jīvanmuktilakṣaṇaṃ māheśvarya khalvasya phalatayā 
pratyapādi | tacca na saṃbhavati, yato dehāvacchedavyatirikta eva kāle 
puṃsāmapavargotpattirityanyeṣāmāgraha ityāśaṅkyāha -



	kramiko bhavati na devastasya kathaṃ kālakalmaṣasparśaḥ |
	nityanirāvaraṇasyāpi ko jīvato mokṣapratyūhaḥ || 53 ||

	devo hi nāma deśakālādiviśvavilāsaṃ prati 
svasvabhāvasaṃvidarpaṇopakalpanayā 
dyotanādyanekārthopapādanavaiśāradyād viśvātmakaḥ parameśvara 
ityavadhāryate | ataścāyaṃ kramiko na bhavati | 
paurvāparyādivikalpakalpanāmayena krameṇa nākramyate | yato 
vaiśvātmyaikavijṛmbhāsaṃrambhottare bhagavati -

	calitvā yāsyate kutra sarvaṃ śivamayaṃ yataḥ |

iti sthityā kasya kasmāt pṛthaktvaṃ taddhetukaḥ kramaśceti 
yuktiparyālocanāyāṃ mūkībhāva evottaraṃ pratyarthino janasya | tadevam 
-

	akramatā me kramikaṃ jñātrādyaṃ sakramākramā tu citiḥ |

iti śrīvirūpākṣapañcāśikāprakriyayā kramavārtānabhijñasyāsya 
kālākhyena kalaṅkena samparko na kenacidapi prakāreṇa sambhavati, 
kālasya tattvavṛttyā kramatayaiva paryavasānāt | yaduktaṃ 
śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	kālaḥ sūryādisañcārastattatpuṣpādijanma vā |
	śītoṣṇe vātha tallakṣyaḥ krama eva sa tattvataḥ || iti |

kalmaṣaśabdenaitadunmīlyate yat kālo'pi pārameśvarī kācicchaktireva, 
yayāsau svayaṃ na vyavacchidyate; pratyuta viśvaṃ vyavacchinattityenaṃ 
prati na kālasya saṅkocakatvalakṣaṇaṃ kalmaṣatvam | taṃ ca kālaṃ 
svātmanā kroḍīkurvāṇo'yaṃ nitya ityāmnāyate | evamasya 
deśāvacchekatvād vyāpakatvamapyūhyam | itthaṃ svātmanaḥ 
sarvāvārakatvayuktyā sarvakālamanyā'nāvṛtasyāpi 
prāṇaśarīrādyupaśleṣarūpaṃ viśeṣaṃ doṣatayā'vasthāpyu yo 
mokṣasya svaparāmarśākhyacidānandalābharūpasya pratyūhaḥ 
pratikūlatarkarūpo vighnaḥ kaścidasambhāvitasvabhāvo'nyairudbhāvyate, 
sa kaḥ ? na kaścidapyupapadyeta, yadi yuktitattvamanviṣyeta | yadi tu jīvatvaṃ 
nāmātmano vikalpaviśeṣaḥ, nirvikalpānubhūtiśca 



mokṣaśabdārtha itītyanyonyāviruddhametaddvitayamityucyate, tarhi 
prāṇaśarīraviśleṣādapyevamiti samaḥ samādhiḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - 
cidānandalābhe dehādiṣu vedyamāneṣvapi 
cidaikātmyapratipattidārḍhyaṃ jīvanmuktiḥ iti 
śrīpratyabhijñāhṛdayamaryādayā 
bhogamokṣasāmarasyasākṣātkāralakṣaṇo jīvanmokṣaḥ sarvo mamāyaṃ 
vibhavaḥ iti svavaiśvātmyānusandhānasandhukṣitaiśvaryāṇāṃ 
pramātṝṇāṃ svabhāva eva, na tvāhāryaḥ kaścidatiśayaḥ | yaduktaṃ 
mayaiva saṃvidullāse -

	viśvaṃ mūrtirvaikharī nāmamālā yasyaiśvarya deśakālātilaṅghi 
|
	tadbhaktānāṃ svairacāraḥ saparyā svecchā śāstraṃ 
svasvabhāvaśca mokṣaḥ || iti |

tat kinnibandhano'yamenaṃ prati kālaniyamanirbandhākrośakleśaḥ | 
etadeva hyasya darśanāntarebhyo vaiśiṣṭyaṃ yad 
bhogamokṣadvitayānubhūtisāmarasyaṃ nāma | yathā śrīratnadeve -

	bhuktirvāpyatha muktiśca nānyatraikapadārthataḥ |
	bhuktimuktī ubhe devi ! viśeṣe parikīrtite || iti |

yathā ca śrīsiddhāmate -

	adhvaṣaṭkaṃ ca dīkṣā ca śivaśāstramiti smṛtam |
	dīkṣādhvā nirbhayo bhogaḥ śāstre bhairavasaṃjñake ||

iti || 53 ||

	nanu jīvanmuktirnāmātmano jananamaraṇādyanusyūtasya 
sarvāvasthānirviśeṣaṃ svānandotsavānubhūtiḥ | sā ca tasya 
sthairyābhāvapakṣe kathaṃ saṅgacchetetyāśaṅkya kṣaṇabhaṅgameva 
bhaṅktumāha -

	yat kimapi yena kenāpi rūpeṇa yatra kutrāpi kiṃ nāsti |
	tasmādātmā nityasthiraḥ kṣaṇabhaṅga evāsthiro bhavati || 54 ||

	iha khalu viśvaṃ prakāśyaṃ prakāśakaścātmetyabhihiteyaṃ 
maryādā | tatra prakāśopaśleṣamahimnaivāsya prakāśyatvam | sa 
copaśleṣaḥ -



	tenānekasya rūpasya śleṣa aikātmyameva saḥ |

iti sambandhasiddhisthityā prakāśyaprakāśakayoraikātmyaparyanta-
mujjṛmbhate | prakāśasya cārthadharmatvamātrāṅgīkāre 
grāhakasavyapekṣaṃ grāhyagrahaṇamityetad vyāhanyeta | tataśca sarva 
sarvasya prakāśeta, na kasyacidvetyutsannaḥ syād viśvavyavahāraḥ | 
ato'rthaḥ prakāśata ityasya prakāśakatāmasāvanubhavatītyartho bhavati | 
yaduktamajaḍapramātṛsiddhivimarśinyām - idaṃ mama jñānamiti 
nāñjasā jñānaprakāśaḥ | api tu jānāmyahamiti 
jñānasyāsmadarthaviśrāntataiva iti | tathātve cāsya 
prakāśāntargatatvamavaśyambhāvīti viśvasya 
tatprakāśakasyātmanaścaikātmyamavarjanīyam | evamaṅgikāre hi 
parameśvarasyānanyamukhaprekṣitvalakṣaṇaṃ 
svātantryamaucityamanubhavati, anyathā 
sajātīyeśvarāntarānapekṣatvalakṣaṇamiti tatra saṅkocakalaṅkasparśaḥ 
prasajyeta | tatraitat praṣṭavyaṃ yat kimapi bhāvarūpamabhāvarūpaṃ vā 
vastu yasmin kasmiṃścid vartamāne bhaviṣyadādau vā kāle tathā 
purovartinyanāsanne vā deśe yena kenāpi sabhāgātmanā 
savibhāgasvabhāvena vā vapuṣā vidyate vā na veti | neti tāvanna śakyate 
vaktum, śūnyatvaprasaṅgāt | prapañcasya śūnyatvapakṣaśca 
pūrvamevādhikṣiptaḥ | tad yatkiñcidvastu vidyata eveti vaktavyam | tadā ca 
tatsvabhāvatayā'yamātmā parisphuratīti kathamasthairyamasyocyate | ayaṃ 
bhāvaḥ - ātmano viśvākāratvāṅgīkāre viśvavartināṃ bhāvānāṃ 
kṣaṇabhaṅgābhyupagame'pi yo'yaṃ kṣaṇabhaṅgo nāma kaścidarthaḥ, 
tasya tāvadanapahnava eveti tāvanmātreṇāpi tanmayasyātmanaḥ 
sthairyamavyākulam, kimuta viśvasyaiva sthairye samarthyamāna iti | kiñca, 
anubhavatadvikalpasmaraṇalakṣaṇānekasaṃvidanusandhānasādhyā hi 
lokayātrā | stambhaṃ paśya kumbhamānaya ityādau 
tattacchabdārthasaṃketānubhūtyanusmaraṇāderavaśyambhāvāt | 
yaduktamapohavāde -

	śabdasya vyavahārārthamarthacintā'vatāryate |
	tadarthameva saṃketastadvyutpattirapīṣyate || iti |



saṃvidāṃ ca tāsāmanyonyānusandhānasāmarthya na sambhavati, 
sarvāsāṃ svayaṃprakāśatvāt | tathābhāve cānyaparāmarśānaucityāt | 
tadanyonyavārtānabhijñānāmāsāmanusandhānakṣamaḥ kaścit 
sthairyaśālī vidyata ityanivāryeyaṃ maryādā |

	nityaścātmā'vagantavyaḥ kālakramavilaṅghanāt |
	sarvalokaprasiddheyaṃ tatra yuktirudīryate ||
	prasavānantaraṃ bālo jananyāḥ stanamāpiban |
	stanyādarthakriyāṃ svasya smaratyeveti kalpyate ||
	smṛtiścānubhavāyattā sa ca nātrāsti janmani |
	ataḥ prācīnayā bhāvyamanubhūtyā kayācana ||
	sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca tayorvidvadbhiriṣyate |
	ata ātmā sadā sthairyānnityo'sāviti budhyatām ||

taduktaṃ mayaiva śrīparāstotre -

	māturgarbhasamudgakādavatarannurvīmimāmarbhaka-
	statpūrva stanacūcukapraṇayinā mugdhena vaktreṇa te |
	ācaṣṭe tadanaśvaraṃ padamahaṃkurvan pare ! kiṃ punaḥ 
	krīḍākañculikā kalevaramayī kutrāpi na tyajyate || iti |

ātmā nityaḥ sthira iti tasya sārvakālikasthairyopanyāsena 
stambhādīnāmapi yāvatpradhvaṃsaṃ sthairyamevetyāsūtryate |

	tathāhi kṣaṇato bhaṅge dṛśyamānasya vastunaḥ |
	atraivāsya kṣaṇe sattvaṃ nīlādernopari kṣaṇe ||
	iti gṛhṇāti yā saṃvit tasyāstatra kṣaṇadvaye |
	avasthānābhyanujñāyāṃ kṣaṇabhaṅgaḥ praṇaśyati ||
	sāpi saṃvit kṣaṇād bhagnetyuktau tattatpṛthak kṣaṇāḥ |
	parasparānabhijñāstā bhinnakālatayā dhiyaḥ ||
	jānīyuḥ kathamanyeṣāṃ kṣaṇamātravyavasthitim |
	lokayātrāpravṛttirvā na kṣīyeta kathaṃ nṛṇām ||



	santatistatkriyāṃ kuryāditi tairyadudīryate |
	asantativadeva syādadṛḍhā santatiryadi ||
	dārḍhye tasyā jagatsthairyaṃ paryāyeṇānumanyate |
	śivadṛṣṭāvidaṃ prāha śrīsomānandadeśikaḥ ||
	itaścāsti jagatyaikyamityādau granthavistare |

ataścoktayuktyā kṣaṇabhaṅga evāsthiro nirvoḍhumaśakyaḥ | tadasthairye 
viśvasya sthairyamarthataḥ siddham | etena parameśvarasya 
jñānasmṛtyapohanaśaktitritayavattvena viśvavyavahartṛtvaṃ vyākhyātam 
| yaduktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	syādekaścidvapurjñānasmṛtyapohanaśaktimān iti |

yaccoktaṃ śrībhagavadgītāsu - mattaḥ smṛtirjñānamapohanaṃ ca iti | tatra 
ca -

	svāntaḥ kroḍīkṛtāśeṣavedyavargo maheśvaraḥ |
	lokayātrāmupaskartu tattadvaicitryaśālinīm ||
	niyatāneva nirbhidya kāṃścidarthān nijecchayā |
	unmajjayati yat svasmād dṛkśaktiḥ sā nigadyate ||
	bahiraunmukhyayoge'pi svātmacidrūpamujjvalam |
	svacchāyāmajahat svacchāṃ bhavejjñānaṃ navaṃ navam ||
	asmin navanavollāse vidyutprāye sphuratyapi |
	yathābhilāṣaṃ lokena vyavahartu na pāryate ||
	tatsaṃvido bahirmukhyā yadantarmukhatāspadam |
	citsvarūpamavasthāsu kālabhede mahatyapi ||
	tasya bāhyaparāmarśaprāgalbhyaṃ smṛtirucyate |
	athāpi tannavābhāsaṃ smṛtaṃ vā vastu vastutaḥ ||
	viśvamayyā svacicchaktyā tādātmyaṃ na parityajet |
	ato bodhaḥ smṛtiśca dau varyau syātāṃ maheśituḥ ||



	tadvedyaṃ vittitastasyā vittirvittyantarādapi |
	vedyaṃ ca vedyādanyasmād yathā vicchedamaśnuyāt ||
	tathā bhagavataḥ kācit svātantryaśrīrapohanam |
	ityākhyātā sphuṭaṃ jñānasmṛtyapohanaśaktayaḥ ||

	tisṛbhiḥ śaktibhirābhiḥ śivaḥ kuvindo bhavan kulālo vā |
	anubhavati sukhaṃ smarati ca bahu ca vikalpayati viśvavaicitryam ||

iti || 54 ||

	nanvastu stharyavata evātmano jīvanmuktirūpaḥ puruṣārthaḥ | tasya 
punarānandasvabhāvatvaṃ vipratipannam | pāṣāṇaprāyatāyā 
muktyavasthāyāṃ kaiścidaṅgīkṛtatvādityāśaṅkyāha -

	nanvātmanaḥ priyārthaṃ sarvasya priyatvaṃ bhaṇati śrutiḥ |
	tasmādānandasvabhāva ātmā mukto'mukto vā || 55 ||

	asti khalu ātmanastu kāmāya sarvaṃ priyaṃ bhavati iti śrutiḥ, 
pārameśvaraparāmarśarūpatayā prakṛtyaiva pramāṇabhūtatvāt ānando 
brahmeti vyajānāt ityādivailakṣaṇyena yuktiviśeṣāvabodhakatvācca 
bādhavidhuramevārthamācaṣṭe | yadutātmanaḥ svasya yat 
priyamicchollāsarūpā prītistāmeva prayojanīkṛtya 
vyavahriyamāṇamakhilamapatyamitrādikaṃ vastu prīṇanatayā'nubhūyata 
iti | nanviti parābhyupagamopapādanārthaḥ | tasmāddhetoḥ 
svahṛdayasphuraṇalakṣaṇasyātmanaḥ -

	pūrṇatvādahamityantarjñānamānanda ucyate |

iti sthityā pūrṇāhantā'nusandhānātmakasvātmaviśrāntisatattvo ya 
ānandaḥ, sa evāsādhāraṇaṃ rūpam | tādṛksvabhāvatve ca tasya 
muktatvamamuktatvamityavasthādvaye'pi na kiñcit tāratamyam | yadi ca 
tasyātmano nānandaḥ syāt svabhāvaḥ, tadapatyakalatrādayaḥ 
śamadamādayo vā bhāvāstasya 



prīṇanā syuḥ | nahyapatyādayaḥ sahasramapi caitanyaśūnyaṃ 
kiñcidākraṣṭuṃ pragalbhante | caitanyasya ca svaviśrāntatvamevānanda 
ityuktam | yadā punarasya svaviśrāntiparāmarśa pratyaudāsīnyam, 
tadānīṃ dāhacchedādiduḥkhānubhūtivyapadeśaḥ | vāstavyā tu 
dṛṣṭyā tādṛkparāmarśaśūnyatve'pi na kadācidapyasya 
svaviśrāntyabhāva iti sarvadā sukhānubhūtivyatirekeṇa na kiñcid 
duḥkhamityālokyate nanvānandātmakatve kathaṃ krodhādau puṃsāṃ 
śastraprahārādikleśānubhūtyaunmukhyamiti cet ! na, 
kṣetrakalatrādyanurāgāveśavaivaśyādevamudyoga iti 
kaścidānandādhyavasāya eva teṣāmetādṛgvyavahāropakrame 
nibandhanamiti duḥkhānāmapi praharaṇādyavasthāvat 
saukhyakakṣyānupraveśāt siddhamasyānandasvabhāvatvam | yaduktaṃ 
śrīmatstotrāvalyām - duḥkhānyapi sukhāyante iti | itthaṃ cātmanaḥ 
svasya yaḥ kāma icchautsukyāśāspṛhādiparyāyo bhāvaḥ svabhāvatvena 
vartate, tamevonmīlayitumapatyāṅganāprabhṛtirakhilo vedyaprapañcaḥ 
prīṇano bhavatīti śrutyartho vyākhyātaḥ | tatra turavadhāraṇe | kāmāyeti 
tumarthācca bhāvavacanāt iti caturthī | evam - ātmārthe pṛthivīṃ tyajet 
ityādau vyatireko'pyūhyaḥ | priyārthamiti prītyarthasya 
priyaśabdenopanyāsādātmasvabhāvabhūtaḥ prītirūpa eva 
svakartṛkaraṇādivaicitryeṇa bahuprakāro'nubhūyate, na 
punaretadvyatiriktaḥ kaścit prīṇanādiḥ padārtha ityāsūtryate - 
mukto'mukto veti | na khalu naiyāyikādimaryādayā muktyavasthāyāṃ 
pāṣāṇaprāyatvādātmano nirānandatvam, yato muktyabhāve'pi 
tasyaivaṃsvabhāvatvamaparihāryaṃ kimuta tadanubhava iti 
dyotanārthamevamanādareṇopanyāsaḥ | kiñca, 
svasyānandasvarūpatvāvaśyambhāve bandhamokṣādivikalpavikṣobho'tra 
na kaścidapyupapadyate, ānandasya 
cittvasattvānubhūtisāmarasyātmakatvāt | yadāhuḥ -

	yatra citsattayorvyāptistatrānando virājate |
	yatrānando bhavedbhāve tatra citsattayoḥ sthitiḥ || iti |

enamevānandamunmīlayitumasmadāmnāyeṣu 
prathamadvitīyādidravyasvīkāranirbandho nibadhyata ityupaniṣat || 55 ||



	athaivamupapāditamātmasvarūpasphurattāparāmarśaṃ 
pratyatispaṣṭānāṇavādīnupāyānupadekṣyan prathamaṃ trīnapyekayaiva 
gāthayodghāṭayati -

	yadi nijahṛdayollāsaṃ nirṇetuṃ nityaniṣkalamicchā |
	madhyatuṭistruṭitavyā'straṃ yatoḥ somasūryayoḥ || 56 ||

	nijaṃ yat sākṣātkārollekhayogyaṃ hṛdayaṃ 
vyākhyātasvabhāvaṃ tasyollāsaścetyacetayitṛtvādivaicitryeṇa 
sphuraṇam, sa prakṛtyā kālavibhāgavyudāsena niṣkalo 
heyopādeyatādyaśeṣavikalpakalpanākalaṅkaśūnyo bhavati | 
tamevaṃvidhamatyantaspaṣṭatayā 
pratyakṣīkāracamatkārākāraniścayāspadaṃ kartuṃ yadīcchā 
yuṣmākamupasannānām

	śraddhāmayo'yaṃ puruṣo yo yacchraddhaḥ sa eva saḥ |

iti śrībhagavadgītāsthityā kācid vāñchā vijṛmbbhate cet, 
tarhyayamatropāya iti vakṣyamāṇasarvārthasādhāraṇo'yamanvaya-
prakāraḥ | tatra yāvetau somasūryau vedyavargānuprāṇanatvādakula-
mārtāṇḍāvibhinnaveditṛsvabhāvatvācca sakārahakārātmānau 
varṇaviśeṣau, tayorastraṃ visarjanīyam, āsthāṃ 
svahṛdayasampuṭīkāralakṣaṇamanusvāraṃ ca yatoraśnuvānayoryā 
madhyasthā tuṭiḥ vibhajyāvasthānakṣaṇalakṣaṇaḥ kālakhaṇḍaḥ | sa 
uttruṭitavyaḥ | māṇikyamālinyādivaduddhartavyaḥ | tadānīṃ haṃsa iti 
vimarśa utpadyeteti yāvat | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - yadyapi mātṛkāpāṭhādau 
hamiti sa iti cānyavarṇasādhāraṇyenānayorgrahaṇamasti, tathāpi tad 
dvayaṃ svahṛdayavartyanuttarāvinābhūtamahaṃ sa iti 
svātmapratyabhijñānopāyatvena haṃ sa iti 
saṃśleṣavaśānmahāmantrātmanā vimraṣṭavyamiti vaiparītyenoddhāro 
mantrasya -

	bahirvyavaharan lokyān sthagayannūhagocarān |
	caran kapaṭamārgeṇa vāmaṃ nayamivonnayet ||



iti śrīlaghubṛṃhaṇīmaryādayā gopanīyatādyotanārthaṃ so'hamiti 
mantrāntarapratyāyanārthaṃ ca | ayaṃ ca 
mantrātmakavarṇaviśeṣaparāmarśarūpatvādāṇavaḥ kaścidupāyaḥ | 
yaduktaṃ śrīmālinīvijaye -

	uccārakaraṇadhyānavarṇasthānaprakalpanaiḥ |
	yo bhavet sa samāveśaḥ samyagāṇava ucyate || iti |

evamuccārādāvapyūhyam | tatra prāṇavyāpārarūpa uccāro yathā -

	kumbhitā recitā vāpi pūritā vā yadā bhavet |
	tadante śāntanāmāsau śāntyā śāntaḥ prakāśate || iti |

kāyasaṃsthāpanātmakaṃ karaṇaṃ yathā -

	amba ! kecidamṛtāmbucinmayīlambikāṅkuraśikhāvalambinīm |
	tālumūlavalayīkṛtāgrayā jihvayā kavalayanti te kalām || iti |

cittollekhasvabhāvaṃ dhyānaṃ yathā -

	anukalamamṛtārdrādālavālāntarālā -
		llalitamudayamānā pallavāpāṭalaśrīḥ |
	aśithilamavalambya sthāṇumudbhāsabhānā
		phalati kamapi bhāvaṃ komalā kāpi vallī || iti |

mulādhārādyanusandhānākāraṃ sthānakalpanaṃ yathā -

	yonau kanakapuñjābhaṃ hṛdi vidyucchaṭojjvalam |
	ājñāyāṃ candrasaṅkāśaṃ mahastava maheśvari ! || iti |

eṣu ca kiñcidanyonyasāṅkarye'pi tattatprādhānyamālocanīyamityalaṃ 
prapañcena | kiñca, soma eṣaṇīyajñeyakāryasvabhāvaḥ prameyollāsaḥ | 
sūrya icchājñānakriyātmakaṃ pramāṇasphuraṇam | anayorarthaṃ svāṃ 
svāmarthakriyāṃ yatoḥ prāpnuvatoḥ satoriti bhāvalakṣaṇe saptamī | tathā 
bhavatoścānayormama svātmanaḥ pramātṛbhūtasya truṭiḥ 
sandehalakṣaṇo doṣa uttruṭitavyaḥ svātmānaṃ pratyunmiṣan saṃśayaḥ 
saṃchedya ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - eṣaṇīyatādyanuprāṇana-
sthūlo'yaṃ prapañcollāsa icchādiśaktitrayapravṛttiṃ vinā na kvacidapi 
sampadyata ityeṣitṛtvādirūpasyātmanaḥ 



spaṣṭāparokṣīkāraviparyayātmānaṃ saṃśayaśaṅkātaṅkaṃ tiraskaroti 
| tata uktarūpasvahṛdayaparāmarśalābha iti | śāktaścāyamupāyaḥ, 
uccārādivyatirekeṇa svasaṃvidvikalpamātrākāratvāt | yaduktaṃ tatraiva -

	uccārarahitaṃ vastu cetasaiva vicintayan |
	yaṃ samāveśamāpnoti śāktaḥ so'trābhidhīyate || iti |

atha ca somo'pānaḥ, sūryaḥ prāṇa iti prasiddhaṃ tayordvādaśāntād 
hṛdayāntaṃ hṛdayād dvādaśāntāntaṃ ca | astamiti bhāve niṣṭhā | 
asanaṃ kṣepalakṣaṇāṃ sphurattāṃ yatoḥ svata eva prāpnuvatoryā 
madhyasthā bāhyāntarbhāvopalakṣyamāṇā tuṭiḥ, tādṛk 
parāmarśakriyātmā camatkāralakṣaṇaḥ sa uttruṭitavyo 
hṛdayaṅgamībhāvaparyantamātmanā nirṇetavya iti yāvat | ayamāśayaḥ - 
aśeṣaśarīrasādhāraṇyena nityoditasvabhāvayoḥ svātmaparispanda-
paramārthayoḥ prāṇāpānayoryugapadubhayavisargāraṇirūpatā-
parāmarśaḥ svataḥ siddho'pi svahṛdayāhlādacamatkārasthairyot-
pādanārthamātmanā'nusandheyaḥ, yasyā'nanusandhāne'pyevaṃ-
rūpatāyā na kācit kṣatiriti | ayaṃ punarupāyaḥ sarvavikalpavikṣobha-
vyudāsena svasvabhāvamātropapādanapravṛttatayā śāmbhava 
ityavagantavyaḥ | yaccoktam -

	akiñciccintakasyaiva guruṇā pratibodhitaḥ |
	utpadyate ya āveśaḥ śāmbhavo'sāvudāhṛtaḥ || iti |

atha punaḥ -

	evamevaṃ durniśāyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣāgame ciram |
	taimiraṃ bhāvayed rūpaṃ bhairavaṃ rūpameṣyati ||

iti śrīvijñānabhaṭṭārakanyāyādastamadriviśeṣaṃ vināśaṃ vā 
yugapadaśnuvānayoścandrārkayormadhyavartī kālaleśaviśeṣo buddhyā 
niṣkraṣṭavya iti tu vyākhyānamatyantaspaṣṭamityāgraheṇa nonmīlitam | 
atra cāstramāsthāmarthamastamiti madhyaṃ mameti tuṭistruṭiriti ca 
prākṛtabhāṣāprābalyāt tantreṇoktam | uktarūpasya cāsyopāyatrayasya 
sūkṣmekṣikayā saṅkaraḥ parasparamaparihāryatayā vartate | kevalaṃ 
prādhānyamātreṇa pṛthagvyapadeśa ityupadeṣṭum -



	saṃvittiphalabhedo'tra na prakalpyo manīṣibhiḥ |

iti nītyā sarvopāyānāṃ phalaṃ prati na kiñcid 
vaiṣamyamityupapādayituṃ caikahelayoktiḥ | yadyupaśleṣeṇoktistu 
vineyajanāvarjanatātparyeṇa | tatra ca -

	abhedopāyamatroktaṃ śāmbhavaṃ śāktamucyate |
	bhedābhedātmakopāyaṃ bhedopāyaṃ tadāṇavam ||
	prāṇādibhūmikairādyāḥ sidhyantyuccāraṇādibhiḥ |
	vikalpairmadhyamāḥ śuddhairanyāḥ praśamitaistu taiḥ ||

iti śrītantrālokopāyadviśatyādinītyā prāṇaspandoccāraṇādya-
śuddhavikalpa āṇavaḥ | cittamātranirvartyaśuddhavikalpātmā śāktaḥ | 
vikalpasarvavilayasvabhāvaḥ śāmbhava iti trividhe'pyupāyatattve 
sthūlasūkṣmāditāratamyayogena śaktyullekhasya sarvatrāpyanusyūtiḥ | 
śākte tu tasyā aulbaṇyamātrādutkarṣa ityanusandheyam | yaduktam -

	sā śaktirāṇavaiḥ śāktaiḥ śāmbhavaiśca tridhoditaiḥ |
	upāyaiḥ śivamābhāsya svasṛṣṭairmocayatyamūn ||

iti || 56 ||

	evaṃ yaugapadyādupāyatrayaṃ pratipādya tadeva vineyajana-
hṛdayādhiropaṇahetoḥ pṛthak prapañcayiṣyannādāvāṇavamālocayati -

	sthūlatareṣvapi prekṣadhvaṃ bhūteṣu khasya niṣkalāvasthām |
	ṣaṭtriṃśikā'tilaṅghī kīdṛśo bhavatu somanāthaḥ saḥ || 57 ||

	vyākhyātaveṣāṇāṃ tattvānāṃ madhye paramaśivabhaṭṭārakaḥ 
sarvopāyapratipādya iti paratvenāvatiṣṭhate | anyeṣu ca śivādīni kānicit 
sūkṣmāṇi, prakṛtipuruṣaprabhṛtīni sthūlānīti kalpanāyāṃ 
pṛthivyādīnāṃ sthūlataratvamapi pratipannam | yatkāryabhūtāḥ 
stambhakumbhādayaḥ sthūlatamā iti vyapadiśyante | teṣu cātisthūleṣvapi 
pṛthivyādeḥ sakāśāt khasyākāśasya niṣkalāṃ pari-



cchinnatvalakṣaṇakalāvilāsaśūnyāṃ svabhāvabhūtāmavasthā-
mālocayadhvam, yayā tasya khecaryādyadhikaraṇatvaprasiddhiḥ | 
prekṣadhvamiti vipratipannāvipratipannabhedavaidhuryeṇa sarve'pi 
svapāṇḍityānukūlaṃ parīkṣadhvamiti yāvat | itthamatyantasthūla-
syāpyasya niṣkalatve'bhyupagamyamāne ṣaṭtriṃśadapi tattvānyatikrāman 
parameśvaraḥ kīdṛśo bhavatu niṣkalatvotkarṣastasya kīdṛk kiyāniti 
cāvadhāryatām, yadi daṇḍāpūpanyāyo vyutpanna iti | ayaṃ cāṇaveṣu 
dhyānarūpa upāyaḥ | tataśca sthūlasthūlavyudāsena 
sūkṣmasūkṣmaprekṣāyāmatyantasūkṣmasvātmavimarśāmṛtāsvāda-
siddhiriti śākto'pyatrāsūcyate | yaccoktamupāyaviṃśatyām -

	sthūlaṃ sthūlaṃ parityajya sūkṣmaṃ sūkṣmaṃ samāśrayet |
	paścāt sūkṣmamapi tyaktvā kevalaṃ cinmayo bhavet || iti |

yaccoktaṃ śrīvijñānabhaṭṭārakeṇa -

	bhuvanādhvādirūpeṇa cintayet kramaśo'khilam |
	sthūlasūkṣmaparasthityā yāvadante manolayaḥ || iti |

upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat | tena tatra tatra tattve niṣkalatvālocanāyāṃ 
sarvākāraniṣkalaṃ pārameśvaraṃ svarūpamasaṃśayamāvirbhavatītya-
nusandheyam | yaduktaṃ mayaiva śrīparāstotre -

	pṛthvī pūrvamito vanaṃ taruratastatrāpi śākhā tataḥ 
	patraṃ tatra ca puṣpamatra ca phalaṃ mādhuryamasmāditi |
	ekasmādapi tāratamyapadavīmutkarṣaṇīṃ paśyato
	viśvasmādapi kāpi sidhyati pare ! tvāmeva tāṃ brūmahe || iti |

evamabhipretya khalvīśvarasiddhivimarśinyāmuktam - gṛhapatiriva 
kuṭumbavarge, narapatiriva gṛhapativarge, cakravartīva rājavarge, lokapāla iva 
cakravartivarge, brahmādiriva lokapālavarge, brahmādivarge'pyadhiṣṭhātā 
iti | evañca -

	sūkṣmasyaiva vikāsaḥ sthaulyaṃ sthūlasya mukulanaṃ saukṣmyam |



iti sthityā veditṛsvabhāvatvena sūkṣmābhimatasya parameśvarasya 
sphuraṇaprakāro'yaṃ sarvo'pi sthūlo vedyollāsaḥ | tasya ca 
sampratīkārayuktyā niṣkṛṣṭaṃ vapuḥ sa parameśvara ityanusandhānena 
bhāvyamiti śāmbhavo'pyudbhāvyate | yaduktaṃ śrīsvacchande -

	tadeva bhavati sthūlaṃ sthūlopādhivaśāt priye |
	sthūlasūkṣmaprabhedena tadekaṃ saṃvyavasthitam || iti |

āgame ca -

	jalaṃ himaṃ ca yo veda guruvaktrāgamāt priye |
	nāstyeva tasya kartavyaṃ tasyāpaścimajanmanaḥ || iti |

etena -

	ātmānamata evāyaṃ jñeyīkuryāt pṛthaksthitiḥ |
	jñeyaṃ na tu tadaunmukhyāt khaṇḍyetāsya svatantratā ||

ityādi vyākhyātam | bhavatviti saṃpraśne loṭ | prekṣadhvamityatisargādau | 
somanātha iti | umā nāmecchāśaktiḥ icchāśaktirupā kumārī ityuktatvāt 
| tayā saha vartate bahu syāṃ prajāyeya ityāmnāyasthityā bahiḥ - 
prathanaucityamanubhavatīti somaśabdenāsya sthaulyam,

	ānantaryaṃ yathetyāhustadasyāsmādayaṃ ca na |

iti śrīpādukodayaprakriyayā sarvavikalpollaṅghanena 
niṣkalatvotkarṣakāṣṭhāprāptirūpaṃ saukṣmyaṃ ca tasya 
nāthapadenopapādyate | saṃbhūya ca tasya sthūlasūkṣmataucityena 
viśvataduttīrṇatvobhayarūpasaṃpatsaubhāgyabhājanatvaṃ pratāyate | sa 
iti sthūlasūkṣmatvādyanyonyaviruddhasāmarasyonmeṣāspadatayā 
tattadauttarāmnāyodghoṣitatvena parāmṛśyamāna iti yāvat | 
etaccopāyatrayaṃ pūrvatra gāthāyāmabhidhayaiva vyāpāreṇopapāditam 
|

	atra tvabhidhayāṇavo vyañjanātmanānyad dvitayamiti viśeṣaḥ 
vyañjanaṃ ca nāma śabdasya kaścidabhidhātātparyalakṣaṇātmaka-
prasiddhaprasthānātikrānto vyāpāro'sti | yadvadātmanaḥ 
śayanāsanabhojanādivyatiriktaḥ svātantyanāmā 



lokottaraḥ kaścit svabhāvaḥ | yaduktaṃ tattvālokakṛtā kāvyāloke -

	pratīyamānaṃ punaranyadeva vastvasti vāṇīṣu mahākavīnām |
	yat tatprasiddhāvayavātiriktamābhāti lāvaṇyamivāṅganāsu || iti |

etatprasādhanavistaraprayāsastvaprastuta ityalam || 57 ||

	atha śāktamabhivyanakti -

	ye kulakumbhasudhāsavapānamahotsavasukhe pravartante |
	te khalu vikalpāṅkurān rasikā upadaṃṣṭuṃ pragalbhante || 58 ||

	ye deśikakaṭākṣapātapūtacetaso mahāpuruṣāḥ kulasya 
ṣaḍadhvasphārātmano vedyollāsasya yaḥ kumbhaḥ 
svānandasvabhāvatayā kumbhayatyavyākulamavasthāpayatīti vyutpattyā 
pratiṣṭhāheturādhāraviśeṣaḥ, tatratyo yo laukikālaukikamādhurya-
sāmarasyāspadatvādamṛtaśabdavācya āsavo bhairavīyaṃ dravyam, tasya 
yat pānamātmaiśvaryapradhānatāparāmarśapūrvako 
nirviśaṅkasvīkāraḥ, sa eva mahān makharūpo'dhavarātmā cotsavaḥ 
svāhlādasākṣātkārasampatsaubhāgyam tasmin viṣaye pravartante 
vedyavedakabhāvādivikalpavigalanalakṣaṇaprakarṣapūrvakaṃ vyāpriyante, 
khalurhetau, te tata eva hetoḥ pāśavaśaivādivicitrakalpanāmayān -

	apītvā'pi bhavadbhaktisudhāmanavalokya ca |
	tvāmīśa ! tvatsamācāramātrāt sidhyanti jantavaḥ ||

iti śrīmatstotrāvalīsthityā saṃskāraśeṣatāmātrānuprāṇanān 
bhedaprathāvilāsān upadaṃṣṭumatyantasāmīpyarūpasvātmatādātmyā-
pādanayuktyā punarutpattiśūnyataucityena carvayituṃ pragalbhante 
prakṛṣṭaṃ sthairyamanubhavanti | yato'mī rasikāḥ raso vai saḥ 
ityādiśrutyupapāditaṃ rasaṃ svakīyatayā'nubhavanti | ayameva hi 
mukhyayā vṛttyā rasa ityucyate | yadupacāreṇa 
mādhuryaprītiśṛṅgārādayo'pyevaṃ vyapadiśyante | yattātparyeṇa ca sa 
vā eṣa puruṣo'nnarasamayaḥ 



ityupaniṣadunmiṣati | anyathā rasaśabdaprayogasya nairarthakyaprasaṅgāt | 
sudhetyāsava iti ca śabdadvayopādānena pāśavaṃ śaivamiti 
svabhāvabhedaḥ, vidhiniṣedha iti codanāvaicitryam, laukikamalaukikamiti 
vyavahāratāratamyam, gopyamagopyamiti bhāvavibhāgaḥ, śraddhā 
jugupsetyavasthāvivekaḥ, āmuṣmikamaihikamiti phalaviśeṣaḥ, devā asurā 
iti devatāvyavasthā ceti lokayātrānubandhī sarvo'pi virodha 
etadāsvādanadhanyānāṃ na kiñcidapi śaṅkātaṅkamaṅkurayitu-
malamityunmīlyate | yato brāhmaṇacāṇḍālādivyavasthāparityāgena -

	ghṛṇā śaṅkā bhayaṃ lajjā jugupsā ceti pañcamī |
	kulaṃ jātiśca śīlaṃ cetyaṣṭau pāśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||

iti sthityā varṇāśramādinaiyatyādinā parisphuran sarvo'pi pāśaprarohaḥ 
svahṛdayasaṃvādasaundaryottaraṃ svata evāpahīyate | atra ca ye 
sautrāmaṇyādidṛṣṭāntadṛṣṭyā prāmāṇyaṃ prasādhayanti, nūnaṃ te 
hiṃsāvalokitakena vigalitamapi tajjihmaṃ brāhmaṇyamālokayanti | 
asmākaṃ punarullaṅghitāśeṣasaṃśayaśailaśṛṅgāṇām -

	dhanyāḥ keciddhanamiva vibhorāgamairgopyamānāṃ
	vyākurvāṇāṃ jagadaparathā vīravikrāntividyām |
	mādhuryasya prathamapadavīṃ maṅgalānāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ
	māntrīṃ śaktiṃ manasi mahatī devatetyādriyante ||

iti saṃvidullāsanyāyādekaśaraṇānāṃ na kadācidatrāprāmāṇya-
śaṅkā prāmāṇyaprasādhanāpekṣā vetyalamārurukṣujanahṛdaya-
parīkṣaṇavyākṣepeṇa | uktarūpamalaukikamarthamabhipretya hi 
śivadharmādiṣu -

āgamatve'pi sāmānye kaḥ pradveṣaḥ śivāgame |
	anāyāsena yatroktā muktirekena janmanā ||

ityādyupapādyate | prastāvaścāyaṃ agghaṃ vejjavilāso, puṇṇāhantāṇa 
muhe ityatra vistīrya paryālocitaḥ | ayaṃ copāyaḥ svīkriyamāṇasyāsya 
dravyaviśeṣasya 



viśvavedyavilāsātmakatayā'vasthāpitasya śaktayo'sya jagat sarvam iti 
sthityā sarvaśaktisāmarasyarūpatayā nirṇītatvād 
anyasamayācārādanuttarācāraṃ prati tadāsvādanasya 
sphurattāparaparyāyaśaktisvabhāvatayā'nubhūyamānatvācca śākta 
ityuktaḥ | paryantadṛṣṭyā 
punarupapāditadravyasvīkārasādhyasyāhlādotkarṣānubhūticamatkārasya 
svasvabhāvātmakatayā'nubhūyamānatvācchāmbhava evetyavagantavyam 
| yato'yaṃ pāmarapravṛttiḥ pramāṇam iti rājabhairavasūtrasthityā 
puṃsāṃ svataḥpravṛttiviṣayatayopalabhyate | yataśca śrīvijñānodyote -

	jagdhipānarasollāsarasānandavijṛmbhaṇāt |
	bhāvayed bharitāvasthāṃ mahānandamayo bhavet ||

ityatra śrīvijñānabhaṭṭārakāṃśe eṣā śāmbhavī bhūtiriti 
śrīmatkṣemarājena vyākhyātam | upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat | tena 
viṣayapañcakāsvādasaukhye sarvatrāpyayameva nyāyaḥ | yathā 
viṣayapañcikāyām -

	madhurasarasavīṇāveṇugītādivādya-
		śravaṇajanitaharṣaḥ śabdamātraikaśeṣaḥ |
	tadanu bhavavirāmaprasphuradbodhamūrti-
		rbhavavibhavavimukto muktimāpnoti samyak ||

ityādi | yathā ca madīye saṃvidullāse -

	puṣpopahāraghanacandanavaṃśatāla-
		nṛttaprayogamadhumugdhavadhūpradhānāḥ |
	bhāvāḥ sahasramapi jāgratu tattadantaḥ-
		prahlādinī vijayate paracitkalaikā || iti |

teṣu ca śāktaśāmbhavatvādikaṃ tattatprakaraṇādinā'vagantavyam | 
yathā ca śrīvijñānodyote ekameva 
priyajanopabhogalakṣaṇamarthamavalambya -

	śaktisaṅgamasaṃkṣubdhaśaktyāveśāvasānikam |
	yat sukhaṃ brahmatattvasya tat sukhaṃ svākyamucyate ||



ityatrāṇavatvam,

	lehanāmanthanākoṭaiḥ strīsukhasya bharāt smṛteḥ |
	śaktyabhāve'pi deveśi ! bhavedānandasaṃplavaḥ ||

ityatra śāktatvam -

	ānande mahati prāpte dṛṣṭe vā bāndhave cirāt |
	ānandamudgataṃ dhyātvā tallakṣyastanmayo bhavet ||

ityatra śāmbhavatvaṃ ca paryālocitamiti || 58 ||

	atha śāmbhavamupadiśati -

	hanta mukhaṃ pratibimbatu pratibimbayatu tathā tadapi darpaṇaḥ |
	darpaṇaḥ punaryasmin pratibimbati so'pi jñātavyaḥ || 59 ||

	loke hi darpaṇādau svacche vastunyābhimukhyenollasannānanādiḥ 
padārthaḥ pratiphalanayuktyā parisphuratītyatiprasiddho'yamarthaḥ | tatra 
darpaṇavadanādīnāṃ vāstavaṃ vapuratraivānantaramupapādayiṣyate | 
sthūlayā tu dṛṣṭyā mukhatayā'bhimato bhāvaḥ 
pratibimbanakriyāmanubhavati | ādarśātmakaśca tadādhāratayā 
tatprayojako bhavatītyastu nāmaitat | yaḥ punarayamādarśaḥ 
svacchatāvaśādānanādipratibimbasthalatayā'nubhūyate, sa eva 
yasminnatyantasvacche svātmani tādṛgrūpatayā pratibimbati so'pi 
jñātavyaḥ, yanmayo'yamaśeṣapratibimbanaprāgalbhyonmeṣaḥ | 
jñātavya iti jñānārhaḥ śakyajñāno vā'vaśyaṃ jñeya iti vā 
praiṣātisargādirvā vineyajanābhimukhīkārāya bahuprakāraḥ 
kṛtyapratyayārtho'nusandheyaḥ | pratibimbatvityādau 
kāmacārakaraṇātmanyāmantraṇe loṭ | hantetyanena 
bimbapratibimbasvabhāvāvabodhavandhyānanyasaiddhāntikān 
pratyanukampā dyotyate | tena ca sarvo'yamādarśānanādyupalakṣitaḥ 
stambhakumbhādirvedyavistāro mālinyakakṣyānupraveśitānyapadārtha-
vaimalye svacchatvotkarṣaśālini svasvabhāvāvibhinnaparameśvarātmake 



mahati mukuramaṇḍale tanmayatvaparyāyayā pratibimbanayuktyā 
parisphuratīti tātparyārthaḥ | upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat | tena -

	śabdo nabhasi cānandasparśadhāmani sundaraḥ |
	sparśo'nyo'pi dṛḍhāghātaśūlaśītādikodbhavaḥ ||
	parasthaḥ pratibimbatvāt svadehoddhūnanākaraḥ |
	evaṃ ghrāṇāntare gandho raso dantodake sphuṭaḥ ||

iti śrītantrālokasthityā viyati pratiśrutkātmā śabdaḥ, 
aṅganānusmṛtyādāvādhāracakre pratyāsannakarkaśasparśānu-
sandhānāt saukumāryaśālināṃ śarīreṣu sparśaḥ, 
anyajanāsvādyamānatintiṇyādyanusandhānād dantodake rasaḥ, candanādi 
dhūpanāvasthāyāṃ ghrāṇāntarāle gandhaḥ, smaraṇotprekṣādā-
vātmamanasyanyajanānubhūyamānasvabhāvo harṣaśokāvirbhāvaśceti 
sārvatrikametat pratibimbasampatsaubhāgyam | evañca bāhyeṣu 
darpaṇādiṣu bimbasavyapekṣaḥ pratibimbopalambhaḥ | svātmarūpe 
punaretadvaiparītyam, aśeṣasyāpi viśvavaicitryasya 
pratibimbatayā'nubhūyamānatvāt | yathā śrītantrāloke -

	pratibimbaṃ ca bimbena bāhyasthena samarpyate |
	tasyaiva pratibimbatve kiṃ bimbamavaśiṣyatām || iti |

tathā ca śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

	vahnivārimukurādiṣu sphuṭaṃ svacchavastuṣu kumāri bimbatā |
	mantritā saha paricchadena yat tena bimbamanavekṣitaṃ tava || iti |

alaukikī ceyaṃ pratibimbayuktiḥ śāmbhava evopāye paryavasyati | 
yadāhuḥ-

	matta evoditamidaṃ mayyeva pratibimbitam |
	madabinnaṃ jagacceti tridhopāpastu śāmbhavaḥ || iti |

yathā śrītantravaṭadhānikāyām -

	yathādarśe ghaṭādīnāṃ sthitirmiśretarātmikā |
	madātmani tathā'mīṣāṃ bhāvānāṃ citrarūpiṇī || iti |



etadeva viśvapratibimbanakṣamatvaṃ parameśvarasya tadatiśayitaṃ 
svātantryamucyate yadantarvidbhirālocanīyam | viśvasya ca 
prakāśasvarūpe parameśvare parisphurataḥ 
pratibimbaprakriyāmenāmantareṇa nānyā rītiraucityamanubhavati | nanvasti 
vivartaḥ pariṇāmādirveti cet ? na | vivarto nāma 
bhrāntyaparaparyāyamanyasyānyatrāropaṇātmakaṃ jñānamucyate | 
tathā ca viśvamātmanyātmā vā viśvasminnāropyata ityaṅgīkāryam | 
tatra prathame pakṣe yathā sarpe dṛṣṭaṃ sarpatvaṃ rajjvāmāropyate, 
evamanyatra kutra dṛṣṭamiti kṛtvā viśvamātmanyāropyeta | kiñca nāyaṃ 
sarpa itivannedaṃ viśvamityauttarakāliko bādho'pyatra nānubhūyate | 
yuktyā tatra bādhaḥ prasādhayiṣyata iti cet ? na | keyaṃ yuktirnāma ? tarko 
vā pramāṇaṃ vā syāt | nādyaḥ, tasyāhāryamūlatayā 
pramāṇānugrāhakatvavyatirekeṇa svātantryeṇārthaprasādhakatayā'na-
bhyupagatatvāt | na dvitīyaḥ | pramāṇaṃ ca kiṃ pratyakṣamutāgamaḥ, 
āhosvidanumānam | na prathamaḥ, pratyakṣeṇa tadbādhasya na 
kenacidupalabhyamānatvāt | pratyuta viśvavyavahārasyārthakriyādinā 
nirbādhaṃ pravartyamānatvācca | na dvitīyaḥ | āgamo hi 
ekamevādvitīyaṃ brahma, neha nānāsti kiñcana ityādi | tasya 
cāyamarthaḥ - brahmaṇastāvadātmasvarūpaparameśvarāvibhinnasya na 
kenacidapyākāreṇa bhedaprathaucityamasti | iyat tu cintyam - tatra 
parisphurataḥ prapañcasya sthitiḥ kīdṛśīti? atraivamācakṣmahe - iha 
vāgvyavahārānukūlaṃ prastāvādidantayā'dhyavasīyamāne brahmaṇi 
gaganapavanādiḥ sarvo'yaṃ prapañco nānā na bhavati | 
bhedaprathāspadaṃ na sampadyate | kiṃ tarhi, upapāditayā 
tatsvātantryamayyā pratibimbadṛṣṭyā 
brahmasvarūpamevāvibhinnamadhyakṣyata iti | anyathā neha satyamasti 
kiñcana iti śrūyeta, śūnyavādo vā prasajyeta | nāpi tṛtīyaḥ, 
uktenaivāgamena vigītaṃ mithyā dṛśyatvāt śuktirajatavadityāderliṅgasya 
kālātikrāntatvāt | evamasiddhyādyapyūhyam | granthagauravabhayāt tu 
tanna vitanyate | api ca, brahmaṇyayaṃ prapañcaḥ kena 
pramātrā'dhyāropyate | tenaiva brahmaṇeti cet 
tarhyanadhiṣṭhānabhramatvaprasaṅgaḥ, tasyāropakatayā svīkṛtatvāt | 
aṃśato'sya tadubhayārthanirvāhakatvamiti



cet ? na | tathāpi tasya bhrāntimattvaprasaṅgāt | astu tarhi 
jīvātmano'dhyāropakatvaṃ brahmaṇaścādhiṣṭhānatvamiti cet ? na | 
bhedavādasyānabhimatatvāt, aṃśata ātmāśrayatvāvaśyambhāvācca | 
nanu kālpaniko bhedaḥ svairaṃ svīkriyata iti cet ? na | tasyaiva 
vivartaśabdenābhidhātumupakrāntatvāt | kiñca, kīdṛśena sādṛśyena 
brahmaṇi prapañca āropyate | tadupekṣāyāṃ ca kathaṃ nātiprasaṅgaḥ | 
brahmaprapañcayośca satyāsatyādivibhāgavaśād 
vaisādṛśyamatiprasiddham | viśvasmin brahmāropyata iti tu pa(kṣaḥ ? 
kṣo')prasiddho maryādātikrāntatvād brahmaṇo 
mithyātvaprasaṅgāccātyantatucchatayā niścetavyaḥ | ataśca 
vaicitryeṇoktaprakārapratibimbanasvabhāvena vartanaṃ viśvasya vivarta iti 
vyākhyāyāṃ na kiñcidanaucityam | yathā śrīkramakelau -

	tadvivartaḥ smṛto raśmipuñjaścakreśipūrvakaḥ (?) |

ityatra vyākhyātam - vivarto vicitreṇa rūpeṇa vartanaṃ na tvavidyāvaśāt, 
api tu svātantryataḥ iti | etena prasiddhasattvāsattvatāṭasthyaśālinyā 
saṃvitsvātantryamayyā mahāsattayā viśvavyavahārasya 
nirvacanīyatvanaiyatyādetadviparyayātmā 
tasyānirvacanīyatvapakṣo'pyapahastita eva syāt | evaṃ pariṇāme'pi 
vācyam | pariṇāmo hi kṣīrādeḥ pūrvākāravināśād 
dadhyādirūpatatsadṛśā'nyākāraparigrahaḥ | tatrāpi kiṃ brahma 
viśvatayā pariṇamati, uta viśvaṃ brahmatayeti praṣṭavyam | nādyaḥ, 
brahmaṇaḥ svarūpanāśaprasaṅgāt | na dvitīyaḥ, viśvasya 
saccidānandādisvabhāvatvāpatteḥ | tadabhyupagame brahmaṇaḥ sakāśād 
viśvasya sarvākāravaiṣamyāt, pariṇāmavārtānupapatteśca | evaṃ 
vṛkṣatvaśiṃśapātvādivallaukikastādātmyapakṣo'pi 
pratikṣipto'vagantavyaḥ | nanvevaṃ pratibimbavāde'pi 
mithyātvaśaṅkāyā aparihāryatvam | tatra hi svacchena darpaṇādidravyeṇa 
pratihatā locanamarīcayaḥ parāvṛttya svamātmānaṃ gṛhṇantīti 
prasiddhyā darpaṇe puruṣa iti pratītirbhrāntirityavadhāryate | tad darpaṇe 
puruṣo nāstītyauttarakāliko bādha eva pragalbhata iti cet ? na | pārameśvarī 
hi pratibimbabhaṅgi-



ralaukikatvādanavadyāmeva kakṣyāmadhirohati | yadamuṣyāmupanyastayā 
nītyā bimbavyapekṣā notpadyate | tatsaṃbhave hi 
bimbānvayavyatirekānuvidhāyinī pratibimbasphuratteti 
mithyātvaśaṅkāyā avakāśaḥ syāt | kiñca, laukike darpaṇādāvapi na 
mithyātvānukūlo bādho'sti kimutālaukika iti brūmaḥ | nanūktameva 
darpaṇe puruṣo nāstīti bādho'nubhūyata iti cet ? na | darpaṇe yadi puruṣa 
āropyate, tadā sa tatra nāstīti bādho'pi syāt | naivamanubhava(ta?)ḥ | tatra 
tacchāyāmātrasyaivopalambhaḥ, na tu tadvataḥ puṃsa 
ityanubhūyamānatvāt  ataśca darpaṇe puruṣo'stīti yuktimatāṃ 
pratītyabhāvādetadbādho'pi nāstītyarthani(ka? ṣka)rṣaḥ syāt | na 
cārthakriyāsadbhāve'pi mithyātvasyaucityam | dṛśyate hi 
darpaṇādyavalokināṃ dantadhāvanādiraneko'rthakriyāprakāraḥ | 
śuktikādīnāṃ tu rajatādyarthakriyā na kvacidapi sampadyate | nanu 
prasiddhasatyamaryādātikrāntaḥ khalvayaṃ pratibimbaprakāra iti cet ? na, 
prasiddhāprasiddhamaryādātikrāntaḥ khalvityapi vaktuṃ śakyatvāt | nanu 
tarhi satyāsatyavyatiriktā tṛtīyā koṭirityāpatatīti cet ? satyam | tṛtīyā 
koṭiryadalaukikamatimahat pārameśvarasvātantryamityākrandyate | 
yadānuguṇyenoktaṃ śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	sadasattvena bhāvānāṃ yuktānāṃ dvitayī sthitiḥ |
	tāmullaṅghya tṛtīyasmai namaścitrāya śambhave || iti |

nanu bimbavyatiriktaḥ pratibimbayogo na kvacidapyupalabhyata iti cet ? - satyam 
| kiṃ kriyatām | ata eva hyetat pārameśvaraṃ 
svātantryamityasakṛdācakṣmahe | nanu bimbamevaitadastviti cet ? na | 
tallakṣaṇāyogāt | bimbaṃ hyanyasaṃmiśraṇaśūnyam, svatantrameva 
vastvityupalabhyamānatvāt | yathā darpaṇānupaśliṣṭaṃ vadanādīti | 
yaduktaṃ śrītantrāloke -

	nanu bimbasya virahe pratibimbaṃ kathaṃ bhavet |
	kiṃ kurmo dṛśyate taddhi nanu tadbimbamucyatām ||



	naivaṃ tallakṣaṇābhāvād bimbaṃ kila kimucyate |
	anyāmiśraṃ svatantraṃ sad bhāsamānaṃ mukhaṃ yathā || iti |

nanvevaṃ pratibimbalakṣaṇasyāpyayoga iti cet ? na | tasyaivaṃ 
lakṣyamāṇatvāt | 

yathoktaṃ tatraiva -

	anyavyāmi'sraṇāyogāt tadbhedāśakyabhāsanam |
	pratibimbamiti prāhurdarpaṇe vadanaṃ yathā ||
	bodhamiśramidaṃ bodhād bhede vā śakyabhāsanam |
	puratattvādibodhe kiṃ pratibimbaṃ na bhāsyate || iti |

	nanu saugatasiddhantasādhitād pratibimbavādād yuṣmadupakṣiptasya 
kiṃ vailakṣaṇyamiti cet ? ucyate - teṣāṃ hi vittau vedyameva pratibimbati, na 
punarvittirvedye | asmākaṃ tu saugatasyāpi saṃvidi saṃvedyaṃ 
pratibimbamarpayati, na tu saṃvedye saṃvit iti śrīpratyabhijñāvivṛti-
vimarśinīsthityā vittau vedyamiva vedye ca vittiḥ pratibimbamutpādayati | 
yadetadubhayamapi vedituḥ parameśvarasya sphuraṇaprakāra iti 
svacchatvotkarṣaṃ prati na kiñcid vaiṣamyamanubhavati | yad vakṣyati - avi 
āroha apāse ityādau | tataśca yadubhayatra pratibimbati (ya? 
ta)dviśvavaicitryamityākhyāyate | yaduktaṃ mayaiva saṃvidullāse -

	ādarśayorivānyonyaṃ lambhitapratibimbayoḥ |
	śivaśaktyoranantāḥ syurantarantaḥ prasaktayaḥ || iti |

tatrāpi -

	bodhyamiśramidaṃ bodhyād bhede vā śakyabhāsanam |
	jñānasmṛtyādi bodhye kiṃ pratibimbaṃ na bhaṇyate ||

iti samāna, eva nyāyaḥ | tatra ca vettari vittyātmani vedyasya pratibimbanam, 
vedyātmani tu vitteriti | sarvathā tasyaivaṃ svātantryasaṃrambhādhīno 
viśvavikṣobhaḥ | evaṃ sāṃkhyādipratibimbavādavailakṣaṇyama-
pyūhyamiti vivartādivyatirekeṇāsmadupakṣiptaḥ pratibimbapakṣa eva 
prauḍhimāḍhaukate |



yadāhuḥ -

	ataḥ prapañcasya mṛṣātvavādī kāryatvavādī pratibhedavādī |
	asatyavādī ca pareśa ! śambho ! tava sthitiṃ neṣadapi spṛśanti || iti |

pratibimbatvityanenaitadāsūtryate yad darpaṇa iva vadanādi kiṃ svātmani 
viśvaṃ pratiphalatīti dṛṣṭāntadṛṣṭyā vipratipanno janaḥ pratisamādhīyata 
iti | sthūlopadeśāvadhīraṇena -

	svasyaiva sphuraṇaṃ mukhyaṃ viśvasminnadhiropyate |
	nanu naukādhirūḍhasya calatīvāpagātaṭam ||

iti sthityā svātmani niṣṭhaṃ viśvapratibimbanakriyāvaidagdhyaṃ 
mukhyaṃ sphaṭikamukurādāvalpanairmalye'pi vastuni sādṛśyenopacaryate 
| anyathā haṃsa(ni? ti hima)kara ityādisādhāraṇasyopamānārthasya 
kvipratyayasya vaiyarthyaprasaṅga iti | yadyapi 
pratibimbatītyādyaupamyavyatirekeṇāpyabhiyuktāḥ prayuñjate, tathāpi 
tatsauśabdyaniṣkarṣāvasthāyāmuktārthāvaśyambhāvaḥ |

	iti gurupadaparicaryātātparyadhano maheśvarānandaḥ |
