	pratibimbavādasampatsaubhāgyaṃ svahṛdayārthamācakhyau ||

	atha ca sarvo'yamupāyaprapañcastattatparāmarśakakṣyāviśeṣā-
rurukṣujanāpekṣayā paryālocitaḥ | pāryantikyāṃ 
punarārūḍhajanādhirūḍhāyāṃ 
svasvabhāvaikapāriśeṣyalakṣaṇāyāmavasthāyāmupāyatvameva 
paryavasyati | tadvyatireke punarupāyasphurattāyā evāsaṃbhavāt | 
yaduktaṃ śrītantrāloke -

	yāvānupāyo bāhyaḥ syādāntaro vāpi kaścana |
	sa sarvastanmukhaprekṣī tatropāyatvabhāk katham || iti |

śrītantravaṭadhānikāyāṃ ca -

	upāyairna śivo bhāti bhāntyamī tatprasādataḥ |
	sa evāhaṃ svaprakāśo bhāse viśvasvarūpakaḥ ||

 iti || 59 ||



	athetthamupadiṣṭopāyaprapañcapratilabdhātmasvarūpaparāmarśa-
māṃsalollāsānāṃ yogināmatiśayamākhyāsyannādāveṣāmantar-
bahiḥsvabhāvadaśavicchedavyudāsaniryantraṇaṃ naiścintyaṃ 
niścetumāha -

	avikārobhayapārśve cāṣapiñjasadṛkṣe'rthe |
	antarmukho yogī bahirmukha iti kalpanā kutaḥ || 60 ||

	arthyate sarvaiḥ prāpyata ityarthaḥ | svātmarūpaṃ kiñcidalaukikaṃ 
tattvam | tat khalu mayūrapicchavailakṣaṇyena cāṣabarhavad dvayorapi 
bhāgayorekarūpatayā.ādhāryate | cāṣamayūrapakṣaprakriyā tu 
lokaprasiddhā | uktarūpasya cārthasyaitadeva pārśvadvayaṃ 
yadahantedantayoraucityādantarbahirbhāvenāsya 
dvidhā'vabhāsamānatvam | tadvyatiriktā ca nānyā deśasphurattā'sti, 
paurastyapāścāttyarūpāyā apyasyā etadantarbhāvenopalabhyamānatvāt 
| tayośca pārśvayoḥ sthūladṛṣṭyavaṣṭambhopakalpito yo vikāro 
vedyavettṛtvādivyavasthānibandhano vaiṣamyānubhavaḥ, sa 
vastuvṛttyālocanāyāmanaucityasaraṇimanusaratītyavikāratvam | na 
khalvahantedantayorubhayorapi saṃvitsvātantryaparispandavyatirekeṇānyā 
kācidanuprāṇanaprakriyāsti, yadevaṃ vikriyā saṃbhāvyeta | artha ityatra 
kartari jñātarītyādivat saptamī siddhasvabhāvatāṃ dyotayati | 
tadarthamasminnarthe'vasthite -

	grāhyagrāhakasaṃvittiḥ sāmānyā sarvadehinām |
	yogināṃ tu viśeṣo'yaṃ saṃbandhe sāvadhānatā ||

iti śrīvijñānabhaṭṭārakasthityā 
vedyaveditṛsaṃbandhaparāmarśasvabhāvaṃ 
yogamātmānuprāṇanatvenāṅgīkurvatastata eva paratattvaikyaśālinaḥ 
pramātuḥ svātmaniṣṭhatārūpamantarmukhatvaṃ vedyavyākṣepalakṣaṇā 
bahiḥpravṛttiśceti yā kalpanā kṛtrimā prakriyā sā kuto hetorastu, na 
kutaścidapi saṃgacchate | nimīlanonmīlanātmanoḥ samādhyorapi 
tādṛkkalpanāmātraniṣpannatvāvaśyambhāvāditi 



bhāvaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati - tattatprauḍhapuruṣakalpanaikanirvyūḍho 
yo'yaṃ veditā vedyam, śaktimān śaktiḥ, patiḥ pāśaḥ, bhavan bhāvaḥ, 
dṛg dṛśyam, puruṣaḥ prakṛtiḥ, ātmā śarīram, antarbahiḥ, pratyak parāk, 
satyamasatyamityādivikalpavyākopaḥ, sa sarvo'pi -

	śivo dātā śivo bhoktā śivaḥ sarvamidaṃ jagat |
	tasmācchabdārthacintāsu sā'vasthā na yā śivaḥ ||
	vastutaḥ śivamaye hṛdi sphuṭaṃ sarvataḥ śivamayaṃ virājate |
	calitvā yāsyate kutra sarva śivamayaṃ yataḥ ||

ityādyanekāmnāyamaryādayā pārameśvaraprakāśaparamārthametad-
akhilamapi prapañcamālocayataḥ sākṣāt parameśvaratāpannasya 
mahāpuruṣasya svātmasvarūpavyatirekeṇa kiṃ pramāṇaṃ kaḥ 
parisphuratviti | yaduktaṃ śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	yo'vikalpamidamarthamaṇḍalaṃ paśyatīśa ! nikhilaṃ bhavadvapuḥ |
	svātmapakṣaparipūrite jagatyasya nityasukhinaḥ kuto bhayam || iti |

yaccoktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	sarvo mamāyaṃ vibhava ityevamabhijānataḥ |
	viśvātmano vikalpānāṃ prasare'pi maheśatā || iti |

yathā coktamabhiyuktaiḥ -

	antarnirañjanaṃ jyotirbahiraṣṭau ca mūrtayaḥ |
	paritaḥ paritaḥ śayyā haṃsāṅgaruhatūlikā ||

iti || 60 ||

	atha deśādhvaneva kālādhvanā'pyasya na kaścit 
saṅkocakalaṅkopalepa ityunmīlayitumāha -



	yogī jāgarasvapnasauṣuptaturīyaparvaparipāṭim |
	citrāmiva maṇimālāṃ vimarśasūtraikagumphitāmudvahati || 61 ||

	yānyetāni jāgaraprabhṛtīni kālakramānuprāṇanāni 
parvāṇyavasthāviśeṣāḥ, tatra laukikyā yuktyā sarvasādhāraṇyenāthaṃ 
viṣayīkṛtya bāhyābhyantarobhayendriyajanyaṃ jāgaraḥ, 
antaḥkaraṇamātraheturasādhāraṇārthanirmāṇātmā vikalpaḥ svapnaḥ, 
sarvākāreṇārthasphuraṇaśūnyatā sauṣuptam | yaduktaṃ 
śrīśivasūtreṣu - jñānaṃ jāgrat, svapno vikalpaḥ, aviveko māyā 
sauṣuptam iti | yaccoktaṃ śrīpratyabhijñāyām -

	sarvākṣagocaratvena yā tu bāhyatayā sthirā |
	sṛṣṭiḥ sādhāraṇī sarvapramātṝṇāṃ sa jāgaraḥ || iti,
	manomātrapatho'pyakṣaviṣayatvena vibhramāt |
	spaṣṭāvabhāsā bhāvānāṃ sṛṣṭiḥ svapnapadaṃ matam || iti,
	tāvanmātrasthitau proktaṃ sauṣuptaṃ pralayopamam | iti ca |

	yogidṛṣṭyā tu dhāraṇādhyānasamādhirūpāṇyetāni | yāni 
cittasya deśabandho dhāraṇā, tattatpratyayapravāho dhyānam, 
vedyavedakabhāvādivigalanaṃ samādhiḥ ityāgameṣu lakṣyante | turīyaṃ 
punaḥ sarvayoginyayoginyapyekarūpatayā'vatiṣṭhate | kevalaṃ 
tādṛkparāmarśakṣamatvameva yogino viśeṣaḥ | turīyaṃ ca nāma triṣu 
caturthaṃ tailavadāsecyam iti śrīśivasūtrasthityā 
jāgradādyavasthātrayānuvṛttā mahāsphurattā | upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat | yata 
ekasmin dvitīyam, dvayostṛtīyam, triṣu caturtham, caturṣu 
pañcamamityasyārthasyāvaśyambhāvaḥ | tat punaraupadeśikatayā sūtre 
saṃgṛhyoktam | eṣāṃ ca parvaṇāṃ yā paripāṭiranyonyarūpatve 
vyavasthātmakatvādekarūpatā, tāṃ vyākhyātasvabhāvo yogī 
mauktikamāṇikyādyanekaratnaśālinīṃ hārayaṣṭimiva vimarśākhyena 
sarvānupraveśapragalbhenaikena tantunā saṃkalitāṃ kurvannudvahati 
laukikaprasthānollaṅghinā kenacidutkarṣeṇa prāpayati 
tattatsvarūpatāparāmarśena svātmanyavasthāpayati | tatra ca 
laukikānāmapi sādhāraṇamavasthātrayamatikramya 
paramapramātṛrūpaturyātītasvabhāvatayā'vadhāryamāṇaṃ



turyamevātra vimarśa ityākhyāyate, yena 
sraksūtranyāyādadhastanamavasthātrayaṃ kroḍīkriyate | tatrāpi 
jāgradādisāhacaryākrāntasyāṅgasya pramātṛsvarūpānu-
praviṣṭenāṃśenānusandhīyamānatayā kroḍīkāryatvaṃ 
kroḍīkaraṇatvaṃ cetyubhayamapi saṅgacchate | tathāhi - jāgradādiṣu 
catasṛṣvavasthāsu jāgrajjāgrat, jāgratsvapnaḥ, jāgratsuṣuptam, 
jāgratturīyamityādinā rūpeṇa parasparānuvedhaḥ pratīyate | 
tatrābuddhaṃ buddhaṃ prabuddhaṃ suprabuddhamiti jāgrataścatvāro 
bhedāḥ |

	gatāgataṃ suvikṣiptaṃ saṃgataṃ susamāhitam |

iti svapnasya, uditaṃ vipulaṃ śāntaṃ suprasannamiti suṣuptasya | turīyasya 
tu -

	naitasyāmapārā turyadaśā saṃbhāvyate kila |

iti śrītantrālokasthityā manonmanamanantaṃ sarvatobhadramiti trayo 
bhedāḥ | turyātīte ca -

	turyātīte bheda ekaḥ satatoditamityayam |

iti nītyā nityavimraṣṭṛtādyotakasatatoditamityeko bheda upacaryate | (eṣā 
ca? etasyāḥ) sphuṭaḥ sphuṭāsphuṭo'sphuṭa ityavabhāsabhedāt 
sarvatrānusyūtyā'vasthānaṃ ca | kramāllakṣaṇānyācakṣate | yaduktaṃ 
śrītantrāloke -

	yasya yad yat sphuṭaṃ rūpaṃ tajjāgraditi manyatām |
	yadevāsthiramābhāti svarūpaṃ svapna īdṛśaḥ ||
	asphuṭaṃ tu yadābhāti suṣuptaṃ tat puro'pi yat |
	trayasyāsyānusandhistu yadvaśādupajāyate ||
	sraksūtrakalpaṃ tat tūrya sarvabhedeṣu gṛhyatām || iti |

turyātītaṃ tu sarvatraikarūpyādavyākulaṃ 
svātmarūpaparamabhairavasvabhāvamanubhūyate | yaduktaṃ tatraiva -

	yattvadvaitabharollāsadrāvitāśeṣabhedakam |
	turyātītaṃ tu tat prāhuritthaṃ sarvatra yojayet || iti |

tena suṣṭhūktaṃ tūryasyobhayārthaprasādhanaprāgalbhyamastīti | 
anenaivāśayenopamānāṃśe citrāmityuktam | tataśca yathā 
māṇikyamauktikamahānīlamarakatādyanekamaṇigumphitā 



hārayaṣṭiraruṇadhavalaśyāmalādivarṇavaicitryavyatikarottaratayā kvacit 
kasyacidasādhāraṇye'pi vyatiriktāśeṣavarṇasaṃsargāvinābhāvamanu-
bhavantyaparokṣīkriyate, evaṃ jāgarādiparvaparamparāyāmapi 
pratipāditena prakāreṇa parasparānuvedhavaicitryamanusandhīyata 
ityāsūtryate | etaduktaṃ bhavati - cicchaktisvarūpo mahāprakāśanāmā 
paramapramātṛrūpo nityodyantṛtāparispandasāro bhagavāṃsturīyātīta-
bhaṭṭārakaḥ svānandātmakapracayanāmadheyāvacchinnapramātṛtā-
rūpānaudāsīnyasvabhāvena tūriyeṇa karaṇabhūtena prameyaṃ 
pramāṇaṃ pramātraudāsīnyamiti, piṇḍaṃ padaṃ rūpamiti, kriyā 
jñānamicchetyādyaśeṣatrikānuprāṇanametajjāgratsvapnasuṣupta-
lakṣaṇamavasthātrayaṃ pratipāditālaukikaiśvaryaparāmarśalakṣaṇāṃ 
yogaśaktimākramya svasvātantryasphurattāparispandamātraparamārtha-
manusandhatta iti | yaduktaṃ śrīśivasūtreṣu - tritayabhoktā vīreśaḥ, 
tripadādyanuprāṇanam iti ca | yaccoktaṃ śrīspande -

	jāgradādivibhede'pi tadabhinne prasarpati |
	nivartate nijānnaiva svabhāvādupalabdhṛtaḥ ||

iti || 61 ||

	nanu vyāvarṇitametaddeśakālādivikalpavaidhuryoddhuraṃ 
śuddhasaṃvitkalākaivalyasvabhāvaṃ yoginaḥ svācchandyaṃ 
svavyatiriktāśeṣadehaprāṇādivikalpopaśleṣavaimukhyaśālina 
evāsyopapadyate | tacca na saṃbhavati, yasya kasyacidvikalpopaśleṣasya 
nityamavarjanīyatvādityāśaṅkyāha -

	ullokānandasudhāśīdhurasodvejitena hṛdayena |
	abhilaṣati lokayātrātintiṇicarvaṇarasāntaraṃ yogī || 62 ||

	yo'yamullokaḥ sa eko brahmaṇa ānandaḥ ityupaniṣatprakriyayā 
manuṣyagandharvādyavacchinnapramātṛhṛdayānandātiśāyī 
pūrṇāhambhāvabhāvanācamatkārasāraḥ 



svāntarviśrāntisampatsaundaryamātrasvaparispandaḥ kaścidāhlādot-
karṣaḥ, sa khalvamṛtāsavasvabhāvarasadvayavyatikarakalpanīyārthakriyā-
kāritayā prakāśavimarśasvabhāvasvarūpamādhuryātiśayānubhava-
prāvīṇyād yogino hṛdayamudvejayati utkṛṣyānyatra svapratiyogini 
padārthe pravartayati | tādṛśā cāyaṃ hṛdayenopakaraṇabhūtena lokasya 
dehākṣabhuvanāderyā yātrā pravāhanityā pravṛttiḥ, sā 
ciñcāphalāsvādavat prāganubhūtamādhuryotkarṣāpekṣayā 
rasāntaramamlādisādṛśyādanyo rasaḥ sampadyate | yatrodvejitahṛdayasya 
yogina icchāśaktirujjṛmbhate | yogīcchāyāśca phalaprāptiparyantatvama-
vipratipannam | yaduktaṃ śrīśivasūtreṣu - cittasthitivat śarīrakaraṇa-
bāhyeṣu iti | kṣīraśarkarādyatyantamadhuropayogodvejitacetasāṃ ca 
puṃsāṃ tintiṇyādyamlapadārthāntarābhilāṣo lokaprasiddhaḥ | ayaṃ 
bhāvaḥ - śarīrendriyādi vedyavikṣobhavyudāsena 
svātmamātrasākṣātkāralakṣaṇaṃ saukhyamavalambyollāso 
bahirviṣayabibhīṣikāviklavātmanaḥ parimitasya yoginaścākityaprakāraḥ | 
aparimitasya tu pūrṇāhantāparāmarśātmakānavacchinnānanda-
parispandāsvādasaṃprītātmanaḥ svātmapakṣanikṣiptāśeṣabāhya-
prapañcatvādidantānubhūtyavaśyambhāvaḥ | tataśca vikalpasarvasva-
vikṣobhānubhūtivaicitryottaramidantāpadāvarohaṇamapyanavacchinnāham
bhāvabhāsure mahati pramātṛparvaṇyeva paryavasyati | yaduktaṃ 
śrīkramasūtreṣu - bāhyādantaḥ praveśo'bhyantarādvā 
bāhyasvarūpānupraveśaḥ iti | yathā ca vyākhyātaṃ śrīmatkṣemarājena 
- tatra ca bāhyād gṛhyamāṇād viṣayagrāmādantaḥ parasyāṃ 
citibhūmau grasanakrameṇaiva praveśaḥ samāveśo bhavati, 
abhyantarāccitiśaktisvarūpāt sākṣātkṛtāt samāveśasāmarthyādeva 
bāhyasvarūpa idantādinirdeśye | viṣayagrāme vamanayuktyā 
praveśaścidrasāśyānatātmā samāveśo jāyate iti | yadabhipretya 
śrīpratyabhijñāyāmuktam -

	viśvarūpo'hamidamityakhaṇḍānandabṛṃhitaḥ iti |

yaccoktamasmadgurubhirānandatāṇḍavavilāsastotre -



	vayaṃ tvimāṃ viśvatayā'vabhānaṃ bahirmukhasyāsya 
tavonmukhasya |
	svasaṃhitaṃ viśvavilāpanodyatsvatantratānandamayīṃ namāmaḥ || 
iti |

idameva hi tad yogino jñānasya śuddhatvam, 
yadidantopaśleṣe'pyavaiyākulyam | yaduktaṃ śrīcandrajñāne -

	yena prabuddhabhāvena bhuñjāno viṣayān svayam |
	na yāti pāśavaṃ bhāvaṃ jñānacandraḥ sa kīrtitaḥ || iti |

	kiñca, yadyadudriktamādhuryopayoge tintiṇyādyākāṅkṣā 
tadāsvādādeva prastutodvejanaśāntyā punarapi madhuradravyasvīkāra-
sāmarthyam, evaṃ pūrṇāhantānusandhānādidantopalambhāvaśyam-
bhāvaḥ | idantānuṣaṅge ca tādṛgahantānupraveśasyārthataḥ 
siddhirityātmanaścakrakasthityā nityodyantṛtvaṃ prati na kaścid 
bhaṅgaśaṅkāvakāśaḥ | yasmādidantānupalabdhāvahambhāvasya 
rājyabhraṣṭasyeva rājñaḥ, tadvadahamaṃśānuparaktāvidamaṃśasya 
bhāgasyārājakasyeva rājyasya na kiñciccamatkāraucityam | yaduktaṃ 
śambhvaikyadīpikāyām - antargatā hi prakāśavyāptiridamiti viśrāmyati 
bahirgatā cāhamiti, etaddvayaṃ sarvajanaprasiddhamanapahnavanīyam iti || 62 
||

	nanvidantāyā māyīyatvāvaśyambhāvādudbhāvito'yamahante-
dantayoraviśeṣaḥ kathaṃ saṅgacchatāmityāśaṅkyāha -

	yābhirgṛhṇāti yogī karaṇapraṇālībhirviṣayasaukhyāni |
	nijahṛdayodvamanaśīlābhiḥ sphuraṇamayaṃ tābhiḥ karoti 
trailokyam || 63 ||

	prakṛtyā paramapramātṛpadādhirūḍho'pi 
svecchāmātreṇopādhinā parimitapramātṛbhāvamavataran ata eva 
prakāśānandasāmarasyāmṛtamahāhradāyamāno yogī 
yābhirantarbahirindriyaśaktilakṣaṇābhiḥ pravāhopāyaprakriyābhiḥ 
śabdasparśādiviṣayavapūṃṣi saukhyāni gṛhṇāti ātmasātkaroti, 
tābhireva



pānīyapānopakramaprakṣaritavamathuśīkarastimyatstamberamahasta-
puṣkaranyāyād vyākhyātarūpamātmahṛdayamanavaratamudvamantībhiḥ 
pramātṛpramāṇaprameyaparamārthametat trailokyaṃ 
svahṛdayalakṣaṇasphurattātmakaṃ vidhatte | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - yogī 
hyātmanaḥ parapramātṛbhāvaṃ parimitapramātṛtāyāmantaḥ-
karaṇeṣvetāni bahirindriyeṣu tāni ca caityabhūmābudvamannanayaiva 
nāḍyā pramātrāditrikamayamakhilamapi lokavaicitryaṃ 
svātmasaṃvidāśyānatāsvabhāvamādhatte | yaduktaṃ 
śrīcidgaganacandrikāyām -

	mātṛmeyamitisādhanātmikā tvatkṛtonmiṣati yā vikalpadhīḥ |
	tvatsvarūpamakalaṅkitaṃ tayā kasya devi ! viduṣo na muktatā || iti |

evañca viśvagrasanayuktyā svāhlādalābhānubhūtimatsvasaṃvidvamana-
mudrayā tasya māyīyatādoṣaśaṅkāpyapamṛjyata iti tātparyārthaḥ | ti u 
ḍima aṃ khu samattha ityādikāyāṃ gāthāyāṃ pramātrādi-
tritayātmanaḥ prapañcasya cinmayatvamunmīlitam | atra tu tasya 
cinmayatvādeva hetormāyīyatārūpāpavādaparyudāsa iti viśeṣaḥ || 63 ||

	nanvidaṃ yogināṃ naiścintyamāṇavādyupāyasavyapekṣatayā 
vikalpakakṣyollaṅghanakṣamatvādanaiścintyapakṣamanupraviśatītyā-
śaṅkyāha -

	yathā tava sthitistathāssva niścinta iti khalu pratiṣṭhito'rthaḥ |
	tatrāpyasti viveka evamupadiśati tasya ko'nyaḥ ||

	yadyapyāṇavādyupāyānāṃ bhedaprathānuprāṇanatayā 
vikalpaparvānupraveśaḥ, tathāpyāṇave vikalpaulbaṇyam | śākte tasya 
sphurattāmātram | śāmbhave tu nirvikalpatvamityuttarottara-
parvānuśīlanāduktarūpameṣāṃ naiścintyamavyāhatam | yat punaḥ 
śāmbhave'pyupāyasāmānyānupraveśāt sūkṣmekṣikayā 
kiñcidvikalpaśaṅkāyā avarjanīyatvam, tadapi nirupāyaparvasannikarṣā-
datyantaśuddhasaṃvinmayaṃ sat paryantato vikalpavārtānabhijñatāyāṃ 
paryavasyati | tathāhi -

 

	yathā sthitastathaivāssva mā gā bāhyamathāntaram |
	kenaciccidvikāsena vikāranikarān jahi ||

iti sthityā tavopadeśasya yena prakāreṇa sthitirvṛttirbhavati, tenaiva 
prakāreṇa vikalpalakṣaṇāṃ cintāmatikrāmannāssva avyākulaṃ 
vartasveti yo'yamupāyabhūmilakṣaṇo'rthaḥ sa 
khalvāṇavādikramollaṅghanena pratiṣṭhitaḥ śāmbhavatātmanā 
pāryantika ityākhyāyate | tatrāpi kaścid viveko'sti | paryālocanayā 
kayācid bhāvyam | yaduta evamityupapāditayā bhaṅgyā yo'yamupadeśo 
rahasyārthaprakhyāpanam, sa khalvāssvetyādervidhyarthasya 
svānyavibhāgarūpabhedajīvitatvāt kiṃkarmakaḥ kiṃkartṛko vā 
syādityubhayathāpi bhavituṃ nārhati, taṃ prati 
karmakartṛbhūtayorupade:syopadeśakayorvastuvṛttyā bhedābhāvāt | 
nanvevamādi(kādi?)bhedabhinno'nantaprakārastattattantravartī 
kalpanāprapañcaḥ kathamiti cet ? upacārāditi brūmaḥ | yad 
vyākhyātamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ śrītriṃśikāśāstrārambhe - 
śrīdevyuvācetyādau svātmadevataiva prabudhyamānāvasthāyā-
mātmānaṃ parāmarśenānavarataṃ pṛcchati ityādi | yaccoktaṃ 
śrīsvacchande -

	guruśiṣyapade sthitvā svayaṃ devaḥ sadāśivaḥ |

ityādi | tataśca yogināmetadakutobhayatvaṃ svabhāvabhūtamiti 
tātparyārthaḥ | sarvākārollasatpārameśvaraprakāśaparāmarśaikātmya-
pāvane prapañce svātmānaṃ pratyavadhānā'navadhānādervikalpa-
kalpanāprakārasya paryantato niṣprayojanatvāt | taduktaṃ śrītantrāloke -

	tyajāvadhānāni nanu kva nāma dhatse'vadhānaṃ cinuhi svayaṃ tat |
	pūrṇe'vadhānānnahi nāma yuktaṃ nāpurṇamabhyeti ca 
satyabhāvaḥ || iti |

kiñca, evamupadiśati kasya ko'nya ityanena sarvo'yamupadeśa-
prakārastattvadṛṣṭāvatāttvikīṃ bhuvamavagāhata ityāsūtryate | yataḥ 
svātmaparameśvarasya pūrṇatvopapattyā prakārakalpanānupapatteryathā 
tathetyanayorvaiyarthyam | bāhyābhyantarātmano vibhāgasyānupapattyā 
tannibandhanasya -



	calitvā yāsyate kutra sarva śivamayaṃ yataḥ |

iti nītyā gamāgamarūpasyārthasyāvāstavatvāt tatpratiyoginoḥ 
sthānāsanayoraupacārikatvamavipratipannam | puruṣaścottamāvadhi iti 
nyāyādahamarthavyatirekeṇa yuṣmacchabdārthasyāsatkalpatvaṃ 
cāparihāryam | kvacit sataḥ kvacinniṣedhaḥ iti sthityā cintāyā evābhāvāt 
tanniṣedharūpanaiścintyaṃ ca na prāmāṇyamanubhavati | 
evamāssvetyāderapravṛttapravartanātmano vidhirūpasyārtha-
syātrāsaṅgatatvamityādi svayamūhyam || 64 ||

	itthamatyāścaryaṃ naiścintyaśālināṃ yogināṃ 
svabhāvamanusandadhānastantrakṛt svātmano'pi tebhyo 
vailakṣaṇyābhāvāt tattādṛksvabhāvatāparāmarśamāṃsala-
māhlādātiśayamanubhavannetadāveśavaivaśyodriktasvasaṃvidāṭopa-
gauravoccalaccittavṛttiścamatkārottaramāha -

	aho saṃsārasukhātiśayaḥ aho sulabhaṃ mokṣamārgasaubhāgyam |
	truṭitātaṅkakalaṅkā aho śivayogināṃ yāmalī siddhiḥ || 65 ||

	āścarya khalvayaṃ saṃsāralakṣaṇaḥ sukhātiśayaḥ, yo 
jananamaraṇādirūpatayā bāhyajanaṃ prati kleśātmakaḥ | tathā heyatayā 
niścīyamāno'pi mokṣaprāyatayā'smābhirāsvādyate | yaduktaṃ 
śrīmatstotrāvalyām -

	duḥkhānyapi sukhāyante viṣamapyamṛtāyate |
	mokṣāyate ca saṃsāro yatra mārgaḥ sa śāṅkaraḥ || iti |

āścarya cedaṃ mokṣalakṣaṇasya sarvajanamṛgyamāṇasya 
parameśvarānugrahaikalabhyasyārthasya saubhāgyaṃ hṛdayahāritvaṃ prati 
saulabhyam -

	adṛṣṭamaṇḍalo'pyetat tilādyāhutivarjitam |

iti śrītriṃśikāśāstrādinyāyādāyāsaśūnyamanubhavanam, 
yadalipiśitādyupabhogādutpadyate | taduktaṃ śrīmahāvanamuninā -



	alipiśitapurandhrībhogaparyākulo'haṃ
		bahuvidhakulayogārambhasaṃbhāvito'ham |
	paśujanavimukho'haṃ bhairavīsaṃśrito'haṃ
		gurucaraṇaparo'haṃ bhairavo'haṃ śivo'ham || iti |

itthamekaikaprādhānye'pyanyonyāviyogarūpamaucityamupalālya 
tayostulādhāraṇavadatya(nta?ntā)vaiṣamyalakṣaṇamaikātmyaṃ 
hetuprayuktyopabṛṃhayannāha - truṭitātaṅketyādi | śivayogino nāma -

	na dhyāyato na japataḥ syād yasyāvidhipūrvakam |
	evameva śivābhāsastaṃ numo bhaktiśālinām ||

iti śrīmatstotrāvalisthityā prāṇadhāraṇādyuparodhavyatirekeṇa 
prakāśātmanā śivena saha bhūmnā praśaṃsayā nityayogena ca yogaṃ 
vimarśātmānaṃ saṃbandhamaśnuvānā mahātmānaḥ | teṣāṃ yā 
siddhiḥ svasvabhāvacamatkārākārā sphurattā, sā yāmalī na kadācid 
vedyavedakadvitayasṛṣṭisvarūpāmubhayasvarūpatāmatikrāmati |

	yasyonmeṣanimeṣābhyāṃ jagataḥ pralayodayau |
	tadantaḥ kālayogena somasūryau prakīrtitau |
	jñānakriyātmanoḥ śambhumarīcyormelanātmakam |

ityanekāmnāyaprakriyayā saṅkocavikāsarūpajñatvakartṛtvā-
paraparyāyaprakāśavimarśasāmarasyātmakaṃ yāmalollāsasvabhāva-
tvamāgameṣvāmnāyate | taduktamācāryābhinavaguptanāthapādaiḥ - 
tatra jñātā nāmonmeṣanimeṣalakṣaṇena saṅkocavikāsātmanā 
jñānakriyālakṣaṇena svabhāvena svaparispandanasāra eva iti | asya ca 
yāmalasyaikā koṭiḥ parabhairavasaṃvitsvātantryaparyantam, anyā 
cātyantajaḍaghaṭādiprakāśaparyantaṃ parisphurati, yat 
svātmaparameśvarasyodayaviśramaṇasvabhāvam | a iti brahma 
tatrāgatamaham ityaitareyopaniṣatprakriyayā nityoditodyogasvabhāvaṃ 
śivaśaktimelāparūpaṃ rudrayāmalamityākhyāyate | taduktaṃ 
śrītriṃśikāśāstre - ityetad rudrayāmalam iti | atra hetustruṭitā-



taṅkalaṅkatvam | ātaṅko hi śaṅkānibandhanaṃ cākityam | śaṅkā ca 
sandehavipratipattyādyanuprāṇanatayā sakṛdvibhātasvabhāva-
svātmasaṃvidaikarasyāsvādaṃ prati pratyūhatayā'nubhūyate | tasya 
caitat kalaṅkatvaṃ yat svacche'pi svātmani mālinyaśaṅkā'nupraveśyate | 
tādṛśaścāyamātaṅkastruṭyati | svavāsanāparyantaṃ praṇaśyati | tadā 
pratipāditasiddhilābha iti tātparyārthaḥ | taduktamācāryābhi-
navaguptapādaiḥ - na tvatra vidyāvratādi kiñcit sahakāribhāvenopayogi | 
kevalaṃ parīkṣaṇaśaṅkātaṅkatvamatropayogi iti | o ityāścaryadyotikayā 
deśyā -

	darśanāntarakāntārakaṇṭakakadrumakoṭarāt |
	kirātaḥ ko'pi kūṭastho datte mantrāmṛtaṃ madhu ||

iti saṃvidullāsasthityā vratopavāsādyupadravabahulopāyaprayojakānanta-
śāstrāntarasaṅkaṭe'pi prapañce śrīmadanuttarakramapraṇayana-
pravīṇasya parameśvarasya paramakāruṇyamātropapāditeyamāsmākīnā 
yāmalī siddhiḥ, yā śrīkulagahvarādiṣu -

	yat tadakṣaramavyaktaṃ priyākaṇṭhe'pyavasthitam |
	dhvanirūpamanicchaṃ tu dhyānadhāraṇavarjitam ||
	tatra cittaṃ vidhāyaivaṃ vaśayed yugapajjagat |

ityādinā bhogamokṣasāmarasyātmakatayopapādyata ityāsutryate | 
taduktaṃ śrītantrāloke -

	asyāṃ bhūmau sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ bandho mokṣaścitirjaḍaḥ |
	ghaṭakumbhavadekārthāḥ śabdāḥ iti || 65 ||

	nanvasminnakutobhayasaṃvidāhlādobhayasvabhāvāsvādavapuṣi 
puruṣārthe tadutkarṣānukūlayā kālakramopakalpanīyayā 
kayācidatyantatīvrayopāstyā bhavitavyam | sā cāsmadādeḥ sadyo na 
sampadyata iti śrūyamāṇatāmātrasaubhāgyametadarthonmīlanam, na 
punaḥ svahṛdayānubhūtiparyavasāyītyāśaṅkāṃ kṣapayitumāha -



	kṣaṇamātraspṛṣṭenāpyamṛtasvabhāvenānena bhavena |
	sarvottirṇaḥ sarvaḥ sarvaciraṃ labhate sarvasaubhāgyam || 66 ||

	yo'yamanvādiśyamāno bhāvaḥ svahṛdayasphurattāparaparyāyā 
mahāsattā sa khalu -

	yasmin kāle tu guruṇā nirvikalpaṃ prabhāṣitam |
	tadaiva kila mukto'sau yantraṃ tiṣṭhati kevalam ||

iti śrīratnamālāmaryādayā gurukaṭākṣapātalakṣaṇāt 
kālaleśaviśeṣātmanaḥ kṣaṇādeva sṛṣṭānupraviṣṭo 
bhavannapyamṛtasvabhāvacidāhlādadvitayasāmarasyākārasārasvarūpa-
tayā jīvanmuktyātmā mahopabhogo bhavati | athavā ata eva 
hetoramṛtasvabhāvo nityāsvādyatānaiyatyayogāt | asyoccāre kṛte 
ityārabhya phalaṃ yad vā samīhitam ityantaṃ śrītriṃśikāśāstrasthityā 
muhūrtapraharavāsarādikālakramotkarṣakakṣyānuvṛtteruparyupari-
parāmarśānusyūtidarśitadārḍhyayogaśca sampadyata ityāvṛttyā yojanā 
| tādṛśā ca bhāvena hetubhūtena sarvastiryaktvamanuṣyatvajīvatvā-
mṛtatvajaḍatvājaḍatvāditāratamyaśūnyaḥ pramātṛvargaḥ sarvasmāt -

	samanāntaṃ pāśajālamunmanyante paraḥ śivaḥ |

iti sthityā viśvavilāsalakṣaṇād bandhahetoruttirṇaḥ 
śuddhacaitanyasvabhāvo bhavan sarvaciram 

	brahmāyuṣo daśaguṇaṃ viṣṇorāyuḥ paraṃ smṛtam |
	sahasraguṇitaṃ tasmād rudrasyāyuḥ paraṃ kila ||
	tasmāllakṣaguṇaṃ proktamīśvarasyāyuruttamam |
	tasmāt sadāśivasyāyuḥ proktaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ budhaiḥ ||
	samudraguṇitaṃ tasmācchivayordehadhāraṇam |



iti śrīlaghubṛṃhaṇīmaryādopapāditabrahmaviṣṇvādipuruṣāyuṣa-
pravṛttiparipāṭīrūpakālakramotkhaṇḍanena yāvatkālatattvaṃ sarva 
pāṣāṇādijaḍapadārthasvabhāvāpattiparyantamidantāsāmrājyaṃ 
paramaśivībhāvarūpacaitanyotkarṣakāṣṭhāvadhikamahantāsampat-
saṃbhogasarvasvaṃ ceti saṃbhūya viśvataduttirṇatvasāmarasyāsvāda-
sāmānādhikaraṇyarūpaṃ saubhāgyamantaḥkaraṇaharaṇaprāvīṇya-
lakṣaṇaṃ ślāghyatvātiśayaṃ labhate svaparispandātmakatayā prāpnoti | 
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ - śrīmaddeśikanāthakaṭākṣapātasamasamayameva 
puṃsāṃ svahṛdayasaṃvādasaundaryaśālino jīvanmokṣalakṣaṇasya 
puruṣārthasyopalambha ityatra na kācid vipratipattiḥ | yaduktaṃ 
śrītriṃśikāśāstre - sadyaḥ kaulikasiddhidam, sadyo yogavimokṣadam, iti 
ca | yathoktamācāryābhinavaguptapādaiḥ - etad yo labhate sa lābhakāla eva 
na paśuḥ iti | tādṛśasyaiva ca bhāvasyāntaḥ parāmarśaḥ 
kriyāsamabhihāre tattadaṇimādyaiśvaryasvabhāvo 
bahirvibhūtiparispando'nubhūyate | taduktaṃ tatraiva -

	etadabhyasyataḥ siddhiḥ sarvajñatvamavāpyate iti |

yad vyākhyātamabhinavaguptapādaiḥ - abhyāsena vināpi jīvanmuktatā 
parā kaulikī siddhiḥ iti | kṣaṇamātreti | kṣaṇika eva hi parāmarśaḥ -

	sakṛjjñāte suvarṇe mā bhāvanākaraṇaṃ vrajet |

iti śrīśivadṛṣṭidṛṣṭyā bahiṣṭheṣvapi padārtheṣvasaṃśayamarthakriyā-
martha prayojayati | kimuta svasvabhāvātmani parameśvarākhye bhāva 
ityarthaḥ | yaduktaṃ tatraiva -

	ekavāraṃ pramāṇena śāstrād vā guruvākyataḥ |
	jñāte śivatve tatrasthapratipattyā dṛḍhātmanā ||
	karaṇena nāsti kṛtyaṃ kvāpi bhāvanayāpi vā || iti |

atra pṛthaganekārthaśālināṃ sarvaśabdānāmarthaprapañcaprastāvo 
vistārāyetyalaṃ granthagauraveṇa | yat saṃbhūya sarvasāmyasvabhāvā 
mahārthasidhāntopaniṣadunmīlyate | yaduktamāgame -



samatā sarvabhāvānāṃ vṛttīnāṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ |
samatā sarvadṛṣṭīnāṃ dravyāṇāṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ ||
bhūmikānāṃ ca sarvāsāmovallīnāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
samatā sarvadevānāṃ varṇānāṃ caiva sarvaśaḥ ||

iti || 66 ||

	nanvevamanāyāsasādhyamartha prati pramātṝṇāṃ 
saulabhyāviśeṣāt kathaṃ muktāmuktavyavasthayā dvaividhyavyavahāra 
ityāśaṅkyāha -

	gūḍhād guḍhataro bhavati sphuṭādapi sphuṭatara eṣaḥ |
	deśikakaṭākṣapāte pakṣaḥ prathamo na bhavati dhanyānām || 67 ||

	eṣa prākaraṇikatayā parāmṛśyamāno'rtho yaḥ sthūlasūkṣma-
prakaṭāprakāśavyaktāvyaktakṣarākṣareti viṣṇupurāṇaprakriyayā 
prākaṭyamaprākaṭyaṃ cetyātmanyavasthādvayamudbhāvayati | tatra ca 
yadagūḍhaṃ stambhakumbhādi vyavahāraṃ prati | gūḍhaṃ vedāntādi-
nirṇītamarthatattvam | tato'pyayaṃ gūḍhataro'tyantaguhyo bhavati | 
yaduktaṃ śrītriṃśikāśāstre -

	etad guhyaṃ mahāguhyaṃ kathayasva mama prabho iti |

yaścāsphuṭamantarikṣaprasūnādiṃ prati sphuṭaḥ stambhakumbhādiḥ 
prakāśamānaḥ prameyaprapañcaḥ, tato'pi sphuṭataraḥ prakṛṣṭaprākaṭyo 
bhavati | tacca so kassa phuṭo na ho i kulaṇāho ityatra vitatya vyākhyātam | 
evamubhayasvabhāvatāyāmasya svātantryavyatirekeṇa nānyaḥ 
kaścidupādhirupapadyate | kathaṃ tarhi muktāmuktavyavastheti cet ? 
viṣayavibhāgāditi brūmaḥ | tathāhi - deśikasya kulācāryasya 
kaṭākṣapāte śaktipātāvinābhūte saṃbhavati 
dhanyānāmapaścimajanmanāṃ keṣāñcit pramātṝṇāṃ prathamaḥ 
pakṣaḥ pūrvārdhaprastuto gūḍhād gūḍhatara ityevaṃrūpaḥ kalpo na 
bhavati, kiṃ tarhi, sphuṭāt sphuṭatara ityevamākāraḥ |

	dīyate śivasadbhāvaḥ kṣapyate pāśasaṃcayaḥ |



iti nītyā dānakṣapaṇalakṣaṇadīkṣāvinābhūto dvitīya eva teṣāṃ 
pakṣaḥ sampadyate | tataśca deśikakaṭākṣapātadhanyānāṃ puṃsāṃ 
paramaśivībhāvalakṣaṇo mokṣaḥ, tadanyeṣāṃ pāśavāveśasvabhāvo 
bandha iti vyavastheti tātparyārthaḥ | atra deśo deśanā 
svātmapratyabhijñāpanārūparahasyārthopadeśanātmā 
svabhāvo'syāstīti vyutpattyā gurunāthaśarīrānupraviṣṭaḥ parameśvara 
eva kāruṇyādavacchinnapramātṛrūpaṃ śiṣyamuttārayatītyudbhāvyate | 
yaduktamāgame -

	kulācāryamadhiṣṭhāya devo dīkṣayitā śivaḥ iti |

yaccoktaṃ śrītantrāloke -

	guruhṛdayaniviṣṭaḥ śaṅkaro'nugrahītā iti |

etena -

	dīkṣayā mucyate jantuḥ prātibhena tathā priye |
	gurvāyattā tu sā dīkṣā baddhabandhanamokṣaṇe ||
	pratibhā svasvabhāvastu kevalībhāvasiddhidaḥ |

iti śrīkiraṇaprakriyayā puṃsāṃ svapratibhāmātraniṣpadyamāna-
mokṣānubhāvānāmapi paryantataḥ kiñciddeśikanāthānugrahā-
vaśyambhāva evetyuktaṃ bhavati | yena parameśvarasvātantryādhīna-
vijṛmbhite viśvasminnetadājñāmantareṇa teṣāṃ tādṛk pratibhaiva na 
saṃpadyate | parameśvarānupraviṣṭaśarīrasyaiva ca bhagavato 
deśikanāthasya dīkṣādyalaukikakriyopakṣepakṣamatvamiti | tasya ca 
kaṭākṣapāte satītyanena kevalaṃ cākṣuṣyeva dīkṣākhyāyate | 
yāvadāṇavaśāktaśāmbhavākhyaprakāratrayānupraviṣṭāstattaddeśa-
kālasvabhāvānuguṇyādyanyatamaucityaśālinaḥ sarve'pi tatprakārāḥ 
parāmṛśyante | yataḥ kaṭākṣo nāma guroranugrāhyaṃ 
pratyanuveśayitumiṣṭā svahṛdayasaṃvinmayī 
śāmbhavyādyaśeṣadīkṣānusyūtā dṛkśaktirityākhyāyate | yadāhuḥ -

	sā ca sarvādhvasampūrṇamātrasaṃvidabhedinā |
	guruṇā'nugrahadhiyā śiṣye yadavalokanam || iti |



tatra gurormantrodbhāvanadvārā śiṣyasya śravaṇamātraprādhānyena 
svarūpasamāveśadāyinī dīkṣāṇavī, kuṇḍalinīśaktyullekhanātmikā 
manovyāpārānusandheyā śāktī, sarvavikalpavaimukhyena 
svasvarūpāvibhinnaśambhusamāveśasvabhāvā śāmbhavīti vivekaḥ | 
evaṃ śaktipātasya tīvratīvrataratvādayo'pi bhedāḥ svayamūhanīyāḥ | 
sarvathā deśikanāthacaraṇaśuśrūṣāmātrānuprāṇanametat 
kaulikasiddhāntānusandhānamiti rahasyam | yaduktaṃ mayaiva 
śrīpādukodaye -

	gurumattaiva naḥ śāstre paramaṃ phalamucyate iti |

yaccoktaṃ śrīmahābhārate -

	ṛtasya dātāramanuttarasya nidhiṃ nidhīnāṃ caturanvayānām |
	ye nādriyante gurumarcanīyaṃ pāpāllokāṃste vrajantyapratiṣṭhān 
|| iti || 67 ||

	nanu srotāṃsi hi catvāri parameśvarapraṇītānyantarvidbhirādriyante 
| atra tvanuttarāmnāyānuśīlanaṃ prācuryāt puruṣārthopāyatayā 
pratyapādi | tadatrāprayojakatvamanyatrānāśvāsaśaṅkā vā prasajyeta | 
yenobhayatrāpyanaucityamityāśaṅkyāha -

	āstāmanyā vidyā catuḥsrotasāmapi sāgarāṇāmiva |
	enamevāmṛtamayaṃ mathnāti manthānabhairavo devaḥ || 68 ||

parameśvaro hi sarvānugrāhakatayā -

	puṣpe gandhastile tailaṃ dehe jīvo jale'mṛtam |
	yathā tathaiva śāstrāṇāṃ kulamantaḥ pratiṣṭhitam ||

iti śrītantrālokasthityā śrutismṛtyādiṣu bāhyavidyāsu 
kaulikyevāvagāhyeti, śrīmatsrotaścatuṣṭaye cainamevārthamupasannaṃ 
janamanubhāvayitumudbhāvayatītyasmanniścayaḥ | yadāśayena -

	sāṃkhyaṃ yogaṃ pāñcarātraṃ vedāṃścaiva na nindayet 

ityāgameṣvākhyāyate | tatra -



	nyāyāgamādi madupekṣitamapyasāraṃ
		svīkṛtya kecidadhikaṃ madamudvahanti |
	nirmālyamujjhitamapi prabhuṇā pṛthivyāṃ
		ceṭyo hi mūrdhni viniveśya paribhramanti ||

iti nītyā bāhyatvenāvabhāsamānatvādanyatayābhimatā 
sāṃkhyayogādisvabhāvā vidyā tāvadāstām, anutthānamevāsyā 
bhavatu | vidyeti jātāvekavacanam | alamatra śrutismṛtyādīnāṃ 
bāhyavidyānāṃ mahārthopāyatayā pratyāyanaprāgalbhyeneti yāvat | 
tacca saṇṇāvisesaṇiṇṇa a (ślo 2) ityatra vistīrya paryālocitam | yāni 
punaralaukikāni vaitatyagāmbhīryākṣobhyatvādisādharmyeṇa 
samudrasthānīyāni catvāri pūrvadakṣiṇādikrameṇa 
parameśvaramukhacatuṣṭayapraṇītāni srotāṃsi 
svacchasvāduśītalābhyantarāhlādarasottaratayā 
svātmasaṃvitpravāharūpā ājñāḥ, teṣāṃ 
saṃbandhinamantastātparyakakṣyārūḍhatayā 
gūḍhasvabhāvamenamevāmṛtamayaṃ svasaṃvidātmanā 
kenacidāhlādena prakṛtamarthātiśayaṃ manthānabhairavātmā devo 
mathnāti | tattatsrotaḥpratipādyārthāntarānādarottaraṃ svātmaśaktyā 
pṛthak piṇḍīkṛtyotthāpayati | etaduktaṃ bhavati - bāhyavidyāsu 
nṛttagītaprabhṛtayo'pi kalāḥ svasphurattānusandhānāvasthāyāṃ 
sahṛdayahṛdayasaṃvidaikāgryalakṣaṇasya camatkārotkarṣasya 
prayojakatayā paryantato mahāntamevārthamunmīlayanti, kimuta 
vedaśāstretihāsapurāṇādayaḥ | kevalaṃ teṣāṃ 
pāramparyādupāyabhūtatvameva bheda ityatra vandhyo 
vākovākyopanyāsaprabandhaḥ | alaukike tu srotaścatuṣṭaye 
jñānayogakriyācaryāprādhānyena pṛthak 
pṛthagupapāditārthāntaraprapañce'pyantato gatvā 
prāpyabhūmikātvenāyamevārtho'vasthāpyate | yaduktamāgame - 
caturājñākośabhūtām iti | yacca vyākhyātamasmatparamagurubhiḥ 
śrīmadṛjuvimarśinyām - catuṣpīṭhādhiṣṭhātṛmahāsaṃvidālambanena 
pravṛttacatuḥsrotorūpā mahāpadavī caturājñā, tasyāḥ kośabhūtā 
mahādhiṣṭhātrī śevadhistām iti | yaccoktaṃ śrīkramakelau - 
vāmadakṣiṇatantrādiṣvapyetanmayameva sarva nirvahatīti mantavyam iti | 
evañca sarvadarśana-



sāratvādamuṣya śrīmahārthakramasya sarvasrototiśāyī ko'pi 
ślāghyatvotkarṣa dhvanyate | yaduktaṃ śrīmahānayaprakāśe -

	evaṃvidhaṃ yadadhigantumihātmatattvaṃ
		mithyāvikalpavibhavoddalanaṃ ca samyak |
	yuktiṃ mahānayamayīṃ na vihāya śakyaṃ
		sarvottamottamatamo hi tato mahārthaḥ || iti |

ata eva hyanyatrāśaraṇānāmetadekaśaraṇatvamatyantadurghaṭaghaṭana-
prāgalbhyodbhaṭamudghāṭyate | yadāhuḥ -

	sarvopāyaparikṣīṇāste mahārthārthinaḥ kila iti |
	asti nānyā gatisteṣāṃ vikalpagrāsasāhasāt iti ca |

yaścāyamevaṃvidhārthamathanapragalbho bhagavān, ata eva 
sarvaparyantapratiṣṭhāsthānarūpo yaḥ kuleśvaraḥ, sa eva yataḥ svaśaktiṃ 
nirmathya sṛṣṭyādikārī, ata evaṃvidhaṃ śrīmanthānaṃ bhairavaṃ 
namāmīti saṃbandhaḥ iti śrīkramakelikḷptyā svecchāmātravijṛmbhita-
viśvakṣobhatayā'nubhūyate | tasya kaulikeṣu tantreṣu bhairava iti prāyo 
vyapadeśo bhavati | bhīravaḥ saṃsāracakitāḥ prāṇinaḥ, 
teṣāmayamabhayapradāyitayā saṃbandhīti vā, bhīḥ saṃsāaracākityaṃ 
tannibandhano ravaḥ prāṇināmākrandastasya pravartaka iti vā, 
pratipāditacākityanivṛttaye manasi parāmṛśyatayā vartata iti vā, bhiyaṃ 
paśusaṃbandhinīmuddiśya ravaṇaṃ yaccha(bdaṃ? bdanaṃ) 
tatsvabhāvānāṃ māhaiśvaryādīnāṃ vargādhiṣṭhātrīṇāṃ svāmīti 
vā, lokasaṃbandhinīṃ bhiyaṃ rauti dātavyatayā parāmṛśatīti 
bhīrurmṛtyukālādiravacchinno bhayaṅkaravargastasyāpi bhayaṅkara iti vā, 
bhāni nakṣatropalakṣitāni candrasūryādīni jyotīṣi tānīrayati prerayatīti 
bheraḥ kālastaṃ vāyayanti śoṣayantīti bheravāḥ kālagrāsarasikā 
mahāyoginasteṣāmadhiṣṭhāteti vā, viśvaṃ prati 
bharaṇaramaṇavasanānāṃ prayojayiteti vā, bhairava ityucyate | yaduktaṃ 
śrīkṣemarājena -



	bhīrūṇāmabhayaprado bhavabhayākrandasya hetustato 
	hṛddhāmni prathitaśca bhīravarucāmīśo'ntakasyāntakaḥ |
	bheraṃ vāyati yaḥ suyoginivahastasya prabhurbhairavo
	viśvasmin bharaṇādikṛd vijayate vijñānarūpaḥ śivaḥ || iti |

sa ca devaḥ, dīvyateḥ krīḍāvijigīṣādyanekārthābhidhānasāmarthyāt | 
tatra heyopādeyatādivikalpavaimukhyena svasaṃvidekaghanatayoccalanaṃ 
krīḍā | sarvotkarṣeṇa vartitumaunmukhyaṃ vijigīṣā | nirvikalpe'pyātmani 
vikalpaprathāparāmarśo vyavahāraḥ | avabhāsyāśeṣaprapañcāva-
bhāsanāvinābhūtā svayamprakāśatā dyutiḥ | svarūpalābhamārabhya 
sarvāmnāyapravṛttiparyavasānāspadatvaṃ stutiḥ | sṛṣṭyādiṣu 
kṛtyeṣvicchājñānakriyātmakaśaktitrayāṅgīkāro gatiriti | taduktaṃ 
mayaiva -

	naiścintyotkaṭamuccalannabhilaṣan sarvottaraṃ vartituṃ
	svasmin bhedamabhedite'pi vimṛśan svātmāviśeṣaṃ sphuran |
	arthānāmudayavyayaprakṛtibhūrucchrāyavānicchayā 
	devo dṛkkriyayoḥ pradīptamahimā bhāveṣu vo dīvyatu || 68 ||

	nanu kathamatyantabhinnamantratantrapaddhatipāramparyādi-
prakārasyāpyasya srotaścatuṣṭayasya phalotpattiṃ pratyetadaika-
rūpyamityākāṅkṣāmadhikṣipan prakrāntasya mahātantrasya 
niṣkṛṣṭamarthatattvaṃ saṃgraheṇodghāṭayitumāha -

	hanta rahasyaṃ bhaṇāmo muḍhā ! mā bhramata garbhagoleṣu |
	atyāsannaṃ hṛdayaṃ paryālocayata tasyodyogam || 69 ||

	he mūḍhāḥ ! māyāmohamālinyakajjalakaluṣitātmānaḥ, tata eva 
śarīrādyahantānusandhānavantaḥ pramātāraḥ ! vayamete kecana 
deśikakaṭākṣapātapratyakṣitātmaiśvaryocchrāyāḥ 
parānujighṛkṣāveśavaivaśyākrāntacittavṛttayaśca santaḥ -

	tadetat paramaṃ gṛhyaṃ yoginīnāṃ mukhe sthitam |



iti sthityā gopyamarthatattvaṃ bhaṇāmo vaikharīvākparyantaṃ 
parāmṛśāmaḥ | anyābhimukhīkārāduktarūpasaṃrambhopanyāso hi 
tanmanasi tādṛkparāmarśārpaṇaṃ vinā na saṅgacchate | anyathā 
vaikharyā vaiyarthyaprasaṅgādityāmantryamāṇān 
puruṣaviśeṣānarthatattvaṃ kiñcit parāmarśayāma ityuktaṃ bhavati | tatra 
bhaṇitavyamarthamavasthāpya madhye tadaparijñānopādhikamupa-
dravādhikyaṃ tatparijñānaprarocanātātparyeṇopapādyate - mā bhramata 
garbhagoleṣviti | kimiti jananījaṭharagahvarāntarvāsakleśamanu-
bhavatā'nāyāsena kasmiṃścit tatpratīkāropāye vidyamāne'pīti yāvat | 
mā bhramateti garbhagahvarabhramaṇaniṣedhābhidhānena 
vakṣyamāṇasyārthasya tatpratibandhapāṭavaṃ pratyavyabhicāro vyajyate | 
garbhavāso hyupadravānubhūtīnāṃ parā bhūmiḥ | bhramaṇena ca tasya 
mātāpitṛsahasrāṇi iti nyāyādanekakālānuvṛttatvamākṣipyate | tacca 
jananam - jātasya hi dhruvo mṛtyuḥ iti bhagavadgītānītyā, vināśāghrāta 
utpādaḥ iti śrīmatkṣemarājoktyā ca maraṇānubhūtyavinābhūtaṃ 
tadubhayāntarbhūtaṃ cākhilamapi duḥkhānubhūtibāhulyamiti 
janmamayyā pīḍayā sarvamapi saṃsāravyasanamupasaṃgṛhyate | 
yadāśayenoktaṃ śrīlaghubhaṭṭārake -

	jñātvetthaṃ na punaḥ spṛśanti jananīgarbhe'rbhakatvaṃ narāḥ iti |

	atha bhaṇitavyamevārtha bhaṇitumasyātyantasulabhatvopapādana-
dvārā pīṭhikāṃ badhnāti - atyāsannaṃ hṛdayamiti | yadidaṃ 
ṣaḍadhvollāsasamaṣṭirūpasya svaśarīrasyānuprāṇanatayā'vatiṣṭha-
mānamavikalpāvasthāyāṃ taduttirṇollekhaṃ ca 
satprakāśavimarśadvitayamelāpalakṣaṇamantastattvaṃ hṛdayamityucyate, 
tasya parvatāgranadītīrādivanna kadācidanāsattiśaṅkā sambhavati, 
svasvarūpāvibhinnatvādetadāsattiṃ pratyatiśayasyānubhūyamānatvāt | 
tādṛśasya cāsya ca udyogo yataḥ kutaścidapyākārādaudāsīnyā-
pahastanasvabhāvamudyantṛtvam, tat parita icchājñānakriyāparispanda-
pravartitāśeṣaviśvavyavahāraprathāparāmarśapūrvakamālocayata 
ātmānukūlyādavalokayadhvam | yasminnekatraiva sādhye catuḥsrotaḥ-
pravartitā mantratantrādayaḥ prabhañjanābhivyañjana iva 
vyajanavātāyana-



vastrāñcalabhastrikāphūtkāraprabhṛtayo bahuprakāramupāyā 
upapādyante | yena ca kṣaṇamātrānubaddhenāpi jananamaraṇādya-
śeṣopadravavyudāsaśālī jīvanmokṣalakṣmīmahopabhogaḥ saṃpadyate | 
sa codyogaḥ kadācidindriyaparispandānubandhī kriyāśaktisphārarūpo 
rūparasādiviṣayagrahaṇakautuhalād bahiḥ prameyaśayyāmadhiśayya 
vyākṣepavibhramamanubhavati, kadācicca jñānaśaktyavaṣṭabdho 
viṣayebhyaḥ pratyāvṛtya svātmamātraviśrāntisvabhāvāṃ 
pratyagānandasampadamupabhuṅkte | yadubhayaṃ daṇḍamuṇḍa-
kramātmakatayā śāstreṣvanuśiṣyate | yaduktamāgame -

	muṇḍanaṃ jñānarūpeṇa daṇḍanaṃ ca kriyātmanā |
	muṇḍadaṇḍakramau tena matau jñānakriyātmakau || iti |

staimityādirūpā vyavasthā hṛdayasya kaścidudyamaprakāraviśeṣaḥ | 
yaduktaṃ lakṣmītantre -

	stimitaṃ yat paraṃ brahma tasya stimitatāsmyaham iti |

tādṛśasya ca hṛdayasyodyogenākrānte lokavyavahāre -

	tasmāt tattvamatattvaṃ vā na bhānena vinā bhavet |
	svasāmarājyavaśād bhānaṃ tat tattvātattvayoḥ samam ||
	yathā yathā prathā puṃsaḥ śāmbhavī sā ca nāparā |

ityādinītyā grāhyaṃ viṣayaṃ prati niṣiddhatvamaniṣiddhatvaṃ veti 
tāratamyacintāyā na kasyāścidapyavakāśaḥ, yato 
vidhiniṣedhaviṣayayordhamādharmayorālocyamānayoḥ 
sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvādadhikārivibhāgavaicitryādanyonyāviruddhāneka-
śāstramaryādābhedavyavasthāpanānuprāṇanatvācca na 
kiñcinnaiyatyamālokyata iti sarvaśrāvyo'rthaḥ | aṣaḍakṣīṇastu -

	dhīkarmākṣagatāṃ devīṃ niṣiddhaireva tarpayet |

iti rahasyāmnāyanyāyādanyaśāstravigarhitāni mahilāmadyamāṃsādī-
nyeva dravyāṇyasmadupāsyānāṃ devatānāṃ saparyāsu 
paribarhatayā'bhyarhitānīti | yaduktaṃ śrīmahānayaprakāśe -



	anyairāvārakatvena ye bhāvāḥ parivarjitāḥ |
	taireva jñānināmarthaṃ jājvalīti parā citiḥ || iti |

prapañcitaprāyaṃ caitat prāgevetyalaṃ romanthānānubandhena | 
bhramatetyādi loṭpratyayena -

	tāteti kiñcit tanayeti kiñci-
		nmameti kiñcinna mameti kiñcit |
	taveti kiñcinna taveti kiñcid
		bhautaṃ svayaṃ bahudhā mālapestvam || iti |

iti śrīmadālasoktiyuktyā māyāvyāmūḍhacetasaṃ paśujanaṃ prati 
śocanīyatā dyotyate | hantetyanena tu tantrakṛtastadaparimṛjyamanyaṃ janaṃ 
pratyamandaḥ kāruṇyotkarṣa iti || 69 ||

	athāsmatprasādhitamauttaramarthatattvamevānyeṣāṃ 
vyāsādīnāmapi mahāmunīnāmantaranusandheyā devatā, yena 
tatpraṇīteṣu prabandheṣu tātparyaparyālocanāyāṃ mahatā 
kaṇṭhenaitadevārtharahasyamudghāṭyate | yathā 
bhagavadgītāsūpaniṣatsvityuccāvacasyāsya prapañcavaicitryasya 
śrīmadanuttarāmnāyasamudraśīkaropasneha 
evānuprāṇanatayā'vatiṣṭhata ityāsūtrayitumāha -

	enameva mahārtha yuddhārambhe pāṇḍuputrasya |
	ṣoḍaśasahasraśaktirdeva upadiśati mādhava iti śivam || 70 ||

	yo'yaṃ mahāprabandhenopakrāntaḥ -

	bhāvārthaḥ sampradāyārtho nigarbhārthaśca kaulikaḥ |
	tathā sarvarahasyārtho mahātattvārtha eva ca ||

ityāmnāyasthityā tattadaśeṣārthatattvavaicitryasamaṣṭyadhiṣṭhāna-
rūpatayā mahān sarvārthabhedaprabhedakroḍīkāravicakṣaṇo'rthaḥ 
prāpyaṃ tattvam, yaśca -



	na sannāsanna sadasanna ca tannobhayātmakam |
	durvijñeyā hi sāvasthā kimapyetadanuttaram ||
	naiṣa dhyeyo dhyātrabhāvānna dhyātā dhyānavarjanāt |
	na pūjyaḥ pūjakābhāvāt pūjābhāvānna pūjakaḥ ||

iti śrītantrālokasthityā vikalpavārtāsārthasarvasvottīrṇasvasvātantryaika-
ghanatopabṛṃhitasvabhāvo'nubhūyate, tamenameva ṣoḍaśasahasraśaktiḥ 
ṣoḍaśādhikā(?) vilāsalakṣaṇamakālakalitaṃ śrīkālasaṃkarṣaṇī-
bhāvamanubhavan ata eva devaḥ krīḍādyanekaparispandapragalbho mādhavo 
mahālakṣmīvallabho madhukulottasaśca bhagavāan yuddhārambhe 
kauravapāṇḍavasenāsaṃgharṣopakramāvasthāyāṃ 
pāṇḍuputrasyārjunasyopadiśati upādikṣaditi yāvat | prākṛtabhāṣāyāṃ 
bhūtavartamānādilakāranaiyatyābhāvāt | yadvā bhagavatā 
pratiyugamevamasya bhāratādivyāpārasya pravartyamānatvāt 
pravāhanityatayā vartamānatvamiti laṭprayogaḥ | tatra yuddhārambha 
ityanena -

	tān samīkṣya sa kauravyaḥ sarvān bandhūnavasthitān |
	kṛpayā parayāviṣṭo viṣīdannidamabravīt ||

ityupakramya

	aho bata mahat pāpaṃ kartu vyavasitā vayam |
	yad rājyasukhalobhena hantuṃ svajanamudyatāḥ ||
	yadi māmapratīkāramaśastraṃ śatrapāṇayaḥ |
	dhārtarāṣṭrā raṇe hanyustanme kṣemataraṃ bhavet ||

ityantamarjunasya prācīnānekavāsanānusyūtāt pāśavāveśāt 
śrautasmārtādibāhyāmnāyaniṣiddhaṃ pitṛpitāmahādisvajanahanana-
lakṣaṇaṃ pātakaṃ prati vicikitsā, tatpravṛttaṃ ca rājyalābhādyaśeṣa-
saukhyavairāgyam, tannibandhano vaiklavyodayaśca dhvanyate | mādhava 
ityanenārjunena sahāsya kiñcid yaunaṃ sambandhamunmī(layitvā ? lya) -



	kutastvā kaśmalamidaṃ viṣame samupasthitam |
	anāryajuṣṭamasvargyamakīrtikaramarjuna ! ||
	mā klaibyaṃ gaccha kaunteya ! naitat tvayyupapadyate |
	kṣudraṃ hṛdayadaurbalyaṃ tyaktvottiṣṭha paraṃtapa ! ||

iti taṃ prati bhagavato rahasyārthatattvapratyabhijñāpanaunmukhyam | 
anantaramasyaiva -

	kārpaṇyadoṣopahatasvabhāvaḥ pṛcchāmi tvāṃ 
dharmasammūḍhacetāḥ |
	yacchreyaḥ syānniścitaṃ brūhi tanme śiṣyaste'haṃ śādhi māṃ 
tvāṃ prapannam ||

iti kārpaṇyalakṣaṇānāthyapravṛttāt śiṣyabhāvād devasya 
kāruṇyākrāntahṛdayatā,

	ya enaṃ vetti hantāraṃ yaścainaṃ manyate hatam |
	ubhau tau na vijānīto nāyaṃ hanti na hanyate ||

ityādinā bhīṣmadroṇādyaśeṣaśarīrāntarbhūtasyātmano 
nityatvavyāpakatvādiyogādanyajanahanyamānatvādyasaṃbhavod-
bhāvanadvārā laukikavat kiṃ bāhyaśāstrabibhīṣakayā 
kātaryamanubhavasīti tasyoparyanugrahodrekaśconmudryate | 
ṣoḍaśasahasraśaktirityanena bahiṣṣoḍaśastrīsahasrasambhogamivāntarapi 
ṣoḍaśādhikā(?) parāmarśaprābalyopārūḍhaṃ 
tattadavāntaraśaktisahasrasamāveśamāvedayatā devasya-

	darśayāmāsa pārthāya paramaṃ rūpamaiśvaram |

iti vaiśvarūpyapradarśanaucityam,

	kālo'smi lokakṣayakṛt pravṛddho lokān samāhartumiha pravṛttaḥ |

ityātmano māhātmyaprakhyāpanaprācaṇḍyam,

	namo namaste'stu sahasrakṛtvaḥ punaśca bhūyo'pi namo namaste |

ityarjunastutisahasrasandhukṣyamāṇatejaskatvāt 

	ityarjunaṃ vāsudevastathoktvā svakaṃ rūpaṃ darśayāmāsa 
bhūyaḥ |
	āśvāsayāmāsa ca bhītamenaṃ bhūtvā punaḥ 
saumyavapurmahātmā ||



iti bhūyaḥ pūrvasvabhāvapratyānayanam,

	atha cet tvamahaṅkārānna śroṣyasi vinaṅkṣyasi |

ityātmoktārthānanuṣṭhāne mahāvipadrūpapratyavāyopapādanaṃ 
cetyanyaduṣkarāṇyāścaryāṇi karmāṇyākhyāyante | deva ityetat 
krīḍādyanekārthābhidhānamukhena -

	raso'hamapsu kaunteya ! prabhā'smi śaśisūryayoḥ |
	praṇavaḥ sarvavedeṣu śabdaḥ khe pauruṣaṃ nṛṣu ||

ityādinā prapañcena tasya bahirvibhūtiparispandāṭopam,

	sarvasya cāhaṃ hṛdi sanniviṣṭo mattaḥ smṛtirjñānamapohanaṃ ca |
	vedaiśca sarvairahameva vedyo vedāntakṛd vedavideva cāham ||

ityuttarasphuraṇaprakāraṃ cāha | mādhavapāṇḍuputraśabdābhyāṃ 
bhagavadarjunayorubhayorbāhyavāsanāvailakṣaṇyābhāve'pi -

	bahūni me vyatītāni janmāni tava cārjuna |
	tānyahaṃ veda sarvāṇi na tvaṃ vettha parantapa ||

ityādyasya sārvajñyādiśaktyaulbaṇyamanyasya 
tadviparyayaścetyanayoranugrāhyānugrāhakabhāvaucityamāsūcyate | 
upadiśatītyetat sarvatrāṣṭādaśādhyāyānusyūtamaṅgāṅgi-
bhāvabhaṅgyā bāhyābhyantarobhayaśāstratattvatātparyāspadaṃ 
sāṃkhyayogavedāntādivaicitryavyatireke'pi -

	api cet sudurācāro bhajate māmananyabhāk |
	sādhureva sa mantavyaḥ samyag vyavasito hi saḥ ||

iti,

	manmanā bhava madbhakto madyājī māṃ namaskuru |
	māmevaiṣyasi satyaṃ te pratijāne priyo'si me ||

iti ca svātmadevatāmātropāstiprādhānyamabhivyanakti | 
mahārthamityanena -

	yo māṃ paśyati sarvatra sarvaṃ ca mayi paśyati |
	tasyāhaṃ na praṇaśyāmi sa ca me na praṇaśyati ||



	api cedasi pāpebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ pāpakṛttamaḥ |
	sarvaṃ jñānaplavenaiva vṛjinaṃ santariṣyasi ||
	sarvadharmān parityajya māmekaṃ śaraṇaṃ vraja |
	ahaṃ tvā sarvapāpebhyo mokṣayiṣyāmi mā śucaḥ ||
	nehābhikramanāśo'sti pratyavāyo na vidyate |
	svalpamapyasya darmasya trāyate mahato bhayāt ||
	iti te jñānamākhyātaṃ guhyād guhyataraṃ mayā |
	vimṛśyaitadaśeṣeṇa yathecchasi tathā kuru ||

iti bhagavataḥ phalgunaprabodhanāvasthāyāṃ śrīmadanuttarasrotaḥ-
prasādhitārtha prakhyāpanapāṇḍityaṃ pratāyate | mādhavaḥ 
pāṇḍuputrāyopadiśatīti guruśiṣyabhāvodbhāvanena -

	imaṃ vivasvate yogaṃ proktavānahamavyayam |
	vivasvān manave prāha manurikṣvākave'bravīt ||
	evaṃ paramparāprāptamimaṃ rājarṣayo viduḥ |
	sa kāleneha mahatā yogo naṣṭaḥ parantapa ! ||
	sa evāyaṃ mayā te'dya yogaḥ proktaḥ purātanaḥ |
	bhakto'si me sakhā ceti rahasyaṃ hyetaduttamam ||

iti guruparvakramātmanaḥ sambandhasyāvaśyānusandheyatā dyotyate | 
pāṇḍuputrasyetyarjunasya kulīnatvādyavinābhūtaṃ saujanyādiguṇa-
yogamulliṅgayatā -

	idaṃ te nātapaskāya nābhaktāya kadācana |
	na cāśuśrūṣave vācyaṃ na ca māṃ yo'bhyasūyati ||

ityayogyaṃ śiṣyaṃ pratyetadarthaprakāśanasyāvihitatvam, yogyaṃ prati tu 
kalpyamānasya puruṣārthaparyavasāyitvaṃ copapādyata iti |

	pārāśaryo mahāyogī dhairyagāmbhīrasāgaraḥ |
	bhārate bhagavadgītāmadhikṛtyedamabravīt ||



	yat kurukṣetramākramya dhārtarāṣṭreṣu dhanviṣu |
	pāṇḍaveṣu ca sajjeṣu saṃgṛhyākṣauhiṇīṃ kṣaṇāt ||
	pitṝn pitāmahān bhrātṝn putrān pautrān gurūnapi |
	hantavyānātmahastena prekṣya vaiklavyavihvalam ||
	trasyantaṃ karmaṇaḥ krūrādavadhūtāhavodyamam |
	bībhatsamānaṃ bībhatsuṃ nispṛhaṃ rājyasampadi ||
	anusandhāya bhagavān mukundo rukmiṇīpatiḥ |
	kāruṇyākrāntahṛdayaḥ syandanasthaṃ tamabhyadhāt |
	hanta kiṃ tava saṃvṛttamakāṇḍe kaśmalottaram |
	vaiklabyaṃ tyajyatāmetallokadvayavigarhitam ||
	kaḥ pitā tava ko bhrātā ko guruḥ ke ca bāndhavāḥ |
	tvameva tāvat ko nāma kāruṇyaṃ nāma kiṃ tava ||
	pātrametasya kaśca syāt kena ko vā'bhihanyate |
	bahvyaḥ sāmānyato bhāṣāḥ kalpyante lokaśāstrayoḥ ||
	viśeṣamaparijñāya tābhyo mā bhūd bibhīṣikā |
	iti lokottarāmarthamudrāmunmudrayan kramāt ||
	prasaṅgānuprasaṅgena viśvavikṣobhakalpanām |
	akālakalitasvacchasvacchandānandacinmayīm ||
	niścinvanniścalasvātmasaṃrambhaikavijṛmbhitām |
	uparyudriktakāruṇyo vīrendraḥ savyasācini ||
	kālagrāsaikarasikāṃ kālasaṅkarṣṇīṃ kalām |
	anupraviśya yogena khecarīkhacitaujasā ||
	svabalākramaṇāṭopavyāptakaulakramo bhavan |
	dattvā lokottarāṃ tasmai dṛṣṭimaskhalitārciṣam ||
	prāṃśuḥ pradarśayāñcakre vaiśvarūpyamanugrahāt |
	bāhavo yatra koṭīṇāṃ koṭayaḥ sphuradaṅgadāḥ ||




	sahasrāṇāṃ sahasrāṇi murdhnā ca mukuṭaspṛśām |
	yadantaḥ sāgarāḥ sarve sarve ca kulaparvatāḥ ||
	kānanāni ca sarvāṇi sarvā sarvaṃsahā'pyabhūt |
	bhīṣmadroṇakṛpadrauṇikarṇaduryodhanādayaḥ ||
	śalabhā iva yadvaktrajvālāmālambya śerate |
	tadālokya sasantrāsaṃ sāścarya ca dhanañjayam ||
	abhragambhīranirghoṣo bhagavānabhyabhāṣata |
	paśya viśvaṃ mayā grastamākīṭādāpitāmaham ||
	vilīyamānaṃ jihvāgre jyotirjvālākalāpini |
	yatra kṣaṇādakhaṇḍānāṃ brahmāṇḍānāṃ paramparāḥ ||
	samudrabudbudanyāyādunmiṣanti truṭanti ca |
	atra bhīṣmamito droṇamiha śalyamitaḥ kṛpam ||
	paśya bhasmīkṛtān bandhūn mayyakṣayyamahārciṣi |
	majjvālālīḍhasāraṃ saccarācaramidaṃ jagat ||
	prācīnaṃ vāsanāśeṣaṃ dagdhaṃ vastramivāśnute |
	tadutīṣṭha jahi svairamāttasārān mahīpatīn ||
	yena te tīkṣṇabāṇasya doṣṇoḥ śaurya prathiṣyate |
	iti phalgunamākarṣannātmaiśvaryavijṛmbhayā ||
	kramādakramacicchaktisvātantryanmīlanakṣamaiḥ |
	avadhīritavedādibāhyavidyāviḍambanaiḥ ||
	ācakṣāṇairavaiṣamyaṃ karmākarmavikarmaṇām |
	vikalpakalpanollāsagrāsatātparyayogibhiḥ ||
	niṣiddhasyāpyanuṣṭhāne vihitasyāpyatikrame |
	pratyavāyaṃ vyudasyadbhiḥ svacchasatsaṃvidāṃ nṛṇām ||



	laukike'pyupayuktānāṃ dharmāṇāmanumantṛbhiḥ |
	svasaṃviddevatāmātrasaparyāpāradṛśvabhiḥ ||
	cittasyāntarniṣiñcadbhiradvaitāmṛtacandrikām |
	unmūlayadbhirāmūlaṃ māyāmālinyavāsanām ||
	saṃcchidya saṃśayātaṅkamarpayadbhirahaṃprathām |
	sthāpayadbhiranāhārya prātibhaṃ sthairyamāśaye ||
	citsvabhāvatayā sthātuṃ smārayadbhiḥ svamudyamam |
	pramāṇairyuktibhistarkairanuvādairnidarśanaiḥ ||
	āśaṅkottaracarcābhiranyaiśca gamakaiḥ kramaiḥ |
	prakāśayadbhirviśvasya pratiṣṭhāmauttaraṃ kramam ||
	spaṣṭairavyākulairvākyairanyonyānvayabandhuraiḥ |
	bahudhā puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ pāṇḍavaṃ pratyabodhayat ||
	so'pi tatkṣaṇamārabhya svavimarśavicakṣaṇaḥ |
	lakṣmīmanubhavan sākṣājjīvanmokṣaprathāmayīm ||
	kṣapayitvā vipakṣaṃ taṃ cicchaktyā tīvravikramaḥ |
	agrajaṃ ca pratiṣṭhāpya svasya rājye yudhiṣṭhiram ||
	svasaṃrambhānuguṇyena svairaṃ vyavaharan bahiḥ |
	kālaṃ mahāntaṃ niścinto vijahāra viśṛṅkhalaḥ ||
	etad vitatya vikhyātaiḥ kramakelau kulāgame |
	nāthābhinavaguptāryaiḥ paryālocitamādarāt ||
	evaṃ rāmāyaṇe kāvye vālmīkīye'pi dhīmatā |
	praharantaṃ śarīreṣu na te paśyanti rāghavam ||
	indriyārtheṣu tiṣṭhantaṃ bhūtātmānamiva prajāḥ |
	ityādisvātmavisphūrtipradhānaṃ svayamuhyatām || iti |

vyāpāropaskṛtābhidhāvyañjanādyanekaśabdaśaktivaicitryārpitaṃ 
saṃkṣepavistaratvānyataro-

śivasvabhāvāvibhinnatvācca śivamayameva | atra ca ṇami ūṇa 
ṇiccasuddhe guluṇo calaṇe (ślo0 1) ityārabhya devo uvadisa ī māhavo tti

	itthamupapāditena prakāreṇa yāni prākṛtāni saṃskṛtavyatirekeṇa

śaṅkitamantaryāgamanupraviṣṭām | svapno hi sphuṭāsphuṭarūpaḥ |

sphuṭatayopalabhyamānāyāmavasthāyām aparokṣitāmiti yāvat | 
pratijñottarāṃ pūjopakaraṇadakṣiṇādyadhikṣipya svahastopakalpitā 
mudraiva kevalamanvarthīkriyatāmiti yeyemāgūḥ, tayā 
mahāntamutkarṣamaśnuvānām | atha ca lokasya vedyavargalakṣaṇasya 
yadullaṅghanaṃ veditṛsvabhāvamātrapāriśeṣyeṇāvasthānam, tatra 
yogyāyā aucityavatyāḥ kaulikaiśvaryānubhūtirūpāyāḥ siddheryā 
padavī durgamatvadīrghatvādiparihāṇyā sadgurūpadarśitā śuddhā

	itthamicchākriyājñānaśaktiśulāmbujāśrayaḥ |

śarīrāhantādhivāsitātmā parimitaḥ pramātā | yaścittamayo

yoginīṃ vanda ityakṣarārthaḥ | prākṛteti | saṃskṛtaṃ hi prakṛtiraśeṣasya

prākṛtamityanenabhāṣāntarātmakavikṛtiśilpavaidagdhyasvīkāraḥ, 
prakṛtisauṣṭhavaparicayāpari

apabhraṃśātmakatvāt tasyāḥ | saṃskṛtavyatirekeṇānyā sarvāpi 
bhāṣāpyapabhraṃśaḥ -

	śāstreṣu saṃskṛtādanyadapabhraṃśatayocyate |

ityuktatvāditi cet ? na | svātmaparameśvaraparāmarśamapahāyānyatra

yatprayogāt ekaḥ śabdaḥ samyag jñātaḥ suṣṭhu prayuktaḥ svarge loke

ityantarvidbhirvyavasthāpyamānatvāt | svapnasamayasākṣātkṛtāyā

asmadupāsyavidyānusandhāne prāyaḥ prākṛtasyaivaucityamujjṛmbhate |

	saptakoṭirmahāmantrā mahākālīmukhodgatāḥ |

ityāmnāyanyāyādekaikakoṭikroḍīkārasūcanārthamekaikadaśaka-

śrīmanmahārthakramaprapañcaḥ sarvo'pi vṛndacakre viśrāmyati | tacca 
catuṣṣaṭiśaktisamaṣṭyātmakamiti vyākhyātam | tatra -

	dhāmamudrāvarṇakalāsaṃvidbhāvasvabhāvataḥ |
	pātāniketadṛṣṭyā ca 					  ||

ityupavarṇitayā bhaṅgyā śāmbhavasiddhādiṣu vyomeśyādīnāṃ pāta 
iti pātakrameṇa vyomeśyādipañcakasvīkārasyāparihāryatvam | tadvat

		vāṇīviśeṣaparimelanapūrvaparva |
	tvadvandanaṃ tripurasundari ! viśvavandyaṃ
		tadvat paraṃ vijayate mahānīyasāram || iti || 71 ||
	āyātiratha tantrasya kathyate kaulikoditā |
	yāmākarṇya pumānatra vimarśaucityamaśnute ||
	purā kadācid bhagavān bhairavo viśvabhāvanaḥ |
	saṃvidākāśamāsthāya mahāntaṃ maṇimaṇḍapam ||
	svacittacaṣakāpūrṇamāpiban viṣayāsavam |
	svānandabhogalaharīṃ svasaṃvedyāṃ parāmṛśan ||
	sadāśivādibhiḥ śiṣyaiḥ sevyamāno madojjhitaiḥ |



	āsāñcakre ciraṃ kālaṃ nirvikalpe nije pade ||
	tatra kriyāyāṃ suptāyāṃ viśrāntāyāṃ ca saṃvidi |
	icchāśaktirabhūdekā prabuddhā parameṣṭhinaḥ ||
	praśāntabāhyasaṃrambhaṃ cakramālakṣya sā tadā |
	tattvamarthasya niścetuṃ jagrāhāntaḥ kutūhalam ||
	atha puṣpāñjaliṃ kṣiptvā saurabhodbhrāntaṣaṭpadam |
	pāṇibhyāṃ padmatāmrābhyāṃ pasparśa padayoḥ prabhum ||
	devo'pi kiñcidunmīlya camatkārottaraṃ jvalan |
	kāruṇyavarṣibhirnetraiḥ preyasīṃ tāmudaikṣata ||
	praṇipatya ca sā bhūyaḥ kalpitāñjalikuḍmalā |
	śucismitā smarārātiṃ babhāṣe śaśibhūṣaṇam ||
	deva ! tvadvadanādeva gopitānyanyadarśane ||
	prākpratyaguttarāvāñci srotāṃsi śrutavatyaham ||
	kintu praṣṭavyametanme kāruṇyaṃ yadi te hṛdi |
	kiṃ tattvamatra sarvatra yajjñānāt pūrṇatā bhavet ||
	iti śrutvā giraṃ devyāḥ śrīmān svacchandabhairavaḥ |
	mānayan mānanīyāṃ tāṃ pṛṣṭaṃ sumukhi ! suṣṭhviti ||
	pavitraṃ svahaviśśeṣaṃ grāhayitvā smitottaram |
	antarlakṣe pade tiṣṭhan babhāṣe tattvamauttaram ||
	yathā syāt svātmanaḥsphūrtiryathā tasya bahiḥ prathā |
	yathā ca mohaḥ prabhraśyed deśikendre prasīdati ||
	prakṣālitamalaścāsau viśuddhiṃ svāṃ parāmṛśan |
	yathā ca nityaṃ bhuñjīta jīvanmukticamatkriyām ||
	tathā tathā śivastasyāścaitanyamupabṛṃhayan |
	arthatattvamupādikṣadauttarāmnāyasaṃvidām ||
	kramaśaḥ śṛṇvatī sā ca rahasyaṃ tattvaniścayam |
	vimṛśantī ca viśadaṃ svaspandānandacinmayīm ||



	viśvasya svātmānaścaikyaṃ pratyabhijñāya vāstavam |
	tena bhairavanāthena tādātmyāmodabhāgabhūt ||
	atha sā kālayogena śivānandasya dhīmataḥ |
	śiṣyasyopādiśad devī cidadvaitasya niścayam ||
	krameṇa tacca nāthānāṃ paripāṭyā bhuvaḥ sthalam |
	divyasiddhamanuṣyaughapravibhāgādavātarat ||
	avatīrṇā'pyasau vidyā mahārthakramagarbhiṇī |
	yogināṃ vadaneṣveva tiṣṭhatyatyantadurlabhā ||
	atha kālakramavaśāccoladeśaśiromaṇiḥ |
	mahāprakāśo nāmāsīd deśiko dṛkkriyottaraḥ ||
	tasya śiṣyo'bhavad dhīmān gorakṣo nāma vaśyavāk |
	maheśvarānanda iti prāptapūjyāhvayomahān ||
	arcayan devatāṃ nityaṃ japan dhyāyaṃśca niścalam |
	paryaṭaṃśca diśāmantān kālaṃ kañcidavāhayat ||
	athaikadā niśīthinyāmāsīno yāgamaṇḍape |
	tarpayitvā parāṃ devīṃ gandhapuṣpākṣatāsavaiḥ ||
	āsvādyānandapātrāṇi trīṇi tīvrāṇi tanmanāḥ |
	svasaṃrambhaparāmarśabhavyāmanubhavan prathām ||
	jāgarāsvapnayormadhyamadhyāsya mahatīṃ daśām |
	dūtyāḥ stanataṭotsaṅgamaparāṅgena pīḍayan ||
	pradīpaiḥ kuśalaireva pradīptairaparokṣitaḥ |
	āste sma vismayākrāntaḥ kahlārotphullalocanaḥ ||
	atrāntare striyaṃ kāñcit kanthāśūlakapālinīm |
	sa dadarśa kilollokāṃ sindūrālaṅkṛtālikām ||
	ālokya ca sa tāṃ siddhāṃ kurvannāsannamāsanam |
	upāharadudāraśrīḥ pūjopakaraṇaṃ kramāt ||
	dakṣiṇāṃ ca yathāśakti dātuṃ dūtīṃ samādiśat |



	kruddheva yoginī sā ca kimebhiriti niḥspṛhā ||
	mahārāṣṭrabhuvaṃ bhāṣāṃ prayuñjānā smitottaram |
	saptasaṃkhyocitāṃ mudrāṃ badhnatī hastapallave ||
	alamarthairiyaṃ kanthā vasordhārāṃ hi varṣati |
	pradīyatāmiyaṃ mudrā phalaṃ ca pratipādyatām ||
	itthamābhāṣamāṇaiva sakapālena pāṇinā |
	spṛśantī mastakaṃ tasya niśśaṅkaṃ sā tirodadhe ||
	atha tanmahadāścaryamaśnuvāno mahāmanāḥ |
	āracayyārcanāśeṣamaśeṣāmanayanniśām ||
	prātargurukulaṃ gatvā praṇamya caraṇau guroḥ |
	rātrivṛttāntamācakhyau prāñjaliḥ praśritaiḥ padaiḥ ||
	deśikendro'pi saṃcintya niścityārtha ca tatkṣaṇam |
	puṇyotsava iti prītaḥ śiṣyaṃ śrīmānabhāṣata ||
	alamarthaprapañcena piṇḍito'rthaḥ prakāśyate |
	alamarthairiti prāha yadiyaṃ siddhayoginī ||
	yacca saptocitāṃ saṃkhyāṃ kurvāṇā karakuḍmale |
	saphalīkriyatāmeṣā bhāvajñenetyabhāṣata |
	tadārthī sṛṣṭimullaṅghya śābdīṃ sā kāñcidicchati |
	yena mantrātmakaiḥ śabdaiḥ parameśvaryupāsyate ||
	saptakoṭīśvarī devī tayā nūnamupāsyate |
	anyathā tādṛśīmeva mudrāṃ na pratipādayet ||
	tat tvayātra vidhātavyā sphītasārasvataśriyā |
	sūtrāṇāṃ saptatistantre mahārthe mantragarbhiṇī ||
	sadyastvadvadanāt tasmāt pādukodayaśodhitāt |
	purātanāgamaprakhyo granthaḥ prakhyāyatāṃ mahān ||
	kiñca bhāṣā tadīyaiva mādhuryāmṛtavarṣiṇī |
	aucityaṃ poṣayatyatra mahāmantrānusāriṇī ||



	ityājñāṃ deśikendrasya dayālormūrdhni dharayan |
	mahārthamañjarīṃ nāma saṃviddarpaṇamaṇḍalam ||
	tantraṃ dinaiḥ katipayaiḥ prababandha svatantradhīḥ |
	kāryārambho hi mahatāmavilambena sidhyati ||
	tacca tattvavidaṃ loke vedaśāstrakalāsvapi |
	mahārthasindhumanthānaṃ śrāvayāmāsa deśikam ||
	svayameva ca tāṃ vidyāṃ svavimarśakutūhalāt |
	śiṣyāṇāmapi nirbandhād vyācacakṣe vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
	yathā hi puṣpama~jaryā grāhyaḥ parimalo bhavet |
	tadvadasyāmapi grāhyā vyākhyā parimalāhvayā ||
	asyāmarthasthitiḥ saiva yā sarvatra kulāgame |
	kintu śabdasya śayyā'nyā nātyantaṃ sā vibhidyate ||
	śuddho janmasvanekeṣu yaḥ kaścijjāyate kṛtī |
	enāmupadiśantyasmai yoginyo bhāgyaśāline ||
	anayaiva śivo viṣṇurbrahmānye'pi mahaujasaḥ |
	antarvimṛṣṭayā śuddhāḥ svādhikāreṣu jāgrati ||
	imāmeva vimṛśyāntarvāmadevaśukādayaḥ |
	adhyatiṣṭhan parāṃ kāṣṭhāṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ sarvasampadām ||
	imāmeva ca saṃgrāme bandhuhatyāparāṅmukham |
	mukundo bodhayāmāsa syandanasthaṃ dhanañjayam ||
	kṣaṇamālocitā'pyeṣā jīvanmuktiṃ prayacchati |
	uparyuparyanusyūtāstattadaiśvaryasampadaḥ ||
	na caināmarhati prāptuṃ krūraḥ kauṭilyavān khalaḥ |
	pramatto matsarī bhīrurāmayāvī madoddhataḥ ||
	nāstikaḥ svalpadhīrduḥkhī darśanāntaratatparaḥ |
	gurubhaktivinirmuktaḥ kṛtaghno durmanāḥ śaṭhaḥ ||
	aśraddhālurahaṅkārī rāgadveṣoparūṣitaḥ |



	capalaprakṛtiḥ pāpo niṣkṛpaḥ kṛpaṇo'lasaḥ ||
	prāpnuyād yadi maurkhyeṇa guroretādṛśaḥ pumān |
	ubhau tau kulayoginyaḥ śapanti kṣubhitāḥ krudhā ||
	akṛtvā kaulikīṃ tṛptiṃ viṣṭarāduddharannimām |
	udghāṭayan paṭhan śṛṇvan vyācakṣāṇaśca neṣyate ||
	amantrabindusaṃsparśaṃ kośamasyāḥ parāmṛśan |
	yoginīnāṃ pracaṇdānāmāpāneṣvāmiṣāyate ||
	mūlasaṃvitkalāmātramadhigamyārcayannimām |
	aśnute saiddhimakhilāmavinaśvarasampadam ||
	siddhāntānāṃ yathānyeṣāṃ sāraṃ syādauttaraḥ kramaḥ |
	tathā tasyāpyaśeṣasya sārameṣā'vadhāryatām ||
	bāhyānāmāntarāṇāṃ ca mantrāṇāmujjvalārciṣām |
	vīryameṣaiva boddhavyā parāhantāmayī kalā ||
	jñātavyānāṃ parā kāṣṭhā kartavyānāṃ parā kriyā |
	bhāvyānāṃ ca parā bhūmiriyamīśena kathyate ||
	sahasraśo'pi santvājñāḥ śivabhaṭṭārakaprabhoḥ |
	iyaṃ tu tasya caitanyasarvasvamiti nirṇayaḥ ||
	iyameva śivenoktā śrutismṛtyādivistare |
	anyathākaraṇe heturvyākhyātṝṇāṃ matibhramaḥ ||
	anyatra pāramparyeṇa sākṣādatrārthaniścayaḥ |
	iti vaiṣamyamapyūce bhagavāninduśekharaḥ ||
	āsthāya dhairyamavadhūya vikalpacintā-
		mālūya saṃśayamupāsya ca deśikendram |
	grāhyeyamauttaramahākramatantragarbhā-
		dāryairudanvadudarānmaṇimañjarīva ||
	ādivākyamupakṣepaḥ pratipādyasya vastunaḥ |
	prayojanādeḥ prathamaṃ pramāṇātikramaḥ prabhoḥ ||



	adhikāriṇyaniyamasthitirvidhiniṣedhayoḥ |
	parīkṣā saṃsṛteḥ svasya sphuṭāsphuṭatayā prathā ||
	svavimarśasya sādhyatvaṃ tatsvarūpavimarśanam |
	ṣaṭtriṃśattattvanirṇītistaduttirṇā vicāraṇāḥ ||
	antarbhāvaḥ prapañcasya svaprakāśavimarśayoḥ |
	śivaśaktyorabhinnatvaṃ śaktyutkarṣaścidātmanaḥ ||
	saṃhṛtāvapi viśvasya svasminnavyākulā sthitiḥ |
	pramātrāditrayasyaikyaṃ sadasadbhedabhañjanam ||
	ullokatā saparyāyā mahārthakramavāsanā |
	pūjāsvarūpaniṣkarṣo devatāyā nirūpaṇam ||
	cintanaṃ mantratattvasya vāgvṛttisphurtiniścayaḥ |
	mudrārūpaparāmarśo vimarśasyātmavartinaḥ ||
	prāgalbhyaṃ bhogamokṣaśrīsāmarasyaphalārpaṇe |
	jīvanmukterupanyāsaḥ kṣaṇabhaṅgatiraskriyā ||
	svasyānandasvabhāvatvamāṇavāditrayaṃ kramāt |
	vivecanamupāyānāṃ naiścintyaṃ yogaśālinām ||
	vimarśasyāvilambatvaṃ phalasya pratipādane |
	gurukāruṇyanighnatvaṃ svaparāmarśasampadaḥ ||
	sarvadarśanasāratvamupakrāntasya vastunaḥ |
	uktasarvārthasaṃkṣepo bhāratādyaviruddhatā ||
	upapattiprayogaśca tantrāvataraṇakrame |
	iti svaspandasaṃsiddhyai gṛhyatāṃ tantrasaṃgrahaḥ ||
	iti gurumukhāmnāyanyāyānmahākramamañjarī-
	parimalamimaṃ śiṣyapremṇā babandha maheśvaraḥ |
	kanakasadaso madhye nṛtyanniva prabhuradbhutaṃ 
	yadiha sulabhaḥ sākṣātkartuṃ vimarśamayaḥ śivaḥ ||



	gāthānāmanubhāṣaṇaṃ tadanu tacchāyā tato vyākṛti-
	rgranthārthagrathanakriyāsu gahano hṛdyaśca kaścit kramaḥ |
	saṃvādoktisahasrasaṅkalanayā tattvārthacarcotsavaḥ
	saubhāgyaṃ ca vimarśasampada iti prasthānamadhyakṣyatām ||
	colāste satatotsavā janapadāḥ ślāghyo guṇairmādhavo
	retodhāḥ sphaṭikāvadātahṛdayo nāthaḥ prakaśo mahān |
	srotaḥ svacchamanuttaraṃ pariṇataṃ pāṇḍityamāsthādhikā
	vaśyā vāgiti hanta tantrakṛdasau sarvottaro vartate ||
	satsaṃvitsamayamahābdhikalpavṛkṣānācāryānabhinavagupta-
nāthapādān |
	āmulādamalamatīnupaghnayantyā vāgvallyāḥ pracuraphalo nanu 
prarohaḥ ||
	sāhityābdhau karṇadhāro'hamāsaṃ kāvyālokaṃ locanaṃ 
cānuśīlya |
	tadvat svacchaṃ labdhavānasmi bodhaṃ pāntho bhūtvā 
pratyabhijñāpadavyām ||
	yo me vāmacamatkriyodyamamayaḥ stabdho'pi sannaśnute
	viśvaṃ vyāpnuvataścidadvayamahaścandrodayād vikriyām |
	tasyāhlādamahārṇavasya na kathaṃ vardheta kūlaṅkaṣo 
	vāgvikṣobhavijṛmbhayā bahumukhaṃ kallolahallohalaḥ ||
	kāveryā iva mādhurya kahlārasyeva saurabham |
	naṭeśasyeva tannṛttamasya granthasya gauravam ||
	viśvotpattivipattibhūḥ sa bhagavānatra pravaktā śiva-
	stasyāhaṃ pratiśabdaparvataguhā yanme na garvagrahaḥ |
	taddoṣo'stu guṇo'pi vā na khalu tat svāyattamitthaṃ sthite 
	mātsaryaṃ mahatāmudetu yadi vā vātsalyamujjṛmbhatām ||
	adhivāsayatu sadā mukhamanyakathālepalabdhadaurgandhyam |
	karpūraśakala iva me śiva śiva iti śītalaḥ śabdaḥ ||

iti śivam ||




iti śrīmahāprakāśaśiṣyasya gorakṣāparaparyāyasya 
śrīmanmaheśvarānandasya kṛtirmahārthamañjarīparimalaḥ sampūrṇaḥ ||


_______

kālacakraghaṭīyantrakalpanāmūlaśilpine |
	namaḥ svacchandacinmātraparibarhāya śambhave ||
	pādukodayamahārthamañjarīkomalāstavaparāstutikramaiḥ |
	sphārayan bhuvi māhārthasaṃvidaṃ deśiko vijayate maheśvaraḥ ||
	jayatyamūlamlānamauttaraṃ tattvamadvayam |
	spandāspandaparispandamakarandamahotpalam ||

|| śubhamastu ||
